Attribute VB_Name = "EnterCard"
Attribute VB_Base = "0{9FFF860D-CE97-456C-A68A-1FB98673C4FE}{61B4BCAC-A322-4772-9078-6348427F5C51}"
Attribute VB_GlobalNameSpace = False
Attribute VB_Creatable = False
Attribute VB_PredeclaredId = True
Attribute VB_Exposed = False
Attribute VB_TemplateDerived = False
Attribute VB_Customizable = False
' ==================================================
' 窗体名称: EnterCard (卡牌录入窗体)
' 功能：卡牌信息录入和编辑界面
' 说明：提供完整的卡牌属性设置、效果录入和动态布局调整
' ==================================================

' ========== 通用点击事件处理程序 ==========
' 说明：处理ToggleButton的互斥逻辑，确保同组只有一个按钮被选中
Private Sub ToggleButton_Click(Btn As MSForms.ToggleButton)
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim Flag As Boolean
    Flag = False ' 记录同组是否有按键按下
    
    ' ========== 同组按钮遍历 ==========
    ' 遍历所有控件，处理同组的ToggleButton
    For Each ctrl In Me.Controls
        ' 只处理同组的 ToggleButton
        If ctrl.Tag = Btn.Tag Then
            ' 核心逻辑：当前按钮按下，其他弹起
            If Btn.Value Then ctrl.Value = (ctrl.Name = Btn.Name)
            If ctrl.Value Then Flag = True
        End If
    Next ctrl
    
    ' ========== 默认选中处理 ==========
    ' 如果同组都没有按钮按下，则选中当前按钮
    If Not Flag Then Btn.Value = True
End Sub

' ==================================================
' 事件功能：卡牌名称变更检测
' 说明：当卡牌名称改变时，自动查询并填充已有卡牌信息
' ==================================================
Private Sub CardName_Change()
    ' ========== 卡牌ID查询 ==========
    Dim StrID As String
    StrID = IDByName(CardName.Value)
    
    ' ========== 已有卡牌信息填充 ==========
    ' 如果查询到已有此卡牌，则自动填充所有属性
    If Val(StrID) > 0 Then
        ' ========== 控件遍历和属性设置 ==========
        For Each ctrl In Me.Controls
            ' 继承职业、种类、稀有度
            If ctrl.Tag = "Class" Then
                If ctrl.Name = DownByID(StrID, "Class") Then ctrl.Value = True
            ElseIf ctrl.Tag = "Type" Then
                If ctrl.Name = DownByID(StrID, "Type") Then ctrl.Value = True
            ElseIf ctrl.Tag = "Rarity" Then
                If ctrl.Name = DownByID(StrID, "Rarity") Then ctrl.Value = True
            ElseIf ctrl.Tag = "Effect" Then
                ' 效果文本翻译和显示
                If Len(DownByID(StrID, ctrl.Name)) > 0 Then ctrl.Caption = SentenceTranslate(DownByID(StrID, ctrl.Name))
            End If
        Next ctrl
        
        ' ========== 数值属性填充 ==========
        ' 费用/攻击力/生命值/吟唱
        Cost.Value = DownByID(StrID, "Cost")
        Attack.Value = DownByID(StrID, "Attack")
        Defense.Value = DownByID(StrID, "Defense")
        Countdown.Value = DownByID(StrID, "Countdown")
        
        ' ========== 扩展属性填充 ==========
        ' 卡包/类型/附属卡牌
        CardSet.Value = DownByID(StrID, "CardSet")
        Trait.Value = DownByID(StrID, "Trait")
        
        ' ========== 令牌标识设置 ==========
        ' 根据ID格式判断是否为令牌卡牌
        If Mid(StrID, 4, 1) = "1" Then IsToken = False Else IsToken = True
        
        ' ========== 界面高度调整 ==========
        Call EffectHeight
    End If
End Sub

' ==================================================
' 事件功能：攻击力/生命值输入自动类型判断
' 说明：输入攻击力或生命值时自动设置为从者类型
' ==================================================
Private Sub Attack_Change()
    If Len(Attack.Value) Then Follower.Value = True
    If Len(Attack.Value) Then Countdown.Value = Empty
End Sub

Private Sub Defense_Change()
    If Len(Defense.Value) Then Follower.Value = True
    If Len(Defense.Value) Then Countdown.Value = Empty
End Sub

' ==================================================
' 事件功能：倒计时输入自动类型判断
' 说明：输入倒计时时自动设置为护符类型
' ==================================================
Private Sub Countdown_Change()
    If Len(Countdown.Value) Then Amulet.Value = True
    If Len(Countdown.Value) Then Attack.Value = Empty
    If Len(Countdown.Value) Then Defense.Value = Empty
End Sub

' ==================================================
' 事件功能：卡牌类型互斥处理
' 说明：从者、法术、护符类型之间的互斥逻辑
' ==================================================
Private Sub Follower_Click()
    ToggleButton_Click Follower
    If Follower.Value Then Countdown.Value = Empty
End Sub

Private Sub Spell_Click()
    ToggleButton_Click Spell
    If Spell.Value Then Attack.Value = Empty
    If Spell.Value Then Defense.Value = Empty
    If Spell.Value Then Countdown.Value = Empty
End Sub

Private Sub Amulet_Click()
    ToggleButton_Click Amulet
    If Amulet.Value Then Attack.Value = Empty
    If Amulet.Value Then Defense.Value = Empty
End Sub

Private Sub Effect1_Click()
    Effect 1
End Sub

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：效果录入处理核心逻辑
' 参数：Num - 效果编号
' 说明：处理复杂的效果录入，包括嵌套效果、纹章、信仰等
' ==================================================
Function Effect(Num As Integer)
    ' ========== 效果数据初始化 ==========
    ' 清空卡片效果变量
    FatherEffect = Empty
    CardEffect = Empty
    
    ' ========== 临时卡片录入 ==========
    ' 临时录入当前卡片信息（不保存到数据库）
    Call EnterCardFunction(False)
    
    ' ========== 时间点窗体显示 ==========
    ' 打开时间点选择窗体
    EnterCard·1·TimePoint.Show
    
    ' ========== 效果解析和处理 ==========
    ' 检测CardEffect中是否包含复杂效果结构
    Dim i%, j%, k%, m%
    Dim Str As String
    Dim Str2 As String
    Dim EffectSplit() As String
    Dim CrestID As String
    Dim FaithID As String
    
    FatherEffect = CardEffect
    EffectSplit = SplitEffect(FatherEffect)
    
    ' ========== 信仰效果处理 ==========
    ' 如果是信仰类型的效果
    m = 0
    FaithID = Empty
    If TimePoint = "Faith" Then
        Str = Translate("Faith") & ":" & CardName.Value
        Call DeleteEffect(IDByName(Str), "1000000")
        FaithID = CreateCrest(Str)
        Call UpByID(FaithID, "Type", "Faith")
    End If
    
    ' ========== 纹章效果处理 ==========
    For j = 0 To UBound(EffectSplit)
        ' 处理纹章效果
        k = 0
        CrestID = Empty
        If Left(EffectSplit(j), Len("Crest&")) = "Crest&" Or Left(EffectSplit(j), Len("Enemy-Crest&")) = "Enemy-Crest&" Then
            Str = Translate("Crest") & ":" & CardName.Value
            Call DeleteEffect(IDByName(Str), "1000000")
            CrestID = CreateCrest(Str)
            
            ' 解析倒计时数值
            If Left(EffectSplit(j), Len("Crest&")) = "Crest&" Then i = Val(Mid(EffectSplit(j), 1 + Len("Crest&"), 10)) Else i = Val(Mid(EffectSplit(j), 1 + Len("Enemy-Crest&"), 10))
            If i > 0 Then Call UpByID(CrestID, "Countdown", i)
        End If
        
        ' ========== 嵌套效果替换处理 ==========
        ' 遍历替换{Effect}占位符
        Str = Empty
        Str2 = Empty
        For i = 1 To 100
            If InStr(EffectSplit(j), "{Effect}") > 0 Then
                ' ========== 嵌套效果录入 ==========
                ' 清空卡片效果并重新录入
                CardEffect = Empty
                Call EnterCardFunction(False)
                EnterCard·1·TimePoint.Show
                
                ' ========== 效果替换 ==========
                ' 替换{Effect}占位符为实际效果
                EffectSplit(j) = Replace(EffectSplit(j), "{Effect}", "{" & CardEffect & "}", 1, 1)
                
                ' ========== 纹章效果嵌套处理 ==========
                ' 处理纹章中的嵌套效果
                If CrestID <> "" Then
                    If Str <> "" Then Str = Replace(Str, "{Effect}", "{" & CardEffect & "}", 1, 1) Else Str = CardEffect
                    If InStr(Str, "{Effect}") = 0 And InStr(Str, "{Ability}") = 0 Then
                        k = k + 1
                        Call UpByID(CrestID, "Effect" & k, Str)
                        Str = Empty
                    End If
                End If
                
                ' ========== 信仰效果嵌套处理 ==========
                ' 处理信仰中的嵌套效果
                If FaithID <> "" Then
                    If Str2 <> "" Then Str2 = Replace(Str2, "{Effect}", "{" & CardEffect & "}", 1, 1) Else Str2 = CardEffect
                    If InStr(Str2, "{Effect}") = 0 And InStr(Str2, "{Ability}") = 0 Then
                        m = m + 1
                        Call UpByID(FaithID, "Effect" & m, Str2)
                        Str2 = Empty
                    End If
                End If
                
            ' ========== 能力效果替换处理 ==========
            ElseIf InStr(EffectSplit(j), "{Ability}") > 0 Then
                ' 清空卡片效果并设置时间点
                CardEffect = TimePoint & "||"
                Call EnterCardFunction(False)
                
                ' ========== 能力窗体显示 ==========
                ' 打开能力录入窗体
                EnterCard·3·Ability.Show
                
                ' ========== 能力替换 ==========
                ' 替换{Ability}占位符为实际能力
                EffectSplit(j) = Replace(EffectSplit(j), "{Ability}", "{" & Mid(CardEffect, 1 + Len(TimePoint & "||"), 1000) & "}", 1, 1)
                
                ' ========== 纹章效果嵌套处理 ==========
                ' 处理纹章中的嵌套效果
                If CrestID <> "" Then
                    If Str <> "" Then Str = Replace(Str, "{Ability}", "{" & Mid(CardEffect, 1 + Len(TimePoint & "||"), 1000) & "}", 1, 1) Else Str = CardEffect
                    If InStr(Str, "{Effect}") = 0 And InStr(Str, "{Ability}") = 0 Then
                        k = k + 1
                        Call UpByID(CrestID, "Effect" & k, Str)
                        Str = Empty
                    End If
                End If
                
                ' ========== 信仰效果嵌套处理 ==========
                ' 处理信仰中的嵌套效果
                If FaithID <> "" Then
                    If Str2 <> "" Then Str2 = Replace(Str2, "{Ability}", "{" & Mid(CardEffect, 1 + Len(TimePoint & "||"), 1000) & "}", 1, 1) Else Str2 = CardEffect
                    If InStr(Str2, "{Effect}") = 0 And InStr(Str2, "{Ability}") = 0 Then
                        m = m + 1
                        Call UpByID(FaithID, "Effect" & m, Str2)
                        Str2 = Empty
                    End If
                End If
            Else
                Exit For
            End If
        Next i
    Next j
    
    ' ========== 效果合并 ==========
    FatherEffect = Join(EffectSplit, "||")
    
    ' ========== 变量效果处理 (X, Y, Z) ==========
    ' 检测并处理效果中的变量占位符
    If InStr(FatherEffect, "X") > 0 And InStr(FatherEffect, "Spellboost|X=") = 0 Then
        XEffect = Empty
        EnterCard·5·X.Show
        
        FatherEffect = FatherEffect & "||X=" & XEffect
        
        ' 纹章中的变量处理
        If CrestID <> "" Then
            For i = 1 To 9
                Str = DownByID(CrestID, "Effect" & i)
                If Str = "" Then Exit For
                If InStr(Str, "X") > 0 Then
                    Str = Str & "||X=" & XEffect
                    Call UpByID(CrestID, "Effect" & i, Str)
                End If
            Next i
        End If
    End If
    
    ' Y变量处理
    If InStr(FatherEffect, "Y") > 0 Then
        XEffect = Empty
        EnterCard·5·X.Show
        
        FatherEffect = FatherEffect & "||Y=" & XEffect
        
        ' 纹章中的Y变量处理
        If CrestID <> "" Then
            For i = 1 To 9
                Str = DownByID(CrestID, "Effect" & i)
                If Str = "" Then Exit For
                If InStr(Str, "Y") > 0 Then
                    Str = Str & "||Y=" & XEffect
                    Call UpByID(CrestID, "Effect" & i, Str)
                End If
            Next i
        End If
    End If
    
    ' Z变量处理
    If InStr(FatherEffect, "Z") > 0 Then
        XEffect = Empty
        EnterCard·5·X.Show
        
        FatherEffect = FatherEffect & "||Z=" & XEffect
        
        ' 纹章中的Z变量处理
        If CrestID <> "" Then
            For i = 1 To 9
                Str = DownByID(CrestID, "Effect" & i)
                If Str = "" Then Exit For
                If InStr(Str, "Z") > 0 Then
                    Str = Str & "||Z=" & XEffect
                    Call UpByID(CrestID, "Effect" & i, Str)
                End If
            Next i
        End If
    End If
    
    ' ========== 效果文本显示 ==========
    ' 遍历效果框并填入处理后的效果文本
    For i = 1 To Num
        For Each ctrl In Me.Controls
            If ctrl.Name = "Effect" & i Then Exit For
        Next ctrl
        
        ' ========== 效果填入逻辑 ==========
        ' 在当前效果框或第一个空效果框中填入文本
        If i = Num Or ctrl.Caption = Translate("ClickToInput") Then
            ctrl.Caption = SentenceTranslate(FatherEffect)
            If Len(FatherEffect) = 0 Then ctrl.Caption = Translate("ClickToInput")
            Call EffectHeight
            Exit For
        End If
    Next i
End Function

' ========== 分割线：窗体初始化部分 ==========

' ==================================================
' 事件功能：窗体激活初始化
' 说明：窗体显示时自动设置默认值和选中状态
' ==================================================
Private Sub UserForm_Activate()
    ' ========== 卡包自动设置 ==========
    ' 自动填入最新卡包
    Dim CardSetrr() As String ' 记录卡包范围
    CardSetrr = Split(Down("CardSet"), "、") ' 获取卡包范围
    CardSet.Value = CardSetrr(UBound(CardSetrr))  ' 自动填入最新卡包
    
    ' ========== 职业自动选择 ==========
    ' 自动按下所选职业
    Dim Class As Variant ' 记录职业
    Class = Translate(Down("Class")) ' 获取职业
    For Each ctrl In Me.Controls ' 遍历控件
        If TypeName(ctrl) = "ToggleButton" And ctrl.Name = Class Then ' 找到对应职业按下
            ctrl.Value = True
            Exit For
        End If
    Next ctrl
    
    ' ========== 默认类型设置 ==========
    ' 默认为从者、铜卡
    Follower.Value = True
    Bronze.Value = True
    
    ' ========== 选中卡牌自动填充 ==========
    ' 如果选中了卡牌，则自动填入名称
    Dim Arr As Variant
    Arr = IsSelected("CardSet·Cards")
    If Not IsEmpty(Arr) Then
        CardName.Value = Arr
    End If
End Sub

' ==================================================
' 事件功能：录入完成处理
' 说明：点击完成按钮时保存卡牌信息并关闭窗体
' ==================================================
Private Sub Finish_Click()
    ' ========== 卡牌信息保存 ==========
    Call EnterCardFunction(True)
    
    ' ========== 窗体关闭 ==========
    Unload EnterCard
End Sub

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：卡牌录入核心逻辑
' 参数：IsTrue - 是否为正式录入（True）或临时录入（False）
' 说明：处理卡牌信息的保存和ID生成逻辑
' ==================================================
Function EnterCardFunction(IsTrue As Boolean)
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim newID As String
    Dim Class As String
    Dim Rarity As String
    Dim CardType As String
    Dim i%
    
    ' ========== 卡牌名称验证 ==========
    If CardName.Value = "" Then Exit Function
    
    ' ========== 选中属性获取 ==========
    ' 遍历控件获取选中的职业、稀有度和类型
    For Each ctrl In Me.Controls
        If TypeName(ctrl) = "ToggleButton" Then
            If ctrl.Tag = "Class" And ctrl.Value Then Class = ctrl.Name
            If ctrl.Tag = "Rarity" And ctrl.Value Then Rarity = ctrl.Name
            If ctrl.Tag = "Type" And ctrl.Value Then CardType = ctrl.Name
        End If
    Next ctrl
    
    ' ========== ID生成规则 ==========
    ' ID规则：AABCDXX
    '   AA代表卡包，从10开始，意味着最多90个卡包
    '   B代表职业，0中立，1精灵，2皇家护卫，3巫师，4龙族，5梦魇，6主教，7超越者
    '   C代表是否可构筑，0不可构筑，1可构筑
    '   D代表稀有度，1虹，2金，3银，4铜
    '   XX代表序号，从01开始，意味着同条件下最多99张卡片
    
    ' ========== ID生成逻辑 ==========
    newID = "1000000" ' 临时录入ID为1000000
    If IsTrue Then
        newID = GetCardSetID(CardSet.Value) & IDTranslate("Class", Class)
        If IsToken Then newID = newID & 0 Else newID = newID & 1
        newID = newID & IDTranslate("Rarity", Rarity)
        newID = GetCardID(newID)
        
        ' ========== 同名记录处理 ==========
        ' 删除已有的同名记录
        Dim StrID As String
        StrID = IDByName(CardName.Value)
        If Val(StrID) > 0 Then Call DeleteEffect(StrID, newID)
    End If
    
    ' ========== 卡牌信息保存 ==========
    ' 基础属性保存
    Call UpByID(newID, "CardSet", CardSet.Value)
    Call UpByID(newID, "Class", Class)
    Call UpByID(newID, "Rarity", Rarity)
    Call UpByID(newID, "Name", CardName.Value)
    Call UpByID(newID, "Type", CardType)
    
    ' ========== 扩展属性保存 ==========
    ' 类型创建和保存
    If Len(Trait.Value) > 0 Then Call IDTranslate("Trait", Trait.Value, True) ' 创建类型ID
    Call UpByID(newID, "Trait", Trait.Value)
    
    ' ========== 数值属性保存 ==========
    Call UpByID(newID, "Cost", Cost.Value)
    Call UpByID(newID, "Attack", Attack.Value)
    Call UpByID(newID, "Defense", Defense.Value)
    Call UpByID(newID, "Countdown", Countdown.Value)
    
    ' ========== 效果文本保存 ==========
    For Each ctrl In Me.Controls
        If ctrl.Tag = "Effect" Then
            If ctrl.Caption <> "点击输入" Then Call UpByID(newID, ctrl.Name, SentenceTranslate(ctrl.Caption))
        End If
    Next ctrl
End Function

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：效果区域高度调整
' 说明：根据效果文本的长度动态调整效果控件的高度
' 返回：无
' ==================================================
Function EffectHeight()
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i%, j%
    Dim Height%
    
    ' ========== 效果控件遍历 ==========
    ' 遍历9个效果控件，逐个调整高度
    For i = 1 To 9
        ' ========== 控件查找 ==========
        ' 查找对应的效果控件
        For Each ctrl In Me.Controls
            If ctrl.Name = "Effect" & i Then Exit For
        Next ctrl
        
        ' ========== 高度计算 ==========
        ' 根据文本长度计算需要调整的高度
        Height = Int(Len(ctrl.Caption) / 50) * 10 + 10 - ctrl.Height
        
        ' ========== 高度调整 ==========
        ' 如果计算出的高度变化不为零，则进行调整
        If Height <> 0 Then
            ' 调整当前效果控件的高度
            ctrl.Height = ctrl.Height + Height
            
            ' ========== 相关控件位置调整 ==========
            ' 调整效果框架控件的位置和高度
            For Each ctrl In Me.Controls
                If ctrl.Tag = "EffectFrame" Then
                    ' 调整后续效果框架的位置
                    If Val(Mid(ctrl.Name, Len("EffectFrame") + 1, 10)) > i Then ctrl.Top = ctrl.Top + Height
                    ' 调整当前效果框架的高度
                    If Val(Mid(ctrl.Name, Len("EffectFrame") + 1, 10)) = i Then ctrl.Height = ctrl.Height + Height
                End If
            Next ctrl
        End If
    Next i
End Function


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·0·1·基础·Down·Up·IsSelec.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·0·1·基础·Down·Up·IsSelec"
' ==================================================
' 模块功能：主界面单元格区域配置与数据操作
' 包含功能：定义界面区域坐标、读取区域数据、写入区域数据、区域选择检测
' ==================================================

Option Explicit

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：获取所有界面区域的坐标配置
' 返回：字符串数组，包含所有区域配置信息
' 格式：区域名称|工作表名|起始行|起始列|结束行|结束列|方向(可选)
' ==================================================
Function SetCellsRC() As String()
    Dim Str As String
    
    ' 构建区域配置字符串
    Str = "EnemyDefense|Main|2|2" & _
        "||EnemyMaxDefense|Main|3|2" & _
        "||EnemyPP|Main|4|2" & _
        "||EnemyMaxPP|Main|5|2" & _
        "||EnemyEP|Main|6|2" & _
        "||EnemySEP|Main|7|2" & _
        "||AlliedDefense|Main|8|2" & _
        "||AlliedMaxDefense|Main|9|2" & _
        "||AlliedPP|Main|10|2" & _
        "||AlliedMaxPP|Main|11|2" & _
        "||AlliedEP|Main|12|2" & _
        "||AlliedSEP|Main|13|2" & _
        "||EnemyField|Main|2|3|4|7|Horizontal" & _
        "||AlliedField|Main|8|3|10|7|Horizontal" & _
        "||Hand|Main|14|1|15|9|Horizontal" & _
        "||Crest|Main|17|2|19|6|Horizontal" & _
        "||Deck·Cards|Main|18|8" & _
        "||Shadows|Main|18|9" & _
        "||ClassKeyWord|Main|19|8|20|8" & _
        "||Turn|Main|4|9" & _
        "||FirstMove|Main|2|9" & _
        "||BattleYourself|Main|3|9" & _
        "||GetPP|Main|8|9" & _
        "||UIEnemyHands|Main|20|9"
    
    ' 数据记录区域配置
    Str = Str & _
        "||StepsArea|Main|21|2|100|2" & _
        "||PlayArea|Main|3|11|100|12" & _
        "||DestroyArea|Main|3|14|100|15" & _
        "||CountArea|Main|3|17|100|18" & _
        "||Data|Data|2|1|999|5" & _
        "||Steps|Data|2|7|9999|10" & _
        "||Count|Data|2|12|99|13" & _
        "||CountNum|Data|2|13|99|13"
        
    ' 卡组和路径配置
    Str = Str & _
        "||Class|Main|2|24" & _
        "||DeckName|Main|4|24" & _
        "||CardSet|Main|11|24" & _
        "||Path|Main|12|24" & _
        "||OrigDeck|Main|3|20|42|22|Vertical" & _
        "||Legendary|Main|5|25|10|25" & _
        "||Gold|Main|5|26|10|26" & _
        "||Silver|Main|5|27|10|27" & _
        "||Bronze|Main|5|28|10|28" & _
        "||CardSet·Cards|Main|5|25|10|28" & _
        "||Path|Main|12|24" & _
        "||Head|Main|1|1" & _
        "||Log|对局日志|2|1|9999|3"


    SetCellsRC = Split(Str, "||")   ' 使用双竖线分割配置字符串
End Function

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：从指定区域读取数据
' 参数：Target - 目标区域名称
'        MatchID - 可选，用于匹配特定ID
' 返回：区域数据，可能是值、数组或空值
' ==================================================
Function Down(Target As String, Optional MatchID As String = "") As Variant
    Dim i As Integer
    Dim CellsRC() As String
    Dim Strr() As String
    Dim Data As Variant
    Dim Str As String
    
    CellsRC = SetCellsRC()   ' 获取所有区域配置
    
    ' 处理特殊数据类型区域
    If Target = "Hand" Or Target = "Field" Or Target = "Crest" Or Target = "Deck" Then
        Data = Down("Data")
        
        ' 根据目标类型和ID过滤数据
        For i = 1 To UBound(Data, 1)
            If Data(i, 4) <> Target Then Data(i, 1) = Empty
            If MatchID <> "" And Left(Data(i, 1), 1) <> Left(MatchID, 1) Then Data(i, 1) = Empty
        Next i
        
        Data = NonEmptyArray(Data)   ' 返回非空数据数组
        Down = Data
        Exit Function
        
    ' 处理战斗属性区域（根据先手判断敌我）
    ElseIf Target = "Defense" Or Target = "MaxDefense" Or _
        Target = "PP" Or Target = "MaxPP" Or _
        Target = "EP" Or Target = "SEP" Then
            If Left(Down("FirstMove"), 1) = Left(MatchID, 1) Then
                Target = "Allied" & Target   ' 我方属性
            Else
                Target = "Enemy" & Target    ' 敌方属性
            End If
            
    ' 处理计数区域查询
    ElseIf MatchID <> "" Then
        Data = Down("Count")
        
        ' 查找匹配的计数项
        For i = 1 To UBound(Data, 1)
            If Data(i, 1) = Left(MatchID, 1) & Target Then
                Down = Data(i, 2)   ' 返回计数值
                Exit Function
            End If
        Next i
    End If
    
    ' 遍历区域配置查找目标区域
    For i = 0 To UBound(CellsRC, 1)
        Strr = Split(CellsRC(i), "|")
        
        If Strr(0) = Target Then
            ' 处理矩形区域（多单元格）
            If UBound(Strr) >= 5 Then
                Down = Range(Sheets(Strr(1)).Cells(Val(Strr(2)), Val(Strr(3))), _
                       Sheets(Strr(1)).Cells(Val(Strr(4)), Val(Strr(5))))
            ' 处理单个单元格
            Else
                Down = Sheets(Strr(1)).Cells(Val(Strr(2)), Val(Strr(3)))
            End If
            
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next i
End Function

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：将数据写入指定区域
' 参数：Target - 目标区域名称
'        Arr - 要写入的数据数组
'        MatchID - 可选，用于匹配特定ID
' 返回：无
' ==================================================
Function Up(Target As String, Arr As Variant, Optional MatchID As String = "") As Variant
    Dim i As Integer
    Dim CellsRC() As String
    Dim Strr() As String
    Dim Data As Variant
    
    CellsRC = SetCellsRC()   ' 获取所有区域配置
    
    ' 处理特殊数据类型区域
    If Target = "Field" Or _
        Target = "Defense" Or Target = "MaxDefense" Or _
        Target = "PP" Or Target = "MaxPP" Or _
        Target = "EP" Or Target = "SEP" Then
            ' 根据先手判断敌我区域
            If Left(Down("FirstMove"), 1) = Left(MatchID, 1) Then
                Target = "Allied" & Target   ' 我方区域
            Else
                Target = "Enemy" & Target    ' 敌方区域
            End If
            
    ' 处理计数区域更新
    ElseIf MatchID <> "" Then
        Data = Down("Count")
        
        ' 更新匹配的计数项
        For i = 1 To UBound(Data, 1)
            If Data(i, 1) = Left(MatchID, 1) & Target Then
                Data(i, 2) = Arr
                Call Up("Count", Data)   ' 写回更新后的计数数据
                Exit Function
            End If
        Next i
    End If
    
    ' 遍历区域配置查找目标区域
    For i = 0 To UBound(CellsRC, 1)
        Strr = Split(CellsRC(i), "|")
        
        If Strr(0) = Target Then
            ' 处理矩形区域（多单元格）
            If UBound(Strr) >= 5 Then
                ' 如果传入数组为空，则重新定义数组大小
                If IsEmpty(Arr) Then
                    ReDim Arr(1 To Val(Strr(4)) - Val(Strr(2)) + 1, _
                             1 To Val(Strr(5)) - Val(Strr(3)) + 1)
                End If
                
                ' 写入数据到指定范围
                Range(Sheets(Strr(1)).Cells(Val(Strr(2)), Val(Strr(3))), _
                      Sheets(Strr(1)).Cells(Val(Strr(4)), Val(Strr(5)))) = Arr
            ' 处理单个单元格
            Else
                Sheets(Strr(1)).Cells(Val(Strr(2)), Val(Strr(3))) = Arr
            End If
            
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next i
End Function

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：检查当前选择是否在目标区域内并返回数据
' 参数：Target - 目标区域名称
' 返回：选中单元格的数据或空值
' ==================================================
Function IsSelected(Target As String) As Variant
    Dim i As Integer
    Dim CellsRC() As String
    Dim Strr() As String
    
    CellsRC = SetCellsRC()   ' 获取所有区域配置
    
    ' 遍历区域配置查找目标区域
    For i = 0 To UBound(CellsRC, 1)
        Strr = Split(CellsRC(i), "|")
        
        If Strr(0) = Target Then
            ' 检查工作表是否匹配
            If ActiveSheet.Name <> Strr(1) Then
                Exit For
                
            ' 处理单个单元格区域
            ElseIf UBound(Strr) = 3 Then
                If Selection.Row = Val(Strr(2)) And Selection.Column = Val(Strr(3)) Then
                    IsSelected = Sheets(Strr(1)).Cells(Selection.Row, Selection.Column)
                End If
                
            ' 处理矩形区域
            ElseIf UBound(Strr) >= 5 Then
                ' 检查选择是否在区域范围内
                If Selection.Row >= Val(Strr(2)) And Selection.Row <= Val(Strr(4)) And _
                   Selection.Column >= Val(Strr(3)) And Selection.Column <= Val(Strr(5)) Then
                   
                    ' 处理有方向设置的区域
                    If UBound(Strr) >= 6 Then
                        If Strr(6) = "Vertical" Then
                            ' 垂直方向：返回整列数据
                            IsSelected = Sheets(Strr(1)).Cells(Selection.Row, Val(Strr(3)))
                            
                            ' OrigDeck特殊处理
                            If Target = "OrigDeck" Then
                                IsSelected = Sheets(Strr(1)).Cells(Selection.Row, Val(Strr(3)) + 1)
                            End If
                            
                        ElseIf Strr(6) = "Horizontal" Then
                            ' 水平方向：返回整行数据
                            IsSelected = Sheets(Strr(1)).Cells(Val(Strr(2)), Selection.Column)
                        End If
                    ' 无方向设置：返回当前单元格
                    Else
                        IsSelected = Sheets("Main").Cells(Selection.Row, Selection.Column)
                    End If
                End If
            End If
            
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next i
    
    IsSelected = Empty   ' 无匹配区域返回空值
End Function

文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·2·1·录卡·GetCardSetID.bas

Attribute VB_Name = "·2·1·录卡·GetCardSetID"
' ==================================================
' 过程功能：打开录卡窗口
' ==================================================
Sub ShowEnterCard()
    ' ========== 优化设置 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    
    EnterCard.Show   ' 显示录卡窗体
    
CleanUp:
    ' ========== 恢复应用程序状态 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
End Sub

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：卡包ID生成器
' 参数：CardSet - 卡包名称
' 返回：卡包ID字符串（两位数字）或错误信息
' ID规则：AABCDXX（整体卡牌ID结构，本函数生成AA部分）
'   AA代表卡包，从10开始，最多90个卡包（10-99）
'   B代表职业（0中立，1精灵，2皇家，3巫师，4龙族，5梦魇，6主教，7超越者）
'   C代表是否可构筑（0不可构筑，1可构筑）
'   D代表稀有度（1虹，2金，3银，4铜）
'   XX代表序号，从01开始，同条件下最多99张卡片
' ==================================================
Function GetCardSetID(CardSet As String) As String
    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler
    
    ' ========== 常量定义 ==========
    Const SHEET_NAME As String = "Effect"   ' 数据源工作表名称
    Const START_ID As Integer = 10           ' 起始卡包ID
    Const END_ID As Integer = 99             ' 结束卡包ID（两位数范围）
    
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim ws As Worksheet              ' 工作表对象
    Dim lastRow As Long              ' 最后数据行号
    Dim i As Long                    ' 循环计数器
    Dim idValue As String            ' 临时存储ID值
    Dim foundID As String            ' 找到的卡包ID
    Dim existingIDs As Object        ' 已使用ID字典
    Dim newID As Integer             ' 新ID编号
    
    ' ========== 工作表初始化 ==========
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.Sheets(SHEET_NAME)   ' 设置Effect工作表
    
    ' 获取B列最后一行数据位置
    lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.rows.count, "B").End(xlUp).Row
    
    ' ========== 已使用ID收集 ==========
    Set existingIDs = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")   ' 创建字典存储已用ID
    
    ' 遍历Effect工作表查找卡包并收集所有已用ID
    For i = 3 To lastRow   ' 从第3行开始（假设第1-2行是标题）
        ' 检查当前行是否匹配目标卡包
        If LCase(Trim(ws.Cells(i, 2).Value)) = LCase(Trim(CardSet)) Then
            idValue = CStr(ws.Cells(i, 1).Value)   ' 获取卡牌ID
            
            ' 提取ID前两位作为卡包ID
            If Len(idValue) >= 2 Then
                foundID = Left(idValue, 2)
                GetCardSetID = foundID   ' 返回找到的卡包ID
                Exit Function
            End If
        End If
        
        ' 收集所有已使用的ID前两位（用于后续新ID生成）
        If Not IsEmpty(ws.Cells(i, 1).Value) Then
            idValue = CStr(ws.Cells(i, 1).Value)
            If Len(idValue) >= 2 Then
                existingIDs(Left(idValue, 2)) = True   ' 记录已使用的两位ID
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 新卡包ID生成 ==========
    newID = START_ID   ' 从起始ID开始查找
    
    ' 在10-99范围内查找第一个可用ID
    Do While newID <= END_ID
        Dim newIDStr As String
        newIDStr = Format(newID, "00")   ' 格式化为两位数字符串
        
        ' 检查ID是否未被使用
        If Not existingIDs.Exists(newIDStr) Then
            ' 注释掉的代码：实际使用时可能需要添加新行到工作表
            'ws.Cells(lastRow + 1, 1).Value = newIDStr    ' A列：卡包ID
            'ws.Cells(lastRow + 1, 2).Value = CardSet     ' B列：卡包名称
            
            GetCardSetID = newIDStr   ' 返回新生成的卡包ID
            Exit Function
        End If
        
        newID = newID + 1   ' 检查下一个ID
    Loop
    
    ' ========== ID范围已满处理 ==========
    GetCardSetID = "ID overflow"   ' 所有ID都被使用
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    ' ========== 错误处理 ==========
    GetCardSetID = "Error: " & Err.Description   ' 返回错误信息
End Function


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·1·1·翻译·Translate.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·1·1·翻译·Translate"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：文本翻译函数，支持中英互译和多种ID转换
' 参数：InputText - 需要翻译的文本
' 返回：翻译后的文本或原始文本（未找到匹配时）
' 功能：1. 中英互译 2. 卡牌ID与名称转换 3. 特性/卡组类型翻译
' ==================================================
Function Translate(InputText As String) As String
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim dictSheet As Worksheet        ' 字典工作表对象
    Dim dictData As Variant           ' 字典数据数组
    Dim lastRow As Long               ' 字典最后行号
    Dim i As Long                     ' 循环计数器
    Dim isChinese As Boolean          ' 输入文本语言标识
    Static dictCache As Variant       ' 静态变量缓存字典数据
    Static lastCacheTime As Date      ' 记录上次缓存时间
    
    ' ========== 输入验证 ==========
    If Len(InputText) = 0 Then
        Translate = "输入不能为空"   ' 空输入处理
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' ========== 字典工作表初始化 ==========
    On Error Resume Next
    Set dictSheet = ThisWorkbook.Sheets("Dictionary")   ' 尝试获取字典工作表
    On Error GoTo 0
    
    If dictSheet Is Nothing Then
        Translate = "未找到Dictionary工作表"   ' 工作表不存在处理
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' ========== 语言检测 ==========
    ' 通过字符编码判断是否为中文（中文字符编码大于127）
    isChinese = False
    For i = 1 To Len(InputText)
        If Abs(AscW(Mid(InputText, i, 1))) > 127 Then
            isChinese = True   ' 检测到中文字符
            Exit For
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 字典数据缓存处理 ==========
    ' 缓存策略：每分钟更新一次，避免频繁读取工作表
    If IsEmpty(dictCache) Or Now - lastCacheTime > TimeValue("00:01:00") Then
        lastRow = dictSheet.Cells(dictSheet.rows.count, 1).End(xlUp).Row
        dictData = dictSheet.Range("A1:B" & lastRow).Value   ' 读取A、B列数据
        dictCache = dictData          ' 更新缓存
        lastCacheTime = Now           ' 更新缓存时间
    Else
        dictData = dictCache          ' 使用缓存数据
    End If
    
    ' ========== 字典查找翻译 ==========
    If isChinese Then
        ' 中文→英文：在第二列（中文）查找，返回第一列（英文）
        For i = 1 To UBound(dictData, 1)
            If Trim(dictData(i, 2)) = Trim(InputText) Then
                Translate = dictData(i, 1)
                Exit Function
            End If
        Next i
    Else
        ' 英文→中文：在第一列（英文）查找，返回第二列（中文）
        For i = 1 To UBound(dictData, 1)
            If Trim(dictData(i, 1)) = Trim(InputText) Then
                Translate = dictData(i, 2)
                Exit Function
            End If
        Next i
    End If
    
    ' ========== 卡牌ID与名称转换 ==========
    Dim Str As String   ' 临时存储转换结果
    
    ' 尝试通过ID查找卡牌名称
    Str = DownByID(InputText, "Name")
    If Str <> "" Then
        Translate = Str
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' 尝试通过名称查找卡牌ID
    Str = IDByName(InputText)
    If Val(Str) > 0 Then
        Translate = Str
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' ========== 特性(Trait)类型翻译 ==========
    If Left(InputText, Len("Trait:")) = "Trait:" Then
        ' 格式："Trait:ID" → "特性:名称"
        Str = IDTranslate("Trait", Mid(InputText, 1 + Len("Trait:"), 10))
        If Str <> "-1" Then
            Translate = Translate("Trait") & ":" & Str   ' 递归调用翻译"Trait"
            Exit Function
        End If
    ElseIf Left(InputText, 3) = Translate("Trait") & ":" Then
        ' 格式："特性:名称" → "Trait:ID"
        Str = IDTranslate("Trait", Mid(InputText, 4, 10))
        If Str <> "-1" Then
            Translate = "Trait:" & Str
            Exit Function
        End If
    
    ' ========== 卡组(Deck)类型翻译 ==========
    ElseIf Left(InputText, Len("Deck:")) = "Deck:" Then
        ' 格式："Deck:ID" → "卡组:名称"
        Str = IDTranslate("Deck", Mid(InputText, 1 + Len("Deck:"), 10))
        If Str <> "-1" Then
            Translate = Translate("Deck") & ":" & Str   ' 递归调用翻译"Deck"
            Exit Function
        End If
    ElseIf Left(InputText, 3) = Translate("Deck") & ":" Then
        ' 格式："卡组:名称" → "Deck:ID"
        Str = IDTranslate("Deck", Mid(InputText, 4, 10))
        If Str <> "-1" Then
            Translate = "Deck:" & Str
            Exit Function
        End If
    End If
    
    ' ========== 默认返回 ==========
    Translate = InputText   ' 未找到任何匹配，返回原始文本
End Function

文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·2·2·录卡·GetCardID.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·2·2·录卡·GetCardID"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：卡牌ID后缀生成器，创建卡牌的下一位ID（仅后两位）
' 参数：prefix - 卡牌ID前缀（通常为卡包ID+职业+可构筑+稀有度）
' 返回：完整卡牌ID字符串或错误信息
' 说明：在给定前缀下查找01-99范围内第一个可用的后缀数字
' ==================================================
Function GetCardID(prefix As String) As String
    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler
    
    ' ========== 常量定义 ==========
    Const SHEET_NAME As String = "Effect"    ' 数据源工作表名称
    Const ID_COL As Integer = 1              ' ID所在列（A列）
    Const MAX_NUM As Integer = 99            ' 最大后缀数字
    
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim ws As Worksheet              ' 工作表对象
    Dim lastRow As Long              ' 最后数据行号
    Dim i As Long                    ' 循环计数器
    Dim idValue As String            ' 临时存储ID值
    Dim existingNumbers As Object    ' 已使用后缀数字字典
    Dim Num As Integer               ' 当前检查的数字
    
    ' ========== 工作表初始化 ==========
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.Sheets(SHEET_NAME)   ' 设置Effect工作表
    
    ' 获取ID列最后一行数据位置
    lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.rows.count, ID_COL).End(xlUp).Row
    
    ' ========== 已使用后缀数字收集 ==========
    Set existingNumbers = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")   ' 创建字典存储已用后缀
    
    ' 遍历Effect工作表查找匹配前缀的ID
    For i = 2 To lastRow   ' 从第2行开始（假设第1行是标题）
        idValue = CStr(ws.Cells(i, ID_COL).Value)   ' 获取卡牌ID字符串
        
        ' 检查ID是否以指定前缀开头
        If Left(idValue, Len(prefix)) = prefix Then
            ' 提取后缀部分（前缀后的两位数字）
            Dim suffix As String
            suffix = Mid(idValue, Len(prefix) + 1, 2)
            
            ' 验证后缀是否为有效数字
            If IsNumeric(suffix) Then
                Dim numValue As Integer
                numValue = CInt(suffix)   ' 转换为整数
                
                ' 只记录01-99范围内的有效数字
                If numValue >= 1 And numValue <= MAX_NUM Then
                    existingNumbers(numValue) = True   ' 记录已使用的后缀数字
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 可用后缀数字查找 ==========
    ' 在1-99范围内顺序查找第一个可用数字
    For Num = 1 To MAX_NUM
        If Not existingNumbers.Exists(Num) Then
            ' 格式化为两位数字符串并与前缀组合
            GetCardID = prefix & Format(Num, "00")
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next Num
    
    ' ========== 无可用后缀处理 ==========
    GetCardID = "ID溢出: 无可用后缀"   ' 所有后缀数字都被使用
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    ' ========== 错误处理 ==========
    GetCardID = "错误: " & Err.Description   ' 返回错误信息
End Function


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·2·5·录卡·UpByID.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·2·5·录卡·UpByID"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：根据ID更新或创建效果记录
' 参数：idValue - 卡牌ID
'        HeaderName - 属性列标题名称
'        NewValue - 新的属性值
' 返回：Boolean - 操作是否成功
' 说明：支持更新现有记录或创建新记录，自动处理列定位
' ==================================================
Function UpByID(idValue As String, HeaderName As String, NewValue As Variant) As Boolean
    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler
    
    ' ========== 常量定义 ==========
    Const SHEET_NAME As String = "Effect"      ' 效果数据工作表名称
    Const ID_HEADER As String = "ID"           ' ID列标题名称
    
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim ws As Worksheet           ' 工作表对象
    Dim headerRow As Range        ' 标题行范围
    Dim idCol As Range            ' ID列范围
    Dim headerCol As Range        ' 目标属性列范围
    Dim idCell As Range           ' ID查找结果单元格
    Dim targetRow As Long         ' 目标行号
    Dim lastRow As Long           ' 最后数据行号
    Dim newRow As Long            ' 新行号
    
    ' ========== 工作表初始化 ==========
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.Sheets(SHEET_NAME)   ' 设置Effect工作表
    
    ' ========== 列标题定位 ==========
    Set headerRow = ws.rows(1)   ' 设置标题行（第一行）
    
    ' 查找ID列位置
    Set idCol = headerRow.Find(What:=ID_HEADER, LookAt:=xlWhole, MatchCase:=False)
    ' 查找目标属性列位置
    Set headerCol = headerRow.Find(What:=HeaderName, LookAt:=xlWhole, MatchCase:=False)
    
    ' ========== 列标题验证 ==========
    If idCol Is Nothing Then
        MsgBox "错误：未找到ID列标题", vbExclamation
        UpByID = False
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    If headerCol Is Nothing Then
        MsgBox "错误：未找到表头'" & HeaderName & "'", vbExclamation
        UpByID = False
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' ========== 现有记录查找 ==========
    ' 在ID列中精确查找指定ID
    Set idCell = ws.Columns(idCol.Column).Find( _
        What:=idValue, _
        LookIn:=xlValues, _
        LookAt:=xlWhole, _
        MatchCase:=False)
    
    ' ========== 记录更新逻辑 ==========
    If Not idCell Is Nothing Then
        ' 找到现有记录：更新对应属性值
        targetRow = idCell.Row   ' 获取目标行号
        ws.Cells(targetRow, headerCol.Column).Value = NewValue   ' 更新属性值
        UpByID = True
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' ========== 新记录创建逻辑 ==========
    ' 获取ID列最后一行位置
    lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.rows.count, idCol.Column).End(xlUp).Row
    If lastRow = 1 Then lastRow = 1   ' 如果只有标题行，从第2行开始
    
    ' 计算新行位置
    newRow = lastRow + 1
    
    ' 写入新记录的ID
    ws.Cells(newRow, idCol.Column).Value = idValue
    
    ' 写入新记录的目标属性值
    ws.Cells(newRow, headerCol.Column).Value = NewValue
    
    ' 可选功能：自动填充其他表头（注释状态）
    ' AutoFillHeaders ws, newRow
    
    UpByID = True   ' 返回操作成功
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    ' ========== 错误处理 ==========
    UpByID = False   ' 返回操作失败
    MsgBox "更新数据时出错: " & Err.Description, vbCritical   ' 显示错误信息
End Function

Attribute VB_Name = "EnterCard·1·TimePoint"

' ==================================================
' 事件功能：特殊效果处理 - 法术增幅
' 说明：处理法术增幅效果，提供多种增幅类型选择
' ==================================================
Private Sub Spellboost_Click()
    ' ========== 增幅类型选择 ==========
    ' 询问法术增幅类型
    Dim options() As String
    options = Split(SentenceTranslate("Spellboost|Cost|Attack&Defense|SetX"), "|")
    Dim i%, j%, k%
    i = Val(ShowChoice(options, 90, 3))
    
    ' ========== 费用增幅处理 ==========
    If i = 1 Then
        j = NumInputBox(Translate("Cost"), "Engage")
        TimePoint = "Spellboost|Cost"
        If j >= 0 Then TimePoint = TimePoint & "+" & j Else TimePoint = TimePoint & j
        
    ' ========== 身材增幅处理 ==========
    ElseIf i = 2 Then
        j = NumInputBox(Translate("Attack"), "Engage")
        TimePoint = "Spellboost|"
        If j >= 0 Then TimePoint = TimePoint & "+" & j Else TimePoint = TimePoint & j
        TimePoint = TimePoint & "/"
        j = NumInputBox(Translate("Defense"), "Engage")
        If j >= 0 Then TimePoint = TimePoint & "+" & j Else TimePoint = TimePoint & j
        
    ' ========== X变量设置处理 ==========
    ElseIf i = 3 Then
        j = NumInputBox("SetX", "Engage")
        TimePoint = "Spellboost|X=" & j
    End If
    
    ' ========== 直接退出处理 ==========
    ' 法术增幅不需要进入能力选择，直接设置效果并退出
    CardEffect = TimePoint
    Unload EnterCard·1·TimePoint
End Sub

    ' ========== 窗体退出 ==========
    Call UnloadForm
End Sub

' ==================================================
' 事件功能：牌组发动处理 - 回合触发
' 说明：处理牌组中回合开始或结束时的触发效果
' ==================================================
Private Sub DeckTurn_Click()
    ' ========== 玩家选择 ==========
    ' 询问自己或对方回合
    Dim options() As String
    options = Split(FieldTurn.Caption & SentenceTranslate("|Allied|Enemy|All"), "|")
    Dim i%
    i = Val(ShowChoice(options, 60, 3))
    
    ' ========== 时间点构建 ==========
    If options(i) = Translate("All") Then
        TimePoint = "ActivateInDeck-When|Turn-"
    Else
        TimePoint = "ActivateInDeck-When|" & Translate(options(i)) & "-Turn-"
    End If
    
    ' ========== 时机选择 ==========
    ' 询问回合开始或结束
    options = Split(FieldTurn.Caption & SentenceTranslate("|Start|End"), "|")
    i = Val(ShowChoice(options, 60, 2))
    
    TimePoint = TimePoint & Translate(options(i))
    
    ' ========== 窗体退出 ==========
    Call UnloadForm
End Sub

' ==================================================
' 事件功能：常驻效果处理
' 说明：处理各种常驻效果选择
' ==================================================
Private Sub Permannent_Click()
    ' ========== 类型验证 ==========
    ' 如果是法术且没有父效果则退出
    If DownByID("1000000", "Type") = "Spell" And Len(FatherEffect) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    
    ' ========== 常驻效果选择 ==========
    Dim options() As String
    options = Split(SentenceTranslate("Permannent|" & _
        "CantBeDestroyed|" & _
        "CantStrike|" & _
        "CanAttackTimes|" & _
        "LeaveBanish|" & _
        "CantPlay|" & _
        "CantSelectOther|" & _
        "CantTakeMoreDamage|" & _
        "TakeDamage|" & _
        "DoDamage|" & _
        "NoEP|" & _
        "NoSEP|" & _
        "EngageCost|" & _
        "IncreaseModes"), "|")
    Dim i%
    i = Val(ShowChoice(options, 200, 2))
    
    ' ========== 效果设置 ==========
    CardEffect = CardEffect & Translate(options(i))
    
    ' ========== 特殊参数处理 ==========
    ' 可攻击次数需要额外参数
    If options(i) = Translate("CanAttackTimes") Then
        CardEffect = CardEffect & "&" & NumInputBox(Translate("CanAttackTimes"), "Engage")
    ElseIf options(i) = Translate("CantTakeMoreDamage") Then
        CardEffect = CardEffect & "&" & NumInputBox(Translate("CantTakeMoreDamage"), "Engage")
    ElseIf options(i) = Translate("TakeDamage") Then
        CardEffect = CardEffect & "&" & NumInputBox(Translate("TakeDamage"), "Engage")
    ElseIf options(i) = Translate("DoDamage") Then
        CardEffect = CardEffect & "&" & NumInputBox(Translate("DoDamage"), "Engage")
    ElseIf options(i) = Translate("EngageCost") Then
        CardEffect = CardEffect & "&" & NumInputBox(Translate("EngageCost"), "Engage")
        
    ElseIf options(i) = Translate("CantStrike") Then
        EnterCard·4·Goal.Tag = "All|Enemy|Field|Follower&Leader"
        EnterCard·4·Goal.Show
        CardEffect = CardEffect & "|" & Goal
    End If
    
    ' ========== 直接退出处理 ==========
    ' 常驻效果不需要进入能力选择，直接退出
    Unload EnterCard·1·TimePoint
End Sub

--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·1·2·翻译·IDTranslate.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·1·2·翻译·IDTranslate"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：简化版双向翻译函数，用于翻译ID和名称的对应关系
' 参数：groupName - 分组名称（如"Class", "Rarity"）
'        inputValue - 输入值（可以是ID或名称）
'        IsCreate - 可选，是否自动创建新条目，默认为False
' 返回：对应的翻译结果（数字ID省略前导零）
' 特性：支持双向查找（ID→名称，名称→ID），自动ID分配
' ==================================================
Function IDTranslate(groupName As String, InputValue As Variant, Optional IsCreate As Boolean = False) As Variant
    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler
    
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim ws As Worksheet           ' ID工作表对象
    Dim lastRow As Long           ' 工作表最后行号
    Dim i As Long                 ' 循环计数器
    Dim found As Boolean          ' 查找状态标识
    
    ' ========== 工作表初始化 ==========
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.Sheets("ID")                    ' 设置ID工作表
    lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(xlUp).Row    ' 获取最后数据行
    
    ' ========== 数字ID输入处理 ==========
    If IsNumeric(InputValue) Then
        Dim idNum As Integer
        idNum = CInt(InputValue)   ' 转换为整数
        
        ' 遍历查找匹配的ID
        For i = 2 To lastRow
            If ws.Cells(i, 1).Value = groupName Then
                ' 将ID列的值转换为整数比较（处理前导零）
                If IsNumeric(ws.Cells(i, 3).Value) Then
                    If CInt(ws.Cells(i, 3).Value) = idNum Then
                        IDTranslate = ws.Cells(i, 2).Value   ' 返回对应名称
                        Exit Function
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        Next i
        
        IDTranslate = "ID不存在"   ' 未找到匹配ID
        
    ' ========== 字符串名称输入处理 ==========
    ElseIf VarType(InputValue) = vbString Then
        Dim InputStr As String
        InputStr = CStr(InputValue)   ' 转换为字符串
        
        ' 第一阶段：精确匹配（不区分大小写）
        For i = 2 To lastRow
            If ws.Cells(i, 1).Value = groupName Then
                If LCase(ws.Cells(i, 2).Value) = LCase(InputStr) Then
                    ' 返回ID（转换为整数自动省略前导零）
                    IDTranslate = CInt(ws.Cells(i, 3).Value)
                    Exit Function
                End If
            End If
        Next i
        
        ' 第二阶段：不区分大小写查找（与第一阶段逻辑相同，原代码重复？）
        For i = 2 To lastRow
            If ws.Cells(i, 1).Value = groupName Then
                If LCase(ws.Cells(i, 2).Value) = LCase(InputStr) Then
                    IDTranslate = CInt(ws.Cells(i, 3).Value)
                    Exit Function
                End If
            End If
        Next i
        
        ' 第三阶段：未找到时的处理
        If IsCreate Then
            ' 自动创建新条目并返回新ID
            IDTranslate = CreateNewSimpleEntry(groupName, InputStr, ws, lastRow)
        Else
            IDTranslate = "-1"   ' 返回未找到标识
        End If
        
    ' ========== 无效输入类型处理 ==========
    Else
        IDTranslate = "无效输入类型"
    End If
    
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    ' 错误处理：返回错误描述
    IDTranslate = "错误: " & Err.Description
End Function

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：创建新ID条目（简化版）
' 参数：groupName - 分组名称
'        newName - 新条目名称
'        ws - ID工作表对象
'        lastRow - 当前最后行号
' 返回：新分配的ID整数，失败返回-1
' 特性：自动查找最小可用ID（1-99），存储为两位数格式
' ==================================================
Private Function CreateNewSimpleEntry(groupName As String, newName As String, ws As Worksheet, lastRow As Long) As Integer
    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler
    
    ' ========== 收集已用ID ==========
    Dim usedIDs As Object
    Set usedIDs = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")   ' 创建字典存储已用ID
    
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 2 To lastRow
        If ws.Cells(i, 1).Value = groupName Then
            If IsNumeric(ws.Cells(i, 3).Value) Then
                usedIDs(CInt(ws.Cells(i, 3).Value)) = True   ' 记录已用ID
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 查找最小可用ID ==========
    Dim newID As Integer
    Dim idFound As Boolean
    idFound = False
    
    ' 在1-99范围内查找第一个可用ID
    For newID = 1 To 99
        If Not usedIDs.Exists(newID) Then
            idFound = True
            Exit For
        End If
    Next newID
    
    ' 检查是否找到可用ID
    If Not idFound Then
        CreateNewSimpleEntry = -1   ' ID范围已满
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' ========== 添加新条目到工作表 ==========
    Dim newRow As Long
    newRow = lastRow + 1   ' 新行位置
    
    ' 写入新条目数据
    ws.Cells(newRow, 1).Value = groupName                     ' A列：分组名称
    ws.Cells(newRow, 2).Value = newName                       ' B列：条目名称
    ws.Cells(newRow, 3).Value = Format(newID, "00")           ' C列：ID（两位数格式存储）
    
    CreateNewSimpleEntry = newID   ' 返回新分配的ID（整数格式）
    
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    CreateNewSimpleEntry = -1   ' 错误标识
End Function


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: EnterCard·4·Goal.frm
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "EnterCard·4·Goal"
Attribute VB_Base = "0{C5D8F18F-9BDD-48E7-966D-BF3357F0E9C0}{7650D0E8-2E02-47C7-80C6-E679381C8790}"
Attribute VB_GlobalNameSpace = False
Attribute VB_Creatable = False
Attribute VB_PredeclaredId = True
Attribute VB_Exposed = False
Attribute VB_TemplateDerived = False
Attribute VB_Customizable = False
' ========== 通用点击事件处理程序 ==========
Function ToggleButton_Click(Btn As MSForms.ToggleButton)
    ' 遍历所有控件，处理同组ToggleButton的互斥逻辑
    Dim Flag As Boolean
    Flag = False ' 记录同组是否有按键按下
    
    For Each ctrl In Me.Controls
        ' 只处理同组的 ToggleButton
        If ctrl.Tag = Btn.Tag Then
            ' 核心逻辑：当前按钮按下，其他弹起
            If Btn.Value Then ctrl.Value = (ctrl.Name = Btn.Name)
            If ctrl.Value Then Flag = True
        End If
    Next ctrl
    
    ' 如果同组都没有按钮按下，则选中当前按钮
    If Not Flag Then Btn.Value = True
End Function



' ========== 目标选择方式：所有/随机/选择 ==========

' 选择所有目标
Private Sub AllCard_Click()
    ToggleButton_Click AllCard
    Call EffectPreview
End Sub


' 随机选择目标
Private Sub RandomCard_Click()
    ' 判断Tag限制
    If AllCard.Tag = "All" Or SelectCard.Tag = "Select" Then
        RandomCard.Value = False
        Exit Sub
    End If

    ToggleButton_Click RandomCard
    
    ' 询问选择数量并更新按钮显示
    If RandomCard.Caption = Translate("Random") Then
        Dim Str As String
        Str = NumInputBox(Translate("Random"), "Random")
        
        ' 处理不同名选项（@前缀）
        If Left(Str, 1) = "@" Then
            RandomCard.Caption = "Random" & Str
        Else
            RandomCard.Caption = "Random&" & Str
        End If
        
        RandomCard.Caption = SentenceTranslate(RandomCard.Caption)
    End If
    
    ' 恢复默认显示
    If Not RandomCard.Value Then RandomCard.Caption = Translate("Random")
    
    Call EffectPreview
End Sub

' 手动选择目标
Private Sub SelectCard_Click()
    ' 判断Tag限制
    If AllCard.Tag = "All" Or RandomCard.Tag = "Random" Then
        SelectCard.Value = False
        Exit Sub
    End If
    
    ' 检查是否在可选择的时点
    Dim IsSelect As Boolean
    IsSelect = False
    Dim Arr() As String
    If TimePoint = "" Then
        IsSelect = True
    Else
        Arr = Split(TimePoint, "&")
        If Arr(0) = "Fanfare" Then IsSelect = True
        If Arr(0) = "LastWords" Then IsSelect = True
        If Arr(0) = "Play" Then IsSelect = True
        If Arr(0) = "Evolve" Then IsSelect = True
        If Arr(0) = "SuperEvolve" Then IsSelect = True
        If Arr(0) = "Engage" Then IsSelect = True
        If Arr(0) = "Enhance" Then IsSelect = True
    End If
    
    If Not IsSelect Then
        SelectCard.Value = False
        Exit Sub
    End If
    
    ToggleButton_Click SelectCard
    
    ' 询问选择数量并更新按钮显示
    If SelectCard.Caption = Translate("Select") Then
        SelectCard.Caption = SentenceTranslate("Select&" & NumInputBox(Translate("Select"), "Select"))
    End If
    
    ' 恢复默认显示
    If Not SelectCard.Value Then SelectCard.Caption = Translate("Select")
    
    ' 选择模式下的一些限制
    If SelectCard.Value Then
        Deck.Value = False
        Crest.Value = False
        Faith.Value = False
    End If
    
    ' 选择对手时限制生效区域为战场
    If SelectCard.Value And Enemy.Value Then
        Field.Value = True
        Hand.Value = False
        Deck.Value = False
        ActionCards.Value = False
    End If
    
    Call EffectPreview
End Sub

' ========== 特殊目标类型 ==========

' 本卡牌目标
Private Sub ThisCard_Click()
    ' 互斥处理
    If ThisCard.Value Then
'        Opposing.Value = False
'        PreviousGoal.Value = False
'        PrePrevious.Value = False
'        PreviousFuse.Value = False
        Call EraseTypes
    End If
    Call EffectPreview
End Sub

' 交战对手目标
Private Sub Opposing_Click()
    If TimePoint = "" Then
        Opposing = False
        Exit Sub
    End If

    ' 检查是否在攻击/交战相关时点
    Dim Arr() As String
    Arr = Split(TimePoint, "|")
    Dim IsStrike As Boolean
    IsStrike = False
    
    If Arr(0) = "Strike" Or Arr(0) = "Clash" Then IsStrike = True
    If UBound(Arr) >= 2 Then
        If Arr(0) = "When" And (Arr(2) = "Strike" Or Arr(2) = "Clash") Then IsStrike = True
    End If
    
    If Not IsStrike Then Opposing = False
    
    ' 互斥处理
    If Opposing.Value Then
'        ThisCard.Value = False
'        PreviousGoal.Value = False
'        PrePrevious.Value = False
'        PreviousFuse.Value = False
        Call EraseTypes
    End If
    
    Call EffectPreview
End Sub

' 上一个限定的目标
Private Sub PreviousGoal_Click()
    If PreviousGoal.Value Then
'        ThisCard.Value = False
'        Opposing.Value = False
'        PrePrevious.Value = False
'        PreviousFuse.Value = False
        Call EraseTypes
    End If
    Call EffectPreview
End Sub

' 上上个限定的目标
Private Sub PrePrevious_Click()
    If PrePrevious.Value Then
'        ThisCard.Value = False
'        Opposing.Value = False
'        PreviousGoal.Value = False
'        PreviousFuse.Value = False
        Call EraseTypes
    End If
    Call EffectPreview
End Sub

' 上一次融合的目标
Private Sub PreviousFuse_Click()
    If PreviousFuse.Value Then
'        ThisCard.Value = False
'        Opposing.Value = False
'        PrePrevious.Value = False
'        PreviousGoal.Value = False
        Call EraseTypes
    End If
    Call EffectPreview
End Sub

' 除上述目标以外
Private Sub Except_Click()
    If Not ThisCard.Value And Not Opposing.Value And Not PreviousGoal.Value And Not PrePrevious.Value And Not PreviousFuse.Value Then
        Except.Value = False
'    ElseIf DifferentName.Value Or SameName.Value Then
'        Except.Value = False
    Else
        Call EffectPreview
    End If
End Sub

' 清空类型选择
Function EraseTypes()
    If Not Except.Value And Not SameName.Value And Not DifferentName.Value Then
        AllCard.Value = True
        Allied.Value = False
        Enemy.Value = False
        Field.Value = False
        Hand.Value = False
        Deck.Value = False
        ActionCards.Value = False
        Follower.Value = False
        Spell.Value = False
        Amulet.Value = False
        Leader.Value = False
        Crest.Value = False
        Faith.Value = False
    End If
End Function

' ========== 名称相关条件 ==========

' 同名目标
Private Sub SameName_Click()
    If SameName.Value Then
        DifferentName.Value = False
        Except.Value = False
    End If
    Call EffectPreview
End Sub

' 不同名目标
Private Sub DifferentName_Click()
    If DifferentName.Value Then
        SameName.Value = False
        Except.Value = False
    End If
    Call EffectPreview
End Sub

End Sub

' 生效方选择：己方/敌方
Private Sub Allied_Click()
    If Allied.Tag = "Enemy" Then Allied.Value = False
    If Allied.Tag = "All" Then Allied.Value = True
    Call EffectPreview("Allied")
End Sub

Private Sub Enemy_Click()
    If Enemy.Tag = "Allied" Then Enemy.Value = False
    If Enemy.Tag = "All" Then Enemy.Value = True
    Call EffectPreview("Enemy")
End Sub

' 生效区域选择：战场/手牌/牌组/墓场
Private Sub Field_Click()
    If InStr(Field.Tag, "Field") = 0 And Field.Tag <> "" Then Field.Value = False
    
    ' 纹章必须在战场
'    If Not Field.Value Then
'        Leader.Value = False
'        Crest.Value = False
'        Faith.Value = False
'    End If
    Call EffectPreview("Field")
End Sub

Private Sub Hand_Click()
    If InStr(Hand.Tag, "Hand") = 0 And Field.Tag <> "" Then Hand.Value = False
    
    ' 选择对手时手牌不生效
    If SelectCard.Value And Enemy.Value Then Hand.Value = False
    Call EffectPreview("Hand")
End Sub

Private Sub Deck_Click()
    If InStr(Deck.Tag, "Deck") = 0 And Field.Tag <> "" Then Deck.Value = False
    
    ' 选择模式下牌堆不生效
    If SelectCard.Value Then Deck.Value = False
    Call EffectPreview("Deck")
End Sub

Private Sub ActionCards_Click()
    If InStr(ActionCards.Tag, "ActionCards") = 0 And Field.Tag <> "" Then ActionCards.Value = False
    
    ' 选择模式下记录卡牌不生效
    If SelectCard.Value Then ActionCards.Value = False

    If ActionCards.Caption = Translate("ActionCardsBtn") Then
        Dim options() As String
        options = Split(ActionCards.Caption & SentenceTranslate("|ThisTurn|ThisMatch"), "|")
        Dim i%
        i = Val(ShowChoice(options, 60, 2))
        ActionCards.Caption = options(i)
        
        EnterCard·6·Action.Show
        
        ActionCards.Caption = ActionCards.Caption & "@" & Translate(Action)
    End If
    
    If Not ActionCards.Value Then ActionCards.Caption = Translate("ActionCardsBtn")
    Call EffectPreview
End Sub

        LeftMost.Caption = "LeftMost&" & NumInputBox(Translate("LeftMost"), "LeftMost")

    End If
    
    If Not LeftMost.Value Then LeftMost.Caption = Translate("LeftMost")

End Sub

        RightMost.Caption = SentenceTranslate(RightMost.Caption)

    If Not RightMost.Value Then RightMost.Caption = Translate("RightMost")
    Call EffectPreview
End Sub

' ========== 数值条件 ==========

' 数值条件1和条件2的设置
Private Sub Model1_Click()
    If Model1.Caption = Translate("Model") Then
        Dim options() As String
        options = Split(SentenceTranslate("Model|OrigCost|Cost|OrigAttack|Attack|OrigDefense|Defense|OrigCountdown|Countdown"), "|")
        Dim i%
        i = Val(ShowChoice(options, 70, 2))
        Model1.Caption = options(i)
        
        ' 比较关系选择
        options = Split(SentenceTranslate("Compare|<|=|>|<>|Highest|Lowest"), "|")
        i = Val(ShowChoice(options, 60, 2))
        
        If options(i) <> Translate("Highest") And options(i) <> Translate("Lowest") Then
            Model1.Caption = Model1.Caption & options(i)
            
            ' 比较对象选择
            options = Split(SentenceTranslate("Model|OrigCost|Cost|OrigAttack|Attack|OrigDefense|Defense|OrigCountdown|Countdown|Const&Variate"), "|")
            i = Val(ShowChoice(options, 70, 2))
            
            If options(i) = SentenceTranslate("Const&Variate") Then
                Model1.Caption = Model1.Caption & NumInputBox(Translate("Compare"), "")
            Else
                Model1.Caption = Model1.Caption & options(i)
            End If
        Else
            Model1.Caption = Model1.Caption & "$" & options(i)
        End If
    End If
    
    If Not Model1.Value Then Model1.Caption = Translate("Model")
    Call EffectPreview
End Sub

Private Sub Model2_Click()
    ' 与Model1相同的逻辑
    If Model2.Caption = Translate("Model") Then
        Dim options() As String
        options = Split(SentenceTranslate("Model|OrigCost|Cost|OrigAttack|Attack|OrigDefense|Defense|OrigCountdown|Countdown"), "|")
        Dim i%
        i = Val(ShowChoice(options, 70, 2))
        Model2.Caption = options(i)
        
        options = Split(SentenceTranslate("Compare|<|=|>|<>|Highest|Lowest"), "|")
        i = Val(ShowChoice(options, 60, 2))
        
        If options(i) <> Translate("Highest") And options(i) <> Translate("Lowest") Then
            Model2.Caption = Model2.Caption & options(i)
            
            options = Split(SentenceTranslate("Model|OrigCost|Cost|OrigAttack|Attack|OrigDefense|Defense|OrigCountdown|Countdown|Const&Variate"), "|")
            i = Val(ShowChoice(options, 70, 2))
            
            If options(i) = SentenceTranslate("Const&Variate") Then
                Model2.Caption = Model2.Caption & NumInputBox(Translate("Compare"), "")
            Else
                Model2.Caption = Model2.Caption & options(i)
            End If
        Else
            Model2.Caption = Model2.Caption & "$" & options(i)
        End If
    End If
    
    If Not Model2.Value Then Model2.Caption = Translate("Model")
    Call EffectPreview
End Sub

    OrLogic.Value = Not AndLogic.Value
    Call EffectPreview
End Sub

Private Sub OrLogic_Click()
    AndLogic.Value = Not OrLogic.Value
    Call EffectPreview
End Sub

' ========== 窗体初始化和效果预览 ==========

' 窗体初始化
Private Sub UserForm_Activate()
    Goal = Empty

    ' 解析Tag设置控件属性
    Dim Arr() As String
    Arr = Split(EnterCard·4·Goal.Tag, "|")
    ReDim Preserve Arr(0 To 3)
    
    ' 设置选择方式
    If Arr(0) = "" Then Arr(0) = "Methods"
    AllCard.Tag = Arr(0)
    RandomCard.Tag = Arr(0)
    SelectCard.Tag = Arr(0)
    
    ' 设置生效方
    Allied.Tag = Arr(1)
    Enemy.Tag = Arr(1)
    
    ' 设置生效区域
    Field.Tag = Arr(2)
    Hand.Tag = Arr(2)
    Deck.Tag = Arr(2)
    ActionCards.Tag = Arr(2)
    
    ' 设置生效种类
    Follower.Tag = Arr(3)
    Spell.Tag = Arr(3)
    Amulet.Tag = Arr(3)
    Leader.Tag = Arr(3)
    Crest.Tag = Arr(3)
    Faith.Tag = Arr(3)
    
    ' 默认设置
    AndLogic.Value = True
    
    ' 根据Tag自动选择
    If RandomCard.Tag = "Random" Then RandomCard.Value = True
    If SelectCard.Tag = "Select" Then SelectCard.Value = True
    
    Call EffectPreview
    
    If CardEffect <> "" Then EnterCard·4·Goal.Caption = SentenceTranslate(CardEffect)
End Sub

' 完成选择
Private Sub Finish_Click()
    Call EffectPreview
    Unload EnterCard·4·Goal
End Sub

' 效果预览生成函数
Function EffectPreview(Optional InputBtn As String = "")
    Dim i%
    Dim Str As String
    Dim Flag As Boolean
    
    ' 检查特殊目标类型
    If Not Except.Value Then _
        If ThisCard.Value Or Opposing.Value Or PreviousGoal.Value Or PrePrevious.Value Or PreviousFuse.Value Then Flag = True
        
    ' 确保至少选择一个生效方、区域和类型
    If Not Flag Then
        ' 生效方选择验证
        For i = 1 To 2
            If Not (Allied.Value Or Enemy.Value) Then
                If InputBtn = "Allied" Then
                    Enemy.Value = True
                ElseIf InputBtn = "Enemy" Then
                    Allied.Value = True
                Else
                    Allied.Value = True
                End If
            Else
                Exit For
            End If
        Next i
        
        ' 生效区域选择验证
        For i = 1 To 5
            If Not (Field.Value Or Hand.Value Or Deck.Value Or ActionCards.Value) Then
                Select Case InputBtn
                    Case "Field": Hand.Value = True
                    Case "Hand": Deck.Value = True
                    Case "Deck": ActionCards.Value = True
                    Case "ActionCards": Field.Value = True
                    Case Else: Field.Value = True
                End Select
            Else
                Exit For
            End If
        Next i
        
        ' 生效种类选择验证
        For i = 1 To 6
            If Not (Follower.Value Or Spell.Value Or Amulet.Value Or Leader.Value Or Crest.Value Or Faith.Value) Then
                Select Case InputBtn
                    Case "Follower": Spell.Value = True
                    Case "Spell": Amulet.Value = True
                    Case "Amulet": Leader.Value = True
                    Case "Leader": Crest.Value = True
                    Case "Crest": Faith.Value = True
                    Case "Faith": Follower.Value = True
                    Case Else: Follower.Value = True
                End Select
            Else
                Exit For
            End If
        Next i
    End If
    
    ' 生成目标描述字符串
    Goal = Empty
    Dim StrID As String
    Dim StrID2 As String
    StrID = IDByName(CardName.Value)
    StrID2 = IDByName(CardName2.Value)
    
    ' 添加选择方式
    If RandomCard.Value Then Goal = Goal & SentenceTranslate(RandomCard.Caption) & "-"
    If SelectCard.Value Then Goal = Goal & SentenceTranslate(SelectCard.Caption) & "-"
    
    ' 添加生效方
    If Len(Allied.Tag) = 0 Then
        If Allied.Value And Not Enemy.Value Then Goal = Goal & "Allied-"
        If Enemy.Value And Not Allied.Value Then Goal = Goal & "Enemy-"
    End If
    
    ' 添加生效区域
    If (InStr(Field.Tag, "&") > 0 Or Len(Field.Tag) = 0) Then
        If Field.Value Then Goal = Goal & "Field&"
        If Hand.Value Then Goal = Goal & "Hand&"
        If Deck.Value Then Goal = Goal & "Deck&"
        If ActionCards.Value Then Goal = Goal & SentenceTranslate(ActionCards.Caption) & "&"
        If Len(Goal) > 0 Then Goal = Left(Goal, Len(Goal) - 1) & "-"
    End If
    
    ' 添加特殊目标条件
    If Except.Value Then
        If ThisCard.Value Then Goal = Goal & "Other-"
        If Opposing.Value Then Goal = Goal & "Opposing&Except-"
        If PreviousGoal.Value Then Goal = Goal & "It&Except-"
        If PrePrevious.Value Then Goal = Goal & "That&Except-"
        If PreviousFuse.Value Then Goal = Goal & "FuseCards&Except-"
    End If
    
    ' 添加状态条件
    If Evolve.Value Then Goal = Goal & Translate(Evolve.Caption) & "-"
    If Strike.Value Then Goal = Goal & Translate(Strike.Caption) & "-"
    If Damaged.Value Then Goal = Goal & Translate(Damaged.Caption) & "-"
    If Engage.Value Then Goal = Goal & Translate(Engage.Caption) & "-"
    
    ' 添加职业和能力关键词
    If Class.Value Then Goal = Goal & Translate(Class.Caption) & "-"
    If Keyword.Value Then Goal = Goal & "Keyword&" & Translate(Keyword.Caption) & "-"
    
    ' 添加类型
    If Len(Trait.Value) > 0 Then
        i = Val(IDTranslate("Trait", Trait.Value))
        If i > -1 Then Goal = Goal & "Trait:" & i & "-"
    End If
    
    ' 添加数值条件
    Str = Empty
    If Model1.Value Then Str = Str & SentenceTranslate(Model1.Caption) & "-"
    If Model2.Value Then Str = Str & SentenceTranslate(Model2.Caption) & "-"
    If OrLogic.Value Then Str = Replace(Str, "-", "&")
    If Len(Str) > 0 Then Goal = Goal & Left(Str, Len(Str) - 1) & "-"
    
    ' 添加名称条件
    If Len(StrID) = Len("1000000") Then
        If DifferentName.Value Then
            Goal = Goal & "DifferentName&"
        Else
            Goal = Goal & "SameName&"
        End If
        If Len(StrID2) = Len("1000000") Then
            Goal = Goal & StrID & "&" & StrID2 & "-"
        Else
            Goal = Goal & StrID & "-"
        End If
    ElseIf (ThisCard.Value Or Opposing.Value Or PreviousGoal.Value Or PrePrevious.Value Or PreviousFuse.Value Or SelectCard.Value) And (DifferentName.Value Or SameName.Value) Then
        If DifferentName.Value Then
            Goal = Goal & "DifferentName&"
        Else
            Goal = Goal & "SameName&"
        End If
        If ThisCard.Value Then
            Goal = Goal & "ThisCard-"
        ElseIf Opposing.Value Then
            Goal = Goal & "Opposing-"
        ElseIf PreviousGoal.Value Then
            Goal = Goal & "It-"
        ElseIf PrePrevious.Value Then
            Goal = Goal & "That-"
        ElseIf PreviousFuse.Value Then
            Goal = Goal & "FuseCards-"
        ElseIf SelectCard.Value Then
            Goal = Goal & "Select-"
        End If
    End If
    
    ' 添加位置条件
    If LeftMost.Value Then Goal = Goal & SentenceTranslate(LeftMost.Caption) & "-"
    If RightMost.Value Then Goal = Goal & SentenceTranslate(RightMost.Caption) & "-"
    
    ' 添加卡牌种类
    If Follower.Value Then Goal = Goal & "Follower&"
    If Spell.Value Then Goal = Goal & "Spell&"
    If Amulet.Value Then Goal = Goal & "Amulet&"
    If Leader.Value Then Goal = Goal & "Leader&"
    If Crest.Value Then Goal = Goal & "Crest&"
    If Faith.Value Then Goal = Goal & "Faith&"
    
    ' 优化描述字符串
    Goal = Replace(Goal, "Follower&Spell&Amulet", "Card")
    If Hand.Value = False And Deck.Value = False Then Goal = Replace(Goal, "Follower&Amulet", "Card")
    
    ' 修正格式
    If Right(Goal, 1) = "&" Then Goal = Left(Goal, Len(Goal) - 1)
    If Len(Goal) > 0 And Flag And Right(Goal, 1) <> "-" Then Goal = Goal & "-"
    
    ' 处理特殊目标
    If Flag And ThisCard.Value Then Goal = Goal & "ThisCard"
    If Flag And Opposing.Value Then Goal = Goal & "Opposing"
    If Flag And PreviousGoal.Value Then Goal = Goal & "It"
    If Flag And PrePrevious.Value Then Goal = Goal & "That"
    If Flag And PreviousFuse.Value Then Goal = Goal & "FuseCards"
    
    ' 更新显示
    Effect.Caption = SentenceTranslate(Goal)
End Function
