--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·1·3·翻译·SentenceTranslate.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·1·3·翻译·SentenceTranslate"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：句子翻译函数，处理包含特殊分隔符的文本翻译
' 参数：InputText - 需要翻译的输入文本
' 返回：翻译后的文本，保留原始分隔符结构
' 特性：智能识别和保留分隔符，仅翻译非分隔符部分
' ==================================================
Function SentenceTranslate(InputText As String) As String
    Dim Str As String
    Dim ReplaceText As String
    ReplaceText = InputText   ' 保存原始输入文本的副本
    
    ' ========== 特殊分隔符预处理 ==========
    ' 将"||"替换为临时占位符"?"，避免与后续处理冲突
    ReplaceText = Replace(ReplaceText, "||", "?")
    ' 将"|"替换为临时占位符","，避免与后续处理冲突
    ReplaceText = Replace(ReplaceText, "|", ",")

    ' ========== 正则表达式初始化 ==========
    Dim regex As Object
    Set regex = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")   ' 创建正则表达式对象

    ' 设置正则表达式模式：匹配所有分隔符字符
    ' 包括：| - + & $ % @ < > = ? , { }
    regex.Pattern = "(\||\-|\+|&|\$|%|@|<|>|=|\?|\,|{|})"
    regex.Global = True   ' 全局匹配模式

    ' ========== 文本分割处理 ==========
    Dim matches As Object
    Dim parts() As String
    Dim i As Long, count As Long, j As Long

    ' 获取所有分隔符匹配项
    Set matches = regex.Execute(ReplaceText)

    ' 初始化结果数组（最大可能长度）
    ReDim parts(0 To matches.count * 2)

    ' 使用正则表达式在分隔符前后添加双竖线标记
    Dim tempText As String
    tempText = regex.Replace(ReplaceText, "||$1||")

    ' 按双竖线分割字符串，将文本和分隔符分离
    parts = Split(tempText, "||")

    ' ========== 逐部分翻译处理 ==========
    For i = LBound(parts) To UBound(parts)
        ' 检查是否为非空且不是分隔符的文本部分
        If Len(parts(i)) > 0 Then
            If Not regex.test(parts(i)) Then
                ' 调用Translate函数翻译非分隔符文本部分
                Str = Translate(parts(i))
                If parts(i) = Str And i + 2 <= UBound(parts) Then
                    If parts(i + 1) = "&" And Val(parts(i)) = 0 Then
                        parts(i) = Translate(parts(i) & "&" & parts(i + 2))
                        parts(i + 1) = ""
                        parts(i + 2) = ""
                    Else
                        parts(i) = Str
                    End If
                Else
                    parts(i) = Str
                End If
            End If
            ' 如果是分隔符，则保留原样不翻译
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 结果重组 ==========
    ' 将翻译后的各部分重新组合成完整字符串
    SentenceTranslate = Join(parts, "")
    
    ' ========== 恢复原始分隔符 ==========
    ' 将临时占位符"?"恢复为原始分隔符"||"
    SentenceTranslate = Replace(SentenceTranslate, "?", "||")
    ' 将临时占位符","恢复为原始分隔符"|"
    SentenceTranslate = Replace(SentenceTranslate, ",", "|")
End Function


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ButtonClick.cls
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "ButtonClick"
Attribute VB_Base = "0{FCFB3D2A-A0FA-1068-A738-08002B3371B5}"
Attribute VB_GlobalNameSpace = False
Attribute VB_Creatable = False
Attribute VB_PredeclaredId = False
Attribute VB_Exposed = False
Attribute VB_TemplateDerived = False
Attribute VB_Customizable = False
' 类模块名称: ButtonClick
Public WithEvents Button As MSForms.ToggleButton
Attribute Button.VB_VarHelpID = -1
Public ButtonIndex As Long
Public ParentForm As Object

Private Sub Button_Click()
    Dim Arr() As String

    If Button.Value Then
        '记录进字符串
        If Me.ButtonIndex > 0 Then ParentForm.SelectedIndex = ParentForm.SelectedIndex & Me.ButtonIndex & "&"
        '拆解目前选项
        Arr = Split(ParentForm.SelectedIndex, "&")
        
        '如果是任选
        If ParentForm.SelectCount < 0 Then
            If Me.ButtonIndex = 0 Then
                If Len(ParentForm.SelectedIndex) > 1 Then ParentForm.SelectedIndex = Left(ParentForm.SelectedIndex, Len(ParentForm.SelectedIndex) - 1)
                ParentForm.Hide
            End If
        '如果不是任选，判断是否满足应选数
        ElseIf UBound(Arr) >= ParentForm.SelectCount Then
            If Len(ParentForm.SelectedIndex) > 1 Then ParentForm.SelectedIndex = Left(ParentForm.SelectedIndex, Len(ParentForm.SelectedIndex) - 1)
            ParentForm.Hide
        End If
    Else
        ParentForm.SelectedIndex = Replace(ParentForm.SelectedIndex, Me.ButtonIndex & "&", "")
    End If
End Sub





--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ChoiceForm.frm
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "ChoiceForm"
Attribute VB_Base = "0{F6F7A0E0-DAE2-445F-8F9A-7B2D225512DA}{3380AB01-74C4-47A6-B49D-5980C5D180F8}"
Attribute VB_GlobalNameSpace = False
Attribute VB_Creatable = False
Attribute VB_PredeclaredId = True
Attribute VB_Exposed = False
Attribute VB_TemplateDerived = False
Attribute VB_Customizable = False
' ==================================================
' 窗体名称: ChoiceForm
' 功能：动态创建选择按钮的窗体
' 说明：支持多列布局、多选模式和自定义按钮尺寸
' ==================================================

' ========== 公共变量声明 ==========
Public SelectedIndex As String
Private ButtonHandlers() As ButtonClick
Public BtnColumns As Integer      ' 按钮列数参数
Public SelectCount As Integer     ' 选择数量限制

' ========== 窗体初始化事件 ==========
Private Sub UserForm_Initialize()
    SelectedIndex = Empty
End Sub

' ==================================================
' 过程功能：动态创建按钮（带列布局）
' 参数：choices - 选项数组, Button_Width - 按钮宽度
'       BtnColumns - 按钮列数, n - 选择数量限制
' 说明：根据参数动态生成按钮布局，支持多列和多选模式
' ==================================================
Public Sub CreateButtons(choices() As String, Button_Width As Integer, BtnColumns As Integer, Optional n As Integer = 1)
    ' ========== 布局参数设置 ==========
    ' 按钮高度固定
    Dim Button_Height As Integer
    Button_Height = 30
    
    ' 水平间距设置
    Dim Button_H_Spacing As Integer
    Button_H_Spacing = 20
    
    ' 垂直间距设置（根据按钮宽度调整）
    Dim Button_V_Spacing As Integer
    Button_V_Spacing = 10
    If Button_Width > 100 Then Button_V_Spacing = 20
    
    ' 窗体边距常量
    Const FORM_MARGIN As Integer = 20
    SelectCount = n
    
    ' ========== 窗体标题设置 ==========
    ' 设置窗体标题为第一个选项（通常是标题文本）
    Me.Caption = choices(0)

    ' ========== 按钮数量计算 ==========
    ' 确定需要创建的按钮数量
    Dim buttonCount As Long
    buttonCount = UBound(choices) - LBound(choices)
    If buttonCount < 1 Then Exit Sub

    ' ========== 布局计算 ==========
    ' 计算需要的行数（向上取整）
    Dim rows As Long
    rows = Application.WorksheetFunction.Ceiling(buttonCount / BtnColumns, 1)

    ' ========== 窗体尺寸调整 ==========
    ' 根据按钮布局计算并设置窗体大小
    Me.Width = (BtnColumns * Button_Width) + _
                ((BtnColumns - 1) * Button_H_Spacing) + _
                FORM_MARGIN * 2 + 10 + 5
    Me.Height = (rows * Button_Height) + _
                ((rows - 1) * Button_V_Spacing) + _
                FORM_MARGIN * 2 + 10 + 30
    
    ' 任选模式下的窗体高度调整
    If SelectCount < 0 Then Me.Height = Me.Height + Button_Height + Button_V_Spacing
    If SelectCount < 0 Then buttonCount = buttonCount + 1
    
    ' ========== 按钮创建准备 ==========
    ' 重置按钮处理器数组
    ReDim ButtonHandlers(1 To buttonCount)

    ' ========== 选项按钮创建 ==========
    Dim i As Long, btnIndex As Long
    btnIndex = 1
    
    Dim Btn As MSForms.ToggleButton
    Dim rowIndex As Long, colIndex As Long
    
    ' 遍历选项数组创建按钮（跳过第一个标题选项）
    For i = LBound(choices) + 1 To UBound(choices)
        Set Btn = Me.Controls.Add("Forms.ToggleButton.1")

        ' ========== 按钮位置计算 ==========
        ' 计算行和列位置 (0-based)
        rowIndex = (btnIndex - 1) \ BtnColumns  ' 整除计算行索引
        colIndex = (btnIndex - 1) Mod BtnColumns ' 取模计算列索引

        ' 计算按钮具体位置坐标
        leftPos = FORM_MARGIN + colIndex * (Button_Width + Button_H_Spacing)
        topPos = FORM_MARGIN + rowIndex * (Button_Height + Button_V_Spacing)

        ' ========== 按钮属性设置 ==========
        With Btn
            .Caption = choices(i)
            .Width = Button_Width
            .Height = Button_Height
            .Top = topPos
            .Left = leftPos
            .Name = "btnChoice" & i
        End With

        ' ========== 事件处理器绑定 ==========
        ' 创建新的事件处理器并绑定到按钮
        Set ButtonHandlers(btnIndex) = New ButtonClick
        With ButtonHandlers(btnIndex)
            Set .Button = Btn
            .ButtonIndex = btnIndex
            Set .ParentForm = Me
        End With

        btnIndex = btnIndex + 1
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 任选模式处理 ==========
    ' 如果SelectCount为-1，代表任选模式，添加完成按钮
    If SelectCount < 0 Then
        Set Btn = Me.Controls.Add("Forms.ToggleButton.1")

        ' ========== 完成按钮位置计算 ==========
        ' 计算完成按钮的位置（居中显示在最后一行）
        rowIndex = (UBound(choices) - 1) \ BtnColumns + 1
        colIndex = 1

        ' 计算按钮具体位置坐标（水平居中）
        leftPos = (Me.Width - Button_Width) / 2
        topPos = FORM_MARGIN + rowIndex * (Button_Height + Button_V_Spacing)

        ' ========== 完成按钮属性设置 ==========
        With Btn
            .Caption = Translate("Finish")
            .Width = Button_Width
            .Height = Button_Height
            .Top = topPos
            .Left = leftPos
            .Name = "Finish"
        End With

        ' ========== 完成按钮事件绑定 ==========
        ' 创建完成按钮的事件处理器
        Set ButtonHandlers(btnIndex) = New ButtonClick
        With ButtonHandlers(btnIndex)
            Set .Button = Btn
            .ButtonIndex = 0  ' 特殊标识：完成按钮
            Set .ParentForm = Me
        End With
    End If
End Sub



--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·0·2·基础·ShowChoice.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·0·2·基础·ShowChoice"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：显示选项选择对话框
' 参数：choices - 选项字符串数组
'        Button_Width - 按钮宽度
'        BtnColumns - 按钮列数
'        SelectCount - 可选，选择数量，默认为1
' 返回：字符串，选中的选项索引（格式："1&2&3"）
' ==================================================
Public Function ShowChoice(choices() As String, Button_Width As Integer, BtnColumns As Integer, Optional SelectCount As Integer = 1) As String
    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler
    Dim i As Integer

    ' 验证输入数组有效性
    If UBound(choices) - LBound(choices) < 1 Then
        ' 数组元素不足时直接返回0
        ' MsgBox "数组至少需要包含一个标题和一个选项", vbExclamation
        ShowChoice = 0
        Exit Function
    End If

    ' 特殊情况处理：选项数量不超过选择数量时直接返回所有选项
    If UBound(choices) <= SelectCount Then
        For i = 1 To UBound(choices)
            ShowChoice = ShowChoice & i & "&"   ' 构建索引字符串
        Next i
        ' 移除末尾多余的"&"符号
        If Len(ShowChoice) > 0 Then ShowChoice = Left(ShowChoice, Len(ShowChoice) - 1)
        Exit Function
    End If

    ' 创建并初始化选择窗体
    Dim ChoiceForm As New ChoiceForm
    Call ChoiceForm.CreateButtons(choices, Button_Width, BtnColumns, SelectCount)

    ' 显示模态窗体并等待用户选择
    ChoiceForm.Show vbModal

    ' 获取用户选择结果
    ShowChoice = ChoiceForm.SelectedIndex

    ' 清理窗体资源
    Unload ChoiceForm
    Set ChoiceForm = Nothing
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    ' 错误处理：显示错误信息并返回0
    MsgBox "错误 " & Err.Number & ": " & Err.Description & vbCrLf & _
           "发生在 " & Erl, vbCritical
    ShowChoice = 0
End Function



--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: EnterCard·3·Ability.frm
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "EnterCard·3·Ability"
Attribute VB_Base = "0{D26C27C6-B726-4DA8-A38F-F8923346603A}{E1075F69-D3DB-4D1F-9B2D-A116FE60A342}"
Attribute VB_GlobalNameSpace = False
Attribute VB_Creatable = False
Attribute VB_PredeclaredId = True
Attribute VB_Exposed = False
Attribute VB_TemplateDerived = False
Attribute VB_Customizable = False
' ========== 生效条件和生效方设置 ==========
' 设置效果生效条件
Private Sub Effect_Click()
    Dim Arr
    Dim response As VbMsgBoxResult
    Arr = Split(CardEffect, "||")
    
    ' 检查是否为改变效果类型
    If Arr(UBound(Arr)) = "Change|" Then
        ' 进化时改为超进化时的确认
        If TimePoint = "Evolve" Then
            response = MsgBox("是否要改为超进化时？", vbYesNo, Translate("SuperEvolve"))
            If response = vbYes Then
                CardEffect = CardEffect & "SuperEvolve||"
                Effect.Caption = SentenceTranslate(CardEffect)
                Exit Sub
            End If
        ' 入场曲/法术发动改为爆能强化的确认
        ElseIf TimePoint = "Fanfare" Or TimePoint = "Play" Then
            response = MsgBox("是否要改为爆能强化？", vbYesNo, Translate("Enhance"))
            If response = vbYes Then
                CardEffect = CardEffect & "Enhance&" & NumInputBox(Translate("Enhance"), "Engage") & "||"
                Effect.Caption = SentenceTranslate(CardEffect)
                Exit Sub
            End If
        End If
    End If
    
    ' 打开条件设置窗体
    EnterCard·2·If.Show
    If IfEffect = "If|" Then IfEffect = Empty
    Effect.Caption = SentenceTranslate(CardEffect & IfEffect)
End Sub

Private Sub UntilTurn_Click()
    If UntilTurn.Caption = Translate("UntilTurnButton") Then
        Dim options() As String
        options = Split(SentenceTranslate("UntilTurnButton|Allied|Enemy|Allied&Enemy"), "|")
        Dim i%
        i = Val(ShowChoice(options, 70, 2))

        If i = 3 Then
            UntilTurn.Caption = SentenceTranslate("Turn-")
        Else
            UntilTurn.Caption = SentenceTranslate(Translate(options(i)) & "-Turn-")
        End If
        
        options = Split(SentenceTranslate("UntilTurnButton|Start|End"), "|")
        i = Val(ShowChoice(options, 50, 2))

        UntilTurn.Caption = UntilTurn.Caption & options(i) & "-" & Translate("Until")
    End If
    
    If Not UntilTurn.Value Then
        UntilTurn.Caption = Translate("UntilTurnButton")
        Whenever.Value = True
    Else
        Whenever.Value = False
    End If
End Sub

Private Sub Repeat_Click()
    If Repeat.Caption = Translate("RepeatButton") Then
        Ability = "Repeat&" & NumInputBox(Translate("Repeat"), Empty) & "|"
        Repeat.Caption = SentenceTranslate(Ability)
    End If
    If Not Repeat.Value Then Repeat.Caption = Translate("RepeatButton")
End Sub

Private Sub Mode_Click()
    Dim i%, j%
    i = NumInputBox("选择几个能力发动？", "Engage")
    j = NumInputBox("总共几个选择？", "Engage")
    Ability = "Mode&" & i
    For i = 1 To j
        Ability = Ability & "|{Ability}"
    Next i
    Call UnloadForm
End Sub

Private Sub Crest_Click()
    If Enemy.Value Then Ability = "Enemy-"
    Dim Str As String
    Ability = Ability & "Crest&" & NumInputBox("输入倒数（0代表没有倒数）", "Engage")
    Str = NumInputBox("希望输入几个效果？", "Engage")
    Dim i%
    For i = 1 To Val(Str)
        Ability = Ability & "|{Effect}"
    Next i
    Call UnloadForm
End Sub

Private Sub Faith_Click()
    Dim Str As String
    Ability = Ability & NumInputBox("输入信仰起始值）", "Engage")
    Str = NumInputBox("希望输入几个效果？", "Engage")
    Dim i%
    For i = 1 To Val(Str)
        Ability = Ability & "|{Effect}"
    Next i
    CardEffect = Left(CardEffect, Len(CardEffect) - Len(TimePoint & "||")) & "Faith||"
    TimePoint = "Faith"
    Call UnloadForm
End Sub

Private Sub NamingCount_Click()
    Dim Arr As Variant
    Dim i%, j%
    Dim Str As String
    Str = "NamingCount"
    Arr = Down("Count")
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
        If Arr(i, 1) = "" Then Exit For
        If Mid(Arr(i, 1), 2, 100) <> "Class" Then
            Str = Str & "|" & Mid(Arr(i, 1), 2, 100)
        End If
        i = i + 1
    Next i
    Dim options() As String
    options = Split(SentenceTranslate(Str), "|")
    i = Val(ShowChoice(options, 50, 4))
    If i = 0 Then Exit Sub
    Ability = Ability & "NamingCount|" & Translate(options(i)) & "|" & NumInputBox(options(i), Empty)
    Call UnloadForm
End Sub

' ========== 窗体初始化和退出处理 ==========

' 窗体激活时初始化变量
Private Sub UserForm_Activate()
    IfEffect = Empty
    Ability = Empty
    Allied.Value = True
    Whenever.Value = True
    Effect.Caption = SentenceTranslate(CardEffect)
End Sub

' 统一退出处理函数
Function UnloadForm()
    ' 记录条件效果
    If Len(IfEffect) > 0 Then CardEffect = CardEffect & IfEffect & "||"
    
    ' 记录能力效果
    CardEffect = CardEffect & Ability
    
    ' 处理特殊效果类型
    If Change.Value Then
        CardEffect = CardEffect & "||Change|"
        Effect.Caption = SentenceTranslate(CardEffect)
        ' 重新初始化并打开效果设置
        IfEffect = Empty
        Ability = Empty
        Change.Value = False
        SameTimePoint.Value = False
        Repeat = False
        Call Effect_Click
    ElseIf SameTimePoint.Value Then
        CardEffect = CardEffect & "||"
        Effect.Caption = SentenceTranslate(CardEffect)
        ' 重新初始化
        IfEffect = Empty
        Ability = Empty
        Change.Value = False
        SameTimePoint.Value = False
        Repeat = False
    Else
        ' 正常退出窗体
        Unload EnterCard·3·Ability
    End If
End Function

' ==================================================
' 系数选择事件处理函数
' 说明：处理系数选择按钮的互斥逻辑
' ==================================================

'减半计数
Private Sub HalveCount_Click()
    If XEffect <> "" Then HalveCount.Value = False
    '互斥逻辑：选择当前按钮时取消其他系数选择
    If HalveCount.Value Then
        DoubleCount.Value = False
        Difference.Value = False
        Sum.Value = False
    End If
End Sub

'加倍计数
Private Sub DoubleCount_Click()
    If XEffect <> "" Then DoubleCount.Value = False
    '互斥逻辑
    If DoubleCount.Value Then
        HalveCount.Value = False
        Difference.Value = False
        Sum.Value = False
    End If
End Sub

'差值计数
Private Sub Difference_Click()
    If XEffect <> "" Then Difference.Value = False
    '互斥逻辑
    If Difference.Value Then
        HalveCount.Value = False
        DoubleCount.Value = False
        Sum.Value = False
    End If
End Sub

'求和计数
Private Sub Sum_Click()
    If XEffect <> "" Then Sum.Value = False
    '互斥逻辑
    If Sum.Value Then
        HalveCount.Value = False
        DoubleCount.Value = False
        Difference.Value = False
    End If
End Sub

'命名计数
Private Sub NamingCount_Click()
    Dim Arr As Variant
    Dim i%, j%
    Dim Str As String
    
    '构建命名计数选项字符串
    Str = "NamingCount"
    Arr = Down("Count")
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
        If Arr(i, 1) = "" Then Exit For
        '排除Class类型
        If Mid(Arr(i, 1), 2, 100) <> "Class" Then
            Str = Str & "|" & Mid(Arr(i, 1), 2, 100)
        End If
        i = i + 1
    Next i
    
    '显示选择对话框
    Dim options() As String
    options = Split(SentenceTranslate(Str), "|")
    i = Val(ShowChoice(options, 50, 4))
    If i = 0 Then Exit Sub
    
    '记录命名计数效果
    XEffect = XEffect & "NamingCount&" & Translate(options(i))
    Call UnloadForm
End Sub

    EnterCard·6·Action.Show
    '打开目标选择窗体
    EnterCard·4·Goal.Show
    
    '构建效果字符串
    If ThisTurn.Value Then XEffect = XEffect & "ThisTurn-" Else XEffect = XEffect & "ThisMatch-"
    XEffect = XEffect & Action
    If Cards.Value Then XEffect = XEffect & "-Times|" Else XEffect = XEffect & "-Types|"
    XEffect = XEffect & Goal
    
    Call UnloadForm
End Sub


' ==================================================
' 窗体初始化和退出函数
' ==================================================

'窗体激活事件 - 初始化
Private Sub UserForm_Activate()
    '清空效果字符串
    XEffect = Empty
    
    '设置默认选择：友方、卡牌计数、本回合
    Allied.Value = True
    Cards.Value = True
    ThisTurn.Value = True
    
    If CardEffect <> "" Then EnterCard·5·X.Caption = SentenceTranslate(CardEffect)
End Sub

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：卸载窗体并处理效果字符串
' 说明：根据选择的系数类型进行相应处理，完成效果字符串构建
' ==================================================
Function UnloadForm()
    Dim i%

    '差值或求和计数处理
    If InStr(XEffect, "Difference") > 0 Or InStr(XEffect, "Sum") > 0 Then
        'XEffect = XEffect & "}"
    '减半计数处理
    ElseIf HalveCount.Value Then
        XEffect = "Halve|" & XEffect
    '加倍计数处理
    ElseIf DoubleCount.Value Then
        XEffect = "DoubleCount|" & XEffect
    '差值计数处理
    ElseIf Difference.Value Then
        XEffect = "Difference|" & XEffect & "|"
        '重置默认选择
        Allied.Value = True
        Cards.Value = True
        ThisTurn.Value = True
        Difference.Value = False
        EnterCard·5·X.Caption = SentenceTranslate(XEffect)
        
        '询问差值常数
        i = Val(InputBox("输入差值：" & Chr(13) & "提示：输入0代表不输入常数", Translate("Difference"), "0"))
        
        If i = 0 Then Exit Function Else XEffect = XEffect & i
    '求和计数处理
    ElseIf Sum.Value Then
        XEffect = "Sum|" & XEffect & "|"
        '重置默认选择
        Allied.Value = True
        Cards.Value = True
        ThisTurn.Value = True
        Sum.Value = False
        EnterCard·5·X.Caption = SentenceTranslate(XEffect)
        
        '询问求和常数
        i = Val(InputBox("输入差值：" & Chr(13) & "提示：输入0代表不输入常数", Translate("Difference"), "0"))
        
        If i = 0 Then Exit Function Else XEffect = XEffect & i
    End If
    
    '卸载当前窗体
    Unload EnterCard·5·X
End Function

Private Sub Keyword_Click()
    Dim options() As String
    options = Split(SentenceTranslate( _
                "Keyword&|Fanfare|LastWords|Evolve|SuperEvolve|Strike|Clash|" & _
                "Fuse|Enhance|Accelerate|Crystallize|" & _
                "EarthRite|Necromancy|Sacrifice|Mode|Reanimate"), "|")
    Dim i%
    i = Val(ShowChoice(options, 60, 4))
    
    '标签Goal窗体
    If i <= 6 Then
        EnterCard·4·Goal.Tag = "All||Field|Follower"
        If options(i) = Translate("Fanfare") Then EnterCard·4·Goal.Tag = "All||Field|Follower&Amulet"
        If options(i) = Translate("LastWords") Then EnterCard·4·Goal.Tag = "All||Field|Follower&Amulet"
        If options(i) = Translate("Engage") Then EnterCard·4·Goal.Tag = "All||Field|Amulet"
    ElseIf i <= 10 Then
        EnterCard·4·Goal.Tag = "All||Hand|Follower&Spell&Amulet"
    Else
        EnterCard·4·Goal.Tag = "All||Field&Hand&Deck|Follower&Spell&Amulet&Crest&Faith"
        If options(i) = Translate("Mode") Then EnterCard·4·Goal.Tag = "All||Field&Hand&Deck|Follower&Spell&Amulet"
    End If
    
    Action = "Keyword&" & Translate(options(i))
    
    Unload EnterCard·6·Action
End Sub


Private Sub UserForm_Activate()

    If CardEffect <> "" Then EnterCard·6·Action.Caption = SentenceTranslate(CardEffect)
End Sub

文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: EnterCard·2·If.frm

Attribute VB_Name = "EnterCard·2·If"
Attribute VB_Base = "0{E8354EF5-0809-4DC1-BB94-CF4E736016B4}{1C18AD37-85DE-41C6-BA17-4C1DFD5F0C58}"
Attribute VB_GlobalNameSpace = False
Attribute VB_Creatable = False
Attribute VB_PredeclaredId = True
Attribute VB_Exposed = False
Attribute VB_TemplateDerived = False
Attribute VB_Customizable = False
' ==================================================
' 窗体名称: EnterCard·2·If (条件设置窗体)
' 功能：设置卡牌效果触发的各种条件
' 说明：提供回合条件、比较条件、职业条件等多种条件设置
' ==================================================

' ==================================================
' 事件功能：回合条件处理 - 回合期间
' 说明：设置效果在特定玩家回合期间触发的条件
' ==================================================
Private Sub DuringTurn_Click()
    ' ========== 互斥逻辑处理 ==========
    ' 与解锁回合条件互斥
    If DuringTurn.Value Then UnlockTurn.Value = False
    
    ' ========== 玩家选择 ==========
    ' 询问自己或对手，并记录在按钮名称上
    If DuringTurn.Caption = Translate("DuringTurnButton") Then
        Dim options() As String
        options = Split(SentenceTranslate("DuringTurnButton|Allied|Enemy"), "|")
        Dim i%
        i = Val(ShowChoice(options, 50, 2))
        
        DuringTurn.Caption = options(i) & "-" & Translate("Turn")
    End If
    
    ' ========== 状态恢复 ==========
    ' 如果未按下，则按钮名称恢复默认
    If Not DuringTurn.Value Then DuringTurn.Caption = Translate("DuringTurnButton")
    
    ' ========== 效果预览更新 ==========
    Call EffectPreview
End Sub

' ==================================================
' 事件功能：回合条件处理 - 解锁回合
' 说明：设置效果在进化或超进化回合触发的条件
' ==================================================
Private Sub UnlockTurn_Click()
    ' ========== 互斥逻辑处理 ==========
    ' 与回合期间条件互斥
    If UnlockTurn.Value Then DuringTurn.Value = False
    
    ' ========== 进化类型选择 ==========
    ' 询问进化或超进化，并记录在按钮名称上
    If UnlockTurn.Caption = Translate("UnlockTurnButton") Then
        Dim options() As String
        options = Split(SentenceTranslate("UnlockTurnButton|Evolve|SuperEvolve"), "|")
        Dim i%
        i = Val(ShowChoice(options, 50, 2))
        
        UnlockTurn.Caption = SentenceTranslate("Unlock") & "-" & options(i)
    End If
    
    ' ========== 状态恢复 ==========
    ' 如果未按下，则按钮名称恢复默认
    If Not UnlockTurn.Value Then UnlockTurn.Caption = Translate("UnlockTurnButton")
    
    ' ========== 效果预览更新 ==========
    Call EffectPreview
End Sub

' ==================================================
' 事件功能：触发次数限制
' 说明：设置每回合中效果可触发的最大次数
' ==================================================
Private Sub Times_Click()
    ' ========== 次数输入 ==========
    ' 询问次数，并记录在按钮名称上
    If Times.Caption = Translate("TimesPerTurn") Then _
        Times.Caption = Translate("TimesPerTurn") & "&" & NumInputBox(Translate("TimesPerTurn"), "Engage")
    
    ' ========== 状态恢复 ==========
    ' 如果未按下，则按钮名称恢复默认
    If Not Times.Value Then Times.Caption = Translate("TimesPerTurn")
    
    ' ========== 效果预览更新 ==========
    Call EffectPreview
End Sub

    If Equal.Value Then
        Less.Value = False
        More.Value = False

    End If
    
    ' ========== 效果预览更新 ==========
    Call EffectPreview
End Sub

    ' 打开X变量设置窗体（复用）
    XEffect = Empty
    EnterCard·5·X.Show

' ==================================================
' 事件功能：比较条件处理 - 确认比较
' 说明：确认并生成完整的比较条件表达式
' ==================================================
Private Sub Compare_Click()
    ' ========== 条件验证 ==========
    ' 检查必要的条件是否已设置
    If SetX.Caption = Translate("ClickToInput") Or _
        (Equal.Value = False And Less.Value = False And More.Value = False And UnEqual.Value = False) Or _
        (SetConst.Value = False And SetY.Caption = Translate("ClickToInput")) Then
        Compare.Value = False
        Exit Sub
    End If
    
    ' ========== 比较表达式生成 ==========
    ' 生成比较条件字符串
    If Compare.Caption = Translate("CompareButton") Then
        Dim Str As String
        Compare.Caption = Empty
        
        ' 获取比较运算符
        If Equal.Value Then Str = "="
        If Less.Value Then Str = "<"
        If More.Value Then Str = ">"
        If UnEqual.Value Then Str = "<>"
        
        ' 构建比较表达式
        If Str <> "" And SetX.Caption <> Translate("ClickToInput") Then
            If SetConst.Value Then
                Compare.Caption = Compare.Caption & SetX.Caption & Str & SetConst.Caption & "|"
            ElseIf SetY.Caption <> Translate("ClickToInput") Then
                Compare.Caption = Compare.Caption & SetX.Caption & Str & SetY.Caption & "|"
            End If
        End If
        
        ' ========== 状态重置 ==========
        ' 生成后重置所有比较条件状态
        SetX.Caption = Translate("ClickToInput")
        SetY.Caption = Translate("ClickToInput")
        SetConst.Value = False
        Equal.Value = False
        Less.Value = False
        More.Value = False
        UnEqual.Value = False
    End If
    
    ' ========== 状态恢复 ==========
    ' 如果未按下，则按钮名称恢复默认
    If Not Compare.Value Then Compare.Caption = Translate("CompareButton")
    
    ' ========== 效果预览更新 ==========
    Call EffectPreview
End Sub

' ==================================================

    If Not Combo.Value Then Combo.Caption = Translate("Combo")
    
    ' ========== 效果预览更新 ==========
    Call EffectPreview
End Sub

    If Rally.Caption = Translate("Rally") Then _
        Rally.Caption = Translate("Rally") & "&" & NumInputBox(Translate("Rally"), Empty)
    
    ' ========== 状态恢复 ==========
    ' 如果未按下，则按钮名称恢复默认
    If Not Rally.Value Then Rally.Caption = Translate("Rally")
    
    ' ========== 效果预览更新 ==========
    Call EffectPreview
End Sub

    ' ========== 效果预览更新 ==========

Private Sub NoDuplicates_Click()
    ' ========== 目标选择 ==========
    ' 询问同名卡限制的目标类型
    If NoDuplicates.Caption = Translate("NoDuplicatesButton") Then
        ' 打开目标选择窗体
        EnterCard·4·Goal.Tag = "All|||Follower&Spell&Amulet"
        EnterCard·4·Goal.Show
        
        NoDuplicates.Caption = SentenceTranslate("NoDuplicates-" & Goal)
    End If

    ' ========== 效果预览更新 ==========

    Call EffectPreview

' ========== 分割线：窗体初始化和工具函数 ==========

' ==================================================
' 事件功能：窗体激活初始化
' 说明：窗体显示时初始化条件字符串
' ==================================================
Private Sub UserForm_Activate()
    ' ========== 变量初始化 ==========
    ' 清空条件字符串并添加"If"前缀
    IfEffect = Translate("If") & "|"
    
    If CardEffect <> "" Then EnterCard·2·If.Caption = SentenceTranslate(CardEffect)
End Sub

' ==================================================
' 事件功能：录入完成处理
' 说明：点击完成按钮时保存条件设置并关闭窗体
' ==================================================
Private Sub Finish_Click()
    ' ========== 条件记录 ==========
    ' 更新效果预览
    Call EffectPreview
    
    ' ========== 窗体关闭 ==========
    Unload EnterCard·2·If
End Sub

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：效果预览生成
' 说明：根据当前设置的条件生成预览字符串
' ==================================================
Function EffectPreview()
    ' ========== 条件字符串初始化 ==========
    IfEffect = Translate("If") & "|"
    
    ' ========== 回合条件添加 ==========
    If DuringTurn.Value Then IfEffect = IfEffect & DuringTurn.Caption & "|"
    If UnlockTurn.Value Then IfEffect = IfEffect & UnlockTurn.Caption & "|"
    
    ' ========== 触发次数条件添加 ==========
    If Times.Value Then IfEffect = IfEffect & Times.Caption & "|"
    
    ' ========== 比较条件添加 ==========
    If Compare.Value Then IfEffect = IfEffect & Compare.Caption
    
    ' ========== 直接比较条件处理 ==========
    ' 处理尚未确认的比较条件
    Dim Str As String
    If Equal.Value Then Str = "="
    If Less.Value Then Str = "<"
    If More.Value Then Str = ">"
    If UnEqual.Value Then Str = "<>"
    
    If Str <> "" And SetX.Caption <> Translate("ClickToInput") Then
        If SetConst.Value Then
            IfEffect = IfEffect & SetX.Caption & Str & SetConst.Caption & "|"
        ElseIf SetY.Caption <> Translate("ClickToInput") Then
            IfEffect = IfEffect & SetX.Caption & Str & SetY.Caption & "|"
        End If
    End If
    
    ' ========== 职业条件添加 ==========
    If Combo.Value Then IfEffect = IfEffect & Combo.Caption & "|"
    If Rally.Value Then IfEffect = IfEffect & Rally.Caption & "|"
    If EarthRite.Value Then IfEffect = IfEffect & EarthRite.Caption & "|"
    If Overflow.Value Then IfEffect = IfEffect & Overflow.Caption & "|"
    If Skybound.Value Then IfEffect = IfEffect & Skybound.Caption & "|"
    If SuperSkybound.Value Then IfEffect = IfEffect & SuperSkybound.Caption & "|"
    If Necromancy.Value Then IfEffect = IfEffect & Necromancy.Caption & "|"
    If Sacrifice.Value Then IfEffect = IfEffect & Sacrifice.Caption & "|"
    If NoDuplicates.Value Then IfEffect = IfEffect & NoDuplicates.Caption & "|"
    
    ' ========== 字符串格式处理 ==========
    ' 删去末尾分隔符
    If Len(IfEffect) = 0 Then IfEffect = "|"
    IfEffect = Left(IfEffect, Len(IfEffect) - 1)
    
    ' ========== 效果翻译和显示 ==========
    ' 翻译条件字符串并更新显示
    Effect.Caption = IfEffect
    IfEffect = SentenceTranslate(IfEffect)
End Function



--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·0·3·基础·NumInputBox.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·0·3·基础·NumInputBox"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：数字输入框函数，支持多种输入模式和格式验证
' 参数：BoxName - 输入框标题
'        Tag - 输入模式标识，控制允许的输入类型和格式
' 返回：格式化后的输入字符串
' 支持模式：Engage, Random, MaxPP, Attack, Select
' ==================================================
Function NumInputBox(BoxName As String, Optional Tag As String = "") As String
    Dim Str As String
    
    ' 基础提示信息
    Str = "请输入数字：" & Chr(13) & _
        "提示：可以输入X"
    
    ' ========== 根据Tag值设置特定输入提示 ==========
    
    ' Engage模式：不允许输入X
    If Tag = "Engage" Then
        Str = "请输入数字："
    
    ' Random模式：允许输入@符号表示不同名卡牌
    ElseIf Tag = "Random" Then
        Str = Str & Chr(13) & "提示：输入@2代表不同名的2张"
    
    ' MaxPP模式：允许输入$符号表示数值变更
    ElseIf Tag = "MaxPP" Then
        Str = Str & Chr(13) & "提示：输入$1代表变为1"
    
    ' Attack模式：Buff类输入，支持多种数学操作符
    ElseIf Tag = "Attack" Then
        Str = "请输入数字：" & Chr(13) & _
            "提示：可以输入+X或-X" & Chr(13) & _
            "提示：输入*2代表变为2倍" & Chr(13) & _
            "提示：输入%2代表除2（进位）" & Chr(13) & _
            "提示：输入$1代表变为1"
    
    ' Select模式：选择相关输入，支持反选操作
'    ElseIf Tag = "Select" Then
'        Str = "请输入数字：" & Chr(13) & _
'            "提示：输入-1代表反选1张"
    End If
    
    ' 显示输入框并获取用户输入，默认值为"1"
    NumInputBox = InputBox(Str, BoxName, "1")
    
    ' ========== 输入验证和格式化处理 ==========
    
    ' 处理X变量输入（Engage和Select模式除外）
    If (NumInputBox = "X" Or NumInputBox = "x") And Tag <> "Engage" And Tag <> "Select" Then
        NumInputBox = "X"
        If Tag = "Attack" Then NumInputBox = "+X"   ' Attack模式下自动添加+号
    
    ' 处理+X输入（仅Attack模式）
    ElseIf (NumInputBox = "+X" Or NumInputBox = "+x") And Tag = "Attack" Then
        NumInputBox = "+X"
    
    ' 处理-X输入（仅Attack模式）
    ElseIf (NumInputBox = "-X" Or NumInputBox = "-x") And Tag = "Attack" Then
        NumInputBox = "-X"
    
    ' 处理-X输入（仅Attack模式）
    ElseIf (NumInputBox = "$X" Or NumInputBox = "$x") And Tag = "Attack" Then
        NumInputBox = "$X"
    
    ' 处理Y变量输入（Engage和Select模式除外）
    ElseIf (NumInputBox = "Y" Or NumInputBox = "y") And Tag <> "Engage" And Tag <> "Select" Then
        NumInputBox = "Y"
        If Tag = "Attack" Then NumInputBox = "+Y"   ' Attack模式下自动添加+号
    
    ' 处理+Y输入（仅Attack模式）
    ElseIf (NumInputBox = "+Y" Or NumInputBox = "+y") And Tag = "Attack" Then
        NumInputBox = "+Y"
    
    ' 处理-Y输入（仅Attack模式）
    ElseIf (NumInputBox = "-Y" Or NumInputBox = "-y") And Tag = "Attack" Then
        NumInputBox = "-Y"
    
    ' 处理+Y输入（仅Attack模式）
    ElseIf (NumInputBox = "$Y" Or NumInputBox = "$y") And Tag = "Attack" Then
        NumInputBox = "$Y"
    
    ' 处理Z变量输入（Engage和Select模式除外）
    ElseIf (NumInputBox = "Z" Or NumInputBox = "z") And Tag <> "Engage" And Tag <> "Select" Then
        NumInputBox = "Z"
        If Tag = "Attack" Then NumInputBox = "+Z"   ' Attack模式下自动添加+号
    
    ' 处理+Z输入（仅Attack模式）
    ElseIf (NumInputBox = "+Z" Or NumInputBox = "+z") And Tag = "Attack" Then
        NumInputBox = "+Z"
    
    ' 处理-Z输入（仅Attack模式）
    ElseIf (NumInputBox = "-Z" Or NumInputBox = "-z") And Tag = "Attack" Then
        NumInputBox = "-Z"
    
    ' 处理-Z输入（仅Attack模式）
    ElseIf (NumInputBox = "$Z" Or NumInputBox = "$z") And Tag = "Attack" Then
        NumInputBox = "$Z"
    
    ' 处理Random模式的@符号输入
    ElseIf Left(NumInputBox, 1) = "@" And Tag = "Random" Then
        NumInputBox = "@" & Val(Mid(NumInputBox, 2, 10))   ' 提取并验证数字部分
    
    ' 处理Attack模式的$符号输入（设为固定值）
    ElseIf Left(NumInputBox, 1) = "$" And (Tag = "Attack" Or Tag = "MaxPP") Then
        NumInputBox = "$" & Val(Mid(NumInputBox, 2, 10))   ' 提取并验证数字部分
    
    ' 处理Attack模式的*符号输入（倍数操作）
    ElseIf Left(NumInputBox, 1) = "*" And Tag = "Attack" Then
        NumInputBox = "*" & Val(Mid(NumInputBox, 2, 10))   ' 提取并验证数字部分
    
    ' 处理Attack模式的%符号输入（除法进位操作）
    ElseIf Left(NumInputBox, 1) = "%" And Tag = "Attack" Then
        NumInputBox = "%" & Val(Mid(NumInputBox, 2, 10))   ' 提取并验证数字部分
    
    ' 处理Attack模式的正数输入（自动添加+号）
    ElseIf Tag = "Attack" And Val(NumInputBox) >= 0 Then
        NumInputBox = "+" & Val(NumInputBox)
    
    ' 默认情况：验证并转换纯数字输入
    Else
        NumInputBox = Val(NumInputBox)
    End If
    
End Function



--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·3·1·牌组·SearchCard.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·3·1·牌组·SearchCard"
' ==================================================
' 过程功能：职业卡筛选主过程
' 说明：根据选择的卡包筛选指定职业的卡牌，并按稀有度分类显示
' ==================================================
Sub SearchClassCard()
    ' ========== 优化设置 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim result As Variant       ' 筛选结果数组
    Dim Arr As Variant          ' 临时数组
    Dim Rarity() As String      ' 稀有度数组
    Dim i As Integer            ' 循环计数器
    
    ' ========== 卡包选择对话框 ==========
    Dim options() As String
    ' 构建选择选项：选择 + 卡包 + 卡包列表
    options = Split(Translate("Select") & Translate("CardSet") & "、" & Down("CardSet"), "、")
    i = Val(ShowChoice(options, 90, 5))   ' 显示选择对话框，按钮宽度90，5列
    
    ' ========== 获取筛选结果 ==========
    ' 根据选择的卡包和当前职业获取卡牌名称
    result = GetNamesByCardSet(options(i), Translate(Down("Class")))
    
    ' ========== 稀有度分类处理 ==========
    Rarity = Split("|Legendary|Gold|Silver|Bronze", "|")   ' 定义稀有度顺序
    
    ' 遍历四种稀有度，更新到对应界面区域
    For i = 1 To 4
        Arr = result(i)                    ' 获取当前稀有度的卡牌数组
        ReDim Preserve Arr(1 To 6)         ' 统一调整为6个元素
        Call Up(Rarity(i), Application.Transpose(Arr))   ' 转置后更新到界面
    Next i
    
CleanUp:
    ' ========== 恢复应用程序状态 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
End Sub

' ==================================================
' 过程功能：中立卡筛选主过程
' 说明：根据选择的卡包筛选中立职业卡牌，并按稀有度分类显示
' ==================================================
Sub SearchNeutralCard()
    ' ========== 优化设置 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim result As Variant       ' 筛选结果数组
    Dim Arr As Variant          ' 临时数组
    Dim Rarity() As String      ' 稀有度数组
    Dim i As Integer            ' 循环计数器
    
    ' ========== 卡包选择对话框 ==========
    Dim options() As String
    ' 构建选择选项：选择 + 卡包 + 卡包列表
    options = Split(Translate("Select") & Translate("CardSet") & "、" & Down("CardSet"), "、")
    i = Val(ShowChoice(options, 90, 5))   ' 显示选择对话框，按钮宽度90，5列
    
    ' ========== 获取筛选结果 ==========
    ' 根据选择的卡包和中立职业获取卡牌名称
    result = GetNamesByCardSet(options(i), "Neutral")
    
    ' ========== 稀有度分类处理 ==========
    Rarity = Split("|Legendary|Gold|Silver|Bronze", "|")   ' 定义稀有度顺序
    
    ' 遍历四种稀有度，更新到对应界面区域
    For i = 1 To 4
        Arr = result(i)                    ' 获取当前稀有度的卡牌数组
        ReDim Preserve Arr(1 To 6)         ' 统一调整为6个元素
        Call Up(Rarity(i), Application.Transpose(Arr))   ' 转置后更新到界面
    Next i
    
CleanUp:
    ' ========== 恢复应用程序状态 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
End Sub

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：根据卡包和职业获取卡牌名称
' 参数：searchString - 卡包名称
'        Class - 职业名称
' 返回：Variant - 四层数组，分别对应四种稀有度的卡牌名称
' 说明：从Effect工作表中筛选指定卡包和职业的可构筑卡牌
' ==================================================
Function GetNamesByCardSet(searchString As String, Class As String) As Variant
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim ws As Worksheet                    ' 工作表对象
    Dim headerRow As Range                 ' 标题行范围
    Dim idCol As Long, cardSetCol As Long  ' 列索引变量
    Dim rarityCol As Long, nameCol As Long, classCol As Long
    Dim lastRow As Long                    ' 最后数据行号
    Dim i As Long                          ' 循环计数器
    Dim ID As String                       ' 卡牌ID
    Dim Rarity As String                   ' 卡牌稀有度
    Dim nameValue As String                ' 卡牌名称
    
    ' 稀有度分类集合
    Dim legendaryNames As Collection, goldNames As Collection
    Dim silverNames As Collection, bronzeNames As Collection
    
    ' ========== 工作表初始化 ==========
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.Sheets("Effect")   ' 设置Effect工作表
    Set headerRow = ws.rows(1)               ' 设置标题行
    
    ' ========== 列索引获取 ==========
    idCol = GetColumnIndex(headerRow, "ID")           ' 获取ID列索引
    cardSetCol = GetColumnIndex(headerRow, "CardSet") ' 获取卡包列索引
    rarityCol = GetColumnIndex(headerRow, "Rarity")   ' 获取稀有度列索引
    nameCol = GetColumnIndex(headerRow, "Name")       ' 获取名称列索引
    classCol = GetColumnIndex(headerRow, "Class")     ' 获取职业列索引
    
    ' 验证必要列是否存在
    If idCol = 0 Or cardSetCol = 0 Or rarityCol = 0 Or nameCol = 0 Then
        GetNamesByCardSet = Array(Array(), Array(), Array(), Array())   ' 返回空数组
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' ========== 数据范围确定 ==========
    lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.rows.count, idCol).End(xlUp).Row   ' 获取ID列最后一行
    
    ' ========== 稀有度集合初始化 ==========
    Set legendaryNames = New Collection   ' 传说卡牌集合
    Set goldNames = New Collection        ' 黄金卡牌集合
    Set silverNames = New Collection      ' 白银卡牌集合
    Set bronzeNames = New Collection      ' 青铜卡牌集合
    
    ' ========== 数据遍历筛选 ==========
    For i = 2 To lastRow   ' 从第2行开始（跳过表头）
        ' 检查卡包是否匹配
        If ws.Cells(i, cardSetCol).Value = searchString Then
            ID = CStr(ws.Cells(i, idCol).Value)   ' 获取卡牌ID
            
            ' 检查是否为可构筑卡牌（ID第4位为1）且职业匹配
            If Len(ID) = 7 And Mid(ID, 4, 1) = "1" And ws.Cells(i, classCol).Value = Class Then
                Rarity = ws.Cells(i, rarityCol).Value     ' 获取稀有度
                nameValue = ws.Cells(i, nameCol).Value    ' 获取卡牌名称
                
                ' 根据稀有度分类存储卡牌名称
                Select Case Rarity
                    Case "Legendary"
                        legendaryNames.Add nameValue   ' 添加到传说集合
                    Case "Gold"
                        goldNames.Add nameValue        ' 添加到黄金集合
                    Case "Silver"
                        silverNames.Add nameValue      ' 添加到白银集合
                    Case "Bronze"
                        bronzeNames.Add nameValue      ' 添加到青铜集合
                End Select
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 结果数组构建 ==========
    Dim result(1 To 4) As Variant
    result(1) = CollectionToArray(legendaryNames)   ' 传说卡牌数组
    result(2) = CollectionToArray(goldNames)        ' 黄金卡牌数组
    result(3) = CollectionToArray(silverNames)      ' 白银卡牌数组
    result(4) = CollectionToArray(bronzeNames)      ' 青铜卡牌数组
    
    GetNamesByCardSet = result   ' 返回分类结果
End Function

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：根据表头名称获取列索引
' 参数：headerRow - 标题行范围
'        HeaderName - 要查找的列标题名称
' 返回：Long - 列索引号，未找到返回0
' ==================================================
Function GetColumnIndex(headerRow As Range, HeaderName As String) As Long
    Dim cell As Range   ' 单元格循环变量
    
    ' 遍历标题行查找匹配的列标题
    For Each cell In headerRow.Cells
        If cell.Value = HeaderName Then
            GetColumnIndex = cell.Column   ' 返回列索引
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next cell
    
    GetColumnIndex = 0   ' 未找到返回0
End Function

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：将集合转换为数组
' 参数：col - 要转换的集合对象
' 返回：Variant - 转换后的数组，空集合返回空数组
' ==================================================
Function CollectionToArray(col As Collection) As Variant
    ' 处理空集合情况
    If col.count = 0 Then
        CollectionToArray = Array()   ' 返回空数组
    Else
        Dim Arr() As Variant
        ReDim Arr(1 To col.count)    ' 根据集合大小重定义数组
        
        Dim i As Long
        For i = 1 To col.count
            Arr(i) = col(i)          ' 将集合元素复制到数组
        Next i
        
        CollectionToArray = Arr      ' 返回填充后的数组
    End If
End Function


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·2·6·录卡·DownByID.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·2·6·录卡·DownByID"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：根据ID和列标题获取对应的属性值
' 参数：idValue - 卡牌ID
'        HeaderName - 属性列标题名称
' 返回：Variant - 属性值或错误信息
' 说明：使用数组读取优化性能，支持快速查找Effect工作表中的数据
' ==================================================
Function DownByID(idValue As String, HeaderName As String) As Variant
    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler
    
    ' ========== 常量定义 ==========
    Const SHEET_NAME As String = "Effect"      ' 效果数据工作表名称
    Const ID_HEADER As String = "ID"           ' ID列标题名称
    
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim ws As Worksheet           ' 工作表对象
    Dim dataRange As Range        ' 数据范围对象
    Dim dataArray As Variant      ' 数据数组（用于快速读取）
    Dim headerRow As Range        ' 标题行范围
    Dim idColIndex As Long        ' ID列索引号
    Dim headerColIndex As Long    ' 目标属性列索引号
    Dim i As Long                 ' 循环计数器
    Dim idCell As Range           ' ID列标题单元格
    Dim headerCell As Range       ' 目标属性列标题单元格
    Dim lastRow As Long           ' 最后数据行号
    
    ' ========== 工作表初始化 ==========
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.Sheets(SHEET_NAME)   ' 设置Effect工作表
    Set headerRow = ws.rows(1)                 ' 设置标题行（第一行）
    
    ' ========== 列标题查找与验证 ==========
    With headerRow
        ' 查找ID列位置
        Set idCell = .Find(What:=ID_HEADER, LookAt:=xlWhole, MatchCase:=False)
        If idCell Is Nothing Then
            MsgBox "错误：未找到ID列标题", vbExclamation
            DownByID = CVErr(xlErrNA)   ' 返回#N/A错误
            Exit Function
        End If
        idColIndex = idCell.Column   ' 获取ID列索引
        
        ' 查找目标属性列位置
        Set headerCell = .Find(What:=HeaderName, LookAt:=xlWhole, MatchCase:=False)
        If headerCell Is Nothing Then
            MsgBox "错误：未找到表头'" & HeaderName & "'", vbExclamation
            DownByID = CVErr(xlErrNA)   ' 返回#N/A错误
            Exit Function
        End If
        headerColIndex = headerCell.Column   ' 获取目标属性列索引
    End With
    
    ' ========== 数据读取优化 ==========
    ' 获取ID列最后一行数据位置
    lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.rows.count, idColIndex).End(xlUp).Row
    
    ' 一次性读取相关列数据到数组（性能优化）
    Set dataRange = ws.Range(ws.Cells(1, idColIndex), ws.Cells(lastRow, headerColIndex))
    dataArray = dataRange.Value   ' 将单元格数据读取到内存数组
    
    ' ========== 数组遍历查找 ==========
    ' 从第2行开始遍历（跳过表头行）
    For i = 2 To UBound(dataArray, 1)
        ' 检查当前行ID是否匹配目标ID
        If dataArray(i, 1) = idValue Then
            ' 计算目标属性在数组中的列位置
            DownByID = dataArray(i, headerColIndex - idColIndex + 1)
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 未找到ID处理 ==========
    DownByID = ""   ' 返回空字符串表示未找到
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    ' ========== 错误处理 ==========
    DownByID = CVErr(xlErrValue)   ' 返回#VALUE!错误
    MsgBox "获取数据时出错: " & Err.Description, vbCritical   ' 显示错误信息
End Function


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·2·7·录卡·IDByName.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·2·7·录卡·IDByName"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：根据卡牌名称查找对应的卡牌ID（跳过指定ID）
' 参数：nameValue - 卡牌名称
' 返回：Variant - 卡牌ID或错误信息
' 说明：在Name列中查找指定名称，返回对应的ID，但跳过特定的ID值（1000000）
' ==================================================
Function IDByName(nameValue As String) As Variant
    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler
    
    ' ========== 常量定义 ==========
    Const SHEET_NAME As String = "Effect"      ' 效果数据工作表名称
    Const NAME_HEADER As String = "Name"       ' 名称列标题
    Const ID_HEADER As String = "ID"           ' ID列标题
    Const SKIP_ID As String = "1000000"        ' 要跳过的特殊ID值
    
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim ws As Worksheet           ' 工作表对象
    Dim headerRow As Range        ' 标题行范围
    Dim nameCol As Range          ' 名称列范围
    Dim idCol As Range            ' ID列范围
    Dim nameCell As Range         ' 名称查找结果单元格
    Dim firstFound As Range       ' 首次找到的单元格（用于循环检测）
    Dim foundID As String         ' 找到的ID值
    
    ' ========== 工作表初始化 ==========
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.Sheets(SHEET_NAME)   ' 设置Effect工作表
    
    ' ========== 列标题查找与验证 ==========
    Set headerRow = ws.rows(1)   ' 设置标题行（第一行）
    
    ' 查找Name列位置
    Set nameCol = headerRow.Find(What:=NAME_HEADER, LookAt:=xlWhole, MatchCase:=False)
    ' 查找ID列位置
    Set idCol = headerRow.Find(What:=ID_HEADER, LookAt:=xlWhole, MatchCase:=False)
    
    ' 验证Name列标题是否存在
    If nameCol Is Nothing Then
        MsgBox "错误：未找到Name列标题", vbExclamation
        IDByName = CVErr(xlErrNA)   ' 返回#N/A错误
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' 验证ID列标题是否存在
    If idCol Is Nothing Then
        MsgBox "错误：未找到ID列标题", vbExclamation
        IDByName = CVErr(xlErrNA)   ' 返回#N/A错误
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' ========== 名称查找与循环处理 ==========
    ' 在Name列中精确查找指定名称
    Set nameCell = ws.Columns(nameCol.Column).Find( _
        What:=nameValue, _
        LookIn:=xlValues, _
        LookAt:=xlWhole, _
        MatchCase:=False)
    
    ' 记录第一个找到的单元格，用于检测循环
    If Not nameCell Is Nothing Then
        Set firstFound = nameCell
    End If
    
    ' ========== 循环查找符合条件的记录 ==========
    ' 遍历所有匹配的名称，直到找到不是SKIP_ID的记录或回到起点
    Do While Not nameCell Is Nothing
        ' 获取当前行对应的ID值
        foundID = CStr(ws.Cells(nameCell.Row, idCol.Column).Value)
        
        ' 检查ID是否为需要跳过的特殊值
        If foundID <> SKIP_ID Then
            IDByName = foundID   ' 返回符合条件的ID
            Exit Function
        End If
        
        ' 继续查找下一个匹配项
        Set nameCell = ws.Columns(nameCol.Column).FindNext(nameCell)
        
        ' 如果回到起点，退出循环（避免无限循环）
        If nameCell.Address = firstFound.Address Then
            Exit Do
        End If
    Loop
    
    ' ========== 未找到符合条件的记录 ==========
    IDByName = -1   ' 返回-1表示未找到
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    ' ========== 错误处理 ==========
    IDByName = CVErr(xlErrValue)   ' 返回#VALUE!错误
    MsgBox "获取ID时出错: " & Err.Description, vbCritical   ' 显示错误信息
End Function



--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·2·3·录卡·CreateFollower.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·2·3·录卡·CreateFollower"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：创建随从卡牌
' 参数：CardName - 卡牌名称
' 返回：新创建的随从卡牌ID
' 说明：基于默认模板创建1费青铜随从卡牌
' ==================================================
Function CreateFollower(CardName As String)
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim newID As String     ' 新卡牌ID
    Dim CardSet As String   ' 卡包名称
    Dim Class As String     ' 职业类型
    
    ' ========== 获取默认配置 ==========
    CardSet = DownByID("1000000", "CardSet")   ' 从默认卡牌获取卡包
    Class = DownByID("1000000", "Class")       ' 从默认卡牌获取职业
    
    ' ========== 生成卡牌ID ==========
    newID = GetCardSetID(CardSet) & IDTranslate("Class", Class)   ' 卡包+职业
    newID = newID & 0                                               ' 可构筑标志（0不可构筑）
    newID = newID & IDTranslate("Rarity", "Bronze")                ' 稀有度（青铜）
    newID = GetCardID(newID)                                       ' 获取完整卡牌ID
    
    ' ========== 卡牌属性录入 ==========
    Call UpByID(newID, "CardSet", CardSet)     ' 设置卡包
    Call UpByID(newID, "Class", Class)         ' 设置职业
    Call UpByID(newID, "Rarity", "Bronze")     ' 设置稀有度
    Call UpByID(newID, "Name", CardName)       ' 设置卡牌名称
    Call UpByID(newID, "Type", "Follower")     ' 设置卡牌类型为随从
    Call UpByID(newID, "Cost", "1")            ' 设置费用为1
    Call UpByID(newID, "Attack", "1")          ' 设置攻击力为1
    Call UpByID(newID, "Defense", "1")         ' 设置生命值为1
    
    CreateFollower = newID   ' 返回新卡牌ID
End Function

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：创建纹章卡牌
' 参数：CardName - 卡牌名称
' 返回：新创建的纹章卡牌ID
' 说明：基于默认模板创建0费纹章卡牌
' ==================================================
Function CreateCrest(CardName As String)
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim newID As String     ' 新卡牌ID
    Dim CardSet As String   ' 卡包名称
    Dim Class As String     ' 职业类型
    Dim Rarity As String    ' 稀有度
    
    ' ========== 获取默认配置 ==========
    CardSet = DownByID("1000000", "CardSet")   ' 从默认卡牌获取卡包
    Class = DownByID("1000000", "Class")       ' 从默认卡牌获取职业
    Rarity = DownByID("1000000", "Rarity")     ' 从默认卡牌获取稀有度
    
    ' ========== 生成卡牌ID ==========
    newID = GetCardSetID(CardSet) & IDTranslate("Class", Class)   ' 卡包+职业
    newID = newID & 0                                               ' 可构筑标志（0不可构筑）
    newID = newID & IDTranslate("Rarity", Rarity)                  ' 稀有度
    newID = GetCardID(newID)                                       ' 获取完整卡牌ID
    
    ' ========== 卡牌属性录入 ==========
    Call UpByID(newID, "CardSet", CardSet)     ' 设置卡包
    Call UpByID(newID, "Class", Class)         ' 设置职业
    Call UpByID(newID, "Rarity", Rarity)       ' 设置稀有度
    Call UpByID(newID, "Name", CardName)       ' 设置卡牌名称
    Call UpByID(newID, "Type", "Crest")        ' 设置卡牌类型为纹章
    Call UpByID(newID, "Cost", "0")            ' 设置费用为0
    
    CreateCrest = newID   ' 返回新卡牌ID
End Function


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·0·4·基础·NameInputBox.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·0·4·基础·NameInputBox"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：卡牌名称输入框函数
' 参数：BoxName - 输入框标题
'        Tag - 可选参数，预留用于扩展功能（当前未使用）
' 返回：卡牌ID字符串
' 说明：根据输入的卡牌名称返回对应ID，如卡牌不存在则自动创建1费从者
' ==================================================
Function NameInputBox(BoxName As String, Optional Tag As String = "") As String
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim Str As String      ' 存储用户输入的卡牌名称
    Dim Arr As Variant
    Dim StrID As String    ' 存储卡牌ID查询结果
    NameInputBox = Empty
    
    ' ========== 输入提示设置 ==========
    Str = "请输入卡牌名称：" & Chr(13) & _
            "提示：如果该卡牌尚未创建，会默认创建为1费从者" & Chr(13) & _
            "提示：如果是类似财宝的加入手牌，用@分开"
    
    ' ========== 获取用户输入 ==========
    Str = InputBox(Str, BoxName, "妖精")   ' 默认值为"妖精"
    Arr = Split(Str, "@")
    For i = 0 To UBound(Arr)
        Str = Arr(i)
        ' ========== 卡牌存在性检查 ==========
        StrID = IDByName(Str)   ' 通过名称查询卡牌ID
        
        ' ========== 卡牌处理逻辑 ==========
        If Val(StrID) < 0 Then
            ' 卡牌不存在：创建新卡牌并返回新卡牌ID
            NameInputBox = NameInputBox & CreateFollower(Str) & "@"
        Else
            ' 卡牌已存在：直接返回查询到的卡牌ID
            NameInputBox = NameInputBox & StrID & "@"
        End If
    Next i
    If Right(NameInputBox, 1) = "@" Then NameInputBox = Left(NameInputBox, Len(NameInputBox) - 1)
End Function



--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·2·4·录卡·DeleteEffect.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·2·4·录卡·DeleteEffect"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：删除效果（卡牌）记录，处理相关引用
' 参数：idValue - 要删除的卡牌ID
'        newID - 替换ID（为空时检查引用，非空时替换引用）
' 返回：Boolean - 删除操作是否成功
' 说明：安全删除卡牌记录，检查并处理其他单元格对该ID的引用
' ==================================================
Function DeleteEffect(idValue As String, newID As String) As Boolean
    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler
    
    ' ========== 输入参数验证 ==========
    ' 验证ID长度是否符合标准（7位数字）
    If Len(idValue) <> Len("1000000") Then Exit Function
    If Len(newID) <> Len("1000000") And newID <> "" Then Exit Function
    
    ' ========== 常量定义 ==========
    Const SHEET_NAME As String = "Effect"     ' 效果数据工作表名称
    Const ID_HEADER As String = "ID"          ' ID列标题
    
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim ws As Worksheet           ' 工作表对象
    Dim headerRow As Range        ' 标题行范围
    Dim idCol As Range            ' ID列范围
    Dim idCell As Range           ' 目标ID单元格
    Dim replaceFound As Boolean   ' 是否找到需要替换的引用
    Dim lastRow As Long           ' 最后数据行号
    Dim col As Range              ' 列循环变量
    Dim cell As Range             ' 单元格循环变量
    
    ' ========== 工作表初始化 ==========
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.Sheets(SHEET_NAME)   ' 设置Effect工作表
    
    ' 查找ID列的标题位置（第一行）
    Set headerRow = ws.rows(1)
    Set idCol = headerRow.Find(What:=ID_HEADER, LookAt:=xlWhole, MatchCase:=False)
    
    ' 验证ID列标题是否存在
    If idCol Is Nothing Then
        MsgBox "错误：未找到ID列标题", vbExclamation
        DeleteEffect = False
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' ========== 引用检查与替换 ==========
    replaceFound = False
    lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.rows.count, idCol.Column).End(xlUp).Row   ' 获取ID列最后一行
    
    ' 遍历所有非ID列，检查是否存在对目标ID的引用
    For Each col In ws.UsedRange.Columns
        If col.Column <> idCol.Column Then   ' 跳过ID列本身
            For Each cell In col.Cells
                ' 跳过标题行，只检查数据行
                If cell.Row > 1 And cell.Row <= lastRow Then
                    ' 检查单元格内容是否包含目标ID
                    If InStr(1, cell.Value, idValue) > 0 Then
                        ' 如果newID为空，说明是删除操作，但存在引用则不允许删除
                        If Len(newID) = 0 Then
                            MsgBox "错误：该卡牌尚存在引用，无法删除", vbExclamation
                            DeleteEffect = False
                            Exit Function
                        End If
                        ' 找到引用并标记，同时进行替换操作
                        replaceFound = True
                        cell.Value = Replace(cell.Value, idValue, newID)   ' 替换引用
                    End If
                End If
            Next cell
        End If
    Next col
    
    ' ========== ID记录删除 ==========
    ' 在ID列中精确查找目标ID
    Set idCell = ws.Columns(idCol.Column).Find( _
        What:=idValue, _
        LookIn:=xlValues, _
        LookAt:=xlWhole, _
        MatchCase:=False)
    
    ' 如果找到目标ID记录，删除整行
    If Not idCell Is Nothing Then
        Application.DisplayAlerts = False   ' 禁用删除确认提示
        idCell.EntireRow.Delete              ' 删除整行记录
        Application.DisplayAlerts = True    ' 恢复确认提示
        DeleteEffect = True                  ' 返回操作成功
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' ========== 未找到目标ID处理 ==========
    DeleteEffect = False   ' 未找到指定ID，返回操作失败
    Exit Function

ErrorHandler:
    ' ========== 错误处理 ==========
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True   ' 确保恢复系统提示设置
    DeleteEffect = False               ' 返回操作失败
    MsgBox "操作时出错: " & Err.Description, vbCritical   ' 显示错误信息
End Function

Attribute VB_Base = "0{00020820-0000-0000-C000-000000000046}"

--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·3·2·牌组·AddToOrigDeck.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·3·2·牌组·AddToOrigDeck"
' ==================================================
' 过程功能：向原卡组添加卡牌
' 说明：检查选中的卡牌，将其添加到原卡组并进行排序
' ==================================================
Sub AddToOrigDeck()
    ' ========== 优化设置 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim CardName As String       ' 选中的卡牌名称
    Dim OrigDeck As Variant      ' 原卡组数据数组
    Dim i As Integer             ' 排序位置索引
    
    ' ========== 选中卡牌检查 ==========
    CardName = CStr(IsSelected("CardSet·Cards"))   ' 获取选中区域的卡牌名称
    
    ' 如果选中了有效卡牌，则进行添加操作
    If CardName <> "" Then
        ' ========== 卡牌排序处理 ==========
        i = OrigDeckSorting(CardName)   ' 获取卡牌排序位置（同时处理重复卡牌）
        
        ' ========== 卡组数据更新 ==========
        OrigDeck = Down("OrigDeck")                     ' 获取当前原卡组数据
        
        OrigDeck(i + 1, 1) = DownByID(IDByName(CardName), "Cost")   ' 设置费用（第1列）
        OrigDeck(i + 1, 2) = CardName                               ' 设置卡牌名称（第2列）
        OrigDeck(i + 1, 3) = 3                                      ' 设置数量为3（第3列）
        
        Call Up("OrigDeck", OrigDeck)   ' 更新原卡组数据
    End If
    
CleanUp:
    ' ========== 恢复应用程序状态 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
End Sub

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：原卡组排序管理
' 参数：CardName - 可选，要处理的卡牌名称
' 返回：Integer - 排序后的非空行数量
' 说明：对原卡组按费用排序，支持卡牌去重和空行清理
' ==================================================
Function OrigDeckSorting(Optional CardName As String = "") As Integer
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim OrigDeck As Variant          ' 原卡组数据数组
    Dim rowCount As Integer          ' 总行数
    Dim colCount As Integer          ' 总列数
    Dim nonEmptyCount As Integer     ' 非空行计数
    Dim i As Integer, j As Integer, k As Integer   ' 循环计数器
    Dim tempArr() As Variant         ' 临时排序数组
    Dim tempVal As Variant           ' 临时交换变量
    
    ' ========== 数据初始化 ==========
    OrigDeck = Down("OrigDeck")      ' 获取原卡组数据
    rowCount = 40                    ' 固定行数：40行
    colCount = 3                     ' 固定列数：3列（费用、名称、数量）
    
    ' ========== 重复卡牌处理 ==========
    ' 如果提供了卡牌名称，检查并删除现有重复项
    If CardName <> "" Then
        For i = 1 To rowCount
            If CStr(OrigDeck(i, 2)) = CardName Then
                ' 找到重复卡牌，清空该行数据
                OrigDeck(i, 1) = Empty
                Exit For
            End If
        Next i
    End If
    
    ' ========== 非空行统计 ==========
    nonEmptyCount = 0
    For i = 1 To rowCount
        If Not IsEmpty(OrigDeck(i, 1)) Then
            nonEmptyCount = nonEmptyCount + 1   ' 统计非空行数量
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 空卡组处理 ==========
    If nonEmptyCount = 0 Then
        OrigDeckSorting = 0   ' 返回0表示卡组为空
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' ========== 临时数组创建 ==========
    ReDim tempArr(1 To nonEmptyCount, 1 To colCount)   ' 创建临时数组存放非空行
    
    ' ========== 数据提取到临时数组 ==========
    k = 1   ' 临时数组行索引
    For i = 1 To rowCount
        If Not IsEmpty(OrigDeck(i, 1)) Then
            ' 复制整行数据到临时数组
            For j = 1 To colCount
                tempArr(k, j) = OrigDeck(i, j)
            Next j
            k = k + 1
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 冒泡排序算法 ==========
    ' 按费用（第1列）升序排序
    For i = 1 To nonEmptyCount - 1
        For j = i + 1 To nonEmptyCount
            If tempArr(i, 1) > tempArr(j, 1) Then
                ' 交换整行数据
                For k = 1 To colCount
                    tempVal = tempArr(i, k)
                    tempArr(i, k) = tempArr(j, k)
                    tempArr(j, k) = tempVal
                Next k
            End If
        Next j
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 排序数据回写 ==========
    ' 将排序后的数据写回原数组前部
    For i = 1 To nonEmptyCount
        For j = 1 To colCount
            OrigDeck(i, j) = tempArr(i, j)
        Next j
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 剩余行清空 ==========
    ' 清空非空行之后的所有行
    For i = nonEmptyCount + 1 To rowCount
        For j = 1 To colCount
            OrigDeck(i, j) = Empty
        Next j
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 数据更新与返回 ==========
    Call Up("OrigDeck", OrigDeck)        ' 更新原卡组数据
    OrigDeckSorting = nonEmptyCount      ' 返回非空行数量
End Function


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·7·1·对局·StartGame.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·7·1·对局·StartGame"
' ==================================================
' 子过程功能：开始新游戏
' 说明：初始化游戏环境，设置先后手，创建卡组，进行起始换牌
' ==================================================
Sub StartGame()
    ' ========== 优化设置 ==========
    ' 关闭屏幕更新和事件处理以提高性能
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    
    '初始化随机数生成器
    Randomize
    
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim options() As String          ' 选项数组
    Dim Arr As Variant               ' 临时数组
    Dim Brr As Variant               ' 临时数组
    Dim Str As String                ' 临时字符串
    Dim Str2 As String                ' 临时字符串
    Dim FirstMove As String          ' 先手标识
    Dim BattleYourself As Boolean    ' 左右互搏
    Dim Effectrr() As String         ' 效果数组
    Dim i%, j%, k%, m%               ' 循环计数器
    Dim AllData As Variant           ' 所有数据
    Dim OrigDeck As Variant          ' 原始牌组
    Dim EnemyDeck As String          ' 牌组数据
    Dim EnemySwap As String          ' 换牌数据
    
    ' ========== 下载并处理原始卡组 ==========
    '获取牌组
    ReDim OrigDeck(1 To 40)
    Arr = Down("OrigDeck")
    m = 0
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
        If Arr(i, 1) = "" Then Exit For
        Str = IDByName(CStr(Arr(i, 2)))
        EnemyDeck = EnemyDeck & "||" & Str & "|" & Arr(i, 3)
        For j = 1 To Arr(i, 3)
            m = m + 1
            OrigDeck(m) = Str
        Next j
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 先后手选择 ==========
    ' 生成随机数并创建选项
    options = Split(".r1d100=" & Int((100 - 1 + 1) * Rnd + 1) & "（点大的先手）" & SentenceTranslate("|FirstMove|SecondMove|BattleYourself"), "|")
    i = Val(ShowChoice(options, 60, 3))
    
    ' 用户取消选择则退出
    If i = 0 Then GoTo CleanUp
    
    ' 处理用户选择
    If options(i) = Translate("BattleYourself") Then
        FirstMove = "FirstMove"
        BattleYourself = True
        Call Up("BattleYourself", True)      ' 设置自战模式
    Else
        FirstMove = Translate(options(i))
        BattleYourself = False
        Call Up("BattleYourself", False)     ' 设置正常对战模式
    End If
    Call Up("FirstMove", FirstMove)          ' 存储先手信息

    ' ========== 主区域初始化 ==========
    ' 初始化双方玩家状态
    Call Up("EnemyDefense", 20)              ' 敌方当前生命值
    Call Up("EnemyMaxDefense", 20)           ' 敌方最大生命值
    Call Up("EnemyPP", 0)                    ' 敌方当前能量点
    Call Up("EnemyMaxPP", 0)                 ' 敌方最大能量点
    Call Up("EnemyEP", 0)                    ' 敌方当前EP
    Call Up("EnemySEP", 0)                   ' 敌方当前SEP
    
    Call Up("AlliedDefense", 20)             ' 我方当前生命值
    Call Up("AlliedMaxDefense", 20)          ' 我方最大生命值
    Call Up("AlliedPP", 0)                   ' 我方当前能量点
    Call Up("AlliedMaxPP", 0)                ' 我方最大能量点
    Call Up("AlliedEP", 0)                   ' 我方当前EP
    Call Up("AlliedSEP", 0)                  ' 我方当前SEP
    
    ' 初始化游戏区域
    Call Up("EnemyField", Empty)             ' 敌方场地
    Call Up("AlliedField", Empty)            ' 我方场地
    Call Up("Hand", Empty)                   ' 手牌区域
    Call Up("Crest", Empty)                  ' 纹章区域
    Call Up("Deck·Cards", 0)                 ' 牌组卡牌数
    Call Up("Turn", "S0")                    ' 当前回合（F先手）
    Call Up("StepsArea", Empty)              ' 步骤记录区域

    ' ========== 数据区域初始化 ==========
    Call Up("Data", Empty)                   ' 卡牌数据
    Call Up("Steps", Empty)                  ' 游戏步骤记录
    Call Up("CountNum", Empty)               ' 计数数据
    Call Up("Log", Empty)                    ' 对局日志

    ' ========== 创建卡组 ==========
    ' 循环处理双方玩家（自战模式时处理两次）
    For k = 1 To 2
        ' 自战模式特殊处理：第二次循环时选择第二个卡组
        If k = 2 Then
            If FirstMove = "FirstMove" Then FirstMove = "SecondMove" Else FirstMove = "FirstMove" ' 第二个玩家为后手
            If BattleYourself Then
                Call SelectDeck              ' 选择第二个卡组
                
                ' ========== 下载并处理原始卡组 ==========
                ' 根据卡组数据填充临时卡组数组
                ReDim OrigDeck(1 To 40)
                Arr = Down("OrigDeck")
                m = 0
                For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
                    If Arr(i, 1) = "" Then Exit For
                    Str = IDByName(CStr(Arr(i, 2)))
                    For j = 1 To Arr(i, 3)
                        m = m + 1
                        OrigDeck(m) = Str
                    Next j
                Next i
            Else
InputAgain:
                Str = EnemyDeck
                EnemyDeck = InputBox("1.「剪切」发送给对手" & Chr(13) & "2.「粘贴」对手提供的信息，然后点确定", "交换牌组", Str)
                If EnemyDeck = Str Then
                    Str = MsgBox("对手的牌组与你完全一样，你确定吗？", vbYesNo, "可能粘贴错误")
                    If Str <> vbYes Then GoTo InputAgain
                End If
                
                ReDim OrigDeck(1 To 40)
                m = 0
                Arr = Split(EnemyDeck, "||")
                For i = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                    If Left(Arr(i), 1) <> 1 Then Exit For
                    Brr = Split(Arr(i), "|")
                    For j = 1 To Brr(1)
                        m = m + 1
                        OrigDeck(m) = Brr(0)
                    Next j
                Next i
                EnemySwap = Arr(i) & "||" & Arr(i + 1) & "||" & Arr(i + 2)
            End If
        End If
        
        ' 检查卡组是否满足40张
        If UBound(OrigDeck) < 40 Then
            MsgBox "错误：牌组不满40张", vbExclamation
            GoTo CleanUp
        End If
        
        ' ========== 设置职业 ==========
        If k = 1 Then
            Str = Translate(Down("Class"))
            Call Up("Class", Str, Left(FirstMove, 1) & 1000)  ' 设置职业
            EnemyDeck = Str & EnemyDeck
        Else
            Arr = Split(EnemyDeck, "||")
            Call Up("Class", Arr(0), Left(FirstMove, 1) & 1000)  ' 设置职业
        End If
        
        ' ========== 生成随机序列 ==========
        Brr = Split("|28|29|8|34|13|27|15|16|21|33|23|3|30|6|1|4|20|11|25|17|9|10|12|40|35|26|19|7|24|37|14|5|18|39|32|2|38|36|31|22", "|")            ' 生成40个随机数用于洗牌
        
        ' ========== 创建卡牌数据 ==========
        AllData = Down("Data")               ' 获取现有数据
        ' 找到第一个空行
        For j = 1 To UBound(AllData, 1)
            If AllData(j, 1) = "" Then Exit For
        Next j
        
        ' 创建主战者卡牌
        AllData(j, 1) = Left(FirstMove, 1) & 1000        ' 主战者ID
        If FirstMove = "FirstMove" Then
            AllData(j, 2) = "1000001"                    ' 先手主战者ID
        Else
            AllData(j, 2) = "1000002"                    ' 后手主战者ID
        End If
        AllData(j, 3) = "S0"                             ' 回合信息
        AllData(j, 4) = "Leader"                         ' 区域：主战者
        
        ' 创建牌组卡牌
        For i = 1 To UBound(OrigDeck)
            AllData(j + i, 1) = Left(FirstMove, 1) & 1000 + i    ' 卡牌ID
            AllData(j + i, 2) = OrigDeck(Brr(i))                 ' 卡牌原型ID（随机洗牌）
            'AllData(j + i, 3) = "S0"                             ' 回合信息
            'AllData(j + i, 4) = "Deck"                           ' 区域：牌组
        Next i
        Call Up("Data", AllData)             ' 更新数据
        For i = 1 To UBound(OrigDeck)
            Call UpByMatchID(CStr(AllData(j + i, 1)), "Area", "Deck")
        Next i
        
        ' ========== 创建信仰效果 ==========
        Arr = Translate("Faith")
        AllData = NonEmptyArray(AllData)     ' 清理空行
        ' 遍历所有卡牌检查信仰效果
        For j = 1 To UBound(AllData, 1)
            Str = IDByName(Arr & ":" & DownByID(CStr(AllData(j, 2)), "Name"))
            If Val(Str) > 0 Then             ' 如果存在信仰卡牌
                Effectrr = GetEffect(CStr(AllData(j, 1)))  ' 获取效果
                ' 查找信仰效果
                For i = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
                    If Left(Effectrr(i), Len("Faith")) = "Faith" Then Exit For
                Next i
                ' 如果找到信仰效果则执行
                If i <= UBound(Effectrr) Then
                    Call GetAbility("||Faith", CStr(AllData(j, 1)))
                    Call Up("Faith", Val(Mid(Effectrr(i), 1 + Len("Faith||"), 10)), CStr(AllData(j, 1)))
                End If
            End If
        Next j
        
        ' ========== 起始换牌阶段 ==========
        ' 从牌组随机获取4张卡牌作为起始手牌选项
        If k = 1 Or BattleYourself Then
            Arr = GetGoal("Random&4-Allied-Deck-Card", Left(FirstMove, 1) & 1000)
            EnemyDeck = EnemyDeck & "||" & PreviousGoal
        Else
            Arr = Split(EnemySwap, "||")
            Arr = Split("&" & Arr(0), "&")
        End If
        
        ReDim options(0 To UBound(Arr))
        options(0) = Translate("CardExchange")           ' 换牌提示
        ' 填充选项数组
        For i = 1 To UBound(Arr)
            options(i) = DownByID(CStr(DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(i)), "ID")), "Name")
        Next i
        
        ' 显示换牌选择界面（负数允许反选）
        If k = 1 Or BattleYourself Then
            Str = ShowChoice(options, 150, 4, -1)
            EnemyDeck = EnemyDeck & "||" & Str
        Else
            Brr = Split(EnemySwap, "||")
            Str = Brr(1)
        End If
        
        ' 处理换牌：未选择的卡牌放回牌组
        Str2 = Empty
        For i = 1 To UBound(Arr)
            If InStr(Str, i) = 0 Then
                Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(i)), "Area", "Hand")  ' 选择的卡牌加入手牌
            Else
                Str2 = Str2 & "&" & Arr(i)
                Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(i)), "Area", "Wait")  ' 选择的卡牌加入手牌
            End If
        Next i
        
        ' ========== 补充手牌至4张 ==========
        ' 计算需要补充的卡牌数量
        If k = 1 Or BattleYourself Then
            Arr = GetGoal("Random&" & (Len(Str) + 1) / 2 & "-Allied-Deck-Card", Left(FirstMove, 1) & 1000)
            EnemyDeck = EnemyDeck & "||" & PreviousGoal
        Else
            Arr = Split(EnemySwap, "||")
            Arr = Split("&" & Arr(2), "&")
        End If
        For i = 1 To UBound(Arr)
            Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(i)), "Area", "Hand")      ' 补充卡牌加入手牌
        Next i
        
        '不要的牌放回牌组
        Arr = Split(Str2, "&")
        For i = 1 To UBound(Arr)
            Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(i)), "Area", "Deck")
        Next i
        
        If k = 2 Then
            If FirstMove = "FirstMove" Then FirstMove = "SecondMove" Else FirstMove = "FirstMove" ' 第二个玩家为后手
        End If
    Next k

    ' ========== 开始先手回合 ==========
    If FirstMove = "FirstMove" Then
        Call Up("Turn", "F1")
        Call UpSteps("TurnStart", Left(FirstMove, 1) & 1000)
        Call TurnStartFunction
    End If

    ' ========== 刷新界面 ==========
    Call UIRefresh
    Call UpStepsArea

CleanUp:
    ' ========== 恢复应用程序状态 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
End Sub

--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·3·4·牌组·DeleteDeck.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·3·4·牌组·DeleteDeck"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：卡组删除核心函数
' 参数：DeckName - 可选，要删除的卡组名称，为空时显示选择对话框
' 说明：从Deck工作表中删除指定卡组的所有数据
' ==================================================
Function DeleteDeckFunction(Optional DeckName As String = "")
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim Decks As Variant         ' Deck工作表数据数组
    Dim i As Integer, j As Integer, k As Integer   ' 循环计数器
    Dim options() As String      ' 选择对话框选项数组
    Dim Str As String            ' 选项字符串
    
    ' ========== Deck工作表数据初始化 ==========
    ' 读取Deck工作表全部数据（42行×100列）
    Decks = Range(Sheets("Deck").Cells(1, 1), Sheets("Deck").Cells(42, 100))
    
    ' ========== 卡组选择逻辑 ==========
    ' 如果未指定要删除的卡组名称，显示选择对话框
    If DeckName = "" Then
        ' 构建选择对话框的选项字符串
        Str = Translate("Select") & Translate("Deck")   ' 基础提示：选择卡组
        
        ' 遍历所有卡组，构建选项列表
        For k = 1 To UBound(Decks, 2)
            If Decks(1, k) <> "" Then
                Str = Str & "|" & Decks(1, k)   ' 添加卡组名称到选项
            End If
            k = k + 3   ' 每组卡组占用3列，跳过2列（循环自动+1）
        Next k
        
        ' 显示选择对话框并获取用户选择
        options = Split(Str, "|")               ' 按竖线分割选项字符串
        i = Val(ShowChoice(options, 100, 4))    ' 显示选择对话框，按钮宽度100，4列
        
        ' 根据用户选择确定要删除的卡组
        If i > 0 Then DeckName = options(i)     ' 有效选择时设置卡组名称
    End If
    
    ' ========== 卡组数据删除 ==========
    ' 遍历Deck工作表查找目标卡组
    For k = 1 To UBound(Decks, 2)
        ' 检查当前列组是否匹配目标卡组名称
        If Decks(1, k) = DeckName Then
            ' 清空该卡组的所有数据（3列×42行）
            For i = 1 To UBound(Decks, 1)
                Decks(i, k) = Empty       ' 清空第1列（费用/卡组名称）
                Decks(i, k + 1) = Empty   ' 清空第2列（卡牌名称）
                Decks(i, k + 2) = Empty   ' 清空第3列（数量/职业）
            Next i
            Exit For   ' 找到并删除后退出循环
        End If
        
        k = k + 3   ' 每组卡组占用3列，跳过2列（循环自动+1）
    Next k
    
    ' ========== 数据回写 ==========
    ' 将更新后的数据写回Deck工作表
    Range(Sheets("Deck").Cells(1, 1), Sheets("Deck").Cells(42, 100)) = Decks
End Function

' ==================================================
' 过程功能：卡组删除用户接口
' 说明：提供给用户调用的卡组删除入口，调用核心删除函数
' ==================================================
Sub DeleteDeck()
    ' ========== 优化设置 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    
    Call DeleteDeckFunction   ' 调用卡组删除核心函数
    
CleanUp:
    ' ========== 恢复应用程序状态 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
End Sub


--------------------------------------------------
