文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·6·4·结算·GetGoal.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·6·4·结算·GetGoal"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：获取符合条件的卡牌目标
' 参数：
'   InputGoal - 输入的目标条件字符串
'   ThisCard - 当前卡牌ID
'   InputEffect - 输入的效果字符串（可选）
' 返回：String() - 符合条件的卡牌ID数组
' 说明：根据多种条件筛选卡牌目标，包括区域、类型、状态、属性等
' ==================================================
Function GetGoal(ByVal InputGoal As String, ThisCard As String, Optional InputEffect As String = "", Optional OrigGoal As String = "None") As String()
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim Goalrr() As String           ' 结果数组
    ReDim Goalrr(0 To 0)             ' 初始化为一个空元素
    GetGoal = Goalrr                 ' 默认返回空数组
    'PreviousGoal = Empty            ' 清空上一次目标记录
    Dim PreviousFuse As String       ' 上一次融合目标记录
    Dim PrePrevious As String       ' 上一次融合目标记录
    
    Dim i%, j%, k%                   ' 循环计数器
    Dim Str As String                ' 临时字符串
    Dim Str2 As String               ' 临时字符串2
    Dim Flag As Boolean              ' 标志位
    Dim Effect As String             ' 效果字符串
    Dim Irr() As String              ' 临时字符串数组
    
    ' ========== 已处理目标直接返回 ==========
    ' 检查是否已经是处理好的Goal格式
    i = InStr(InputGoal, "Goal&")
    If i > 0 Then
        PreviousGoal = Mid(InputGoal, i + Len("Goal&"), 1000)
        Goalrr = Split("&" & PreviousGoal, "&")
        GetGoal = Goalrr
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' ========== 处理过程变量 ==========
    Dim EraseFlag As Boolean         ' 删除标志
    Dim AllGoal As Variant           ' 所有目标数据
    Dim TempGoal As Variant          ' 临时目标数据

    ' ========== 设置交战对手 ==========
    Dim Opposing As String
    If Left(ThisCard, 1) = Left(StrikeMatchID, 1) Then
        If ClashMatchID = "" Then Opposing = "None" Else Opposing = ClashMatchID
    Else
        Opposing = StrikeMatchID
    End If

    ' ========== 设置上一个限定目标 ==========
    i = InStr(InputEffect, InputGoal)
    If i = 0 Then i = InStr(InputEffect, OrigGoal)
    If i = 0 Then i = Len(InputEffect) + 1
    Str = Left(InputEffect, i - 1)
    For j = 1 To i - 5
        If Mid(InputEffect, i - 5 - j + 1, 5) = "Goal&" Then
            Str = Mid(Str, i - 5 - j + 1 + 5, 100)
            Exit For
        End If
    Next j
    If j <= i - 5 Then
        i = InStr(Str, "|")
        If i > 0 Then PreviousGoal = Left(Str, i - 1)
    Else
        PreviousGoal = Empty
    End If

    ' ========== 设置上上个限定目标 ==========
    i = InStr(InputEffect, InputGoal)
    If i = 0 Then i = InStr(InputEffect, OrigGoal)
    If i > 0 Then
        Str = Left(InputEffect, i - 1)
        k = 0
        For j = 1 To i - 5
            If Mid(InputEffect, i - 5 - j + 1, 5) = "Goal&" Then
                Str = Mid(Str, i - 5 - j + 1 + 5, 100)
                k = k + 1
            End If
            If k = 2 Then Exit For
        Next j
        If j <= i - 5 Then
            i = InStr(Str, "|")
            If i > 0 Then PrePrevious = Left(Str, i - 1)
        Else
            PrePrevious = Empty
        End If
    End If
    
    ' ========== 设置上一次融合目标 ==========
    If InStr(InputGoal, "FuseCards") > 0 Then
        TempGoal = NonEmptyArray(Down("Steps"))
        PreviousFuse = Empty
        For i = 1 To UBound(TempGoal, 1)
            j = UBound(TempGoal, 1) - i + 1
            If TempGoal(j, 2) = "Keyword&Fuse" Then Exit For
        Next i
        For i = j To UBound(TempGoal, 1)
            If TempGoal(i, 2) = "Fused" Then PreviousFuse = PreviousFuse & TempGoal(i, 3) & "&"
        Next i
        If PreviousFuse <> "" Then PreviousFuse = Left(PreviousFuse, Len(PreviousFuse) - 1)
    End If
    
    ' ========== 处理XYZ变量 ==========
    ' 将条件中的X、Y、Z替换为实际数值
    For j = 1 To Len(InputGoal)
        Str = Mid(InputGoal, j, 1)
        If InStr("XYZ", Str) > 0 Then
            i = InStr(InputEffect, Str & "=")
            Str = Mid(InputEffect, i + 2, 100)
            i = InStr(Str, "||")
            If i > 0 Then Str = Left(Str, i - 1)
            
            k = Val(GetX(Str, ThisCard, "Goal&" & PreviousGoal & "||" & Str))
            InputGoal = Replace(InputGoal, Mid(InputGoal, j, 1), k)
        End If
    Next j
    
    Dim Arr() As String              ' 输入条件分割数组
    Arr = Split(InputGoal, "-")
    AllGoal = Down("Data")           ' 获取所有卡牌数据

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
    ' ========== 具体目标筛选 ==========
    ' 处理特殊目标类型：ThisCard、Opposing、It、FuseCards
    If Arr(UBound(Arr)) = "ThisCard" Then
        ' 遍历找到当前卡牌
        For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal)
            If AllGoal(i, 1) = ThisCard Then Exit For
        Next i
        ' 如果没找到直接退出函数
        If i > UBound(AllGoal) Then Exit Function
        ' 提取当前卡牌数据
        ReDim TempGoal(1 To 1, 1 To UBound(AllGoal, 2))
        For j = 1 To UBound(TempGoal, 2)
            TempGoal(1, j) = AllGoal(i, j)
        Next j
        AllGoal = TempGoal
        
    ElseIf Arr(UBound(Arr)) = "Opposing" Then
        ' 遍历找到交战对手
        For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal)
            If AllGoal(i, 1) = Opposing Then Exit For
        Next i
        ' 如果没找到直接退出函数
        If i > UBound(AllGoal) Then Exit Function
        ' 提取对手数据
        ReDim TempGoal(1 To 1, 1 To UBound(AllGoal, 2))
        For j = 1 To UBound(TempGoal, 2)
            TempGoal(1, j) = AllGoal(i, j)
        Next j
        AllGoal = TempGoal
        
    ElseIf Arr(UBound(Arr)) = "It" Then
        ' 如果没找到上一个目标直接退出函数
        If PreviousGoal = "" Then Exit Function
        
        Goalrr = Split(PreviousGoal, "&")
        ReDim AllGoal(1 To UBound(Goalrr) + 1, 1 To UBound(AllGoal, 2))
        
        ' 遍历找It目标
        For i = 0 To UBound(Goalrr)
            TempGoal = Down("Data")
            For j = 1 To UBound(TempGoal, 1)
                If TempGoal(j, 1) = Goalrr(i) Then Exit For
            Next j
            If j <= UBound(TempGoal, 1) Then
                For k = 1 To UBound(TempGoal, 2)
                    AllGoal(i + 1, k) = TempGoal(j, k)
                Next k
            End If
        Next i
        
    ElseIf Arr(UBound(Arr)) = "That" Then
        ' 如果没找到上一个目标直接退出函数
        If PrePrevious = "" Then Exit Function
        
        Goalrr = Split(PrePrevious, "&")
        ReDim AllGoal(1 To UBound(Goalrr) + 1, 1 To UBound(AllGoal, 2))
        
        ' 遍历找It目标
        For i = 0 To UBound(Goalrr)
            TempGoal = Down("Data")
            For j = 1 To UBound(TempGoal, 1)
                If TempGoal(j, 1) = Goalrr(i) Then Exit For
            Next j
            If j <= UBound(TempGoal, 1) Then
                For k = 1 To UBound(TempGoal, 2)
                    AllGoal(i + 1, k) = TempGoal(j, k)
                Next k
            End If
        Next i
        
    ElseIf Arr(UBound(Arr)) = "FuseCards" Then
        ' 如果没找到融合目标直接退出函数
        If PreviousFuse = "" Then Exit Function
        
        Goalrr = Split(PreviousFuse, "&")
        ReDim AllGoal(1 To UBound(Goalrr) + 1, 1 To UBound(AllGoal, 2))
        
        ' 遍历找融合目标
        For i = 0 To UBound(Goalrr)
            TempGoal = Down("Data")
            For j = 1 To UBound(TempGoal, 1)
                If TempGoal(j, 1) = Goalrr(i) Then Exit For
            Next j
            If j <= UBound(TempGoal, 1) Then
                For k = 1 To UBound(TempGoal, 2)
                    AllGoal(i + 1, k) = TempGoal(j, k)
                Next k
            End If
        Next i
    End If

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
    ' ========== 生效方筛选 ==========
    ' 根据Allied（友方）或Enemy（敌方）条件筛选
    If InStr(InputGoal, "Allied-") > 0 Then
        For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal, 1)
            If Left(AllGoal(i, 1), 1) <> Left(ThisCard, 1) Then AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
        Next i
        AllGoal = NonEmptyArray(AllGoal)
    ElseIf InStr(InputGoal, "Enemy-") > 0 Then
        For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal, 1)
            If Left(AllGoal(i, 1), 1) = Left(ThisCard, 1) Then AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
        Next i
        AllGoal = NonEmptyArray(AllGoal)
    End If

    ' ========== 生效区域筛选 ==========
    ' 根据区域条件筛选：场地、手牌、牌组等
    If InStr(InputGoal, "AnyArea-") > 0 Then
    
    ElseIf InStr(InputGoal, "Field") > 0 Or InStr(InputGoal, "Hand") > 0 Or InStr(InputGoal, "Deck") > 0 Or _
        InStr(InputGoal, "ThisTurn") > 0 Or InStr(InputGoal, "ThisMatch") > 0 Then
        
        '处理本回合
        Str = ""
        j = InStr(InputGoal, "ThisTurn")
        If j > 0 Then
            Str = Mid(InputGoal, j + Len("ThisTurn&"), 100)
            j = InStr(Str, "-")
            If j > 0 Then Str = Left(Str, j - 1)
            j = InStr(Str, "&")
            If j > 0 Then Str = Left(Str, j - 1)
        End If
        '处理本次对战
        j = InStr(InputGoal, "ThisMatch")
        If j > 0 Then
            Str = Mid(InputGoal, j + Len("ThisMatch&"), 100)
            j = InStr(Str, "-")
            If j > 0 Then Str = Left(Str, j - 1)
            j = InStr(Str, "&")
            If j > 0 Then Str = Left(Str, j - 1)
        End If
        Str2 = Down("Turn")
        
        For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal)
            EraseFlag = True
            If InStr(InputGoal, "ThisTurn") > 0 Then
                If SearchSteps(CStr(AllGoal(i, 1)), Str, Str2) Then EraseFlag = False
            ElseIf InStr(InputGoal, "ThisMatch") > 0 Then
                If SearchSteps(CStr(AllGoal(i, 1)), Str) Then EraseFlag = False
            ElseIf InStr(InputGoal, AllGoal(i, 4)) > 0 Then
                EraseFlag = False
            ElseIf InStr(InputGoal, "Field") > 0 And (AllGoal(i, 4) = "Crest" Or AllGoal(i, 4) = "Faith" Or AllGoal(i, 4) = "Leader") Then
                EraseFlag = False
            End If
            
            If EraseFlag Then AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
        Next i
        AllGoal = NonEmptyArray(AllGoal)
    End If

    ' ========== 生效种类筛选 ==========
    ' 处理卡牌类型条件：从者、法术、护符等
    If InStr(InputGoal, "Hand-") > 0 Or InStr(InputGoal, "Deck-") > 0 Or InStr(InputGoal, "AnyArea-") > 0 Then
        InputGoal = Replace(InputGoal, "-Card", "-Follower&Spell&Amulet")
        If Left(InputGoal, Len("Card")) = "Card" Then InputGoal = "Follower&Spell&Amulet" & Mid(InputGoal, 1 + Len("Card"), 100)
    Else
        InputGoal = Replace(InputGoal, "-Card", "-Follower&Amulet")
        If Left(InputGoal, Len("Card")) = "Card" Then InputGoal = "Follower&Amulet" & Mid(InputGoal, 1 + Len("Card"), 100)
    End If
    
    ' 根据具体类型筛选
    If InStr(InputGoal, "Follower") > 0 Or InStr(InputGoal, "Spell") > 0 Or InStr(InputGoal, "Amulet") > 0 Or _
        InStr(InputGoal, "Leader") > 0 Or InStr(InputGoal, "Crest") > 0 Or InStr(InputGoal, "Faith") > 0 Then
        
        ' 遍历检查类型匹配
        For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal)
            Str = DownByID(CStr(AllGoal(i, 2)), "Type")
            If InStr(InputGoal, Str) = 0 Then AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
        Next i
        AllGoal = NonEmptyArray(AllGoal)
    End If

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
    ' ========== 排除特定目标 ==========
    ' 处理排除条件：Other、Opposing、It等
    Str = ""
    If InStr(InputGoal, "Other-") > 0 Then
        Str = ThisCard
    ElseIf InStr(InputGoal, "Opposing&Except-") > 0 Then
        If Opposing <> "" Then Str = Opposing
    ElseIf InStr(InputGoal, "It&Except-") > 0 Then
        Str = PreviousGoal
    ElseIf InStr(InputGoal, "That&Except-") > 0 Then
        Str = PrePrevious
    ElseIf InStr(InputGoal, "FuseCards&Except-") > 0 Then
        Str = PreviousFuse
    End If
    If Str <> "" Then
        For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal)
            If InStr(Str, AllGoal(i, 1)) > 0 Then AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
        Next i
        AllGoal = NonEmptyArray(AllGoal)
    End If

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
    ' ========== 进化状态筛选 ==========
    ' 根据进化状态筛选
    If InStr(InputGoal, "UnEvolved-") > 0 Then
        For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal)
            ' 检查是否已进化
            If InStr(AllGoal(i, 5), "Evolved||") > 0 Or InStr(AllGoal(i, 5), "SuperEvolved||") > 0 Then AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
        Next i
        AllGoal = NonEmptyArray(AllGoal)
    ElseIf InStr(InputGoal, "SuperEvolved-") > 0 Then
        For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal)
            ' 检查是否未超进化
            If InStr(AllGoal(i, 5), "SuperEvolved||") = 0 Then AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
        Next i
        AllGoal = NonEmptyArray(AllGoal)
    ElseIf InStr(InputGoal, "Evolved-") > 0 Then
        For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal)
            ' 检查是否未进化
            If InStr(AllGoal(i, 5), "Evolved||") = 0 And InStr(AllGoal(i, 5), "SuperEvolved||") = 0 Then AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
        Next i
        AllGoal = NonEmptyArray(AllGoal)
    End If

    ' ========== 攻击状态筛选 ==========
    ' 根据是否已攻击筛选
    If InStr(InputGoal, "UnStriked-") > 0 Then
        For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal)
            ' 检查是否已攻击
            If InStr(AllGoal(i, 5), "Striked&") > 0 Then AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
        Next i
        AllGoal = NonEmptyArray(AllGoal)
    ElseIf InStr(InputGoal, "Striked-") > 0 Then
        For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal)
            ' 检查是否未攻击
            If InStr(AllGoal(i, 5), "Striked&") = 0 Then AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
        Next i
        AllGoal = NonEmptyArray(AllGoal)
    End If

    ' ========== 受伤状态筛选 ==========
    ' 根据受伤状态筛选
    If InStr(InputGoal, "UnDamaged-") > 0 Then
        For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal)
            ' 检查受伤数值
            j = InStr(AllGoal(i, 5), "Damaged&")
            If j > 0 Then j = Val(Mid(AllGoal(i, 5), j + Len("Damaged&"), 10))
            If j > 0 Then AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
        Next i
        AllGoal = NonEmptyArray(AllGoal)
    ElseIf InStr(InputGoal, "Damaged-") > 0 Then
        For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal)
            ' 检查未受伤
            j = InStr(AllGoal(i, 5), "Damaged&")
            If j > 0 Then j = Val(Mid(AllGoal(i, 5), j + Len("Damaged&"), 10))
            If j = 0 Then AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
        Next i
        AllGoal = NonEmptyArray(AllGoal)
    End If
    
    ' ========== 启动状态筛选 ==========
    ' 根据是否已启动筛选
    If InStr(InputGoal, "UnEngaged-") > 0 Then
        For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal)
            ' 检查是否已启动
            If InStr(AllGoal(i, 5), "Engaged||") > 0 Then AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
        Next i
        AllGoal = NonEmptyArray(AllGoal)
    ElseIf InStr(InputGoal, "Engaged-") > 0 Then
        For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal)
            ' 检查是否未启动
            If InStr(AllGoal(i, 5), "Engaged||") = 0 Then AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
        Next i
        AllGoal = NonEmptyArray(AllGoal)
    End If

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
    ' ========== 职业筛选 ==========
    ' 根据卡牌职业筛选
    Str = ""
    If InStr(InputGoal, "Forestcraft-") > 0 Then
        Str = "Forestcraft"
    ElseIf InStr(InputGoal, "Swordcraft-") > 0 Then
        Str = "Swordcraft"
    ElseIf InStr(InputGoal, "Runecraft-") > 0 Then
        Str = "Runecraft"
    ElseIf InStr(InputGoal, "Dragoncraft-") > 0 Then
        Str = "Dragoncraft"
    ElseIf InStr(InputGoal, "Abysscraft-") > 0 Then
        Str = "Abysscraft"
    ElseIf InStr(InputGoal, "Havencraft-") > 0 Then
        Str = "Havencraft"
    ElseIf InStr(InputGoal, "Portalcraft-") > 0 Then
        Str = "Portalcraft"
    ElseIf InStr(InputGoal, "Neutral-") > 0 Then
        Str = "Neutral"
    End If
    If Str <> "" Then
        For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal)
            If DownByID(CStr(AllGoal(i, 2)), "Class") <> Str Then AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
        Next i
        AllGoal = NonEmptyArray(AllGoal)
    End If

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
    ' ========== 能力关键词筛选 ==========
    ' 根据能力关键词筛选
    i = InStr(InputGoal, "Keyword&")
    If i > 0 Then
        Str = Mid(InputGoal, i + Len("Keyword&"), 100)
        i = InStr(Str, "-")
        Str = Left(Str, i - 1)
        
        ' 处理特定关键词
        If InStr("Fanfare|LastWords|Evolve|SuperEvolve|Strike|Clash|Engage|Spellboost|Fuse|Enhance|Accelerate|Crystallize|Rush|Storm|Ambush|Bane|Ward|Drain|Intimidate|Aura|Barrier", Str) > 0 Then
            For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal)
                EraseFlag = True
                ' 检查基础能力
                For j = 1 To 9
                    Effect = DownByID(CStr(AllGoal(i, 2)), "Effect" & j)
                    If Effect = "" Then Exit For
                    If Left(Effect, Len(Str)) = Str Then EraseFlag = False
                Next j
                
                ' 检查获得的能力
                If InStr(AllGoal(i, 5), "{" & Str) > 0 Then EraseFlag = False
                
                ' 检查已失去的能力
                If InStr(AllGoal(i, 5), "Remove|" & Str) > 0 Then EraseFlag = True
                
                If EraseFlag Then AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
            Next i
        ElseIf Str = "Countdown" Then
            ' 处理倒数关键词
            For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal)
                If DownByID(CStr(AllGoal(i, 2)), "Countdown") = "" Then AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
            Next i
        Else
            ' 处理其他关键词
            For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal)
                EraseFlag = True
                For j = 1 To 9
                    Effect = DownByID(CStr(AllGoal(i, 2)), "Effect" & j)
                    If Effect = "" Then Exit For
                    If InStr(Effect, Str) > 0 Then EraseFlag = False
                Next j

                If EraseFlag Then AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
            Next i
        End If
        AllGoal = NonEmptyArray(AllGoal)
    End If

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
    ' ========== 类型筛选 ==========
    ' 根据卡牌类型特征筛选
    i = InStr(InputGoal, "Trait:")
    If i > 0 Then
        Str = Val(Mid(InputGoal, i + Len("Trait:"), 100))
        For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal)
            EraseFlag = True
            If DownByID(CStr(AllGoal(i, 2)), "Trait") = IDTranslate("Trait", Str) Then EraseFlag = False
            ' 检查获得的效果类型
            If InStr(AllGoal(i, 5), "Trait:" & Str) Then EraseFlag = False
            If EraseFlag Then AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
        Next i
        AllGoal = NonEmptyArray(AllGoal)
    End If

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
    ' ========== 名称筛选 ==========
    ' 根据同名或不同名条件筛选
    i = InStr(InputGoal, "SameName&")
    If i = 0 Then i = InStr(InputGoal, "DifferentName&")
    If i > 0 Then
        Str = Mid(InputGoal, i, 100)
        i = InStr(Str, "&")
        Effect = Left(Str, i - 1)
        Str = Mid(Str, i + 1, 100)
        i = InStr(Str, "-")
        Str = Left(Str, i - 1)
        
        ' 提取比较对象的ID
        If Str = "ThisCard" Then
            Str = DownByMatchID(ThisCard, "ID")
        ElseIf Str = "Opposing" Then
            Str = DownByMatchID(Opposing, "ID")
        ElseIf Str = "It" Then
            If PreviousGoal = "" Then
                Str = "1000000"
            Else
                Goalrr = Split(PreviousGoal, "&")
                ' 遍历获取It目标的ID
                For j = 0 To UBound(Goalrr)
                    If Goalrr(j) <> "" Then Goalrr(j) = DownByMatchID(Goalrr(j), "ID")
                Next j
                Str = Join(Goalrr, "&")
            End If
        ElseIf Str = "That" Then
            If PrePrevious = "" Then
                Str = "1000000"
            Else
                Goalrr = Split(PrePrevious, "&")
                ' 遍历获取It目标的ID
                For j = 0 To UBound(Goalrr)
                    If Goalrr(j) <> "" Then Goalrr(j) = DownByMatchID(Goalrr(j), "ID")
                Next j
                Str = Join(Goalrr, "&")
            End If
        ElseIf Str = "FuseCards" Then
            If PreviousFuse = "" Then
                Str = "1000000"
            Else
                Goalrr = Split(PreviousFuse, "&")
                ' 遍历获取融合目标的ID
                For j = 0 To UBound(Goalrr)
                    If Goalrr(j) <> "" Then Goalrr(j) = DownByMatchID(Goalrr(j), "ID")
                Next j
                Str = Join(Goalrr, "&")
            End If
        ElseIf Str = "Select" Then
            Str = ""
        Else
            Str = Str
        End If
        
        ' 根据同名或不同名进行筛选
        If Str <> "" Then
            If Effect = "SameName" Then
                For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal)
                    If InStr(Str, AllGoal(i, 2)) = 0 Then AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
                Next i
            ElseIf Effect = "DifferentName" Then
                For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal)
                    If InStr(Str, AllGoal(i, 2)) > 0 Then AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
                Next i
            End If
            AllGoal = NonEmptyArray(AllGoal)
        End If
    End If

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
    ' ========== 属性比较筛选 ==========
    ' 根据费用、攻击力、生命值、倒计数等属性筛选
    Str = Empty
    Str2 = Empty
    For i = 0 To UBound(Arr)
        If Left(Arr(i), Len("Orig")) = "Orig" Or _
        Left(Arr(i), Len("Cost")) = "Cost" Or _
        Left(Arr(i), Len("Attack")) = "Attack" Or _
        Left(Arr(i), Len("Defense")) = "Defense" Or _
        Left(Arr(i), Len("Countdown")) = "Countdown" Then
            If Str = "" Then Str = Arr(i) Else Str2 = Arr(i)
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' 处理属性比较条件
    i = InStr(Str, "&")
    If i > 0 Then
        Str2 = Mid(Str, 1 + i, 100)
        Str = Left(Str, i - 1)
        AllGoal = CompareModel(Str, AllGoal, Str2)
    ElseIf InStr(Str, "Highest") = 0 And InStr(Str, "Lowest") = 0 And Str <> "" Then
        AllGoal = CompareModel(Str, AllGoal)
        AllGoal = CompareModel(Str2, AllGoal)
    ElseIf Str <> "" Then
        AllGoal = CompareModel(Str2, AllGoal)
        AllGoal = CompareModel(Str, AllGoal)
    End If

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
    ' ========== 位置筛选 ==========
    ' 根据在场地中的位置筛选
    i = InStr(InputGoal, "LeftMost&")
    j = 0
    If i > 0 Then j = Val(Mid(InputGoal, i + Len("LeftMost&"), 10))
    If j > 0 Then
        For i = j + 1 To UBound(AllGoal, 1)
            AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
        Next i
        AllGoal = NonEmptyArray(AllGoal)
    End If
    
    ' 最后位置筛选
    i = InStr(InputGoal, "RightMost&")
    j = 0
    If i > 0 Then j = Val(Mid(InputGoal, i + Len("RightMost&"), 10))
    If j > 0 Then
        For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal, 1) - j
            AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
        Next i
        AllGoal = NonEmptyArray(AllGoal)
    End If

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
    ' ========== 防报错处理 ==========
    ' 如果没有找到任何目标，返回空结果
    ReDim Goalrr(0 To 0)
    If IsEmpty(AllGoal) Or AllGoal(1, 1) = "" Then
        PreviousGoal = ""
        GetGoal = Goalrr
        Exit Function
    End If

    ' ========== 选择操作前置处理 ==========
    ' 为玩家选择操作做准备
    If InStr(InputGoal, "Select&") > 0 Then
        ' 去掉灵气和土之印等不可选目标
        Str = Down("Turn")
        For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal, 1)
            If Left(Str, 1) = Left(AllGoal(i, 1), 1) Then
                
            ElseIf AllGoal(i, 4) = "Field" Or AllGoal(i, 4) = "Leader" Then
                If (InStr(AllGoal(i, 5), "Aura") > 0 Or InStr(AllGoal(i, 5), "Ambush") > 0 Or InStr(AllGoal(i, 5), "EarthSigil&") > 0) Then
                    Arr = SplitEffect(CStr(AllGoal(i, 5)))
                    Flag = False
                    For j = 0 To UBound(Arr)
                        If Arr(j) = "Aura" Or Arr(j) = "Ambush" Or InStr(Arr(j), "{Aura}") > 0 Or InStr(AllGoal(i, 5), "{Ambush}") > 0 Or InStr(Arr(j), "EarthSigil&") > 0 Then
                            Flag = True
                        ElseIf Arr(j) = "Remove|Aura" Or Arr(j) = "Remove|Ambush" Then
                            Flag = False
                            Exit For
                        End If
                    Next j
                    If Flag Then AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
                ElseIf InStr(AllGoal(i, 5), "CantSelectOther") > 0 Then
                    ' 处理不能选择其他的效果
                    For j = 1 To UBound(AllGoal, 1)
                        If InStr(AllGoal(j, 5), "CantSelectOther") = 0 Then AllGoal(j, 1) = Empty
                    Next j
                    Exit For
                End If
            End If
        Next i
        AllGoal = NonEmptyArray(AllGoal)

        ' 调换自己与对手的顺序（对手方优先）
        ReDim TempGoal(1 To UBound(AllGoal, 1), 1 To UBound(AllGoal, 2))
        Str = Down("FirstMove")
        If Str = "FirstMove" Then Str = "S" Else Str = "F"
        k = 1
        For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal, 1)
            If Left(AllGoal(i, 1), 1) = Str Then
                For j = 1 To UBound(AllGoal, 2)
                    TempGoal(k, j) = AllGoal(i, j)
                Next j
                k = k + 1
            End If
        Next i
        For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal, 1)
            If Left(AllGoal(i, 1), 1) <> Str Then
                For j = 1 To UBound(AllGoal, 2)
                    TempGoal(k, j) = AllGoal(i, j)
                Next j
                k = k + 1
            End If
        Next i
        AllGoal = TempGoal
        
        ' 敌我方边界插入空档（界面美化）
        ReDim TempGoal(1 To 10 + UBound(AllGoal, 1), 1 To UBound(AllGoal, 2))
        For j = 1 To UBound(AllGoal, 2)
            TempGoal(1, j) = AllGoal(1, j)
        Next j
        k = 2
        For i = 2 To UBound(AllGoal, 1)
            ' 在敌我方边界插入空档
            If Left(AllGoal(i - 1, 1), 1) <> Left(AllGoal(i, 1), 1) Then
                For j = 1 To 6
                    If (k - 1) Mod 6 = 0 Then
                        Exit For
                    Else
                        TempGoal(k, 1) = 9999
                        k = k + 1
                    End If
                Next j
            End If
            For j = 1 To UBound(AllGoal, 2)
                TempGoal(k, j) = AllGoal(i, j)
            Next j
            k = k + 1
        Next i
        AllGoal = NonEmptyArray(TempGoal)
    End If

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
    ' ========== 最终目标选择处理 ==========
    ' 处理选择、随机或全部返回
    If InStr(InputGoal, "Select&") > 0 Then
        ' 获取需选择数量
        i = InStr(InputGoal, "Select&")
        Str = Mid(InputGoal, i + Len("Select&"), 10)
        j = Val(Str)
        
        ' 需选择数大于选项数
        If AllGoal(1, 1) = "" Then
            PreviousGoal = "-1"
            Exit Function
        End If
        If j > UBound(AllGoal, 1) Then
            If DownByID(DownByMatchID(ThisCard, "ID"), "Type") = "Spell" Then
                PreviousGoal = ""
                Exit Function
            Else
                j = UBound(AllGoal, 1)
            End If
        End If
        
        ' 生成选项供玩家选择
        Dim options() As String
        Str = Translate("Select") & "&" & j
        For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal, 1)
            Str = Str & "|" & DownByID(CStr(AllGoal(i, 2)), "Name")
        Next i
        options = Split(Str, "|")
        If j = 0 Then j = -1
        Str = ShowChoice(options, 150, 6, j)
        If Str = "" Then
            PreviousGoal = ""
            Exit Function
        End If
        Irr = Split(Str, "&")
        
        ' 直接退出处理
        For i = 0 To UBound(Irr)
            If options(Irr(i)) = "" Then Exit Function
        Next i
        
        ' 处理名称相关选择
        If InStr(InputGoal, "DifferentName&Select") > 0 Then
            Str = Empty
            For i = 0 To UBound(Irr)
                Str = Str & AllGoal(Irr(i), 2) & "&"
            Next i
            For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal)
                If InStr(Str, AllGoal(i, 2)) > 0 Then AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
            Next i
        ElseIf InStr(InputGoal, "SameName&Select") > 0 Then
            Str = Empty
            For i = 0 To UBound(Irr)
                Str = Str & AllGoal(Irr(i), 2) & "&"
            Next i
            For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal)
                If InStr(Str, AllGoal(i, 2)) = 0 Then AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
            Next i
        Else
            ' 普通选择处理
            For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal, 1)
                If InStr(Str, i) = 0 Then AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
            Next i
        End If
        
        ' 清理空元素
        For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal, 1)
            If AllGoal(i, 1) = "9999" Then AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
        Next i
        AllGoal = NonEmptyArray(AllGoal)
        
        ' 构建结果数组
        ReDim Goalrr(1 To UBound(AllGoal, 1))
        For i = 1 To UBound(Goalrr)
            Goalrr(i) = AllGoal(i, 1)
        Next i
        
    ElseIf InStr(InputGoal, "Random") > 0 And QueueAction = "" Then
        ' 随机选择处理
        i = InStr(InputGoal, "Random")
        Str = Mid(InputGoal, i + Len("Random&"), 10)
        j = Val(Str)
        If j > UBound(AllGoal, 1) Then j = UBound(AllGoal, 1)
        
        ' 产生随机数组
        Dim Randomrr As Variant
        Randomrr = GetRandom(UBound(AllGoal, 1))
        
        ' 随机选择处理
        If j >= 1 Then
            ' 处理不同名随机
            If InStr(InputGoal, "Random@") > 0 Then
                ReDim TempGoal(1 To j, 1 To 2)
                TempGoal(1, 1) = AllGoal(Randomrr(1), 1)
                TempGoal(1, 2) = AllGoal(Randomrr(1), 2)
                k = 2
                For i = 2 To UBound(Randomrr)
                    If k > UBound(TempGoal, 1) Then Exit For
                    For j = 1 To k - 1
                        If TempGoal(j, 2) = AllGoal(Randomrr(i), 2) Then Exit For
                    Next j
                    If j > k - 1 Then
                        TempGoal(k, 1) = AllGoal(Randomrr(i), 1)
                        TempGoal(k, 2) = AllGoal(Randomrr(i), 2)
                        k = k + 1
                    End If
                Next i
                ReDim Goalrr(1 To k - 1)
                For i = 1 To UBound(Goalrr)
                    Goalrr(i) = TempGoal(i, 1)
                Next i
            Else
                ' 普通随机选择
                ReDim Goalrr(1 To j)
                For i = 1 To UBound(Goalrr)
                    Goalrr(i) = AllGoal(Randomrr(i), 1)
                Next i
            End If
        End If
        
    ElseIf InStr(InputGoal, "Random") > 0 And QueueAction <> "" Then
        PreviousGoal = EraseQueueAction("Random")
        If PreviousGoal = "" Then
            MsgBox ("出现bug，请将excel发给sgg，交换控制方继续对局")
            Application.DisplayAlerts = False
            ThisWorkbook.Close False
            Application.DisplayAlerts = True
        ElseIf PreviousGoal = "0" Then
        
        Else
            TempGoal = Split(PreviousGoal, "&")
            ReDim Goalrr(1 To UBound(TempGoal, 1) + 1)
            For i = 1 To UBound(Goalrr)
                Goalrr(i) = TempGoal(i - 1)
            Next i
        End If
    Else
        ' 返回所有目标
        ReDim Goalrr(1 To UBound(AllGoal, 1))
        For i = 1 To UBound(Goalrr)
            Goalrr(i) = AllGoal(i, 1)
        Next i
    End If
    
    ' ========== 存储并返回结果 ==========
    GetGoal = Goalrr
    PreviousGoal = Join(Goalrr, "&")
    If InStr(InputGoal, "Random") > 0 Then
        If PreviousGoal = "" Then Call UpSteps("Random", "0") Else Call UpSteps("Random", PreviousGoal)
    End If
End Function

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：比较卡牌属性模型
' 参数：
'   InputStr - 输入比较字符串
'   AllGoal - 所有目标数据
'   OrStr - 或条件字符串（可选）
' 返回：Variant - 筛选后的目标数据
' 说明：根据属性比较条件筛选卡牌，支持最高/最低值比较
' ==================================================
Function CompareModel(InputStr As String, AllGoal As Variant, Optional OrStr As String = "") As Variant
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i%, j%                           ' 循环计数器
    Dim Str As String                    ' 临时字符串
    Dim Strr() As String                 ' 属性类型数组
    Dim EraseFlag As Boolean             ' 删除标志
    
    Dim CompareX As String, CompareY As String, CompareLogic As String    ' 比较参数
    Dim ResultX As Integer, ResultY As Integer                           ' 比较结果
    Dim CompareX2 As String, CompareY2 As String, CompareLogic2 As String ' 或条件比较参数
    Dim ResultX2 As Integer, ResultY2 As Integer                         ' 或条件比较结果
    
    Dim Irr() As Integer                 ' 临时整数数组

    CompareModel = AllGoal
    If InputStr = "" Then Exit Function

    ' ========== 解析比较条件 ==========
    ' 查找比较属性类型
    Strr = Split("OrigCost|Cost|OrigAttack|Attack|OrigDefense|Defense|OrigCountdown|Countdown", "|")
    For i = 0 To UBound(Strr)
        If Left(InputStr, Len(Strr(i))) = Strr(i) Then
            CompareX = Strr(i)
            Str = Mid(InputStr, 1 + Len(Strr(i)), 100)
            Exit For
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 最高/最低值处理 ==========
    ' 处理$Highest和$Lowest特殊条件
    If Left(Str, 1) = "$" Then
        ReDim Irr(1 To UBound(AllGoal, 1))
        For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal, 1)
            Irr(i) = GetModel(CompareX, CStr(AllGoal(i, 2)), CStr(AllGoal(i, 5)), CStr(AllGoal(i, 1)))
            ' 倒数特殊处理
            If (CompareX = "Countdown" Or CompareX = "OrigCountdown") And Irr(i) = 0 Then
                If Str = "$Highest" Then Irr(i) = 999 Else Irr(i) = -1
            End If
        Next i
        If Str = "$Highest" Then ResultY = Application.WorksheetFunction.Max(Irr) Else ResultY = Application.WorksheetFunction.Min(Irr)
        Str = "=" & ResultY
    End If
    
    ' ========== 提取比较逻辑和数值 ==========
    CompareY = Mid(Str, 2, 10)
    CompareLogic = Left(Str, 1)
    If Left(Str, 2) = "<>" Then
        CompareY = Mid(Str, 3, 10)
        CompareLogic = Left(Str, 2)
    End If

    ' ========== 或条件处理 ==========
    ' 解析或条件比较参数
    If OrStr <> "" Then
        For i = 0 To UBound(Strr)
            If Left(OrStr, Len(Strr(i))) = Strr(i) Then
                CompareX2 = Strr(i)
                Str = Mid(OrStr, 1 + Len(Strr(i)), 100)
                Exit For
            End If
        Next i
        
        ' 或条件的最高/最低值处理
        If Left(Str, 1) = "$" Then
            ReDim Irr(1 To UBound(AllGoal, 1))
            For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal, 1)
                Irr(i) = GetModel(CompareX2, CStr(AllGoal(i, 2)), CStr(AllGoal(i, 5)), CStr(AllGoal(i, 1)))
                ' 倒数特殊处理
                If (CompareX2 = "Countdown" Or CompareX2 = "OrigCountdown") And Irr(i) = 0 Then
                    If Str = "$Highest" Then Irr(i) = 999 Else Irr(i) = -1
                End If
            Next i
            If Str = "$Highest" Then ResultY2 = Application.WorksheetFunction.Max(Irr) Else ResultY2 = Application.WorksheetFunction.Min(Irr)
            Str = "=" & ResultY2
        End If
        
        ' 提取或条件比较逻辑和数值
        CompareY2 = Mid(Str, 2, 10)
        CompareLogic2 = Left(Str, 1)
        If Left(Str, 2) = "<>" Then
            CompareY2 = Mid(Str, 3, 10)
            CompareLogic2 = Left(Str, 2)
        End If
    End If

    ' ========== 属性比较筛选 ==========
    ' 遍历所有目标进行属性比较
    For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal, 1)
        EraseFlag = True
        
        ' 获取比较数值
        ResultX = GetModel(CompareX, CStr(AllGoal(i, 2)), CStr(AllGoal(i, 5)), CStr(AllGoal(i, 1)))
        If CompareY = "0" Or Val(CompareY) > 0 Then
            ResultY = Val(CompareY)
        Else
            ResultY = GetModel(CompareY, CStr(AllGoal(i, 2)), CStr(AllGoal(i, 5)), CStr(AllGoal(i, 1)))
        End If
        
        ' 倒数特殊处理：没有倒数的卡牌直接排除
        If (CompareX = "Countdown" Or CompareX = "OrigCountdown") And ResultX = 0 Then AllGoal(i, 1) = 0
        
        ' ========== 主要条件比较 ==========
        ' 根据比较逻辑进行数值比较
        If CompareLogic = "<>" Then
            If ResultX <> ResultY Then EraseFlag = False
        ElseIf CompareLogic = "<" Then
            If ResultX < ResultY Then EraseFlag = False
        ElseIf CompareLogic = "=" Then
            If ResultX = ResultY Then EraseFlag = False
        ElseIf CompareLogic = ">" Then
            If ResultX > ResultY Then EraseFlag = False
        End If
        
        ' ========== 或条件比较 ==========
        ' 处理或条件比较
        If OrStr <> "" Then
            ResultX2 = GetModel(CompareX2, CStr(AllGoal(i, 2)), CStr(AllGoal(i, 5)), CStr(AllGoal(i, 1)))
            If CompareY2 = "0" Or Val(CompareY2) > 0 Then
                ResultY2 = Val(CompareY2)
            Else
                ResultY2 = GetModel(CompareY2, CStr(AllGoal(i, 2)), CStr(AllGoal(i, 5)), CStr(AllGoal(i, 1)))
            End If
            
            ' 倒数特殊处理
            If (CompareX2 = "Countdown" Or CompareX2 = "OrigCountdown") And ResultX2 = 0 Then AllGoal(i, 1) = 0
            
            ' 或条件比较逻辑
            If CompareLogic2 = "<>" Then
                If ResultX2 <> ResultY2 Then EraseFlag = False
            ElseIf CompareLogic2 = "<" Then
                If ResultX2 < ResultY2 Then EraseFlag = False
            ElseIf CompareLogic2 = "=" Then
                If ResultX2 = ResultY2 Then EraseFlag = False
            ElseIf CompareLogic2 = ">" Then
                If ResultX2 > ResultY2 Then EraseFlag = False
            End If
        End If
        
        ' ========== 标记删除 ==========
        ' 根据比较结果标记需要删除的目标
        If EraseFlag Then AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 清理并返回结果 ==========
    AllGoal = NonEmptyArray(AllGoal)
    CompareModel = AllGoal
End Function



--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·4·1·辅助·GetRandom.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·4·1·辅助·GetRandom"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：生成不重复的随机整数序列
' 参数：n - 序列的最大值，序列从1开始到n
' 返回：Variant - 包含1到n的随机不重复序列的数组
' 算法：Fisher-Yates洗牌算法（Knuth洗牌算法）
' ==================================================
Function GetRandom(n As Long) As Variant
    ' ========== 输入参数验证 ==========
    If n < 1 Then
        GetRandom = Array()   ' 无效输入返回空数组
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i As Long, j As Long   ' 循环计数器
    Dim temp As Long           ' 临时交换变量
    Dim Arr() As Long          ' 结果数组
    
    ' ========== 初始有序数组创建 ==========
    ' 创建从1到n的有序数组 [1, 2, 3, ..., n]
    ReDim Arr(1 To n)
    For i = 1 To n
        Arr(i) = i   ' 填充顺序数值
    Next i
    
    ' ========== Fisher-Yates洗牌算法 ==========
    Randomize   ' 初始化随机数生成器种子
    
    ' 从后向前遍历数组
    For i = n To 2 Step -1
        ' 生成随机索引j，范围在1到i之间
        j = Int(i * Rnd) + 1
        
        ' 交换当前元素Arr(i)和随机元素Arr(j)
        temp = Arr(i)
        Arr(i) = Arr(j)
        Arr(j) = temp
    Next i
    
    GetRandom = Arr   ' 返回随机序列数组
End Function


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·4·2·辅助·NonEmptyArray.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·4·2·辅助·NonEmptyArray"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：过滤非空数组，移除空行或保留数组结构
' 参数：inputArray - 输入数组
'        IsPreserve - 可选，是否保留原数组结构，默认为False
' 返回：Variant - 处理后的数组
' 说明：根据第一列的值过滤数组，移除空值、零值和空字符串所在的行
' ==================================================
Function NonEmptyArray(inputArray As Variant, Optional IsPreserve As Boolean = False) As Variant
    ' ========== 输入验证 ==========
    ' 检查输入是否为有效数组
    If Not IsArray(inputArray) Then GoTo Unload
    
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim resultArray() As Variant   ' 结果数组
    Dim numRows As Long, numCols As Long   ' 输入数组的行列数
    Dim keepCount As Long          ' 需要保留的行数
    Dim i As Long, j As Long, k As Long   ' 循环计数器
    
    ' ========== 数组维度计算 ==========
    ' 计算输入数组的行数
    numRows = UBound(inputArray, 1) - LBound(inputArray, 1) + 1
    If numRows < 0 Then GoTo Unload   ' 无效行数处理
    
    ' 计算输入数组的列数
    numCols = UBound(inputArray, 2) - LBound(inputArray, 2) + 1
    If numCols < 0 Then GoTo Unload   ' 无效列数处理
    
    ' ========== 结果数组初始化 ==========
    If IsPreserve Then
        ' 保留模式：创建与原数组相同大小的结果数组
        ReDim resultArray(1 To numRows, 1 To numCols)
    Else
        ' 紧凑模式：计算需要保留的非空行数
        keepCount = 0
        
        ' 遍历所有行，统计第一列非空的行数
        For i = LBound(inputArray, 1) To UBound(inputArray, 1)
            ' 检查第一列是否包含有效数据
            If Not IsEmpty(inputArray(i, LBound(inputArray, 2))) And _
               Not IsNull(inputArray(i, LBound(inputArray, 2))) And _
               inputArray(i, LBound(inputArray, 2)) <> "" And _
               inputArray(i, LBound(inputArray, 2)) <> 0 Then
                keepCount = keepCount + 1   ' 增加有效行计数
            End If
        Next i
        
        ' ========== 无有效数据处理 ==========
        If keepCount = 0 Then
            ' 没有有效数据时返回1行空数组
            ReDim resultArray(1 To 1, 1 To numCols)
            NonEmptyArray = resultArray
            Exit Function
        End If
        
        ' 根据有效行数创建紧凑的结果数组
        ReDim resultArray(1 To keepCount, 1 To numCols)
    End If
    
    ' ========== 数据填充 ==========
    k = 1   ' 结果数组的行索引
    
    ' 遍历输入数组的所有行
    For i = LBound(inputArray, 1) To UBound(inputArray, 1)
        ' 检查当前行第一列是否包含有效数据
        If Not IsEmpty(inputArray(i, LBound(inputArray, 2))) And _
           Not IsNull(inputArray(i, LBound(inputArray, 2))) And _
           inputArray(i, LBound(inputArray, 2)) <> "" And _
           inputArray(i, LBound(inputArray, 2)) <> 0 Then
            
            ' 复制整行数据到结果数组
            For j = LBound(inputArray, 2) To UBound(inputArray, 2)
                resultArray(k, j) = inputArray(i, j)
            Next j
            
            k = k + 1   ' 移动到结果数组的下一行
        End If
    Next i
    
    NonEmptyArray = resultArray   ' 返回处理后的数组
    Exit Function

Unload:
    ' ========== 错误处理 ==========
    ' 输入无效时返回原始数组
    NonEmptyArray = inputArray
End Function



--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·4·4·辅助·DownByMatchID.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·4·4·辅助·DownByMatchID"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：根据匹配ID获取数据记录
' 参数：idValue - 匹配ID值
'        HeaderName - 要获取的字段名称
' 返回：String - 对应的字段值
' 说明：从Data区域中查找指定ID的记录并返回对应字段值
' ==================================================
Function DownByMatchID(idValue As String, HeaderName As String) As String
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i As Integer           ' 循环计数器
    Dim AllData As Variant     ' 全部数据数组
    
    ' ========== 数据获取 ==========
    ' 获取全部Data区域数据
    AllData = Down("Data")
    
    ' ========== 数据遍历查找 ==========
    ' 遍历所有数据记录查找匹配的ID
    For i = 1 To UBound(AllData)
        If AllData(i, 1) = idValue Then
            ' ========== 字段值返回 ==========
            ' 根据字段名称返回对应的值
            If HeaderName = "ID" Then
                DownByMatchID = AllData(i, 2)   ' 返回卡牌ID
            ElseIf HeaderName = "Turn" Then
                DownByMatchID = AllData(i, 3)   ' 返回回合信息
            ElseIf HeaderName = "Area" Then
                DownByMatchID = AllData(i, 4)   ' 返回区域信息
            ElseIf HeaderName = "Effect" Then
                DownByMatchID = AllData(i, 5)   ' 返回效果信息
            End If
            Exit For   ' 找到匹配记录后退出循环
        End If
    Next i
End Function


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·4·3·辅助·UpByMatchID.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·4·3·辅助·UpByMatchID"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：根据匹配ID更新数据记录
' 参数：idValue - 匹配ID值
'        HeaderName - 要更新的字段名称
'        NewValue - 新的字段值
' 返回：Boolean - 操作是否成功
' 说明：处理卡牌区域移动、效果更新等复杂逻辑
' ==================================================
Function UpByMatchID(idValue As String, HeaderName As String, NewValue As Variant) As Boolean
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i As Integer, j As Integer, k As Integer, m As Integer   ' 循环计数器
    Dim Arr As Variant          ' 临时数组
    Dim Brr As Variant          ' 临时数组
    Dim AllData As Variant      ' 全部数据数组
    Dim EffectSplit() As String ' 效果分割数组
    Dim Effectrr() As String    ' 效果数组
    
    UpByMatchID = True   ' 默认操作成功
    
    ' ========== 数据获取 ==========
    ' 获取全部Data区域数据
    AllData = Down("Data")
    
    ' ========== 数据遍历处理 ==========
    ' 遍历所有数据记录查找匹配的ID
    For i = 1 To UBound(AllData)
        If AllData(i, 1) = idValue Then
            ' ========== 不同字段类型处理 ==========
            If HeaderName = "ID" Then
                ' 更新ID字段
                AllData(i, 2) = NewValue
                
            ElseIf HeaderName = "Turn" Then
                ' 更新回合字段
                AllData(i, 3) = NewValue
                
            ElseIf HeaderName = "Area" Then
                ' ========== 区域移动处理 ==========
                ' 复制当前记录到最后一行
                AllData(UBound(AllData, 1), 1) = AllData(i, 1)
                AllData(UBound(AllData, 1), 2) = AllData(i, 2)
                AllData(UBound(AllData, 1), 3) = Down("Turn")
                
                ' ========== 区域容量检查 ==========
                ' 判断目标区域是否有足够空间
                If NewValue = "Hand" And AllData(i, 4) <> "Hand" Then
                    ' 手牌区域检查（最多9张）
                    Arr = Down("Hand", idValue)
                    If UBound(Arr, 1) >= 9 Then
                        NewValue = "HandDestroy"   ' 手牌已满，标记为销毁
                        UpByMatchID = False        ' 操作失败
                        Call Up("Shadows", Down("Shadows", idValue) + 1, idValue) ' 墓场+1
                    End If
                    
                ElseIf NewValue = "Field" And AllData(i, 4) <> "Field" Then
                    ' 场地区域检查（最多5个）
                    Arr = Down("Field", idValue)
                    If UBound(Arr, 1) >= 5 Then
                        NewValue = "FieldDestroy"   ' 场地已满，标记为销毁
                        UpByMatchID = False         ' 操作失败
                    End If
                    
                ElseIf NewValue = "Crest" Or NewValue = "Faith" Then
                    ' 纹章/信仰区域检查（最多5个）
                    Arr = Down("Crest", idValue)
                    If UBound(Arr, 1) >= 5 Then
                        NewValue = "CrestDestroy"   ' 纹章已满，标记为销毁
                        UpByMatchID = False         ' 操作失败
                    End If
                    For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                        If Arr(j, 2) = AllData(i, 2) Then
                            NewValue = "CrestDestroy"   ' 纹章已满，标记为销毁
                            UpByMatchID = False         ' 操作失败
                            Exit For
                        End If
                    Next j
                End If
                
                ' ========== 新区域设置 ==========
                AllData(UBound(AllData, 1), 4) = NewValue   ' 设置新区域
                AllData(i, 1) = Empty                       ' 清空原记录ID
                    
                If UpByMatchID Then
                    ' ========== 效果清理 ==========
                    ' 分割效果字符串进行处理
                    EffectSplit = SplitEffect(AllData(i, 5))
                    
                    ' 遍历所有效果，清理特定效果
                    For j = 0 To UBound(EffectSplit)
                        If EffectSplit(j) = "When" Then
                            EffectSplit(j) = "\"
                        ElseIf EffectSplit(j) = "ActivateInHand-When" Then
                            EffectSplit(j) = "\"
                        ElseIf EffectSplit(j) = "ActivateInDeck-When" Then
                            EffectSplit(j) = "\"
                        ElseIf EffectSplit(j) = "Ambush" Then
                            EffectSplit(j) = "\"
                        ElseIf EffectSplit(j) = "Bane" Then
                            EffectSplit(j) = "\"
                        ElseIf EffectSplit(j) = "Ward" Then
                            EffectSplit(j) = "\"
                        ElseIf EffectSplit(j) = "Intimidate" Then
                            EffectSplit(j) = "\"
                        ElseIf EffectSplit(j) = "Aura" Then
                            EffectSplit(j) = "\"
                        ElseIf EffectSplit(j) = "Barrier" Then
                            EffectSplit(j) = "\"
                        ElseIf EffectSplit(j) = "CantSelectOther" Then
                            EffectSplit(j) = "\"
                        ElseIf EffectSplit(j) = "IncreaseModes" Then
                            EffectSplit(j) = "\"
                        ElseIf Left(EffectSplit(j), Len("EngageCost")) = "EngageCost" Then
                            EffectSplit(j) = "\"
                        ElseIf Left(EffectSplit(j), Len("Cost")) = "Cost" Then
                            EffectSplit(j) = "\"
                        Else
                            Arr = Split(EffectSplit(j), "|")
                            If UBound(Arr) >= 1 Then
                                If Right(Arr(0), Len("Until")) = "Until" And Left(Arr(1), Len("Cost")) = "Cost" Then EffectSplit(j) = "\"
                            End If
                        End If
                    Next j
                    
                    ' ========== 效果字符串重组 ==========
                    AllData(i, 5) = Join(EffectSplit, "||")                    ' 合并效果
                    If AllData(i, 5) <> "" Then AllData(i, 5) = AllData(i, 5) & "||"   ' 添加分隔符
                    AllData(i, 5) = Replace(AllData(i, 5), "\||", "")          ' 清理标记的效果
                    
                    ' ========== 触发效果处理 ==========
                    ' 获取卡牌的所有效果
                    Effectrr = GetEffect(idValue)
                    
                    ' 当进入场地、纹章、信仰区域时
                    If NewValue = "Field" Or NewValue = "Crest" Or NewValue = "Faith" Then
                        For j = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
                            If Effectrr(j) = "" Then Exit For
                            If Left(Effectrr(j), Len("When")) = "When" Then
                                AllData(i, 5) = AllData(i, 5) & "When||"   ' 添加触发效果
                                Exit For
                            End If
                        Next j
                        
                    ' 当进入手牌区域时
                    ElseIf NewValue = "Hand" Then
                        For j = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
                            If Effectrr(j) = "" Then Exit For
                            If Left(Effectrr(j), Len("ActivateInHand-When")) = "ActivateInHand-When" Then
                                If InStr(AllData(i, 5), "ActivateInHand-When||") = 0 Then AllData(i, 5) = AllData(i, 5) & "ActivateInHand-When||" ' 添加手牌触发效果
                            End If
                            If InStr(Effectrr(j), "Skybound") > 0 Then
                                If InStr(AllData(i, 5), "Skybound&") = 0 Then AllData(i, 5) = AllData(i, 5) & "Skybound&0||" ' 添加奥义
                            End If
                        Next j
                        
                    ' 当进入牌组区域时
                    ElseIf NewValue = "Deck" Then
                        For j = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
                            If Effectrr(j) = "" Then Exit For
                            If Left(Effectrr(j), Len("ActivateInDeck-When")) = "ActivateInDeck-When" Then
                                If InStr(AllData(i, 5), "ActivateInDeck-When||") = 0 Then AllData(i, 5) = AllData(i, 5) & "ActivateInDeck-When||" ' 添加牌组触发效果
                                Exit For
                            End If
                        Next j
                    End If
                    
                    ' ========== 常驻词条处理 ==========
                    ' 当进入场地区域时，添加常驻效果
                    If NewValue = "Field" Then
                        For j = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
                            If Effectrr(j) = "" Then Exit For
                            If Effectrr(j) = "Ambush" _
                            Or Effectrr(j) = "Bane" _
                            Or Effectrr(j) = "Ward" _
                            Or Effectrr(j) = "Intimidate" _
                            Or Effectrr(j) = "Aura" _
                            Or Effectrr(j) = "Barrier" _
                            Or Effectrr(j) = "CantSelectOther" _
                            Then
                                AllData(i, 5) = AllData(i, 5) & Effectrr(j) & "||"   ' 添加常驻效果
                            End If
                        Next j
                    End If
                    
                    ' 当进入场地区域时，添加常驻效果
                    If NewValue = "Field" Or NewValue = "Crest" Then
                        For j = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
                            If Effectrr(j) = "" Then Exit For
                            If Effectrr(j) = "IncreaseModes" _
                            Or Left(Effectrr(j), Len("EngageCost")) = "EngageCost" _
                            Then
                                AllData(i, 5) = AllData(i, 5) & Effectrr(j) & "||"   ' 添加常驻效果
                            End If
                        Next j
                    End If
                    
                    ' ========== 倒数处理 ==========
                    ' 当进入场地或纹章区域时，处理倒数效果
                    If NewValue = "Field" Or NewValue = "Crest" Then
                        j = Val(DownByID(CStr(AllData(i, 2)), "Countdown"))   ' 获取倒数数值
                        If j > 0 Then AllData(i, 5) = AllData(i, 5) & "Countdown&" & j & "||"   ' 添加倒数效果
                    End If
                    
                     ' ========== 土之印处理 ==========
                    ' 当进入场地区域时，处理土之印特性
                    If NewValue = "Field" Then
                        If DownByID(CStr(AllData(i, 2)), "Trait") = Translate("EarthSigil") Then
                            ' 查找场上已有的土之印
                            Arr = Down("Field", idValue)
                            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
                                If InStr(Arr(j, 5), "EarthSigil&") > 0 Then Exit For
                            Next j
                            
                            ' 如果找到已有的土之印，则消失它
                            If j <= UBound(Arr, 1) Then
                                Brr = SplitEffect(Arr(j, 5))
                                For k = 0 To UBound(Brr)
                                    If Left(Brr(k), Len("EarthSigil&")) = "EarthSigil&" Then
                                        m = Val(Mid(Brr(k), 1 + Len("EarthSigil&"), 10)) + 1   ' 土之印计数+1
                                        Exit For
                                    End If
                                Next k
                                QueueAbility = QueueAbility & "\" & "||Banish|Goal&" & CStr(Arr(j, 1))   ' 添加到消失队列
                                QueueMatchID = QueueMatchID & "\" & CStr(AllData(i, 1))                  ' 记录匹配ID
                            Else
                                m = 1   ' 第一个土之印
                            End If
                            AllData(i, 5) = AllData(i, 5) & "EarthSigil&" & m & "||"   ' 添加土之印效果
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
                
                ' ========== 新记录效果复制 ==========
                AllData(UBound(AllData, 1), 5) = AllData(i, 5)   ' 复制效果到新记录
                AllData = NonEmptyArray(AllData, True)           ' 清理空记录
                
            ElseIf HeaderName = "Effect" Then
                ' ========== 效果字段更新 ==========
                AllData(i, 5) = NewValue   ' 直接更新效果字段
            End If
            Exit For   ' 找到匹配记录后退出循环
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 数据保存 ==========
    Call Up("Data", AllData)   ' 更新Data区域
End Function



--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·7·2·对局·TurnStart·End.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·7·2·对局·TurnStart·End"
' ==================================================
' 子过程功能：切换至对手回合
' 说明：处理回合切换逻辑，执行回合结束和开始效果
' ==================================================
Sub TurnStart()
    ' ========== 优化设置 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    
    ThisWorkbook.Save
    
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim Turn As String

    ' ========== 自战模式检查 ==========
    ' 如果是左右互搏模式，直接退出
    If Down("BattleYourself") Then GoTo CleanUp

    ' ========== 回合权限检查 ==========
    ' 如果是自己的回合，退出（防止重复操作）
    Turn = Down("Turn")
    'If Left(Turn, 1) = Left(Down("FirstMove"), 1) Then GoTo CleanUp
    
    ' ========== 回合交换 ==========
    ' 切换回合标识（F/S交替）
    If Left(Turn, 1) = "F" Then
        Turn = "S" & Val(Mid(Turn, 2, 10))
    Else
        Turn = "F" & Val(Mid(Turn, 2, 10)) + 1
    End If
    Call Up("Turn", Turn)
    
    ' ========== 获取对手步骤 ==========
    Dim inputForm As UserInput
    Set inputForm = New UserInput
    
    ' 设置窗体属性
    'inputForm.Caption = "回合开始"
    'inputForm.TextBox1.Text = "请在此粘贴对手提供的信息"
    
InputAgain:
    inputForm.Show
    
    If inputForm.Cancelled Then
        ' 用户取消，回退回合
        If Left(Turn, 1) = "F" Then
            Turn = "S" & Val(Mid(Turn, 2, 10)) - 1
        Else
            Turn = "F" & Val(Mid(Turn, 2, 10))
        End If
        Call Up("Turn", Turn)
        GoTo CleanUp
    Else
        QueueAction = inputForm.UserInput
        If Right(QueueAction, Len("TurnEnd")) <> "TurnEnd" Then
            MsgBox "信息有误，请确保包含完整的TurnEnd结尾"
            GoTo InputAgain
        End If
    End If
'InputAgain:
'    QueueAction = InputBox("「粘贴」对手提供的信息，然后确定", "回合开始")
'    If QueueAction = "" Then
'        If Left(Turn, 1) = "F" Then
'            Turn = "S" & Val(Mid(Turn, 2, 10))
'        Else
'            Turn = "F" & Val(Mid(Turn, 2, 10)) + 1
'        End If
'        Call Up("Turn", Turn)
'
'        GoTo CleanUp
'    ElseIf Right(QueueAction, Len("TurnEnd")) <> "TurnEnd" Then
'        MsgBox ("信息有误")
'        GoTo InputAgain
'    End If

    ' ========== 对手回合开始记录 ==========
    Call TurnStartFunction
    Call Up("StepsArea", Empty)
    Call UpSteps("TurnStart", Left(Turn, 1) & 1000)
    
    ' ========== 获取对手步骤 ==========
    Call InputSteps

    ' ========== 回合结束处理 ==========
    ' 结算对手回合结束效果
    Call UpSteps("TurnEnd", Turn)
    Call TurnEndFunction
    
    ' ========== 回合交换 ==========
    ' 切换回合标识（F/S交替）
    If Left(Turn, 1) = "F" Then
        Turn = "S" & Val(Mid(Turn, 2, 10))
    Else
        Turn = "F" & Val(Mid(Turn, 2, 10)) + 1
    End If
    Call Up("Turn", Turn)

    ' ========== 新回合开始处理 ==========
    ' 结算新回合开始效果
    Call Up("StepsArea", Empty)
    Call UpSteps("TurnStart", Left(Turn, 1) & 1000)
    Call TurnStartFunction

    ' ========== 界面刷新 ==========
    ' 更新回合标题和界面显示
    If Left(Turn, 1) = "F" Then
        Call Up("Head", "先手第" & Mid(Turn, 2, 10) & "回合")
    Else
        Call Up("Head", "后手第" & Mid(Turn, 2, 10) & "回合")
    End If
    Call UIRefresh
    Call UpStepsArea

CleanUp:
    ' ========== 恢复应用程序状态 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
End Sub

' ==================================================
' 子过程功能：结束当前回合
' 说明：处理回合结束逻辑，执行回合结束效果并切换到下一回合
' ==================================================
Sub TurnEnd()
    ' ========== 优化设置 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i%, j%
    Dim Turn As String

    ' ========== 回合权限检查 ==========
    ' 如果不是自己的回合，退出
    Turn = Down("Turn")
    If Left(Turn, 1) <> Left(Down("FirstMove"), 1) Then GoTo CleanUp
    If SearchSteps(Left(Turn, 1) & 1000, "TurnEnd", Turn) Then
        Call OutputSteps
        GoTo CleanUp
    End If

    ' ========== 回合结束处理 ==========
    ' 结算当前回合结束效果
    Call UpSteps("TurnEnd", Left(Turn, 1) & 1000)
    Call TurnEndFunction

    ' ========== 自战模式特殊处理 ==========
    ' 左右互搏模式下的状态交换
    If Down("BattleYourself") Then
        ' 切换回合标识
        If Left(Turn, 1) = "F" Then
            Turn = "S" & Val(Mid(Turn, 2, 10))
        Else
            Turn = "F" & Val(Mid(Turn, 2, 10)) + 1
        End If
        Call Up("Turn", Turn)
    
        ' 交换先后手标识
        If Left(Turn, 1) = "F" Then
            Call Up("FirstMove", "FirstMove")
        Else
            Call Up("FirstMove", "SecondMove")
        End If
        
        ' 交换生命值状态
        i = Down("Defense", "F1000")
        j = Down("MaxDefense", "F1000")
        Call Up("Defense", Down("Defense", "S1000"), "F1000")
        Call Up("MaxDefense", Down("MaxDefense", "S1000"), "F1000")
        Call Up("Defense", i, "S1000")
        Call Up("MaxDefense", j, "S1000")
        
        ' 交换能量点状态
        i = Val(Down("PP", "F1000"))
        j = Val(Down("MaxPP", "F1000"))
        Call Up("PP", Down("PP", "S1000"), "F1000")
        Call Up("MaxPP", Down("MaxPP", "S1000"), "F1000")
        Call Up("PP", i, "S1000")
        Call Up("MaxPP", j, "S1000")
        
        ' 交换进化点状态
        i = Down("EP", "F1000")
        j = Down("SEP", "F1000")
        Call Up("EP", Down("EP", "S1000"), "F1000")
        Call Up("SEP", Down("SEP", "S1000"), "F1000")
        Call Up("EP", i, "S1000")
        Call Up("SEP", j, "S1000")
        
        ' 清空步骤记录区域
        Call Up("StepsArea", Empty)
        Call UpSteps("TurnStart", Left(Turn, 1) & 1000)

        ' ========== 新回合开始处理 ==========
        Call TurnStartFunction
    
    ' ========== 输出自己步骤 ==========
    Else
        Call OutputSteps
    End If
    
    ' ========== 界面刷新 ==========
    ' 更新回合标题和界面显示
    If Left(Turn, 1) = "F" Then
        Call Up("Head", "先手第" & Mid(Turn, 2, 10) & "回合")
    Else
        Call Up("Head", "后手第" & Mid(Turn, 2, 10) & "回合")
    End If
    Call UIRefresh
    Call UpStepsArea

CleanUp:
    ' ========== 恢复应用程序状态 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
End Sub

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：回合开始效果处理
' 说明：执行回合开始时的各种状态更新和效果触发
' ==================================================
Function TurnStartFunction()
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i%, j%, k%, m%                   ' 循环计数器
    Dim Arr As Variant                   ' 临时数组
    Dim Turn As String                   ' 当前回合
    Dim Enemy As String                  ' 对手标识
    Dim Effectrr() As String             ' 效果数组
    Dim EffectSplit() As String          ' 效果分割数组

    ' ========== 初始化回合信息 ==========
    Turn = Down("Turn")
    If Left(Turn, 1) = "F" Then
        Enemy = "S1000"
    Else
        Enemy = "F1000"
    End If

    ' ========== 能量点更新 ==========
    ' 每回合增加1点最大PP和当前PP
    i = Val(Down("MaxPP", Turn)) + 1
    If i > 10 Then i = 10                ' PP上限为10
    Call Up("PP", i, Turn)
    Call Up("MaxPP", i, Turn)

    ' ========== 连击计数清空 ==========
    Call Up("Combo", 0, Turn)

    ' ========== 进化点更新 ==========
    ' 在特定回合给予EP和SEP
    If Turn = "S4" Then Call Up("EP", 2, Turn)      ' 后手第4回合获得EP
    If Turn = "S6" Then Call Up("SEP", 2, Turn)     ' 后手第6回合获得SEP
    If Turn = "F5" Then Call Up("EP", 2, Turn)      ' 先手第5回合获得EP
    If Turn = "F7" Then Call Up("SEP", 2, Turn)     ' 先手第7回合获得SEP

    ' ========== 重置攻击和启动状态 ==========
    ' 重置场上单位的攻击状态和启动状态
    Arr = Down("Field", Turn)
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
        ' 检查是否有攻击或启动状态
        If InStr(Arr(i, 5), "Striked&") > 0 Or InStr(Arr(i, 5), "Engaged") > 0 Then
            EffectSplit = SplitEffect(Arr(i, 5))
            ' 查找攻击或启动状态标记
            For j = 0 To UBound(EffectSplit)
                If Left(EffectSplit(j), Len("Striked&")) = "Striked&" Or EffectSplit(j) = "Engaged" Then Exit For
            Next j
            ' 移除状态标记
            If j <= UBound(EffectSplit) Then
                EffectSplit(j) = "\"
                Arr(i, 5) = Join(EffectSplit, "||") & "||"
                Arr(i, 5) = Replace(Arr(i, 5), "\||", "")
                Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(i, 1)), "Effect", Arr(i, 5))
            End If
        End If
    Next i

    ' ========== 重置融合状态 ==========
    ' 重置手牌中卡牌的融合状态
    Arr = Down("Hand", Turn)
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
        If InStr(Arr(i, 5), "Fused") > 0 Then
            EffectSplit = SplitEffect(Arr(i, 5))
            ' 查找融合状态标记
            For j = 0 To UBound(EffectSplit)
                If EffectSplit(j) = "HaveFused" Then Exit For
            Next j
            ' 移除融合状态标记
            If j <= UBound(EffectSplit) Then
                EffectSplit(j) = "\"
                Arr(i, 5) = Join(EffectSplit, "||") & "||"
                Arr(i, 5) = Replace(Arr(i, 5), "\||", "")
                Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(i, 1)), "Effect", Arr(i, 5))
            End If
        End If
    Next i

    ' ========== 重置进化按钮状态 ==========
    Sheets("Main").Shapes("EvolveBtn").TextFrame.Characters.Text = Translate("Evolve")
    Sheets("Main").Shapes("SuperEvolveBtn").TextFrame.Characters.Text = Translate("SuperEvolve")

    ' ========== 幸运币按钮状态 ==========
    ' 在后手特定回合启用幸运币按钮
    If Turn = "S1" Then Call Up("GetPP", "能量值+1")
    If Turn = "S6" Then Call Up("GetPP", "能量值+1")
    If Left(Turn, 1) = "F" Then
        Sheets("Main").Shapes("PPBtn").TextFrame.Characters.Text = "无法使用"
    Else
        Sheets("Main").Shapes("PPBtn").TextFrame.Characters.Text = Down("GetPP")
    End If

    ' ========== 手牌回合开始效果触发 ==========
    ' 处理自己手牌的回合开始效果
    Arr = Down("Hand", Turn)
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
        If InStr(Arr(i, 5), "ActivateInHand-When||") > 0 Or InStr(Arr(i, 5), "{ActivateInHand-When|") > 0 Then
            Effectrr = GetEffect(CStr(Arr(i, 1)))
            ' 查找并触发回合开始效果
            For k = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
                If Left(Effectrr(k), Len("ActivateInHand-When|Allied-Turn-Start")) = "ActivateInHand-When|Allied-Turn-Start" Or _
                Left(Effectrr(k), Len("ActivateInHand-When|Turn-Start")) = "ActivateInHand-When|Turn-Start" Then
                    Call GetTimePoint(Effectrr(k), CStr(Arr(i, 1)))
                End If
            Next k
        End If
    Next i

    ' 处理对手手牌的回合开始效果
    Arr = Down("Hand", Enemy)
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
        If InStr(Arr(i, 5), "ActivateInHand-When||") > 0 Or InStr(Arr(i, 5), "{ActivateInHand-When|") > 0 Then
            Effectrr = GetEffect(CStr(Arr(i, 1)))
            ' 查找并触发回合开始效果
            For k = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
                If Left(Effectrr(k), Len("ActivateInHand-When|Enemy-Turn-Start")) = "ActivateInHand-When|Enemy-Turn-Start" Or _
                Left(Effectrr(k), Len("ActivateInHand-When|Turn-Start")) = "ActivateInHand-When|Turn-Start" Then
                    Call GetTimePoint(Effectrr(k), CStr(Arr(i, 1)))
                End If
            Next k
        End If
    Next i

    ' ========== 纹章和战场回合开始效果触发 ==========
    ' 处理自己纹章和战场的回合开始效果
    For m = 1 To 2
        If m = 1 Then
            Arr = Down("Crest", Turn)    ' 纹章区域
        Else
            Arr = Down("Field", Turn)    ' 战场区域
        End If
        
        For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
            If InStr(Arr(i, 5), "When||") > 0 Or InStr(Arr(i, 5), "{When|") > 0 Or InStr(Arr(i, 5), "Countdown&") > 0 Then
                EffectSplit = SplitEffect(CStr(Arr(i, 5)))
                For j = 0 To UBound(EffectSplit)
                    ' 处理When效果触发
                    If EffectSplit(j) = "When" Then
                        Effectrr = GetEffect(CStr(Arr(i, 1)))
                        For k = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
                            If Left(Effectrr(k), Len("When|Allied-Turn-Start")) = "When|Allied-Turn-Start" Or _
                            Left(Effectrr(k), Len("When|Turn-Start")) = "When|Turn-Start" Then
                                Call GetTimePoint(Effectrr(k), CStr(Arr(i, 1)))
                            End If
                        Next k
                    ' 处理倒数减少
                    ElseIf Left(EffectSplit(j), Len("Countdown&")) = "Countdown&" Then
                        Call GetTimePoint("When|Allied-Turn-Start||Countdown-1|ThisCard", CStr(Arr(i, 1)))
                    End If
                Next j
            End If
        Next i
    Next m

    ' 处理对手纹章和战场的回合开始效果
    For m = 1 To 2
        If m = 1 Then
            Arr = Down("Crest", Enemy)   ' 对手纹章区域
        Else
            Arr = Down("Field", Enemy)   ' 对手战场区域
        End If
        
        For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
            If InStr(Arr(i, 5), "When||") > 0 Or InStr(Arr(i, 5), "{When|") > 0 Then
                Effectrr = GetEffect(CStr(Arr(i, 1)))
                For k = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
                    If Left(Effectrr(k), Len("When|Enemy-Turn-Start")) = "When|Enemy-Turn-Start" Or _
                    Left(Effectrr(k), Len("When|Turn-Start")) = "When|Turn-Start" Then
                        Call GetTimePoint(Effectrr(k), CStr(Arr(i, 1)))
                    End If
                Next k
            End If
        Next i
    Next m

    ' ========== 牌组回合开始效果触发 ==========
    ' 处理自己牌组的回合开始效果
    Arr = Down("Deck", Turn)
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
        If InStr(Arr(i, 5), "ActivateInDeck-When||") > 0 Or InStr(Arr(i, 5), "{ActivateInDeck-When|") > 0 Then
            Effectrr = GetEffect(CStr(Arr(i, 1)))
            ' 查找并触发回合开始效果
            For k = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
                If Left(Effectrr(k), Len("ActivateInDeck-When|Allied-Turn-Start")) = "ActivateInDeck-When|Allied-Turn-Start" Or _
                Left(Effectrr(k), Len("ActivateInDeck-When|Turn-Start")) = "ActivateInDeck-When|Turn-Start" Then
                    Call GetTimePoint(Effectrr(k), CStr(Arr(i, 1)))
                End If
            Next k
        End If
    Next i

    ' 处理对手牌组的回合开始效果
    Arr = Down("Deck", Enemy)
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
        If InStr(Arr(i, 5), "ActivateInDeck-When||") > 0 Or InStr(Arr(i, 5), "{ActivateInDeck-When|") > 0 Then
            Effectrr = GetEffect(CStr(Arr(i, 1)))
            ' 查找并触发回合开始效果
            For k = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
                If Left(Effectrr(k), Len("ActivateInDeck-When|Enemy-Turn-Start")) = "ActivateInDeck-When|Enemy-Turn-Start" Or _
                Left(Effectrr(k), Len("ActivateInDeck-When|Turn-Start")) = "ActivateInDeck-When|Turn-Start" Then
                    Call GetTimePoint(Effectrr(k), CStr(Arr(i, 1)))
                End If
            Next k
        End If
    Next i

    ' ========== 清空持续效果 ==========
    ' 移除回合开始到期的持续效果
    Arr = Down("Data")
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
        If Arr(i, 1) = "" Then
            Exit For
        ElseIf InStr(CStr(Arr(i, 5)), "-Until") > 0 Then
            EffectSplit = SplitEffect(CStr(Arr(i, 5)))
            For j = 0 To UBound(EffectSplit)
                Effectrr = Split(EffectSplit(j), "|")
                ' 根据所属方移除对应的持续效果
                If Left(Arr(i, 1), 1) = Left(Turn, 1) Then
                    If Effectrr(0) = "Allied-Turn-Start-Until" Or Effectrr(0) = "Turn-Start-Until" Then EffectSplit(j) = "\"
                Else
                    If Effectrr(0) = "Enemy-Turn-Start-Until" Or Effectrr(0) = "Turn-Start-Until" Then EffectSplit(j) = "\"
                End If
            Next j
            Arr(i, 5) = Join(EffectSplit, "||") & "||"
            Arr(i, 5) = Replace(Arr(i, 5), "\||", "")
            Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(i, 1)), "Effect", Arr(i, 5))
        End If
    Next i

    ' ========== 清空能力队列 ==========
    Call EraseQueue

    ' ========== 回合开始抽牌 ==========
    Call GetAbility("||Draw|1", Turn)
End Function

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：回合结束效果处理
' 说明：执行回合结束时的各种状态更新和效果触发
' ==================================================
Function TurnEndFunction()
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i%, j%, k%, m%                   ' 循环计数器
    Dim Arr As Variant                   ' 临时数组
    Dim Effectrr() As String             ' 效果数组
    Dim EffectSplit() As String          ' 效果分割数组
    Dim Turn As String                   ' 当前回合
    Dim Enemy As String                  ' 对手标识

    ' ========== 初始化回合信息 ==========
    Turn = Down("Turn")
    If Left(Turn, 1) = "F" Then
        Enemy = "S1000"
    Else
        Enemy = "F1000"
    End If

    ' ========== 手牌回合结束效果触发 ==========
    ' 处理自己手牌的回合结束效果
    Arr = Down("Hand", Turn)
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
        If InStr(Arr(i, 5), "ActivateInHand-When||") > 0 Or InStr(Arr(i, 5), "{ActivateInHand-When|") > 0 Then
            Effectrr = GetEffect(CStr(Arr(i, 1)))
            For k = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
                If Left(Effectrr(k), Len("ActivateInHand-When|Allied-Turn-End")) = "ActivateInHand-When|Allied-Turn-End" Or _
                Left(Effectrr(k), Len("ActivateInHand-When|Turn-End")) = "ActivateInHand-When|Turn-End" Then
                    Call GetTimePoint(Effectrr(k), CStr(Arr(i, 1)))
                End If
            Next k
        End If
    Next i

    ' 处理对手手牌的回合结束效果
    Arr = Down("Hand", Enemy)
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
        If InStr(Arr(i, 5), "ActivateInHand-When||") > 0 Or InStr(Arr(i, 5), "{ActivateInHand-When|") > 0 Then
            Effectrr = GetEffect(CStr(Arr(i, 1)))
            For k = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
                If Left(Effectrr(k), Len("ActivateInHand-When|Enemy-Turn-End")) = "ActivateInHand-When|Enemy-Turn-End" Or _
                Left(Effectrr(k), Len("ActivateInHand-When|Turn-End")) = "ActivateInHand-When|Turn-End" Then
                    Call GetTimePoint(Effectrr(k), CStr(Arr(i, 1)))
                End If
            Next k
        End If
    Next i

    ' ========== 纹章和战场回合结束效果触发 ==========
    ' 处理自己纹章和战场的回合结束效果
    For m = 1 To 2
        If m = 1 Then
            Arr = Down("Crest", Turn)    ' 纹章区域
        Else
            Arr = Down("Field", Turn)    ' 战场区域
        End If
        
        For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
            If InStr(Arr(i, 5), "When||") > 0 Or InStr(Arr(i, 5), "{When|") > 0 Then
                Effectrr = GetEffect(CStr(Arr(i, 1)))
                For k = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
                    If Left(Effectrr(k), Len("When|Allied-Turn-End")) = "When|Allied-Turn-End" Or _
                    Left(Effectrr(k), Len("When|Turn-End")) = "When|Turn-End" Then
                        Call GetTimePoint(Effectrr(k), CStr(Arr(i, 1)))
                    End If
                Next k
            End If
        Next i
    Next m

    ' 处理对手纹章和战场的回合结束效果
    For m = 1 To 2
        If m = 1 Then
            Arr = Down("Crest", Enemy)   ' 对手纹章区域
        Else
            Arr = Down("Field", Enemy)   ' 对手战场区域
        End If
        
        For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
            If InStr(Arr(i, 5), "When||") > 0 Or InStr(Arr(i, 5), "{When|") > 0 Then
                Effectrr = GetEffect(CStr(Arr(i, 1)))
                For k = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
                    If Left(Effectrr(k), Len("When|Enemy-Turn-End")) = "When|Enemy-Turn-End" Or _
                    Left(Effectrr(k), Len("When|Turn-End")) = "When|Turn-End" Then
                        Call GetTimePoint(Effectrr(k), CStr(Arr(i, 1)))
                    End If
                Next k
            End If
        Next i
    Next m

    ' ========== 清空持续效果 ==========
    ' 移除回合结束到期的持续效果
    Arr = Down("Data")
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
        If Arr(i, 1) = "" Then
            Exit For
        End If
