        ' 处理Until类型的持续效果
        If InStr(CStr(Arr(i, 5)), "-Until") > 0 Then
            EffectSplit = SplitEffect(CStr(Arr(i, 5)))
            For j = 0 To UBound(EffectSplit)
                Effectrr = Split(EffectSplit(j), "|")
                ' 根据所属方移除对应的持续效果
                If Left(Arr(i, 1), 1) = Left(Turn, 1) Then
                    If Effectrr(0) = "Allied-Turn-End-Until" Or Effectrr(0) = "Turn-End-Until" Then EffectSplit(j) = "\"
                Else
                    If Effectrr(0) = "Enemy-Turn-End-Until" Or Effectrr(0) = "Turn-End-Until" Then EffectSplit(j) = "\"
                End If
            Next j
            Arr(i, 5) = Join(EffectSplit, "||") & "||"
            Arr(i, 5) = Replace(Arr(i, 5), "\||", "")
            Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(i, 1)), "Effect", Arr(i, 5))
        End If
        
        ' 处理每回合次数限制效果
        If InStr(CStr(Arr(i, 5)), "TimesPerTurn&") > 0 Then
            EffectSplit = SplitEffect(CStr(Arr(i, 5)))
            For j = 0 To UBound(EffectSplit)
                If Left(EffectSplit(j), Len("TimesPerTurn&")) = "TimesPerTurn&" Then EffectSplit(j) = "\"
            Next j
            Arr(i, 5) = Join(EffectSplit, "||") & "||"
            Arr(i, 5) = Replace(Arr(i, 5), "\||", "")
            Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(i, 1)), "Effect", Arr(i, 5))
        End If
    Next i

    ' ========== 清空能力队列 ==========
    Call EraseQueue
End Function



--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·6·3·结算·GetAbility.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·6·3·结算·GetAbility"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：清空能力队列
' 说明：逐个处理队列中的能力，直到队列为空或达到最大处理次数
' ==================================================
Function EraseQueue()
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim Arr() As String     ' 能力队列分割数组
    Dim Brr() As String     ' 匹配ID队列分割数组
    Dim i%                  ' 循环计数器
    Dim Str As String       ' 临时存储能力字符串
    Dim Str2 As String      ' 临时存储匹配ID字符串

    ' ========== 队列处理循环 ==========
    ' 最多处理100次，防止无限循环
    For i = 1 To 100
        If QueueAbility = "" Then Exit For   ' 队列为空时退出
        
        ' 分割队列字符串
        Arr = Split(QueueAbility, "\")
        Brr = Split(QueueMatchID, "\")
        
        ' 获取队列首项并移除
        Str = Arr(1)
        Arr(1) = "/"
        QueueAbility = Replace(Join(Arr, "\"), "\/", "")
        
        Str2 = Brr(1)
        Brr(1) = "/"
        QueueMatchID = Replace(Join(Brr, "\"), "\/", "")
        
        ' 执行能力效果
        Call GetAbility(Str, Str2)
    Next i
End Function

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：计算XYZ变量值
' 参数：
'   EffectSplit - 效果分割数组
'   ThisCard - 当前卡牌ID
'   i - 当前处理的位置索引
'   InputX - 输入变量标识（默认"X"）
' 返回：Integer - 计算得到的变量值
' 说明：处理效果中的X、Y、Z变量计算
' ==================================================
Function GetXFunction(EffectSplit() As String, ThisCard As String, i As Integer, Optional InputX As String = "X") As Integer
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim j%                  ' 循环计数器
    Dim Str As String       ' 临时字符串
    Dim MatchXYZ As Integer ' 匹配的XYZ值

    ' ========== 构建效果字符串 ==========
    ' 将前面的效果段组合
    Str = Empty
    For j = 0 To i
        Str = Str & EffectSplit(j) & "||"
    Next j
    
    ' ========== 查找变量定义 ==========
    ' 找到XYZ变量定义的位置
    For j = i + 1 To UBound(EffectSplit)
        If Left(EffectSplit(j), 2) = Right(InputX, 1) & "=" Then Exit For
    Next j
    
    ' ========== 变量计算处理 ==========
    ' 如果找到变量定义
    If j <= UBound(EffectSplit) Then
        ' 连接效果字符串
        Str = Str & EffectSplit(j)
        
        ' 确定要匹配的变量
        If Right(InputX, 1) = "X" Then MatchXYZ = MatchX
        If Right(InputX, 1) = "Y" Then MatchXYZ = MatchY
        If Right(InputX, 1) = "Z" Then MatchXYZ = MatchZ
        
        ' 计算变量值
        If MatchXYZ = -1 Then
            GetXFunction = Val(GetX(Mid(EffectSplit(j), 3, 100), ThisCard, Str))
            ' 更新全局变量值
            If Right(InputX, 1) = "X" Then MatchX = GetXFunction
            If Right(InputX, 1) = "Y" Then MatchY = GetXFunction
            If Right(InputX, 1) = "Z" Then MatchZ = GetXFunction
        Else
            GetXFunction = MatchXYZ
        End If
        
        ' 处理负值情况
        If Len(InputX) > 1 Then GetXFunction = GetXFunction * -1
    Else
        GetXFunction = 0
    End If
End Function

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：执行能力效果
' 参数：
'   InputEffect - 输入效果字符串
'   ThisCard - 当前卡牌ID
' 说明：解析并执行各种卡牌能力效果，包括抽牌、召唤、属性调整等
' ==================================================
Function GetAbility(InputEffect As String, ThisCard As String)
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i%, j%, k%, m%, n%, Ia%, Ib%   ' 循环计数器
    Dim Str As String                   ' 临时字符串
    Dim Arr As Variant                  ' 临时数组
    Dim Brr As Variant                  ' 临时数组2
    Dim Crr As Variant                  ' 临时数组3
    Dim Flag As Boolean                 ' 标志位
    Dim PreviousStr As String
    
    Dim LastGoal As String
    
    Dim Effectrr() As String            ' 效果数组
    Dim EffectSplit() As String         ' 效果分割数组
    Dim AbilitySplit() As String        ' 能力分割数组

    ' ========== 初始化变量 ==========
    MatchX = -1
    MatchY = -1
    MatchZ = -1

    ' ========== 嵌套效果处理 ==========
    ' 提取花括号内的嵌套效果
    ReDim Effectrr(1 To 10)
    Flag = False
    k = 1
    For i = 1 To Len(InputEffect)
        Select Case Mid(InputEffect, i, 1)
            Case "{"
                j = j + 1
                If j = 1 Then
                    m = i   ' 记录起始位置
                    Flag = True
                End If
            Case "}"
                If j > 0 Then
                    j = j - 1
                    If j = 0 And Flag Then
                        ' 提取当前第一层{}内的内容
                        Effectrr(k) = Mid(InputEffect, m, i - m + 1)
                        k = k + 1
                        Flag = False
                    End If
                End If
        End Select
    Next i
    
    ' 替换嵌套效果为占位符
    For i = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
        If Effectrr(i) = "" Then Exit For
        InputEffect = Replace(InputEffect, Effectrr(i), "{Effect" & i & "}")
    Next i
    
    ' 分割效果字符串
    EffectSplit = Split(InputEffect, "||")
    
    ' ========== 区域条件检查 ==========
    ' 检查卡牌是否在正确的区域执行能力
    Flag = False
    If EffectSplit(0) = "Fanfare" Or EffectSplit(0) = "Evolve" Or EffectSplit(0) = "SuperEvolve" Or _
        EffectSplit(0) = "Strike" Or EffectSplit(0) = "Clash" Or Left(EffectSplit(0), Len("Enhance")) = "Enhance" Then
        Flag = True
    ElseIf EffectSplit(0) = "When|Goal&" & ThisCard & "|LeaveField" Or _
        EffectSplit(0) = "When|Goal&" & ThisCard & "Destroy" Or _
        EffectSplit(0) = "When|Goal&" & ThisCard & "|Banish" Or _
        EffectSplit(0) = "When|Goal&" & ThisCard & "|ReturnToHand" Then
        Flag = False
    ElseIf Left(EffectSplit(0), Len("When")) = "When" Then
        Flag = True
    End If
    
    If Flag Then
        Str = DownByID(DownByMatchID(ThisCard, "ID"), "Type")
        If Str = "Follower" Or Str = "Amulet" Or Str = "Crest" Or Str = "Faith" Then
            Str = DownByMatchID(ThisCard, "Area")
            If Str <> "Field" And Str <> "Crest" And Str <> "Leader" Then Exit Function
        End If
    End If

    ' ========== Change条件处理 ==========
    ' 处理Change条件，确定执行范围
    Ia = 1
    Ib = UBound(EffectSplit)
    For i = 1 To UBound(EffectSplit)
        AbilitySplit = Split(EffectSplit(i), "|")
        ' 遍历选项
        If AbilitySplit(0) = "Change" Then
            If AbilitySplit(1) = "True" Then
                Ia = i + 1
                Ib = UBound(EffectSplit)
            Else
                Ia = 1
                Ib = i - 1
            End If
            Exit For
        End If
    Next i

    ' ========== 能力效果遍历 ==========
    ' 遍历所有能力效果段
    For i = Ia To Ib
        AbilitySplit = Split(EffectSplit(i), "|")
        
        ' ========== 重复次数处理 ==========
        RepeatTimes = 1
        If Left(AbilitySplit(0), Len("Repeat&")) = "Repeat&" Then
            ' 处理重复次数中的变量
            Str = Mid(AbilitySplit(0), 1 + Len("Repeat&"), 10)
            If InStr("-X-Y-Z", Str) > 0 Then
                Str = GetXFunction(EffectSplit, ThisCard, i, Str)
            End If
            RepeatTimes = Val(Str)
            
            ' 清理重复标记
            AbilitySplit(0) = "\"
            EffectSplit(i) = Join(AbilitySplit, "|")
            EffectSplit(i) = Replace(EffectSplit(i), "\|", "")
            AbilitySplit = Split(EffectSplit(i), "|")
        End If
        
Repeat:  ' 重复执行标签

        ' ========== 回合条件调整 ==========
        ' 处理回合条件的能力顺序调整
        If UBound(AbilitySplit) >= 1 And InStr(AbilitySplit(0), "Turn-") > 0 Then
            If Left(AbilitySplit(1), Len("Cost")) = "Cost" Or InStr(AbilitySplit(1), "/") > 0 Or AbilitySplit(1) = "Give" Or AbilitySplit(1) = "Remove" Then
                Str = AbilitySplit(0)
                For j = 0 To UBound(AbilitySplit) - 1
                    AbilitySplit(j) = AbilitySplit(j + 1)
                Next j
                AbilitySplit(UBound(AbilitySplit)) = Str
            End If
        End If
        
        ' ========== 能力类型分发处理 ==========
        ' 根据能力类型执行不同的处理逻辑
        
'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 条件判断能力 ==========
        If AbilitySplit(0) = "True" Then
            ' ========== 关键词能力处理 ==========
            Flag = True
            For j = 1 To UBound(AbilitySplit)
                ' 唤灵能力处理
                If Left(AbilitySplit(j), Len("Necromancy&")) = "Necromancy&" Then
                    n = Down("Shadows", ThisCard)                                ' 当前墓场数量
                    m = Val(Mid(AbilitySplit(j), 1 + Len("Necromancy&"), 10))    ' 要求的墓场数量
                    If n < m Then
                        Flag = False
                        Exit For
                    End If
                    
                    k = Val(Mid(AbilitySplit(j), 1 + Len("Necromancy&"), 10))
                    Call Up("Shadows", Val(Down("Shadows", ThisCard)) - k, ThisCard)
                    
                    ' 触发其他卡牌的唤灵响应
                    Call GetAction("Keyword&Necromancy", ThisCard, k)
                    
                ' 血祭能力处理
                ElseIf Left(AbilitySplit(j), Len("Sacrifice&")) = "Sacrifice&" Then
                    n = Down("BloodPool", ThisCard)                                ' 当前墓场数量
                    m = Val(Mid(AbilitySplit(j), 1 + Len("Sacrifice&"), 10))    ' 要求的墓场数量
                    If n < m Then
                        Flag = False
                        Exit For
                    End If
                    
                    k = Val(Mid(AbilitySplit(j), 1 + Len("Sacrifice&"), 10))
                    Call Up("BloodPool", Val(Down("BloodPool", ThisCard)) - k, ThisCard)
                    
                    ' 触发其他卡牌的唤灵响应
                    Call GetAction("Keyword&Sacrifice", ThisCard, k)
                
                ' 土之秘术能力处理
                ElseIf Left(AbilitySplit(j), Len("EarthRite&")) = "EarthRite&" Then
                    k = Val(Mid(AbilitySplit(j), 1 + Len("EarthRite&"), 10))
                    ' 查找土之印
                    Arr = Down("Field", ThisCard)
                    For m = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
                        If InStr(Arr(m, 5), "EarthSigil&") > 0 Then Exit For
                    Next m
                    ' 消耗土之印
                    If m <= UBound(Arr, 1) Then
                        Brr = SplitEffect(Arr(m, 5))
                        For n = 0 To UBound(Brr)
                            If Left(Brr(n), Len("EarthSigil&")) = "EarthSigil&" Then
                                k = Val(Mid(Brr(n), 1 + Len("EarthSigil&"), 10)) - k
                                Exit For
                            End If
                        Next n
                        If k < 0 Then
                            Flag = False
                            Exit For
                        ElseIf k = 0 Then
                            ' 完全消耗，移除土之印
                            Brr(n) = "\"
                            Arr(m, 5) = Join(Brr, "||") & "||"
                            Arr(m, 5) = Replace(Arr(m, 5), "\||", "")
                            Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(m, 1)), "Effect", Arr(m, 5))
                            Call DestroyFunction(CStr(Arr(m, 1)))
                        ElseIf k > 0 Then
                            ' 部分消耗，更新土之印计数
                            Brr(n) = "EarthSigil&" & k
                            Arr(m, 5) = Join(Brr, "||") & "||"
                            Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(m, 1)), "Effect", Arr(m, 5))
                        End If
                    Else
                        Exit For
                    End If
                    
                    ' 触发其他卡牌的土之秘术响应
                    Call GetAction("Keyword&EarthRite", ThisCard, Val(Mid(AbilitySplit(j), 1 + Len("EarthRite&"), 10)))
                    
                ' 限次能力处理
                ElseIf Left(AbilitySplit(j), Len("TimesPerTurn&")) = "TimesPerTurn&" Then
                    Arr = SplitEffect(DownByMatchID(ThisCard, "Effect"))   ' 获取当前效果
            
                    ' 查找已有的次数记录
                    For k = 0 To UBound(Arr)
                        If Left(Arr(k), Len("TimesPerTurn&")) = "TimesPerTurn&" Then
                            m = Val(Mid(Arr(k), 1 + Len("TimesPerTurn&"), 10))   ' 已触发次数
                            n = Val(Mid(AbilitySplit(j), 1 + Len("TimesPerTurn&"), 10))          ' 允许的最大次数
                            Arr(k) = "TimesPerTurn&" & m + 1   ' 更新次数
                            Exit For
                        End If
                    Next k
                    If m >= n Then
                        Flag = False
                        Exit For
                    End If
                    
                    ' 如果没有找到次数记录，添加新的记录\
                    Str = Empty
                    If UBound(Arr) >= 0 Then Str = Join(Arr, "||") & "||"
                    If k > UBound(Arr) Then Str = Str & "TimesPerTurn&1||"
                    
                    ' 更新效果记录
                    Call UpByMatchID(ThisCard, "Effect", Str)
                End If
            Next j
            If Not Flag Then Exit For

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 条件终止 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "False" Then
            Exit For

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 抽牌能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "Draw" Or AbilitySplit(0) = "Enemy-Draw" Then
            ' 处理抽牌数量变量
            If InStr("-X-Y-Z", AbilitySplit(1)) > 0 Then
                AbilitySplit(1) = GetXFunction(EffectSplit, ThisCard, i, AbilitySplit(1))
            End If
            
            ' 确定抽牌方并获取目标
            If Left(AbilitySplit(0), Len("Enemy-")) = "Enemy-" Then
                Arr = GetGoal("Random&" & AbilitySplit(1) & "-Enemy-Deck-Card", ThisCard, InputEffect, EffectSplit(i))
            Else
                Arr = GetGoal("Random&" & AbilitySplit(1) & "-Allied-Deck-Card", ThisCard, InputEffect, EffectSplit(i))
            End If
            
            '记录
            PreviousStr = PreviousGoal
            
            ' 处理抽到的卡牌
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                Flag = UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Area", "Hand")
                If Flag Then
                    Call GetAction("AddToHand", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    Call GetAction("DrawToHand", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    Call GetAction("AddToHand", CStr(Arr(j)))
                    Call GetAction("DrawToHand", CStr(Arr(j)))
                Else
                    Call GetAction("HandDestroy", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    Call GetAction("HandDestroy", CStr(Arr(j)), DownByID(DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "ID"), "Name"))
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal&" & PreviousStr

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 召唤能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "Summon" Or AbilitySplit(0) = "Enemy-Summon" Then
            ' 处理召唤数量变量
            If InStr("-X-Y-Z", AbilitySplit(2)) > 0 Then
                AbilitySplit(2) = GetXFunction(EffectSplit, ThisCard, i, AbilitySplit(2))
            End If
            
            '随机
            Str = Empty
            Arr = Split(AbilitySplit(1), "@")
            If UBound(Arr) = 0 Then
                For m = 1 To Val(AbilitySplit(2))
                    Str = Str & Arr(0) & "&"
                Next m
                If Right(Str, 1) = "&" Then Str = Left(Str, Len(Str) - 1)
            ElseIf QueueAction = "" Then
                If UBound(Arr) + 1 < AbilitySplit(2) Then AbilitySplit(2) = UBound(Arr) + 1
                
                Brr = GetRandom(UBound(Arr) + 1)
                For m = 1 To Val(AbilitySplit(2))
                    Str = Str & Arr(Brr(m) - 1) & "&"
                Next m
                If Right(Str, 1) = "&" Then Str = Left(Str, Len(Str) - 1)
                
                Call UpSteps("Random", Str)
            Else
                Str = EraseQueueAction("Random")
                If Str = "" Then
                    MsgBox ("出现bug，请将excel发给sgg，交换控制方继续对局")
                    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
                    ThisWorkbook.Close False
                    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
                End If
            End If
            Crr = Split(Str, "&")
            
            ' 确定召唤方
            If Left(AbilitySplit(0), Len("Enemy-")) = "Enemy-" Then
                If Left(ThisCard, 1) = "F" Then Brr = "S" Else Brr = "F"
            Else
                Brr = Left(ThisCard, 1)
            End If
            
            ' 寻找最大序号创建新卡牌
            Arr = Down("Data")
            k = 0
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                If Arr(j, 1) = "" Then Exit For
                If Left(Arr(j, 1), 1) = Brr Then
                    If Val(Mid(Arr(j, 1), 2, 10)) > k Then k = Val(Mid(Arr(j, 1), 2, 10))
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 创建卡牌
            Str = Empty
            For n = 0 To UBound(Crr)
                Arr(j, 1) = Brr & k + 1
                Arr(j, 2) = Crr(n)
                Str = Str & "&" & Arr(j, 1)
                j = j + 1
                k = k + 1
            Next n
            Call Up("Data", Arr)
            
            ' 进入战场
            Arr = Split(Str, "&")
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                Flag = UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Area", "Field")
                If Flag Then
                    Call GetAction("EnterField", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    Call GetAction("EffectEnter", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    Call GetAction("EnterField", CStr(Arr(j)), DownByID(DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "ID"), "Name"))
                    Call GetAction("EffectEnter", CStr(Arr(j)))
                    
                    Call Up("Rally", Down("Rally", ThisCard) + 1, ThisCard) '协作+1
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal" & Str

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 加入手牌能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "AddToHand" Or AbilitySplit(0) = "Enemy-AddToHand" Then
            ' 处理数量变量
            If InStr("-X-Y-Z", AbilitySplit(2)) > 0 Then
                AbilitySplit(2) = GetXFunction(EffectSplit, ThisCard, i, AbilitySplit(2))
            End If
            
            '随机
            Str = Empty
            Arr = Split(AbilitySplit(1), "@")
            If UBound(Arr) = 0 Then
                For m = 1 To Val(AbilitySplit(2))
                    Str = Str & Arr(0) & "&"
                Next m
                If Right(Str, 1) = "&" Then Str = Left(Str, Len(Str) - 1)
            ElseIf QueueAction = "" Then
                If UBound(Arr) + 1 < AbilitySplit(2) Then AbilitySplit(2) = UBound(Arr) + 1
                
                Brr = GetRandom(UBound(Arr) + 1)
                For m = 1 To Val(AbilitySplit(2))
                    Str = Str & Arr(Brr(m) - 1) & "&"
                Next m
                If Right(Str, 1) = "&" Then Str = Left(Str, Len(Str) - 1)
                
                Call UpSteps("Random", Str)
            Else
                Str = EraseQueueAction("Random")
                If Str = "" Then
                    MsgBox ("出现bug，请将excel发给sgg，交换控制方继续对局")
                    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
                    ThisWorkbook.Close False
                    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
                End If
            End If
            Crr = Split(Str, "&")
            
            ' 确定生效方
            If Left(AbilitySplit(0), Len("Enemy-")) = "Enemy-" Then
                If Left(ThisCard, 1) = "F" Then Brr = "S" Else Brr = "F"
            Else
                Brr = Left(ThisCard, 1)
            End If
            
            ' 寻找最大序号创建新卡牌
            Arr = Down("Data")
            k = 0
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                If Arr(j, 1) = "" Then Exit For
                If Left(Arr(j, 1), 1) = Brr Then
                    If Val(Mid(Arr(j, 1), 2, 10)) > k Then k = Val(Mid(Arr(j, 1), 2, 10))
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 创建卡牌
            Str = Empty
            For n = 0 To UBound(Crr)
                Arr(j, 1) = Brr & k + 1
                Arr(j, 2) = Crr(n)
                Str = Str & "&" & Arr(j, 1)
                j = j + 1
                k = k + 1
            Next n
            Call Up("Data", Arr)
            
            ' 加入手牌
            Arr = Split(Str, "&")
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                Flag = UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Area", "Hand")
                If Flag Then
                    Call GetAction("AddToHand", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    Call GetAction("AddToHand", CStr(Arr(j)))
                Else
                    Call GetAction("HandDestroy", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    Call GetAction("HandDestroy", CStr(Arr(j)), DownByID(DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "ID"), "Name"))
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal" & Str

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 加入牌组能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "AddToDeck" Or AbilitySplit(0) = "Enemy-AddToDeck" Then
            ' 处理数量变量
            If InStr("-X-Y-Z", AbilitySplit(2)) > 0 Then
                AbilitySplit(2) = GetXFunction(EffectSplit, ThisCard, i, AbilitySplit(2))
            End If
            
            '随机
            Str = Empty
            Arr = Split(AbilitySplit(1), "@")
            If UBound(Arr) = 0 Then
                For m = 1 To Val(AbilitySplit(2))
                    Str = Str & Arr(0) & "&"
                Next m
                If Right(Str, 1) = "&" Then Str = Left(Str, Len(Str) - 1)
            ElseIf QueueAction = "" Then
                If UBound(Arr) + 1 < AbilitySplit(2) Then AbilitySplit(2) = UBound(Arr) + 1
                
                Brr = GetRandom(UBound(Arr) + 1)
                For m = 1 To Val(AbilitySplit(2))
                    Str = Str & Arr(Brr(m) - 1) & "&"
                Next m
                If Right(Str, 1) = "&" Then Str = Left(Str, Len(Str) - 1)
                
                Call UpSteps("Random", Str)
            Else
                Str = EraseQueueAction("Random")
                If Str = "" Then
                    MsgBox ("出现bug，请将excel发给sgg，交换控制方继续对局")
                    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
                    ThisWorkbook.Close False
                    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
                End If
            End If
            Crr = Split(Str, "&")
            
            ' 确定生效方
            If Left(AbilitySplit(0), Len("Enemy-")) = "Enemy-" Then
                If Left(ThisCard, 1) = "F" Then Brr = "S" Else Brr = "F"
            Else
                Brr = Left(ThisCard, 1)
            End If
            
            ' 寻找最大序号创建新卡牌
            Arr = Down("Data")
            k = 0
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                If Arr(j, 1) = "" Then Exit For
                If Left(Arr(j, 1), 1) = Brr Then
                    If Val(Mid(Arr(j, 1), 2, 10)) > k Then k = Val(Mid(Arr(j, 1), 2, 10))
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 创建卡牌
            Str = Empty
            For n = 0 To UBound(Crr)
                Arr(j, 1) = Brr & k + 1
                Arr(j, 2) = Crr(n)
                Str = Str & "&" & Arr(j, 1)
                j = j + 1
                k = k + 1
            Next n
            Call Up("Data", Arr)
            
            ' 加入牌组
            Arr = Split(Str, "&")
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Area", "Deck")
            Next j
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal" & Str

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 能量点调整能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "PP" Or AbilitySplit(0) = "Enemy-PP" Then
            ' 处理数值变量
            If InStr("-X-Y-Z", AbilitySplit(1)) > 0 Then
                AbilitySplit(1) = GetXFunction(EffectSplit, ThisCard, i, AbilitySplit(1))
            End If
            
            ' 确定生效方
            If Left(AbilitySplit(0), Len("Enemy-")) = "Enemy-" Then
                If Left(ThisCard, 1) = "F" Then Str = "S1000" Else Str = "F1000"
            Else
                Str = ThisCard
            End If
            
            ' 调整能量点
            j = Down("PP", Str)
            k = Down("MaxPP", Str)
            j = j + AbilitySplit(1)
            If j > k Then j = k
            Call Up("PP", j, Str)

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 能量点最大值调整能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "MaxPP" Or AbilitySplit(0) = "Enemy-MaxPP" Then
            ' 处理数值变量
            If InStr("-X-Y-Z", AbilitySplit(1)) > 0 Then
                AbilitySplit(1) = GetXFunction(EffectSplit, ThisCard, i, AbilitySplit(1))
            End If
            
            ' 确定生效方
            If Left(AbilitySplit(0), Len("Enemy-")) = "Enemy-" Then
                If Left(ThisCard, 1) = "F" Then Str = "S1000" Else Str = "F1000"
            Else
                Str = ThisCard
            End If
            
            ' 调整能量点最大值
            If Left(AbilitySplit(1), 1) = "$" Then
                j = Val(Mid(AbilitySplit(1), 2, 10))
            Else
                j = Down("MaxPP", Str)
                j = j + AbilitySplit(1)
            End If
            If j > 10 Then j = 10
            Call Up("MaxPP", j, Str)

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== EP调整能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "EP" Or AbilitySplit(0) = "Enemy-EP" Then
            ' 处理数值变量
            If InStr("-X-Y-Z", AbilitySplit(1)) > 0 Then
                AbilitySplit(1) = GetXFunction(EffectSplit, ThisCard, i, AbilitySplit(1))
            End If
            
            ' 确定生效方
            If Left(AbilitySplit(0), Len("Enemy-")) = "Enemy-" Then
                If Left(ThisCard, 1) = "F" Then Str = "S1000" Else Str = "F1000"
            Else
                Str = ThisCard
            End If
            
            ' 调整EP
            j = Down("EP", Str)
            j = j + AbilitySplit(1)
            If j > 2 Then j = 2
            Call Up("EP", j, Str)

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== SEP调整能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "SEP" Or AbilitySplit(0) = "Enemy-SEP" Then
            ' 处理数值变量
            If InStr("-X-Y-Z", AbilitySplit(1)) > 0 Then
                AbilitySplit(1) = GetXFunction(EffectSplit, ThisCard, i, AbilitySplit(1))
            End If
            
            ' 确定生效方
            If Left(AbilitySplit(0), Len("Enemy-")) = "Enemy-" Then
                If Left(ThisCard, 1) = "F" Then Str = "S1000" Else Str = "F1000"
            Else
                Str = ThisCard
            End If
            
            ' 调整SEP
            j = Down("SEP", Str)
            j = j + AbilitySplit(1)
            If j > 2 Then j = 2
            Call Up("SEP", j, Str)

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 最大防御调整能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "MaxDefense" Or AbilitySplit(0) = "Enemy-MaxDefense" Then
            ' 处理数值变量
            If InStr("-X-Y-Z", AbilitySplit(1)) > 0 Then
                AbilitySplit(1) = GetXFunction(EffectSplit, ThisCard, i, AbilitySplit(1))
            End If
            
            ' 确定生效方
            If Left(AbilitySplit(0), Len("Enemy-")) = "Enemy-" Then
                If Left(ThisCard, 1) = "F" Then Str = "S1000" Else Str = "F1000"
            Else
                Str = ThisCard
            End If
            
            ' 调整最大防御
            If Left(AbilitySplit(1), 1) = "$" Then
                j = Val(Mid(AbilitySplit(1), 2, 10))
            Else
                j = Down("MaxDefense", Str)
                j = j + AbilitySplit(1)
            End If
            Call Up("MaxDefense", j, Str)

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 回手能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "ReturnToHand" Then
            ' 获取目标并回手
            Arr = GetGoal("Field-" & AbilitySplit(1), ThisCard, InputEffect, EffectSplit(i))
            
            '记录
            PreviousStr = PreviousGoal
            
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Effect", "")
                Flag = UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Area", "Hand")
                Call GetAction("LeaveField", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                Call GetAction("ReturnToHand", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                Call GetAction("LeaveField", CStr(Arr(j)))
                Call GetAction("ReturnToHand", CStr(Arr(j)))
                If Flag Then
                    Call GetAction("AddToHand", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    Call GetAction("AddToHand", CStr(Arr(j)))
                Else
                    Call GetAction("HandDestroy", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    Call GetAction("HandDestroy", CStr(Arr(j)), DownByID(DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "ID"), "Name"))
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal&" & PreviousStr

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 回牌组能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "ReturnToDeck" Then
            ' 获取目标并回牌组
            Arr = GetGoal("Hand-" & AbilitySplit(1), ThisCard, InputEffect, EffectSplit(i))
            
            '记录
            PreviousStr = PreviousGoal
            
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                Flag = UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Area", "Deck")
            Next j
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal&" & PreviousStr

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 手牌召唤能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "HandSummon" Then
            ' 获取目标并回牌组
            Arr = GetGoal("Hand-" & AbilitySplit(1), ThisCard, InputEffect, EffectSplit(i))
            
            Str = Empty
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                Flag = UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Area", "Field")
                If Flag Then
                    Call GetAction("EnterField", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    Call GetAction("EffectEnter", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    Call GetAction("EnterField", CStr(Arr(j)), DownByID(DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "ID"), "Name"))
                    Call GetAction("EffectEnter", CStr(Arr(j)))
                    Call Up("Rally", Down("Rally", ThisCard) + 1, ThisCard) '协作+1
                    Str = Str & "&" & Arr(j)
                Else
                    Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Area", "Hand")
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal&" & Str

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 检索能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "Search" Then
            ' 检索牌组中的卡牌
            Arr = GetGoal("Allied-Deck-" & AbilitySplit(1), ThisCard, InputEffect, EffectSplit(i))
            
            '记录
            PreviousStr = PreviousGoal
            
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                Flag = UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Area", "Hand")
                If Flag Then
                    Call GetAction("AddToHand", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    Call GetAction("DrawToHand", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    Call GetAction("AddToHand", CStr(Arr(j)))
                    Call GetAction("DrawToHand", CStr(Arr(j)))
                Else
                    Call GetAction("HandDestroy", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    Call GetAction("HandDestroy", CStr(Arr(j)), DownByID(DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "ID"), "Name"))
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal&" & PreviousStr

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 检索召唤能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "SearchSummon" Then
            ' 检索并召唤
            Arr = GetGoal("Allied-Deck-" & AbilitySplit(1), ThisCard, InputEffect, EffectSplit(i))
            Str = Empty
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                Flag = UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Area", "Field")
                If Flag Then
                    Call GetAction("EnterField", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    Call GetAction("EffectEnter", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    Call GetAction("EnterField", CStr(Arr(j)), DownByID(DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "ID"), "Name"))
                    Call GetAction("EffectEnter", CStr(Arr(j)))
                    Call Up("Rally", Down("Rally", ThisCard) + 1, ThisCard) '协作+1
                    Str = Str & "&" & Arr(j)
                Else
                    Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Area", "Deck")
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal" & Str

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 瞬念召唤能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "Invoke" Then
            Str = Down("Turn")
            Flag = SearchSteps("AnyCard", "Invoke", Str)
            
            If Not Flag Then
                ' 瞬念召唤
                Flag = UpByMatchID(ThisCard, "Area", "Field")
                If Flag Then
                    Call GetAction("EnterField", ThisCard, True)
                    Call GetAction("Invoke", ThisCard, True)
                    Call GetAction("EffectEnter", ThisCard, True)
                    Str = DownByID(DownByMatchID(ThisCard, "ID"), "Name")
                    Call GetAction("EnterField", ThisCard, Str)
                    Call GetAction("Invoke", ThisCard, Str)
                    Call GetAction("EffectEnter", ThisCard)
                    
                    Call Up("Rally", Down("Rally", ThisCard) + 1, ThisCard) '协作+1
                Else
                    Call UpByMatchID(ThisCard, "Area", "Deck")
                End If
            End If
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal" & ThisCard

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 复制加入手牌能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "Copy" Then
            ' 获取同名卡目标
            Brr = GetGoal(AbilitySplit(1), ThisCard, InputEffect)

            ' 寻找最大序号创建新卡牌
            Arr = Down("Data")
            k = 0
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                If Arr(j, 1) = "" Then Exit For
                If Left(Arr(j, 1), 1) = Left(ThisCard, 1) Then
                    If Val(Mid(Arr(j, 1), 2, 10)) > k Then k = Val(Mid(Arr(j, 1), 2, 10))
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 创建同名卡
            Str = Empty
            For m = 1 To UBound(Brr)
                Arr(j, 1) = Left(ThisCard, 1) & k + 1
                Arr(j, 2) = DownByMatchID(CStr(Brr(m)), "ID")
                Arr(j, 5) = DownByMatchID(CStr(Brr(m)), "Effect")
                Crr = SplitEffect(Arr(j, 5))
                For n = 0 To UBound(Crr)
                    If Crr(n) = "Engaged" Then
                        Crr(n) = "\"
                    ElseIf Left(Crr(n), Len("Striked&")) = "Striked&" Then
                        Crr(n) = "\"
                    End If
                Next n
                Arr(j, 5) = Join(Crr, "||") & "||"
                Arr(j, 5) = Replace(Arr(j, 5), "\||", "")
                
                Str = Str & "&" & Arr(j, 1)
                j = j + 1
                k = k + 1
            Next m
            Call Up("Data", Arr)
                
            ' 加入手牌
            Arr = Split(Str, "&")
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                Flag = UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Area", "Hand")
                If Flag Then
                    Call GetAction("AddToHand", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    Call GetAction("AddToHand", CStr(Arr(j)))
                Else
                    Call GetAction("HandDestroy", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    Call GetAction("HandDestroy", CStr(Arr(j)), DownByID(DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "ID"), "Name"))
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal" & Str


'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 复制召唤能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "CopySummon" Then
            ' 复制目标并召唤
            Brr = GetGoal(AbilitySplit(1), ThisCard, InputEffect)

            ' 寻找最大序号创建新卡牌
            Arr = Down("Data")
            k = 0
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                If Arr(j, 1) = "" Then Exit For
                If Left(Arr(j, 1), 1) = Left(ThisCard, 1) Then
                    If Val(Mid(Arr(j, 1), 2, 10)) > k Then k = Val(Mid(Arr(j, 1), 2, 10))
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 复制卡牌
            Str = Empty
            For m = 1 To UBound(Brr)
                Arr(j, 1) = Left(ThisCard, 1) & k + 1
                Arr(j, 2) = DownByMatchID(CStr(Brr(m)), "ID")
                Arr(j, 5) = DownByMatchID(CStr(Brr(m)), "Effect")
                Crr = SplitEffect(Arr(j, 5))
                For n = 0 To UBound(Crr)
                    If Crr(n) = "Engaged" Then
                        Crr(n) = "\"
                    ElseIf Left(Crr(n), Len("Striked&")) = "Striked&" Then
                        Crr(n) = "\"
                    End If
                Next n
                Arr(j, 5) = Join(Crr, "||") & "||"
                Arr(j, 5) = Replace(Arr(j, 5), "\||", "")
                
                Str = Str & "&" & Arr(j, 1)
                j = j + 1
                k = k + 1
            Next m
            Call Up("Data", Arr)
                
            ' 进入战场
            Arr = Split(Str, "&")
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                Brr = DownByID(DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "ID"), "Type")
                If Brr = "Crest" Or Brr = "Faith" Then
                    Flag = UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Area", "Crest")
                    If Flag Then
                        Call GetAction("EnterField", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                        'Call GetAction("EffectEnter", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                        Call GetAction("EnterField", CStr(Arr(j)), DownByID(DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "ID"), "Name"))
                        'Call GetAction("EffectEnter", CStr(Arr(j)))
                        'Call Up("Rally", Down("Rally", ThisCard) + 1, ThisCard) '协作+1
                    End If
                Else
                    Flag = UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Area", "Field")
                    If Flag Then
                        Call GetAction("EnterField", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                        Call GetAction("EffectEnter", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                        Call GetAction("EnterField", CStr(Arr(j)), DownByID(DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "ID"), "Name"))
                        Call GetAction("EffectEnter", CStr(Arr(j)))
                        If Brr = "Follower" Then Call Up("Rally", Down("Rally", ThisCard) + 1, ThisCard) '协作+1
                    End If
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal" & Str

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 同名卡加入手牌能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "SameNameToHand" Then
            ' 获取同名卡目标
            Brr = GetGoal(AbilitySplit(1), ThisCard, InputEffect)

            ' 寻找最大序号创建新卡牌
            Arr = Down("Data")
            k = 0
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                If Arr(j, 1) = "" Then Exit For
                If Left(Arr(j, 1), 1) = Left(ThisCard, 1) Then
                    If Val(Mid(Arr(j, 1), 2, 10)) > k Then k = Val(Mid(Arr(j, 1), 2, 10))
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 创建同名卡
            Str = Empty
            For m = 1 To UBound(Brr)
                Arr(j, 1) = Left(ThisCard, 1) & k + 1
                Arr(j, 2) = DownByMatchID(CStr(Brr(m)), "ID")
                Str = Str & "&" & Arr(j, 1)
                j = j + 1
                k = k + 1
            Next m
            Call Up("Data", Arr)
                
            ' 加入手牌
            Arr = Split(Str, "&")
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                Flag = UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Area", "Hand")
                If Flag Then
                    Call GetAction("AddToHand", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    Call GetAction("AddToHand", CStr(Arr(j)))
                Else
                    Call GetAction("HandDestroy", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    Call GetAction("HandDestroy", CStr(Arr(j)), DownByID(DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "ID"), "Name"))
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal" & Str


'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 同名召唤能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "SameSummon" Then
            ' 复制目标并召唤
            Brr = GetGoal(AbilitySplit(1), ThisCard, InputEffect)

            ' 寻找最大序号创建新卡牌
            Arr = Down("Data")
            k = 0
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                If Arr(j, 1) = "" Then Exit For
                If Left(Arr(j, 1), 1) = Left(ThisCard, 1) Then
                    If Val(Mid(Arr(j, 1), 2, 10)) > k Then k = Val(Mid(Arr(j, 1), 2, 10))
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 复制卡牌
            Str = Empty
            For m = 1 To UBound(Brr)
                Arr(j, 1) = Left(ThisCard, 1) & k + 1
                Arr(j, 2) = DownByMatchID(CStr(Brr(m)), "ID")
                Str = Str & "&" & Arr(j, 1)
                j = j + 1
                k = k + 1
            Next m
            Call Up("Data", Arr)
                
            ' 进入战场
            Arr = Split(Str, "&")
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                Brr = DownByID(DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "ID"), "Type")
                If Brr = "Crest" Or Brr = "Faith" Then
                    Flag = UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Area", "Crest")
                    If Flag Then
                        Call GetAction("EnterField", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                        'Call GetAction("EffectEnter", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                        Call GetAction("EnterField", CStr(Arr(j)), DownByID(DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "ID"), "Name"))
                        'Call GetAction("EffectEnter", CStr(Arr(j)))
                        'Call Up("Rally", Down("Rally", ThisCard) + 1, ThisCard) '协作+1
                    End If
                Else
                    Flag = UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Area", "Field")
                    If Flag Then
                        Call GetAction("EnterField", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                        Call GetAction("EffectEnter", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                        Call GetAction("EnterField", CStr(Arr(j)), DownByID(DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "ID"), "Name"))
                        Call GetAction("EffectEnter", CStr(Arr(j)))
                        If Brr = "Follower" Then Call Up("Rally", Down("Rally", ThisCard) + 1, ThisCard) '协作+1
                    End If
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal" & Str


'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 费用调整能力 ==========
        ElseIf Left(AbilitySplit(0), Len("Cost")) = "Cost" Then
            ' 处理费用中的变量
            For j = 1 To Len(AbilitySplit(0))
                Str = Mid(AbilitySplit(0), j, 1)
                If InStr("XYZ", Str) > 0 Then
                    k = GetXFunction(EffectSplit, ThisCard, i, Str)
                    AbilitySplit(0) = Replace(AbilitySplit(0), Str, k)
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 获取目标并应用费用效果
            Arr = GetGoal(AbilitySplit(1), ThisCard, InputEffect)
            
            '记录
            PreviousStr = PreviousGoal
            
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                Str = DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Effect")
                ' 添加持续时间
                If UBound(AbilitySplit) >= 2 Then
                    If Left(Arr(j), 1) = Left(ThisCard, 1) Then
                        Str = Str & AbilitySplit(2) & "|"
                    ElseIf Left(AbilitySplit(2), Len("Allied-")) = "Allied-" Then
                        Str = Str & "Enemy-" & Mid(AbilitySplit(2), 1 + Len("Allied-"), 100) & "|"
                    ElseIf Left(AbilitySplit(2), Len("Enemy-")) = "Enemy-" Then
                        Str = Str & "Allied-" & Mid(AbilitySplit(2), 1 + Len("Enemy-"), 100) & "|"
                    Else
                        Str = Str & AbilitySplit(2) & "|"
                    End If
                End If
                
                Str = Str & AbilitySplit(0) & "||"
                
                ' 合并上传效果
                If UBound(AbilitySplit) < 2 Then Str = JoinEffect(Str)
                Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Effect", Str)
                
                ' 触发其他卡牌响应
                Call GetAction("GetBuff", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                Call GetAction("GetBuff", CStr(Arr(j)), AbilitySplit(0))
            Next j
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal&" & PreviousStr

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 属性Buff能力 ==========
        ElseIf InStr(AbilitySplit(0), "/") > 0 Then
            ' 处理Buff中的变量
            For j = 1 To Len(AbilitySplit(0))
                Str = Mid(AbilitySplit(0), j, 1)
                If InStr("XYZ", Str) > 0 Then
                    k = GetXFunction(EffectSplit, ThisCard, i, Str)
                    AbilitySplit(0) = Replace(AbilitySplit(0), Str, k)
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 获取目标并应用Buff
            Arr = GetGoal(AbilitySplit(1), ThisCard, InputEffect)
            
            '记录
            PreviousStr = PreviousGoal
            
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                Str = DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Effect")
                ' 添加持续时间
                If UBound(AbilitySplit) >= 2 Then
                    If Left(Arr(j), 1) = Left(ThisCard, 1) Then
                        Str = Str & AbilitySplit(2) & "|"
                    ElseIf Left(AbilitySplit(2), Len("Allied-")) = "Allied-" Then
                        Str = Str & "Enemy-" & Mid(AbilitySplit(2), 1 + Len("Allied-"), 100) & "|"
                    ElseIf Left(AbilitySplit(2), Len("Enemy-")) = "Enemy-" Then
                        Str = Str & "Allied-" & Mid(AbilitySplit(2), 1 + Len("Enemy-"), 100) & "|"
                    Else
                        Str = Str & AbilitySplit(2) & "|"
                    End If
                End If
                
                Str = Str & AbilitySplit(0) & "||"
                
                ' 特殊效果处理
                ' 固定生命值处理
                If InStr(AbilitySplit(0), "/$") > 0 Then
                    Brr = SplitEffect(Str)
                    For k = 0 To UBound(Brr)
                        If Left(Brr(k), Len("Damaged&")) = "Damaged&" Then Brr(k) = "\"
                    Next k
                    Str = Join(Brr, "||") & "||"
                    Str = Replace(Str, "\||", "")
                ' 生命值翻倍处理
                ElseIf InStr(AbilitySplit(0), "/*") > 0 Then
                    m = InStr(AbilitySplit(0), "/*")
                    m = Val(Mid(AbilitySplit(0), m + Len("/*"), 10))
                    Brr = SplitEffect(Str)
                    For k = 0 To UBound(Brr)
                        If Left(Brr(k), Len("Damaged&")) = "Damaged&" Then
                            Brr(k) = "Damaged&" & Val(Mid(Brr(k), 1 + Len("Damaged&"), 10)) * m
                        End If
                    Next k
                    Str = Join(Brr, "||") & "||"
                End If
                
                ' 合并上传效果
                If UBound(AbilitySplit) < 2 Then Str = JoinEffect(Str)
                Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Effect", Str)
                
                ' 检查是否破坏
                k = GetModel("Defense", DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "ID"), Str)
                If k <= 0 Then Call DestroyFunction(CStr(Arr(j)))
                
                ' 触发其他卡牌响应
'                If Left(AbilitySplit(0), 1) = "+" Then
'                    Call GetAction("AttackIncreased", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
'                    Call GetAction("AttackIncreased", CStr(Arr(j)), AbilitySplit(0))
'                ElseIf Left(AbilitySplit(0), 1) = "-" Then
'                    Call GetAction("AttackDecreased", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
'                    Call GetAction("AttackDecreased", CStr(Arr(j)), AbilitySplit(0))
'                End If
                If InStr(AbilitySplit(0), "/+") > 0 Then
                    'Call GetAction("DefenseIncreased", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    'Call GetAction("DefenseIncreased", CStr(Arr(j)), AbilitySplit(0))
                ElseIf InStr(AbilitySplit(0), "/-") > 0 Then
                    Call GetAction("DefenseDecreased", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    Call GetAction("DefenseDecreased", CStr(Arr(j)), AbilitySplit(0))
                End If
                If Left(AbilitySplit(0), 1) = "+" Or InStr(AbilitySplit(0), "/+") > 0 Then
                    Call GetAction("Increased", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    Call GetAction("Increased", CStr(Arr(j)), AbilitySplit(0))
                ElseIf Left(AbilitySplit(0), 1) = "-" Or InStr(AbilitySplit(0), "/-") > 0 Then
                    Call GetAction("Decreased", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    Call GetAction("Decreased", CStr(Arr(j)), AbilitySplit(0))
                End If
                Call GetAction("GetBuff", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                Call GetAction("GetBuff", CStr(Arr(j)), AbilitySplit(0))
            Next j
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal&" & PreviousStr

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 给予能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "Give" Then
            ' 获取目标并给予能力
            Arr = GetGoal(AbilitySplit(1), ThisCard, InputEffect)
            
            '记录
            PreviousStr = PreviousGoal
            
            k = Val(Mid(AbilitySplit(2), 1 + Len("{Effect"), 10))
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                Str = DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Effect")
                ' 添加持续时间
                If UBound(AbilitySplit) >= 3 Then
                    If Left(Arr(j), 1) = Left(ThisCard, 1) Then
                        Str = Str & AbilitySplit(3) & "|"
                    ElseIf Left(AbilitySplit(3), Len("Allied-")) = "Allied-" Then
                        Str = Str & "Enemy-" & Mid(AbilitySplit(3), 1 + Len("Allied-"), 100) & "|"
                    ElseIf Left(AbilitySplit(3), Len("Enemy-")) = "Enemy-" Then
                        Str = Str & "Allied-" & Mid(AbilitySplit(3), 1 + Len("Enemy-"), 100) & "|"
                    Else
                        Str = Str & AbilitySplit(3) & "|"
                    End If
                End If
                
                Str = Str & Effectrr(k) & "||"
                
                Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Effect", Str)
                
                ' 触发其他卡牌响应
                Call GetAction("GetBuff", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                Call GetAction("GetBuff", CStr(Arr(j)), Effectrr(k))
            Next j
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal&" & PreviousStr & "|" & AbilitySplit(2)

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 移除能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "Remove" Then
            ' 获取目标并移除能力
            Arr = GetGoal(AbilitySplit(2), ThisCard, InputEffect)
            
            '记录
            PreviousStr = PreviousGoal
            
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                Str = DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Effect")
                
                '移除全部能力
                If AbilitySplit(1) = "All" Then
                    Brr = SplitEffect(Str)
                    Str = Empty
                    For k = 0 To UBound(Brr)
                        If Brr(k) = "SuperEvolved" Then
                            Str = Str & Brr(k) & "||"
                        ElseIf Brr(k) = "Evolved" Then
                            Str = Str & Brr(k) & "||"
                        ElseIf Left(Brr(k), Len("Striked&")) = "Striked&" Then
                            Str = Str & Brr(k) & "||"
                        ElseIf Left(Brr(k), Len("Damaged&")) = "Damaged&" Then
                            Str = Str & Brr(k) & "||"
                        ElseIf Left(Brr(k), Len("Countdown&")) = "Countdown&" Then
                            Str = Str & Brr(k) & "||"
                        End If
                    Next k
                End If
                
                ' 添加持续时间
                If UBound(AbilitySplit) >= 3 Then
                    If Left(Arr(j), 1) = Left(ThisCard, 1) Then
                        Str = Str & AbilitySplit(3) & "|"
                    ElseIf Left(AbilitySplit(3), Len("Allied-")) = "Allied-" Then
                        Str = Str & "Enemy-" & Mid(AbilitySplit(3), 1 + Len("Allied-"), 100) & "|"
                    ElseIf Left(AbilitySplit(3), Len("Enemy-")) = "Enemy-" Then
                        Str = Str & "Allied-" & Mid(AbilitySplit(3), 1 + Len("Enemy-"), 100) & "|"
                    Else
                        Str = Str & AbilitySplit(3) & "|"
                    End If
                End If
                
                Str = Str & AbilitySplit(0) & "|" & AbilitySplit(1) & "||"
                
                Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Effect", Str)
            Next j
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|" & AbilitySplit(1) & "|Goal&" & PreviousStr

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 破坏能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "Destroy" Then
            ' 获取目标并破坏
            Arr = GetGoal("Field-" & AbilitySplit(1), ThisCard, InputEffect, EffectSplit(i))
            
            '记录
            PreviousStr = PreviousGoal
            
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                Call DestroyFunction(CStr(Arr(j)))
            Next j
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal&" & PreviousStr

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 消失能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "Banish" Then
            ' 获取目标并消失
            Arr = GetGoal(AbilitySplit(1), ThisCard, InputEffect)
            
            '记录
            PreviousStr = PreviousGoal
            
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Area", "Banish")
                Call GetAction("Banish", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                Call GetAction("LeaveField", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                Call GetAction("Banish", CStr(Arr(j)), DownByID(DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "ID"), "Name"))
                Call GetAction("LeaveField", CStr(Arr(j)))
            Next j
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal&" & PreviousStr

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 变身能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "Transform" Then
            ' 获取目标并变身
            Arr = GetGoal(AbilitySplit(1), ThisCard, InputEffect)
            
            '记录
            PreviousStr = PreviousGoal
            
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "ID", AbilitySplit(2))
                Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Effect", Empty)
                Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Area", DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Area"))
            Next j
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal&" & PreviousStr & "|" & AbilitySplit(2)

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 弃牌能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "Discard" Then
            ' 获取目标并弃牌
            Arr = GetGoal("Hand-" & AbilitySplit(1), ThisCard, InputEffect, EffectSplit(i))
            
            '记录
            PreviousStr = PreviousGoal
            
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Area", "Discard")
                Call GetAction("Discard", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                Call GetAction("Discard", CStr(Arr(j)))
            Next j
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal&" & PreviousStr

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 倒数调整能力 ==========
        ElseIf Left(AbilitySplit(0), Len("Countdown")) = "Countdown" Then
            ' 处理倒数中的变量
            For j = 1 + Len("Countdown") To Len(AbilitySplit(0))
                Str = Mid(AbilitySplit(0), j, 1)
                If InStr("XYZ", Str) > 0 Then
                    k = GetXFunction(EffectSplit, ThisCard, i, Str)
                    AbilitySplit(0) = Replace(AbilitySplit(0), Str, k)
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 调整倒数
            k = Val(Mid(AbilitySplit(0), 1 + Len("Countdown"), 10))
            Arr = GetGoal(AbilitySplit(1), ThisCard, InputEffect)
            
            '记录
            PreviousStr = PreviousGoal
            
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                Brr = SplitEffect(DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Effect"))
                For m = 0 To UBound(Brr)
                    If Left(Brr(m), Len("Countdown&")) = "Countdown&" Then
                        n = Val(Mid(Brr(m), 1 + Len("Countdown&"), 10))
                        n = n + k
                        If n <= 0 Then
                            Call DestroyFunction(CStr(Arr(j)))
                        Else
                            Brr(m) = "Countdown&" & n
                            Str = Join(Brr, "||") & "||"
                            Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Effect", Str)
                        End If
                    End If
                Next m
            Next j
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal&" & PreviousStr

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 伤害能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "Damage" Then
            ' 处理伤害数值变量
            If InStr("-X-Y-Z", AbilitySplit(2)) > 0 Then
                AbilitySplit(2) = GetXFunction(EffectSplit, ThisCard, i, AbilitySplit(2))
            End If
            
            ' 获取目标并造成伤害
            Arr = GetGoal("Field-" & AbilitySplit(1), ThisCard, InputEffect, EffectSplit(i))
            
            '记录
            PreviousStr = PreviousGoal
            
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                Str = DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Effect")
                Brr = SplitEffect(Str)
                
                ' 检查屏障
                Flag = False
                For k = 0 To UBound(Brr)
                    If Brr(k) = "Barrier" Or InStr(Brr(k), "{Barrier}") > 0 Then
                        ' 屏障抵消伤害
                        Flag = True
                        Brr(k) = "\"
                        Str = Join(Brr, "||") & "||"
                        Str = Replace(Str, "\||", "")
                        Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Effect", Str)
                    ElseIf Brr(k) = "Remove|Barrier" Then
                        Flag = False
                        Exit For
                    End If
                Next k
                
                '计算伤害
                n = Val(AbilitySplit(2))
                Crr = GetEffect(ThisCard)
                For k = 1 To UBound(Crr)
                    If Left(Crr(k), Len("DoDamage&")) = "DoDamage&" Then
                        m = Val(Mid(Crr(k), 1 + Len("DoDamage&"), 10))
                        n = n + m
                    End If
                Next k
                Crr = GetEffect(CStr(Arr(j)))
                For k = 1 To UBound(Crr)
                    If Left(Crr(k), Len("TakeDamage&")) = "TakeDamage&" Then
                        m = Val(Mid(Crr(k), 1 + Len("TakeDamage&"), 10))
                        n = n + m
                    End If
                Next k
                For k = 1 To UBound(Crr)
                    If Left(Crr(k), Len("CantTakeMoreDamage&")) = "CantTakeMoreDamage&" Then
                        m = Val(Mid(Crr(k), 1 + Len("CantTakeMoreDamage&"), 10))
                        If n > m Then n = m
                    End If
                Next k
                If n < 0 Then n = 0
                    
                ' 处理伤害
                If Flag Then
                    ' 触发失去屏障响应
                    'Call GetAction("LostBarrier", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    'Call GetAction("LostBarrier", CStr(Arr(j)), DownByID(DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "ID"), "Name"))
                ElseIf InStr(Str, "SuperEvolved||") > 0 And Left(Down("Turn"), 1) = Left(Arr(j), 1) Then
                    ' 超进化免伤处理
                ElseIf Mid(Arr(j), 2, 10) = "1000" Then
                    ' 主战者受伤
                    Call Up("Defense", Down("Defense", CStr(Arr(j))) - n, CStr(Arr(j)))
                    Call Up("BloodPool", Down("BloodPool", CStr(Arr(j))) + n, CStr(Arr(j)))  '血池+
                    Call GetAction("Damaged", CStr(Arr(j)), n)
                Else
                    ' 从者受伤
                    For k = 0 To UBound(Brr)
                        If Left(Brr(k), Len("Damaged&")) = "Damaged&" Then Exit For
                    Next k
                    If k <= UBound(Brr) Then
                        Brr(k) = "Damaged&" & Val(Mid(Brr(k), 1 + Len("Damaged&"), 10)) + n
                        Str = Join(Brr, "||") & "||"
                    Else
                        Str = Str & "Damaged&" & n & "||"
                    End If
                    Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Effect", Str)
                    ' 检查是否破坏
                    m = GetModel("Defense", DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "ID"), Str)
                    If m <= 0 Then Call DestroyFunction(CStr(Arr(j)))
                End If
                
                ' 触发受伤响应
                If DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Area") = "Field" Then
                    Call GetAction("Damaged", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    Call GetAction("Damaged", CStr(Arr(j)), Val(AbilitySplit(2)))
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 失去潜行
            If UBound(Arr) > 0 Then
                If Arr(1) <> "" Then
                    Str = DownByMatchID(ThisCard, "Effect")
                    Brr = SplitEffect(Str)
                    For j = 0 To UBound(Brr)
                        If Brr(j) = "Ambush" Or InStr(Brr(j), "{Ambush}") > 0 Then
                            ' 移除潜行
                            Brr(j) = "\"
                            Brr = Join(Brr, "||") & "||"
                            Brr = Replace(Str, "\||", "")
                            Call UpByMatchID(ThisCard, "Effect", Str)
                        End If
                    Next j
                End If
            End If
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal&" & PreviousStr & "|" & AbilitySplit(2)

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 分摊伤害能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "SplitDamage" Then
            ' 处理伤害数值变量
            If InStr("-X-Y-Z", AbilitySplit(2)) > 0 Then
                AbilitySplit(2) = GetXFunction(EffectSplit, ThisCard, i, AbilitySplit(2))
            End If
            
            ' 获取目标并分摊伤害
            Arr = GetGoal("Field-" & AbilitySplit(1), ThisCard, InputEffect, EffectSplit(i))
            
            '记录
            PreviousStr = PreviousGoal
            
            ' 主战者置后处理
            If PreviousGoal <> "" Then
                If Mid(Arr(1), 2, 10) = "1000" Then
                    ReDim Brr(1 To UBound(Arr))
                    For j = 2 To UBound(Arr)
                        Brr(j - 1) = Arr(j)
                    Next j
                    Brr(j - 1) = Arr(1)
                    Arr = Brr
                End If
            End If
            
            ' 分摊伤害处理
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                ' 伤害减为0时退出循环
                If AbilitySplit(2) <= 0 Then Exit For
                
                ' 获取目标效果
                Str = DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Effect")
                Brr = SplitEffect(Str)
                
                ' 分配伤害
                If Mid(Arr(j), 2, 10) = "1000" Then
                    n = 1000
                ElseIf j = UBound(Arr) Then
                    n = 1000
                Else
                    n = GetModel("Defense", DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "ID"), Str)
                End If
                If n > AbilitySplit(2) Then n = AbilitySplit(2)
                AbilitySplit(2) = AbilitySplit(2) - n
                
                '计算伤害
                Crr = GetEffect(ThisCard)
                For k = 1 To UBound(Crr)
                    If Left(Crr(k), Len("DoDamage&")) = "DoDamage&" Then
                        m = Val(Mid(Crr(k), 1 + Len("DoDamage&"), 10))
                        n = n + m
                    End If
                Next k
                Crr = GetEffect(CStr(Arr(j)))
                For k = 1 To UBound(Crr)
                    If Left(Crr(k), Len("TakeDamage&")) = "TakeDamage&" Then
                        m = Val(Mid(Crr(k), 1 + Len("TakeDamage&"), 10))
                        n = n + m
                    End If
                Next k
                For k = 1 To UBound(Crr)
                    If Left(Crr(k), Len("CantTakeMoreDamage&")) = "CantTakeMoreDamage&" Then
                        m = Val(Mid(Crr(k), 1 + Len("CantTakeMoreDamage&"), 10))
                        If n > m Then n = m
                    End If
                Next k
                If n < 0 Then n = 0
                
                ' 检查屏障
                Flag = False
                For k = 0 To UBound(Brr)
                    If Brr(k) = "Barrier" Or InStr(Brr(k), "{Barrier}") > 0 Then
                        ' 屏障抵消伤害
                        Flag = True
                        Brr(k) = "\"
                        Str = Join(Brr, "||") & "||"
                        Str = Replace(Str, "\||", "")
                        Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Effect", Str)
                    ElseIf Brr(k) = "Remove|Barrier" Then
                        Flag = False
                        Exit For
                    End If
                Next k
                
                ' 处理伤害
                If Flag Then
                    ' 触发失去屏障响应
                    'Call GetAction("LostBarrier", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    'Call GetAction("LostBarrier", CStr(Arr(j)), DownByID(DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "ID"), "Name"))
                ElseIf InStr(Str, "SuperEvolved||") > 0 And Left(Down("Turn"), 1) = Left(Arr(j), 1) Then
                    ' 超进化免伤处理
                ElseIf Mid(Arr(j), 2, 10) = "1000" Then
                    ' 主战者受伤
                    Call Up("Defense", Down("Defense", CStr(Arr(j))) - n, CStr(Arr(j)))
                    Call Up("BloodPool", Down("BloodPool", CStr(Arr(j))) + n, CStr(Arr(j)))  '血池+
                    Call GetAction("Damaged", CStr(Arr(j)), n)
                Else
                    ' 从者受伤
                    For k = 0 To UBound(Brr)
                        If Left(Brr(k), Len("Damaged&")) = "Damaged&" Then Exit For
                    Next k
                    If k <= UBound(Brr) Then
                        Brr(k) = "Damaged&" & Val(Mid(Brr(k), 1 + Len("Damaged&"), 10)) + n
                        Str = Join(Brr, "||") & "||"
                    Else
                        Str = Str & "Damaged&" & n & "||"
                    End If
                    Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Effect", Str)
                    ' 检查是否破坏
                    m = GetModel("Defense", DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "ID"), Str)
                    If m <= 0 Then Call DestroyFunction(CStr(Arr(j)))
                End If
                
                ' 触发受伤响应
                If DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Area") = "Field" Then
                    Call GetAction("Damaged", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    Call GetAction("Damaged", CStr(Arr(j)), n)
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 失去潜行
            If UBound(Arr) > 0 Then
                If Arr(1) <> "" Then
                    Str = DownByMatchID(ThisCard, "Effect")
                    Brr = SplitEffect(Str)
                    For j = 0 To UBound(Brr)
                        If Brr(j) = "Ambush" Or InStr(Brr(j), "{Ambush}") > 0 Then
                            ' 移除潜行
                            Brr(j) = "\"
                            Brr = Join(Brr, "||") & "||"
                            Brr = Replace(Str, "\||", "")
                            Call UpByMatchID(ThisCard, "Effect", Str)
                        End If
                    Next j
                End If
            End If
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal&" & PreviousStr & "|" & AbilitySplit(2)

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 回复能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "Restore" Then
            ' 处理回复数值变量
            If InStr("-X-Y-Z", AbilitySplit(2)) > 0 Then
                AbilitySplit(2) = GetXFunction(EffectSplit, ThisCard, i, AbilitySplit(2))
            End If
            
            ' 获取目标并回复
            Arr = GetGoal("Field-" & AbilitySplit(1), ThisCard, InputEffect, EffectSplit(i))
            
            '记录
            PreviousStr = PreviousGoal
            
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                Str = DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Effect")
