                Brr = SplitEffect(Str)
                
                If Mid(Arr(j), 2, 10) = "1000" Then
                    ' 主战者回复
                    k = Down("Defense", CStr(Arr(j))) + Val(AbilitySplit(2))
                    m = Down("MaxDefense", CStr(Arr(j)))
                    If k > m Then k = m
                    Call Up("Defense", k, CStr(Arr(j)))
                    Call GetAction("Restore", CStr(Arr(j)), Val(AbilitySplit(2)))
                Else
                    ' 从者回复
                    For k = 0 To UBound(Brr)
                        If Left(Brr(k), Len("Damaged&")) = "Damaged&" Then Exit For
                    Next k
                    If k <= UBound(Brr) Then
                        m = Val(Mid(Brr(k), 1 + Len("Damaged&"), 10)) - Val(AbilitySplit(2))
                        If m > 0 Then
                            Brr(k) = "Damaged&" & m
                        Else
                            Brr(k) = "\"
                        End If
                        Str = Join(Brr, "||") & "||"
                        Str = Replace(Str, "\||", "")
                        Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Effect", Str)
                    End If
                    Call GetAction("Restore", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    Call GetAction("Restore", CStr(Arr(j)), Val(AbilitySplit(2)))
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal&" & PreviousStr & "|" & AbilitySplit(2)

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 进化能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "Evolve" Then
            ' 获取目标并进化
            Arr = GetGoal("Field-" & AbilitySplit(1), ThisCard, InputEffect, EffectSplit(i))
            
            '记录
            PreviousStr = PreviousGoal
            
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                Str = DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Effect")
                ' 检查是否已进化
                If InStr(Str, "Evolved||") = 0 Then
                    Str = Str & "{Rush}||Evolved||"
                    Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Effect", Str)
                    Call GetAbility("||+2/+2|ThisCard", CStr(Arr(j)))
                    Call GetAction("Evolve", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    
                    '奥义
                    Brr = Down("Hand", ThisCard)
                    For k = 1 To UBound(Brr)
                        Crr = SplitEffect(CStr(Brr(k, 5)))
                        For m = 0 To UBound(Crr)
                            If Left(Crr(m), Len("Skybound&")) = "Skybound&" Then
                                n = Val(Mid(Crr(m), 1 + Len("Skybound&"), 10))
                                n = n + 1
                                Crr(m) = "Skybound&" & n
                                Exit For
                            End If
                        Next m
                        Brr(k, 5) = Join(Crr, "||")
                        If Brr(k, 5) <> "" Then Brr(k, 5) = Brr(k, 5) & "||"
                        Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Brr(k, 1)), "Effect", CStr(Brr(k, 5)))
                    Next k
                    
                    Call GetAction("Evolve", CStr(Arr(j)))
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal&" & PreviousStr

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 超进化能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "SuperEvolve" Then
            ' 获取目标并超进化
            Arr = GetGoal("Field-" & AbilitySplit(1), ThisCard, InputEffect, EffectSplit(i))
            
            '记录
            PreviousStr = PreviousGoal
            
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                Str = DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Effect")
                ' 检查是否已超进化
                If InStr(Str, "SuperEvolved||") = 0 Then
                    Str = Str & "{Rush}||SuperEvolved||"
                    Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Effect", Str)
                    Call GetAbility("||+3/+3|ThisCard", CStr(Arr(j)))
                    Call GetAction("SuperEvolve", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                    
                    '奥义
                    Brr = Down("Hand", ThisCard)
                    For k = 1 To UBound(Brr)
                        Crr = SplitEffect(CStr(Brr(k, 5)))
                        For m = 0 To UBound(Crr)
                            If Left(Crr(m), Len("Skybound&")) = "Skybound&" Then
                                n = Val(Mid(Crr(m), 1 + Len("Skybound&"), 10))
                                n = n + 1
                                Crr(m) = "Skybound&" & n
                                Exit For
                            End If
                        Next m
                        Brr(k, 5) = Join(Crr, "||")
                        If Brr(k, 5) <> "" Then Brr(k, 5) = Brr(k, 5) & "||"
                        Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Brr(k, 1)), "Effect", CStr(Brr(k, 5)))
                    Next k
                    
                    Call GetAction("Evolve", CStr(Arr(j)))
                    Call GetAction("SuperEvolve", CStr(Arr(j)))
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal&" & PreviousStr

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 未攻击/未启动 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "UnStriked" Then
            ' 获取目标
            Arr = GetGoal("Field-" & AbilitySplit(1), ThisCard, InputEffect, EffectSplit(i))
            
            '记录
            PreviousStr = PreviousGoal
            
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                Brr = SplitEffect(DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Effect"))
                For k = 0 To UBound(Brr)
                    If Left(Brr(k), Len("Striked&")) = "Striked&" Then
                        Brr(k) = "\"
                    ElseIf Brr(k) = "Engaged" Then
                        Brr(k) = "\"
                    End If
                Next k
                Str = Join(Brr, "||") & "||"
                Str = Replace(Str, "\||", "")
                If Str = "||" Then Str = ""
                Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Effect", Str)
            Next j
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal&" & PreviousStr

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 选择能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "AuxSelect" Then
            ' 获取目标并超进化
            Arr = GetGoal(AbilitySplit(1), ThisCard, InputEffect)
            
            '记录
            PreviousStr = PreviousGoal
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal&" & PreviousStr

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 替换牌组能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "ReplaceDeck" Then
            ' 翻译牌组ID
            Str = IDTranslate("Deck", Mid(AbilitySplit(1), 1 + Len("Deck:"), 100))
            
            ' 清除现有牌组
            Arr = Down("Data")
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                If Arr(j, 4) = "Deck" Then
                    If Left(Arr(j, 1), 1) = Left(ThisCard, 1) Then Arr(j, 1) = Empty
                ElseIf Arr(j, 1) = "" Then
                    Exit For
                End If
            Next j
            Arr = NonEmptyArray(Arr, True)
            Call Up("Data", Arr)
            
            ' 获得新牌组
            Arr = Sheets("SetDeck").UsedRange
            For k = 1 To UBound(Arr, 2)
                If Arr(1, k) = Str Then Exit For
            Next k
            If k <= UBound(Arr, 2) Then
                For j = 2 To UBound(Arr, 1)
                    If Arr(j, k) = "" Then
                        Exit For
                    Else
                        Call GetAbility("||AddToDeck|" & IDByName(CStr(Arr(j, k))) & "|1", ThisCard)
                    End If
                Next j
            End If

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 去重消失能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "BanishDuplicates" Then
            ' 获取目标并去重
            Arr = GetGoal(AbilitySplit(1), ThisCard, InputEffect)
            Call GetGoal("Random@1000-" & AbilitySplit(1), ThisCard, InputEffect, EffectSplit(i))
            Str = "|Goal"
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                If InStr(PreviousGoal, Arr(j)) = 0 Then Str = Str & "&" & Arr(j)
            Next j
            If Str <> "|Goal" Then
                Call GetAbility("||Banish" & Str, ThisCard)
                
                ' 更新效果字符串
                EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & Str
            End If

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 纹章能力 ==========
        ElseIf Left(AbilitySplit(0), Len("Crest&")) = "Crest&" Or Left(AbilitySplit(0), Len("Enemy-Crest&")) = "Enemy-Crest&" Then
            ' 生成纹章ID
            Crr = Translate("Crest") & ":" & DownByID(DownByMatchID(ThisCard, "ID"), "Name")
            Str = IDByName(CStr(Crr))
            
            ' 确定生效方
            If Left(AbilitySplit(0), Len("Enemy-")) = "Enemy-" Then
                If Left(ThisCard, 1) = "F" Then Brr = "S" Else Brr = "F"
            Else
                Brr = Left(ThisCard, 1)
            End If
            
            ' 检查是否已存在
            Arr = Down("Crest", CStr(Brr))
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                If Arr(j, 2) = Str Then Exit For
            Next j
            
            ' 不存在则创建
            If j > UBound(Arr) Then
                ' 创建纹章卡牌
                Arr = Down("Data")
                k = 0
                For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                    If Arr(j, 1) = "" Then Exit For
                    If Left(Arr(j, 1), 1) = Brr Then
                        If Val(Mid(Arr(j, 1), 2, 10)) > k Then k = Val(Mid(Arr(j, 1), 2, 10))
                    End If
                Next j
                Arr(j, 1) = Brr & k + 1
                Arr(j, 2) = Str
                Str = Arr(j, 1)
                Call Up("Data", Arr)
                
                ' 进入战场
                Flag = UpByMatchID(Str, "Area", "Crest")
                If Flag Then
                    Call GetAction("EnterField", Str, True)
                    Call GetAction("EnterField", Str, Crr)
                End If
                
                ' 更新效果字符串
                EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal&" & Str
            End If
            
'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 信仰能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "Faith" Then
            ' 生成信仰ID
            Crr = Translate("Faith") & ":" & DownByID(DownByMatchID(ThisCard, "ID"), "Name")
            Str = IDByName(CStr(Crr))
            
            ' 确定生效方
            Brr = Left(ThisCard, 1)
            
            ' 检查是否已存在
            Arr = Down("Crest", CStr(Brr))
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                If Arr(j, 2) = Str Then Exit For
            Next j
            
            ' 不存在则创建
            If j > UBound(Arr) Then
                ' 创建信仰卡牌
                Arr = Down("Data")
                k = 0
                For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                    If Arr(j, 1) = "" Then Exit For
                    If Left(Arr(j, 1), 1) = Brr Then
                        If Val(Mid(Arr(j, 1), 2, 10)) > k Then k = Val(Mid(Arr(j, 1), 2, 10))
                    End If
                Next j
                Arr(j, 1) = Brr & k + 1
                Arr(j, 2) = Str
                Str = Arr(j, 1)
                Call Up("Data", Arr)
                
                ' 进入战场
                Flag = UpByMatchID(Str, "Area", "Crest")
                If Flag Then
                    Call GetAction("EnterField", Str, True)
                    Call GetAction("EnterField", Str, Crr)
                End If
                
                ' 更新效果字符串
                EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal&" & Str
            End If

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 魔力增幅能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "Spellboost" Then
            ' 处理增幅次数变量
            If InStr("-X-Y-Z", AbilitySplit(2)) > 0 Then
                AbilitySplit(2) = GetXFunction(EffectSplit, ThisCard, i, AbilitySplit(2))
            End If
            
            ' 获取目标并魔力增幅
            Arr = GetGoal("Allied-" & AbilitySplit(1), ThisCard, InputEffect, EffectSplit(i))
            
            '记录
            PreviousStr = PreviousGoal
            
            For m = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                Str = DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(m)), "Effect")
                Brr = GetEffect(CStr(Arr(m)))
                Flag = False
                For n = 1 To UBound(Brr, 1)
                    If Left(Brr(n), Len("Spellboost|X=")) = "Spellboost|X=" Then
                        ' 处理X=类型的魔力增幅
                        Flag = True
                        Crr = SplitEffect(Str)
                        For k = 0 To UBound(Crr)
                            If Left(Crr(k), Len("Spellboost&")) = "Spellboost&" Then
                                Crr(k) = "Spellboost&" & Val(Mid(Crr(k), 1 + Len("Spellboost&"), 10)) + AbilitySplit(2)
                                Str = Join(Crr, "||") & "||"
                                Exit For
                            End If
                        Next k
                        If k > UBound(Crr) Then Str = Str & "||Spellboost&" & AbilitySplit(2)
                    ElseIf Left(Brr(n), Len("Spellboost|")) = "Spellboost|" Then
                        ' 处理其他类型的魔力增幅
                        Flag = True
                        For k = 1 To AbilitySplit(2)
                            Str = Str & Mid(Brr(n), 1 + Len("Spellboost|"), 10) & "||"
                        Next k
                    End If
                Next n
                Str = JoinEffect(Str)
                Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(m)), "Effect", Str)
                If Flag Then Call GetAction("Spellboost", CStr(Arr(m)), True)
                If Flag Then Call GetAction("Spellboost", CStr(Arr(m)), AbilitySplit(2))
            Next m
            
            ' 更新效果字符串
            EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal&" & PreviousStr & "|" & AbilitySplit(2)

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 冠名计数能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "NamingCount" Then
            ' 处理计数变量
            If InStr("-X-Y-Z", AbilitySplit(2)) > 0 Then
                AbilitySplit(2) = GetXFunction(EffectSplit, ThisCard, i, AbilitySplit(2))
            End If
            
            ' 更新冠名计数
            Call Up(AbilitySplit(1), Down(AbilitySplit(1), ThisCard) + AbilitySplit(2), ThisCard)

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 土之印能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "EarthSigil" Then
            ' 处理土之印数量变量
            If InStr("-X-Y-Z", AbilitySplit(1)) > 0 Then
                AbilitySplit(1) = GetXFunction(EffectSplit, ThisCard, i, AbilitySplit(1))
            End If
            
            ' 查找土之印
            Arr = Down("Field", ThisCard)
            For m = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
                If InStr(Arr(m, 5), "EarthSigil&") > 0 Then Exit For
            Next m
            ' 增加土之印
            If m <= UBound(Arr, 1) Then
                Brr = SplitEffect(Arr(m, 5))
                For n = 0 To UBound(Brr)
                    If Left(Brr(n), Len("EarthSigil&")) = "EarthSigil&" Then
                        k = Val(Mid(Brr(n), 1 + Len("EarthSigil&"), 10)) + AbilitySplit(1)
                        Exit For
                    End If
                Next n
                
                Brr(n) = "EarthSigil&" & k
                Arr(m, 5) = Join(Brr, "||") & "||"
                Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(m, 1)), "Effect", Arr(m, 5))
            Else
                ' 没有土之印则召唤一个
                Call GetAbility("||Summon|1030401|1", ThisCard)
                '重新获取新召唤的土之印
                Arr = Down("Field", ThisCard)
                For m = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
                    If InStr(Arr(m, 5), "EarthSigil&") > 0 Then Exit For
                Next m
                '给新土之印补加 (AbilitySplit(1) - 1)
                If AbilitySplit(1) > 1 Then
                    Brr = SplitEffect(Arr(m, 5))
                    For n = 0 To UBound(Brr)
                        If Left(Brr(n), Len("EarthSigil&")) = "EarthSigil&" Then
                        k = Val(Mid(Brr(n), 1 + Len("EarthSigil&"), 10)) + (AbilitySplit(1) - 1)
                        Brr(n) = "EarthSigil&" & k
                    Exit For
                End If
                Next n
                Arr(m, 5) = Join(Brr, "||") & "||"
                Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(m, 1)), "Effect", Arr(m, 5))
            End If
        End If

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 亡者召还能力 ==========
        ElseIf AbilitySplit(0) = "Reanimate" Then
            ' 处理召还费用变量
            If InStr("-X-Y-Z", AbilitySplit(1)) > 0 Then
                AbilitySplit(1) = GetXFunction(EffectSplit, ThisCard, i, AbilitySplit(1))
            End If
            
            ' 获取符合条件的亡者
            Call GetGoal("Cost<" & AbilitySplit(1) + 1 & "-Allied-ThisMatch@Destroy-Follower", ThisCard, InputEffect, EffectSplit(i))
            Brr = GetGoal("Random&1-Cost$Highest-It", ThisCard, "Goal&" & PreviousGoal & "||Random&1-Cost&Highest-It", "Random&1-Cost&Highest-It")
            
            ' 召还亡者
            If PreviousGoal <> "-1" And PreviousGoal <> "" Then
                Arr = Down("Data")
                k = 0
                For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                    If Arr(j, 1) = "" Then Exit For
                    If Left(Arr(j, 1), 1) = Left(ThisCard, 1) Then
                        If Val(Mid(Arr(j, 1), 2, 10)) > k Then k = Val(Mid(Arr(j, 1), 2, 10))
                    End If
                Next j
                Str = Empty
                For m = 1 To 1
                    Arr(j, 1) = Left(ThisCard, 1) & k + 1
                    Arr(j, 2) = DownByMatchID(CStr(Brr(1)), "ID")
                    Arr(j, 5) = "Trait:5||"
                    Str = Str & "&" & Arr(j, 1)
                    j = j + 1
                    k = k + 1
                Next m
                Call Up("Data", Arr)
                
                ' 进入战场
                Arr = Split(Str, "&")
                For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                    Flag = UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "Area", "Field")
                    If Flag Then
                        Call GetAction("EnterField", CStr(Arr(j)), True)
                        Call GetAction("EnterField", CStr(Arr(j)), DownByID(DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "ID"), "Name"))
                    End If
                Next j
                
                Call GetAction("Reanimate", ThisCard, True)
                Call GetAction("Reanimate", ThisCard, AbilitySplit(1))
                
                ' 更新效果字符串
                EffectSplit(i) = AbilitySplit(0) & "|Goal" & Str
            End If

'========================================================================================================================================================================================================
        ' ========== 其他能力 ==========
        Else
            ' 未定义的能力类型
        
        End If
        
        ' ========== 效果字符串更新 ==========
        ' 更新输入效果字符串
        InputEffect = Join(EffectSplit, "||")
        
        ' ========== 重复执行检查 ==========
        RepeatTimes = RepeatTimes - 1
        If RepeatTimes > 0 Then GoTo Repeat
    Next i
    
    Call OutputLog(InputEffect, ThisCard)
End Function


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·5·1·步骤·UpSteps.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·5·1·步骤·UpSteps"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：更新步骤记录
' 参数：InputAction - 输入的动作描述
'        MatchID - 匹配ID值
' 说明：将当前回合的动作记录添加到Steps区域，用于游戏步骤追踪
' ==================================================
Function UpSteps(InputAction As String, MatchID As String, Optional InputValue As String = "")
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i As Integer        ' 循环计数器
    Dim Arr As Variant      ' 步骤数据数组
    
    ' ========== 步骤数据获取 ==========
    ' 获取Steps区域的所有步骤记录
    Arr = Down("Steps")
    
    ' ========== 空位查找与记录添加 ==========
    ' 遍历步骤数组查找第一个空位置
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr)
        If Arr(i, 1) = "" Then
            ' ========== 步骤信息填充 ==========
            Arr(i, 1) = Down("Turn")       ' 记录当前回合数
            Arr(i, 2) = InputAction        ' 记录动作描述
            Arr(i, 3) = MatchID            ' 记录匹配ID
            Arr(i, 4) = InputValue         ' 记录动作值
            Exit For                       ' 找到空位后退出循环
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 数据更新 ==========
    ' 将更新后的步骤数据写回Steps区域
    Call Up("Steps", Arr)
End Function


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·8·1·对局·UIRefresh.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·8·1·对局·UIRefresh"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：用户界面刷新函数
' 说明：更新游戏主界面的所有显示元素
' 包括战场、手牌、纹章、牌堆、墓场、职业关键字等
' ==================================================
Function UIRefresh()
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i%, j%
    Dim Str As String
    Dim Arr As Variant
    Dim Brr As Variant
    Dim AlliedMove As String
    Dim EnemyMove As String
    
    ' ========== 先后手确定 ==========
    ' 查询先后手并设置玩家标识
    If Down("FirstMove") = "FirstMove" Then
        AlliedMove = "F"
        EnemyMove = "S"
    Else
        AlliedMove = "S"
        EnemyMove = "F"
    End If
    
    ' ========== 敌方战场刷新 ==========
    ' 下载敌方战场信息并格式化显示
    Arr = Down("Field", EnemyMove & "1000")
    ReDim Brr(1 To 3, 1 To 5)
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
        Brr(1, i) = DownByID(CStr(Arr(i, 2)), "Name")
        If DownByID(CStr(Arr(i, 2)), "Type") = "Amulet" Then
            ' 护符类型：显示倒计时
            j = GetModel("Countdown", CStr(Arr(i, 2)), CStr(Arr(i, 5)))
            If j > 0 Then
                Brr(2, i) = Translate("Countdown") & j
                Arr(i, 5) = Replace(Arr(i, 5), "Countdown&" & j & "||", "")
            End If
        ElseIf DownByID(CStr(Arr(i, 2)), "Type") = "Follower" Then
            ' 从者类型：显示攻击力/防御力
            Brr(2, i) = GetModel("Attack", CStr(Arr(i, 2)), CStr(Arr(i, 5))) & "/" & GetModel("Defense", CStr(Arr(i, 2)), CStr(Arr(i, 5)))
        End If
        Brr(3, i) = SentenceTranslate(CStr(Arr(i, 5)))
    Next i
    Call Up("EnemyField", Brr)
    
    ' ========== 己方战场刷新 ==========
    ' 下载己方战场信息并格式化显示
    Arr = Down("Field", AlliedMove & "1000")
    ReDim Brr(1 To 3, 1 To 5)
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
        Brr(1, i) = DownByID(CStr(Arr(i, 2)), "Name")
        If DownByID(CStr(Arr(i, 2)), "Type") = "Amulet" Then
            ' 护符类型：显示倒计时
            j = GetModel("Countdown", CStr(Arr(i, 2)), CStr(Arr(i, 5)))
            If j > 0 Then
                Brr(2, i) = Translate("Countdown") & j
                Arr(i, 5) = Replace(Arr(i, 5), "Countdown&" & j & "||", "")
            End If
        ElseIf DownByID(CStr(Arr(i, 2)), "Type") = "Follower" Then
            ' 从者类型：显示攻击力/防御力
            Brr(2, i) = GetModel("Attack", CStr(Arr(i, 2)), CStr(Arr(i, 5))) & "/" & GetModel("Defense", CStr(Arr(i, 2)), CStr(Arr(i, 5)))
        End If
        Brr(3, i) = SentenceTranslate(CStr(Arr(i, 5)))
    Next i
    Call Up("AlliedField", Brr)
    
    ' ========== 手牌区域刷新 ==========
    ' 下载手牌信息并格式化显示
    Arr = Down("Hand", AlliedMove & "1000")
    ReDim Brr(1 To 2, 1 To 9)
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
        Brr(1, i) = DownByID(CStr(Arr(i, 2)), "Name")
        Brr(2, i) = SentenceTranslate(CStr(Arr(i, 5)))
    Next i
    Call Up("Hand", Brr)
    
    ' ========== 纹章区域刷新 ==========
    ' 下载纹章信息并格式化显示
    Arr = Down("Crest", AlliedMove & "1000")
    ReDim Brr(1 To 3, 1 To 5)
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
        Brr(1, i) = DownByID(CStr(Arr(i, 2)), "Name")
        j = GetModel("Countdown", CStr(Arr(i, 2)), CStr(Arr(i, 5)))
        If j > 0 Then
            Brr(2, i) = Translate("Countdown") & j
            Arr(i, 5) = Replace(Arr(i, 5), "Countdown&" & j & "||", "")
        End If
        Brr(3, i) = SentenceTranslate(CStr(Arr(i, 5)))
    Next i
    Call Up("Crest", Brr)
    Sheets("Main").Shapes("CrestBtn").TextFrame.Characters.Text = "查看对手纹章"
    
    ' ========== 牌堆计数刷新 ==========
    ' 计算并显示牌堆剩余卡牌数量
    Arr = Down("Data")
    j = 0
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
        If Arr(i, 1) = "" Then Exit For
        If Arr(i, 4) = "Deck" And Left(Arr(i, 1), 1) = AlliedMove Then j = j + 1
    Next i
    Call Up("Deck·Cards", j)
    
    ' ========== 墓场区域刷新 ==========
    ' 下载并显示墓场信息
    Call Up("Shadows", Down("Shadows", AlliedMove & "1000"))
    
    ' ========== 职业关键字刷新 ==========
    ' 根据职业显示相应的关键字信息
    Str = Down("Class", AlliedMove & "1000")
    ReDim Arr(1 To 2, 1 To 1)
    If Str = "Forestcraft" Then
        ' 精灵职业：显示连击数
        Arr(1, 1) = Translate("Combo")
        Arr(2, 1) = Down("Combo", AlliedMove & "1000")
    ElseIf Str = "Swordcraft" Then
        ' 皇家职业：显示协作数
        Arr(1, 1) = Translate("Rally")
        Arr(2, 1) = Down("Rally", AlliedMove & "1000")
    ElseIf Str = "Dragoncraft" Then
        ' 龙族职业：显示觉醒状态
        Arr(1, 1) = Translate("IsOverflow")
        If Down("AlliedMaxPP") + 0 >= 7 Then Arr(2, 1) = Translate("Overflow") Else Arr(2, 1) = Translate("UnOverflow")
    ElseIf Str = "Abysscraft" Then
        ' 梦魇职业：显示血池数
        Arr(1, 1) = Translate("BloodPool")
        Arr(2, 1) = Down("BloodPool", AlliedMove & "1000")
    End If
    Call Up("ClassKeyWord", Arr)
    
    ' ========== 对手手牌刷新 ==========
    ' 显示对手手牌张数
    Arr = Down("Hand", EnemyMove & "1000")
    Call Up("UIEnemyHands", UBound(Arr, 1))
    
    ' ========== 已使用卡片统计 ==========
    ' 统计并显示本局已使用的卡片
    Arr = Down("Steps")
    Brr = Down("PlayArea")
    ReDim Brr(1 To UBound(Brr, 1), 1 To UBound(Brr, 2))
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr)
        If Arr(i, 2) = "Play" Then
            If Left(Arr(i, 3), 1) = AlliedMove Then
                Str = DownByID(DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(i, 3)), "ID"), "Name")
                For j = 1 To UBound(Brr, 1)
                    If Brr(j, 1) = Str Then
                        Brr(j, 2) = Brr(j, 2) + 1
                        Exit For
                    ElseIf Brr(j, 1) = "" Then
                        Brr(j, 1) = Str
                        Brr(j, 2) = 1
                        Exit For
                    End If
                Next j
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    Call Up("PlayArea", Brr)
    
    ' ========== 已破坏卡片统计 ==========
    ' 统计并显示本局已被破坏的卡片
    Arr = Down("Steps")
    Brr = Down("DestroyArea")
    ReDim Brr(1 To UBound(Brr, 1), 1 To UBound(Brr, 2))
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr)
        If Arr(i, 2) = "Destroy" Then
            If Left(Arr(i, 3), 1) = AlliedMove Then
                Str = DownByID(DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(i, 3)), "ID"), "Name")
                ' 过滤纹章和信仰卡牌（注释标明不严谨实现）
                If Left(Str, 3) <> Translate("Crest") & ":" And Left(Str, 3) <> Translate("Faith") & ":" Then
                    For j = 1 To UBound(Brr, 1)
                        If Brr(j, 1) = Str Then
                            Brr(j, 2) = Brr(j, 2) + 1
                            Exit For
                        ElseIf Brr(j, 1) = "" Then
                            Brr(j, 1) = Str
                            Brr(j, 2) = 1
                            Exit For
                        End If
                    Next j
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    Call Up("DestroyArea", Brr)
    
    ' ========== 计数区域刷新 ==========
    ' 显示各种计数器的当前值
    Arr = Down("Count")
    Brr = Down("CountArea")
    ReDim Brr(1 To UBound(Brr, 1), 1 To UBound(Brr, 2))
    j = 0
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr)
        If Left(Arr(i, 1), 1) = AlliedMove Then
            Str = Mid(Arr(i, 1), 2, 100)
            If Str <> "Class" And Str <> "Shadows" And Str <> "Combo" Then
                j = j + 1
                Brr(j, 1) = Translate(Str)
                Brr(j, 2) = Val(Arr(i, 2))
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    Call Up("CountArea", Brr)
    
    ' ========== 标题栏刷新 ==========
    ' 更新主界面标题栏显示当前回合信息
    If AlliedMove = "F" Then Str = "FirstMove" Else Str = "SecondMove"
    Str = Translate(Str)
    Str = Str & Translate("No") & Mid(Down("Turn"), 2, 10) & Translate("Turn")
    Sheets("Main").Cells(1, 1) = Str
End Function


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·7·3·对局·MatchPlay.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·7·3·对局·MatchPlay"
' ==================================================
' 子过程功能：处理卡牌使用操作
' 说明：检查条件并执行卡牌使用逻辑
' ==================================================
Sub MatchPlay()
    ' ========== 优化设置 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim Arr As Variant           ' 临时数组
    Dim MatchID As String        ' 卡牌匹配ID

    ' ========== 回合权限检查 ==========
    ' 如果不是自己的回合直接退出
    If Left(Down("Turn"), 1) <> Left(Down("FirstMove"), 1) Then Exit Sub

    ' ========== 选择检查 ==========
    ' 检查是否选择了手牌区域
    Arr = IsSelected("Hand")
    If IsEmpty(Arr) Then
        Arr = IsSelected("AlliedField")
        If Not IsEmpty(Arr) Then Call MatchStrike
        GoTo CleanUp
    End If

    ' ========== 获取选中卡牌 ==========
    ' 下载当前玩家的手牌数据
    Arr = Down("Hand", Left(Down("FirstMove"), 1) & 1000)
    ' 检查选择是否有效
    If Selection.Column > UBound(Arr, 1) Or Arr(1, 1) = Empty Then
        Call UIRefresh
        Exit Sub
    End If
    MatchID = Arr(Selection.Column, 1)   ' 获取选中卡牌的MatchID
    
    ' ========== 执行使用功能 ==========
    Arr = PlayFunction(MatchID)
    If Not Arr Then GoTo CleanUp
    
    ' ========== 战场信息获取 ==========
    ' 下载战场信息
    Arr = Down("Field", Left(Down("FirstMove"), 1) & 1000)
    ' 选择最新的单位
    Sheets("Main").Activate
    Sheets("Main").Cells(10, UBound(Arr, 1) + 2).Activate

    ' ========== 界面刷新 ==========
    Call UIRefresh
    Call UpStepsArea

CleanUp:
    ' ========== 恢复应用程序状态 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
End Sub

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：执行卡牌使用逻辑
' 参数：MatchID - 卡牌匹配ID
' 说明：处理卡牌使用的完整流程，包括费用检查、效果触发等
' ==================================================
Function PlayFunction(MatchID As String) As Boolean
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i%, j%                           ' 循环计数器
    Dim Arr As Variant                   ' 临时数组
    Dim Flag As Boolean                  ' 执行标志
    Dim ID As String                     ' 卡牌原型ID
    Dim Cost As Integer                  ' 使用费用
    Dim AlliedPP As Integer              ' 当前能量点
    Dim CardType As String               ' 卡牌类型
    Dim Effect As String                 ' 单个效果
    Dim MatchEffect As String            ' 卡牌当前效果
    Dim FanfareFlag As Boolean           ' 入场曲标志
    Dim EnhanceFlag As Boolean           ' 爆能强化标志
    Dim Effectrr() As String             ' 效果数组
    PlayFunction = True
    
    ' ========== 初始化队列 ==========
    ' 清空能力队列
    QueueAbility = Empty
    QueueMatchID = Empty

    ' ========== 获取卡牌基本信息 ==========
    ID = DownByMatchID(MatchID, "ID")                    ' 卡牌原型ID
    MatchEffect = DownByMatchID(MatchID, "Effect")       ' 卡牌当前效果
    Cost = GetModel("Cost", ID, MatchEffect)             ' 计算当前费用
    AlliedPP = Down("PP", MatchID)                       ' 当前能量点
    CardType = DownByID(ID, "Type")                      ' 卡牌类型

    ' ========== 战场格子检查 ==========
    ' 检查战场是否已满（法术卡不受此限制）
    Arr = Down("Field", MatchID)
    If UBound(Arr, 1) = 5 And CardType <> "Spell" Then Exit Function

    ' ========== 费用检查 ==========
    ' 检查能量点是否足够
    If AlliedPP < Cost Then
        MsgBox "能量点不足", vbExclamation
        PlayFunction = False
        Exit Function
    End If

    ' ========== 连击计数 ==========
    ' 提前增加连击计数
    Call Up("Combo", Down("Combo", MatchID) + 1, MatchID) '连击+1
    Call UpSteps("Play", MatchID, DownByID(ID, "Name"))

    ' ========== 效果遍历处理 ==========
    Flag = True
    Effectrr = GetEffect(MatchID)
    For i = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
        Effect = Effectrr(i)
        
        ' ========== 爆能强化条件检查 ==========
        ' 处理Change类型的爆能强化效果
        If InStr(Effect, "Change|Enhance&") > 0 Then
            j = InStr(Effect, "Change|Enhance&")
            j = Val(Mid(Effect, j + Len("Change|Enhance&"), 10))
            ' 检查能量点是否满足爆能强化条件
            If AlliedPP >= j Then
                If j > Cost Then Cost = j                ' 更新费用为爆能强化费用
                Flag = GetTimePoint(Effect, MatchID, "Enhance")  ' 执行爆能强化效果
                EnhanceFlag = True                       ' 标记使用了爆能强化
                FanfareFlag = True
            Else
                Flag = GetTimePoint(Effect, MatchID, "Fanfare")  ' 执行爆能强化效果
                FanfareFlag = True
            End If
        
        ' ========== 使用时效果 ==========
        ElseIf Left(Effect, Len("Play")) = "Play" Then
            Flag = GetTimePoint(Effect, MatchID)         ' 执行使用时效果
        
        ' ========== 入场曲效果 ==========
        ElseIf Left(Effect, Len("Fanfare")) = "Fanfare" And InStr(MatchEffect, "Remove|Fanfare") = 0 Then
            Flag = GetTimePoint(Effect, MatchID)         ' 执行入场曲效果
            FanfareFlag = True                           ' 标记触发了入场曲
        
        ' ========== 爆能强化效果 ==========
        ElseIf Left(Effect, Len("Enhance")) = "Enhance" And InStr(MatchEffect, "Remove|Enhance") = 0 Then
            j = Val(Mid(Effect, Len("Enhance&") + 1, 10))
            ' 检查能量点是否满足爆能强化条件
            If AlliedPP >= j Then
                If j > Cost Then Cost = j                ' 更新费用为爆能强化费用
                Flag = GetTimePoint(Effect, MatchID, "Enhance")  ' 执行爆能强化效果
                EnhanceFlag = True                       ' 标记使用了爆能强化
            End If
        
        ' ========== 禁止使用效果 ==========
        ElseIf Effect = "CantPlay" Then
            Flag = False                                 ' 标记不能使用
        End If
        
        ' ========== 执行中断检查 ==========
        ' 如果选择未成功，立即退出并恢复状态
        If Not Flag Then
            QueueAbility = Empty                         ' 清空队列
            QueueMatchID = Empty
            Call Up("Combo", Down("Combo", MatchID) - 1, MatchID) '连击-1
            Call EraseSteps("Play")
            
            PlayFunction = False
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next i

    ' ========== 费用消耗 ==========
    Call Up("PP", AlliedPP - Cost, MatchID)

    ' ========== 区域变更处理 ==========
    If CardType = "Spell" Then
        ' 法术卡：移动到使用区域
        Call UpByMatchID(MatchID, "Area", "Play")
        Call Up("Shadows", Down("Shadows", MatchID) + 1, MatchID) '墓场+1
        Call GetAbility("||Spellboost|Hand-Card|1", MatchID) '立刻魔力增幅
    Else
        ' 从者/护符卡：移动到战场区域
        Call UpByMatchID(MatchID, "Area", "Field")
        If CardType = "Follower" Then Call Up("Rally", Down("Rally", MatchID) + 1, MatchID) '协作+1
    End If

    ' ========== 入场处理 ==========
    ' 非法术卡触发入场效果
    If CardType <> "Spell" Then Call GetAction("EnterField", MatchID, True)

    ' ========== 其他卡牌响应 ==========
    Call GetAction("Play", MatchID, "")         ' 使用卡牌响应
    If CardType <> "Spell" Then Call GetAction("EnterField", MatchID, DownByID(ID, "Name"))  ' 入场响应
    If FanfareFlag Then Call GetAction("Keyword&Fanfare", MatchID) ' 入场曲响应
    If EnhanceFlag Then Call GetAction("Keyword&Enhance", MatchID) ' 爆能强化响应

    ' ========== 队列处理 ==========
    Call EraseQueue
End Function


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·6·1·结算·GetTimePoint.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·6·1·结算·GetTimePoint"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：处理效果时机点和条件判断
' 参数：InputEffect - 输入的效果字符串
'        ThisCard - 当前卡牌的匹配ID
'        InputTimePoint - 可选，输入的时间点类型
' 返回：Boolean - 处理是否成功
' 说明：处理效果中的条件判断、模式选择、目标选择等复杂逻辑，构建效果队列
' ==================================================
Function GetTimePoint(InputEffect As String, ThisCard As String, Optional InputTimePoint As String = "") As Boolean
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i%, j%, k%, m%, n%  ' 循环计数器
    Dim Ia As Integer, Ib As Integer                            ' 循环范围控制
    Dim Str As String                                           ' 临时字符串
    Dim Arr As Variant                                          ' 临时数组
    Dim Brr As Variant                                          ' 临时数组
    Dim Flag As Boolean                                         ' 状态标记
    Dim IFFlag As Boolean
    
    Dim Effectrr() As String    ' 效果块数组
    Dim EffectSplit() As String ' 效果分割数组
    Dim IfSplit() As String     ' 条件分割数组
    Dim options() As String     ' 选项数组
    
    GetTimePoint = True   ' 默认返回成功
    IFFlag = True
    
    ' ========== 沉默 ==========
    Str = DownByMatchID(ThisCard, "Effect")
    If InStr(Str, "Remove|All") > 0 Then
        GetTimePoint = False
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' 重新开始处理时间点（用于循环处理）
StartTimePoint:

    ' ========== 效果块提取 ==========
    ' 查询并提取所有{}包围的效果块
    ReDim Effectrr(1 To 10)   ' 初始化效果块数组
    Flag = False
    k = 0
    j = 0   ' 花括号嵌套层级计数器
    
    ' 遍历效果字符串，提取所有最外层的{}块
    For i = 1 To Len(InputEffect)
        Select Case Mid(InputEffect, i, 1)
            Case "{"
                j = j + 1   ' 进入嵌套层级
                If j = 1 Then
                    m = i   ' 记录起始位置
                    Flag = True   ' 标记进入效果块
                End If
            Case "}"
                If j > 0 Then
                    j = j - 1   ' 退出嵌套层级
                    If j = 0 And Flag Then
                        ' 提取最外层{}内的完整内容（包含花括号）
                        k = k + 1
                        Effectrr(k) = Mid(InputEffect, m, i - m + 1)
                        Flag = False   ' 标记退出效果块
                    End If
                End If
        End Select
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 效果块替换 ==========
    ' 将{}块替换为占位符，便于后续处理
    For i = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
        If Effectrr(i) = "" Then Exit For
        InputEffect = Replace(InputEffect, Effectrr(i), "{Effect" & i & "}", , 1)
    Next i
    

    ' 分割效果字符串
    EffectSplit = Split(InputEffect, "||")
    
    ' ========== Change条件处理 ==========
    ' 查找并处理Change条件，确定后续处理范围
    Ia = 1
    Ib = UBound(EffectSplit)
    
    For i = 1 To UBound(EffectSplit)
        If Left(EffectSplit(i), Len("Change|")) = "Change|" Then
            ' 恢复效果块占位符为原始内容
            For j = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
                If Effectrr(j) = "" Then Exit For
                EffectSplit(i) = Replace(EffectSplit(i), "{Effect" & j & "}", Effectrr(j), , 1)
            Next j
            InputEffect = Join(EffectSplit, "||")
        
            ' 解析Change条件
            Str = Mid(EffectSplit(i), 1 + Len("Change|"), 100)
            
            ' 处理超级进化条件
            If Left(Str, Len("SuperEvolve")) = "SuperEvolve" Then
                If InputTimePoint = "SuperEvolve" Then
                    EffectSplit(i) = "Change|True"
                Else
                    EffectSplit(i) = "Change|False"
                End If
                
            ' 处理强化条件
            ElseIf Left(Str, Len("Enhance")) = "Enhance" Then
                If InputTimePoint = "Enhance" Then
                    EffectSplit(i) = "Change|True"
                Else
                    EffectSplit(i) = "Change|False"
                End If
                
            ' 处理其他条件
            ElseIf Str = "False" Then
                
            Else
                EffectSplit(i) = "Change|" & GetIf(Str, ThisCard, InputEffect)
            End If
            
            InputEffect = Join(EffectSplit, "||")
            
            ' 根据Change结果确定后续处理范围
            If Mid(EffectSplit(i), 1 + Len("Change|"), Len("True")) = "True" Then
                Ia = i + 1   ' True分支：处理Change之后的效果
                Ib = UBound(EffectSplit)
            Else
                Ia = 1       ' False分支：处理Change之前的效果
                Ib = i - 1
            End If
            
            Exit For
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 效果遍历处理 ==========
    For i = Ia To Ib
        IfSplit = Split(EffectSplit(i), "|")   ' 分割当前效果
        
        ' 恢复效果块占位符为原始内容
        For j = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
            If Effectrr(j) = "" Then Exit For
            EffectSplit(i) = Replace(EffectSplit(i), "{Effect" & j & "}", Effectrr(j), , 1)
        Next j
        InputEffect = Join(EffectSplit, "||")
        
        ' ========== 模式选择处理 ==========
        If Left(IfSplit(0), Len("Mode")) = "Mode" Then
            ' 构建选项数组
            Str = Translate("Mode")   ' 基础选项文本
            For k = 1 To UBound(IfSplit)
                For j = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
                    If Effectrr(j) = "" Then Exit For
                    IfSplit(k) = Replace(IfSplit(k), "{Effect" & j & "}", Effectrr(j), , 1)
                Next j
                Str = Str & "\" & SentenceTranslate(Mid(IfSplit(k), 2, Len(IfSplit(k)) - 2))
            Next k
            
            options = Split(Str, "\")
            
            ' 计算选择数量（考虑增加模式的效应）
            m = Val(Mid(IfSplit(0), 1 + Len("Mode&"), 10))   ' 基础选择数
            
            ' 检查场地上增加模式的效应
            Arr = Down("Field", ThisCard)
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
                If InStr(Arr(j, 5), "IncreaseModes") > 0 Then
                    Brr = GetEffect(CStr(Arr(j, 1)))
                    For k = 1 To UBound(Brr)
                        If Brr(k) = "IncreaseModes" Then m = m + 1
                    Next k
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 检查纹章区域增加模式的效应
            Arr = Down("Crest", ThisCard)
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
                If InStr(Arr(j, 5), "IncreaseModes") > 0 Then
                    Brr = GetEffect(CStr(Arr(j, 1)))
                    For k = 1 To UBound(Brr)
                        If Brr(k) = "IncreaseModes" Then m = m + 1
                    Next k
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 显示选择对话框
            If QueueAction = "" Then
                Str = ShowChoice(options, 300, 1, m)
            Else
                Str = EraseQueueAction("Select")
                If Str = "" Then
                    MsgBox ("出现bug，请将excel发给sgg，交换控制方继续对局")
                    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
                    ThisWorkbook.Close False
                    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
                End If
            End If
            
            If Str = "" Then
                GetTimePoint = False   ' 用户取消选择
                Exit Function
            End If
            
            ' 处理选择结果
            Arr = Split(Str, "&")
            EffectSplit(i) = Empty
            For j = 0 To UBound(Arr)
                EffectSplit(i) = EffectSplit(i) & SentenceTranslate(options(Arr(j)))
                If j < UBound(Arr) Then EffectSplit(i) = EffectSplit(i) & "||"
            Next j
            InputEffect = Join(EffectSplit, "||")
            
            ' 记录选择动作
            Call UpSteps("Select", Str)
            Call GetAction("Keyword&Mode", ThisCard)
            
            ' 重新开始处理（选择后可能改变后续逻辑）
            GoTo StartTimePoint
        End If
        
        ' ========== 依序处理 ==========
        If Left(IfSplit(0), Len("InOrder")) = "InOrder" Then
            '查看当前序号
            Arr = DownByMatchID(ThisCard, "Effect")
            Arr = SplitEffect(Arr)
            k = 1
            For j = 0 To UBound(Arr)
                If Left(Arr(j), Len("InOrder|")) = "InOrder|" Then
                    k = Val(Mid(Arr(j), 1 + Len("InOrder|"), 10))
                    Exit For
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 计算依序数量（考虑增加模式的效应）
            m = Val(Mid(IfSplit(0), 1 + Len("InOrder&"), 10))   ' 基础选择数
            
            ' 处理依序结果
            EffectSplit(i) = Empty
            
            For j = 1 To m
                For n = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
                    If Effectrr(n) = "" Then Exit For
                    IfSplit(k) = Replace(IfSplit(k), "{Effect" & n & "}", Effectrr(n), , 1)
                Next n
                
                EffectSplit(i) = EffectSplit(i) & Mid(IfSplit(k), 2, Len(IfSplit(k)) - 2)
                If j < m Then EffectSplit(i) = EffectSplit(i) & "||"
                k = k + 1
                If k > UBound(IfSplit) Then k = 1
            Next j
            InputEffect = Join(EffectSplit, "||")
            
            '更新当前序号
            For j = 0 To UBound(Arr)
                If Left(Arr(j), Len("InOrder|")) = "InOrder|" Then
                    Arr(j) = "InOrder|" & k
                    Exit For
                End If
            Next j
            If j > UBound(Arr) Then
                Arr = Join(Arr, "||") & "||InOrder|" & k & "||"
            Else
                Arr = Join(Arr, "||") & "||"
            End If
            Call UpByMatchID(ThisCard, "Effect", Arr)
            
            ' 重新开始处理（选择后可能改变后续逻辑）
            GoTo StartTimePoint
        End If
        
        ' ========== 重复发动处理 ==========
        If Left(IfSplit(0), Len("Replicate")) = "Replicate" Then
            Arr = GetEffect(ThisCard)
            For j = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                If Left(Arr(j), Len(IfSplit(1))) = IfSplit(1) Then
                    ' 修改效果并递归处理
                    Arr(j) = EffectSplit(0) & Mid(Arr(j), 1 + Len(IfSplit(1)), 1000)
                    GetTimePoint = GetTimePoint(CStr(Arr(j)), ThisCard, InputTimePoint)
                End If
            Next j
        End If
        
        ' ========== 条件判断处理 ==========
        If IfSplit(0) = "If" Then
            ' 处理If条件
            EffectSplit(i) = GetIf(EffectSplit(i), ThisCard, InputEffect)
            InputEffect = Join(EffectSplit, "||")
            If Left(EffectSplit(i), Len("False")) = "False" Then IFFlag = False  ' 条件为False时退出
            
        ' ========== 变量赋值处理 ==========
        ElseIf Left(IfSplit(0), 2) = "X=" Or Left(IfSplit(0), 2) = "Y=" Or Left(IfSplit(0), 2) = "Z=" Then
            ' 变量赋值，暂不处理具体逻辑
            
        ' ========== 目标选择处理 ==========
        Else
            ' 查询效果中是否有选择目标的要求
            For j = 0 To UBound(IfSplit)
                If Left(IfSplit(j), Len("Select&")) = "Select&" And IFFlag Then
                    If QueueAction = "" Then
                        ' 根据效果类型确定选择范围
                        If IfSplit(0) = "ReturnToHand" Or IfSplit(0) = "Destroy" Or IfSplit(0) = "Countdown" Or _
                        IfSplit(0) = "Damage" Or IfSplit(0) = "SplitDamage" Or IfSplit(0) = "Restore" Or _
                        IfSplit(0) = "Evolve" Or IfSplit(0) = "SuperEvolve" Then
                            Call GetGoal("Field-" & IfSplit(j), ThisCard, InputEffect, IfSplit(j))  ' 从场地选择
                        ElseIf IfSplit(0) = "ReturnToDeck" Or IfSplit(0) = "Discard" Or IfSplit(0) = "HandSummon" Then
                            Call GetGoal("Hand-" & IfSplit(j), ThisCard, InputEffect, IfSplit(j))   ' 从手牌选择
                        Else
                            Call GetGoal(IfSplit(j), ThisCard, InputEffect)              ' 通用选择
                        End If
                    Else
                        PreviousGoal = EraseQueueAction("Select")
                        If PreviousGoal = "" Then
                            MsgBox ("出现bug，请将excel发给sgg，交换控制方继续对局")
                            Application.DisplayAlerts = False
                            ThisWorkbook.Close False
                            Application.DisplayAlerts = True
                        ElseIf PreviousGoal = "0" Then
                            PreviousGoal = ""
                        End If
                    End If
                    
                    ' 处理选择结果
                    If PreviousGoal = "" Then
                        If DownByID(DownByMatchID(ThisCard, "ID"), "Type") = "Spell" Then
                            GetTimePoint = False   ' 选择失败
                        Else
                            GetTimePoint = True
                            Call UpSteps("Select", "0")
                        End If
                        Exit Function
                    Else
                        ' 替换选择占位符为实际目标
                        EffectSplit(i) = Replace(EffectSplit(i), IfSplit(j), "Goal&" & PreviousGoal)
                        InputEffect = Join(EffectSplit, "||")
                        
                        ' 记录选择动作
                        Call UpSteps("Select", PreviousGoal)
                    End If
                End If
            Next j
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 效果队列构建 ==========
    ' 将处理后的效果添加到队列
    QueueAbility = QueueAbility & "\" & InputEffect
    QueueMatchID = QueueMatchID & "\" & ThisCard
    
    GetTimePoint = True   ' 返回处理成功
End Function



--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·4·5·辅助·GetModel.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·4·5·辅助·GetModel"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：获取卡牌属性模型值
' 参数：Model - 模型类型（OrigCountdown、Countdown、OrigAttack、Attack等）
'        ID - 卡牌ID
'        Effect - 效果字符串
'        MatchID - 可选，匹配ID（用于特殊角色处理）
' 返回：Integer - 计算后的属性值
' 说明：根据模型类型计算卡牌的各种属性值，包括攻击力、生命值、费用、倒数等
' ==================================================
Function GetModel(Model As String, ID As String, Effect As String, Optional MatchID As String = "") As Integer
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i As Integer            ' 返回值
    Dim j As Integer, k As Integer   ' 循环计数器和位置索引
    Dim Str As String           ' 临时字符串
    Dim Arr() As String         ' 效果分割数组
    Dim Brr() As String         ' 临时分割数组
    
    ' ========== 倒数相关模型 ==========
    ' 原始倒数值（从卡牌数据获取）
    If Model = "OrigCountdown" Then
        i = Val(DownByID(ID, "CountDown"))
        
    ' 当前倒数值（从效果字符串解析）
    ElseIf Model = "Countdown" Then
        Arr = SplitEffect(Effect)
        For j = 0 To UBound(Arr)
            If Left(Arr(j), Len("Countdown&")) = "Countdown&" Then
                i = Val(Mid(Arr(j), 1 + Len("Countdown&"), 10))   ' 提取倒数值
                Exit For
            End If
        Next j
        
    ' ========== 攻击力相关模型 ==========
    ' 原始攻击力（从卡牌数据获取）
    ElseIf Model = "OrigAttack" Then
        i = Val(DownByID(ID, "Attack"))
        
    ' 当前攻击力（考虑效果修正）
    ElseIf Model = "Attack" Then
        i = Val(DownByID(ID, "Attack"))   ' 获取基础攻击力
        Arr = SplitEffect(Effect)
        
        ' 遍历所有效果，处理攻击力修正
        For j = 0 To UBound(Arr)
            ' 查找包含"/"但不包含"{"的效果（排除条件效果）
            If InStr(Arr(j), "/") > 0 And InStr(Arr(j), "{") = 0 Then
                Brr = Split(Arr(j), "|")
                Str = Brr(UBound(Brr))   ' 获取效果数值部分
                
                ' 根据操作符类型计算攻击力
                If Left(Str, 1) = "$" Then
                    i = Val(Mid(Str, 2, 10))        ' 设为固定值
                ElseIf Left(Str, 1) = "*" Then
                    i = i * Val(Mid(Str, 2, 10))    ' 倍数修正
                ElseIf Left(Str, 1) = "%" Then
                    i = -1 * Int(-1 * i / Val(Mid(Str, 2, 10)))   ' 除法修正（进位取整）
                Else
                    i = i + Val(Str)                ' 加减修正
                End If
            End If
        Next j
        If i < 0 Then i = 0   ' 攻击力不能为负
        
    ' ========== 生命值相关模型 ==========
    ' 原始生命值
    ElseIf Model = "OrigDefense" Then
        ' 特殊角色处理（F1000或S1000）
        If MatchID = "F1000" Or MatchID = "S1000" Then
            i = Down("MaxDefense", MatchID)   ' 从界面获取最大生命值
        Else
            i = Val(DownByID(ID, "Defense"))  ' 从卡牌数据获取生命值
        End If
        
    ' 当前生命值（考虑效果修正和伤害）
    ElseIf Model = "Defense" Then
        ' 特殊角色处理（F1000或S1000）
        If MatchID = "F1000" Or MatchID = "S1000" Then
            i = Down("Defense", MatchID)   ' 从界面获取当前生命值
        Else
            i = Val(DownByID(ID, "Defense"))   ' 获取基础生命值
            Arr = SplitEffect(Effect)
            
            ' 处理生命值修正效果
            For j = 0 To UBound(Arr)
                k = InStr(Arr(j), "/")
                ' 查找包含"/"但不包含"{"的效果（排除条件效果）
                If k > 0 And InStr(Arr(j), "{") = 0 Then
                    ' 根据操作符类型计算生命值
                    If Mid(Arr(j), k + 1, 1) = "$" Then
                        i = Val(Mid(Arr(j), k + 2, 10))        ' 设为固定值
                    ElseIf Mid(Arr(j), k + 1, 1) = "*" Then
                        i = i * Val(Mid(Arr(j), k + 2, 10))    ' 倍数修正
                    ElseIf Mid(Arr(j), k + 1, 1) = "%" Then
                        i = -1 * Int(-1 * i / Val(Mid(Arr(j), k + 2, 10)))   ' 除法修正（进位取整）
                    Else
                        i = i + Val(Mid(Arr(j), k + 1, 10))    ' 加减修正
                    End If
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' 处理伤害效果（减少生命值）
            For j = 0 To UBound(Arr)
                If Left(Arr(j), Len("Damaged&")) = "Damaged&" Then
                    i = i - Val(Mid(Arr(j), 1 + Len("Damaged&"), 10))   ' 减去伤害值
                End If
            Next j
        End If
        
    ' ========== 费用相关模型 ==========
    ' 原始费用
    ElseIf Model = "OrigCost" Then
        i = Val(DownByID(ID, "Cost"))
        
    ' 当前费用（考虑效果修正）
    ElseIf Model = "Cost" Then
        i = Val(DownByID(ID, "Cost"))   ' 获取基础费用
        Arr = SplitEffect(Effect)
        
        ' 处理费用修正效果
        For j = 0 To UBound(Arr)
            k = InStr(Arr(j), "Cost")
            ' 查找包含"Cost"但不包含"{"的效果
            If k > 0 And InStr(Arr(j), "{") = 0 Then
                Str = Mid(Arr(j), k + Len("Cost"), 10)   ' 提取费用修正部分
                
                ' 根据操作符类型计算费用
                If Left(Str, 1) = "$" Then
                    i = Val(Mid(Str, 2, 10))        ' 设为固定值
                ElseIf Left(Str, 1) = "*" Then
                    i = i * Val(Mid(Str, 2, 10))    ' 倍数修正
                ElseIf Left(Str, 1) = "%" Then
                    i = -1 * Int(-1 * i / Val(Mid(Str, 2, 10)))   ' 除法修正（进位取整）
                Else
                    i = i + Val(Str)                ' 加减修正
                End If
            End If
        Next j
        If i < 0 Then i = 0   ' 费用不能为负
    End If
    
    GetModel = i   ' 返回计算结果
End Function


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·6·2·结算·GetIf.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·6·2·结算·GetIf"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：条件判断处理器
' 参数：InputIf - 输入的条件字符串
'        ThisCard - 当前卡牌的匹配ID
'        InputEffect - 输入的效果字符串
' 返回：String - 判断结果（True/False）和修改后的条件字符串
' 说明：处理游戏中的各种条件判断，包括回合条件、解锁条件、次数限制、特性条件等
' ==================================================
Function GetIf(InputIf As String, ThisCard As String, InputEffect As String) As String
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i%, j%, k%, m%  ' 循环计数器
    Dim Str As String                               ' 临时字符串
    Dim Arr As Variant                              ' 临时数组
    Dim result As Boolean                           ' 判断结果
    Dim Ifrr() As String                            ' 条件分割数组
    Dim EffectSplit() As String                     ' 效果分割数组
    Dim Sign As String                              ' 比较运算符
    Dim CompareX As String, CompareY As String      ' 比较操作数
    
    ' ========== 条件块合并 ==========
    ' 分割条件字符串，合并被{}包围的多部分条件
    Ifrr = Split(InputIf, "|")
    For i = 1 To UBound(Ifrr)
        ' 检查是否以{开头但未闭合（多部分条件）
        If Left(Ifrr(i), 1) = "{" And InStr(Ifrr(i), "}") = 0 Then
            ' 合并后续部分直到找到闭合}
            For j = i + 1 To UBound(Ifrr)
                Ifrr(i) = Ifrr(i) & "|" & Ifrr(j)   ' 合并条件部分
                Ifrr(j) = "\"                        ' 标记已合并的部分
                If InStr(Ifrr(i), "}") > 0 Then Exit For   ' 找到闭合后退出
            Next j
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 条件判断主循环 ==========
    result = True   ' 默认结果为True
    
    ' 遍历所有条件部分进行判断
    For k = 1 To UBound(Ifrr)
        ' ========== 回合条件判断 ==========
        ' 检查回合相关条件（如Allied-Turn, Enemy-Turn）
        If Right(Ifrr(k), Len("Turn")) = "Turn" Then
            Str = Down("Turn")       ' 获取当前回合数
            
            ' 检查我方回合条件
            If InStr(Ifrr(k), "Allied-") > 0 And Left(Str, 1) <> Left(ThisCard, 1) Then result = False
            ' 检查敌方回合条件
            If InStr(Ifrr(k), "Enemy-") > 0 And Left(Str, 1) = Left(ThisCard, 1) Then result = False
        End If
        
        ' ========== 解锁条件判断 ==========
        ' 检查进化/超级进化解锁条件
        If Left(Ifrr(k), Len("Unlock")) = "Unlock" Then
            Str = Down("Turn")   ' 获取当前回合
            i = Mid(Str, 2, 10)  ' 提取回合数（去掉F/S前缀）
            
            ' 根据先手和进化类型确定解锁回合
            If Left(Str, 1) = "F" Then
                If InStr(Ifrr(k), "SuperEvolve") > 0 Then j = 7 Else j = 5   ' 先手方解锁回合
            Else
                If InStr(Ifrr(k), "SuperEvolve") > 0 Then j = 6 Else j = 4   ' 后手方解锁回合
            End If
            
            ' 检查是否达到解锁回合
            If i < j Then result = False
        End If
        
        ' ========== 每回合次数限制 ==========
        ' 检查每回合可触发次数限制
        If Left(Ifrr(k), Len("TimesPerTurn&")) = "TimesPerTurn&" Then
            EffectSplit = SplitEffect(DownByMatchID(ThisCard, "Effect"))   ' 获取当前效果
            
            ' 查找已有的次数记录
            For i = 0 To UBound(EffectSplit)
                If Left(EffectSplit(i), Len("TimesPerTurn&")) = "TimesPerTurn&" Then
                    m = Val(Mid(EffectSplit(i), 1 + Len("TimesPerTurn&"), 10))   ' 已触发次数
                    j = Val(Mid(Ifrr(k), 1 + Len("TimesPerTurn&"), 10))          ' 允许的最大次数
                    'If m >= j Then result = False Else EffectSplit(i) = "TimesPerTurn&" & m + 1   ' 更新次数
                    If m >= j Then result = False
                    Exit For
                End If
            Next i
            
            ' 重新构建效果字符串
            Str = Empty
            If UBound(EffectSplit) >= 0 Then Str = Join(EffectSplit, "||") & "||" Else Str = Empty
            ' 如果没有找到次数记录，添加新的记录
            'If i > UBound(EffectSplit) Then Str = Str & "TimesPerTurn&1||"
            
            ' 更新效果记录
            Call UpByMatchID(ThisCard, "Effect", Str)
        End If
        
        ' ========== 连击条件判断 ==========
        ' 检查连击次数条件
        If Left(Ifrr(k), Len("Combo&")) = "Combo&" Then
            i = Down("Combo", ThisCard)                                  ' 当前连击数
            j = Val(Mid(Ifrr(k), 1 + Len("Combo&"), 10))                ' 要求的连击数
            If i < j Then result = False
        End If
        
        ' ========== 协作条件判断 ==========
        ' 检查协作次数条件
        If Left(Ifrr(k), Len("Rally&")) = "Rally&" Then
            i = Down("Rally", ThisCard)                                  ' 当前协作数
            j = Val(Mid(Ifrr(k), 1 + Len("Rally&"), 10))                ' 要求的协作数
            If i < j Then result = False
        End If
        
        ' ========== 土之秘术条件判断 ==========
        ' 检查土之印数量条件
        If Left(Ifrr(k), Len("EarthRite&")) = "EarthRite&" Then
            Arr = Down("Field", ThisCard)   ' 获取场地上所有单位
            
            ' 查找场上的土之印
            For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
                If InStr(Arr(i, 5), "EarthSigil&") > 0 Then Exit For
            Next i
            
            ' 如果找到土之印，检查数量条件
            If i <= UBound(Arr, 1) Then
                EffectSplit = SplitEffect(Arr(i, 5))   ' 分割土之印效果
                
                ' 提取土之印数量
                For j = 0 To UBound(EffectSplit)
                    If Left(EffectSplit(j), Len("EarthSigil&")) = "EarthSigil&" Then
                        i = Val(Mid(EffectSplit(j), 1 + Len("EarthSigil&"), 10))   ' 当前土之印数量
                        j = Val(Mid(Ifrr(k), 1 + Len("EarthRite&"), 10))           ' 要求的土之印数量
                        If i < j Then result = False
                        Exit For
                    End If
                Next j
            Else
                result = False   ' 没有找到土之印
            End If
        End If
        
        ' ========== 觉醒条件判断 ==========
        ' 检查最大PP是否达到7（觉醒条件）
        If Left(Ifrr(k), Len("Overflow")) = "Overflow" Then
            i = Down("MaxPP", ThisCard)   ' 获取最大PP
            If i < 7 Then result = False
        End If
        
        ' ========== 唤灵条件判断 ==========
        ' 检查墓场数量条件
        If Left(Ifrr(k), Len("Necromancy")) = "Necromancy" Then
            i = Down("Shadows", ThisCard)                                ' 当前墓场数量
            j = Val(Mid(Ifrr(k), 1 + Len("Necromancy&"), 10))           ' 要求的墓场数量
            If i < j Then result = False
        End If
        
        ' ========== 血祭条件判断 ==========
        ' 检查墓场数量条件
        If Left(Ifrr(k), Len("Sacrifice")) = "Sacrifice" Then
            i = Down("BloodPool", ThisCard)                                ' 当前墓场数量
            j = Val(Mid(Ifrr(k), 1 + Len("Sacrifice&"), 10))           ' 要求的墓场数量
            If i < j Then result = False
        End If
        
        ' ========== 查重条件判断 ==========
        ' 检查是否存在重复名称的单位
        If Left(Ifrr(k), Len("NoDuplicates-")) = "NoDuplicates-" Then
            Str = Mid(Ifrr(k), 1 + Len("NoDuplicates-"), 100)           ' 提取检查范围
            
            ' 获取随机选择的结果（用于比较）
            Arr = GetGoal("Random@100-" & Str, ThisCard)
            i = UBound(Arr, 1)                                          ' 随机选择的数量
            
            ' 获取实际选择的结果
            Arr = GetGoal(Str, ThisCard)
            j = UBound(Arr, 1)                                          ' 实际选择的数量
            
            ' 如果实际选择数量大于随机选择数量，说明有重复
            If i < j Then result = False
        End If
        
        ' ========== 奥义条件判断 ==========
        ' 检查最大PP是否达到7（觉醒条件）
        If Left(Ifrr(k), Len("Skybound")) = "Skybound" Then
            i = Val(Mid(Down("Turn", ThisCard), 2, 10)) ' 获取当前回合数
            
            Str = DownByMatchID(ThisCard, "Effect")
            Arr = SplitEffect(Str)
            For j = 0 To UBound(Arr)
                If Left(Arr(j), Len("Skybound&")) = "Skybound&" Then
                    j = Val(Mid(Arr(j), 1 + Len("Skybound&"), 10))
                    Exit For
                End If
            Next j
            
            If i < 10 - j Then result = False
        End If
        
        ' ========== 解放奥义条件判断 ==========
        ' 检查最大PP是否达到7（觉醒条件）
        If Left(Ifrr(k), Len("SuperSkybound")) = "SuperSkybound" Then
            i = Val(Mid(Down("Turn", ThisCard), 2, 10)) ' 获取当前回合数
            
            Str = DownByMatchID(ThisCard, "Effect")
            Arr = SplitEffect(Str)
            For j = 0 To UBound(Arr)
                If Left(Arr(j), Len("Skybound&")) = "Skybound&" Then
                    j = Val(Mid(Arr(j), 1 + Len("Skybound&"), 10))
                    Exit For
                End If
            Next j
            
            If i < 15 - j Then result = False
        End If
        
        ' ========== 数值比较条件判断 ==========
        ' 提取比较运算符
        Sign = Empty
        If InStr(Ifrr(k), "<>") > 0 Then
            Sign = "<>"
        ElseIf InStr(Ifrr(k), "<") > 0 Then
            Sign = "<"
        ElseIf InStr(Ifrr(k), "=") > 0 Then
            Sign = "="
        ElseIf InStr(Ifrr(k), ">") > 0 Then
            Sign = ">"
        End If
        
        ' 如果找到比较运算符，进行数值比较
        If Sign <> "" Then
            i = InStr(Ifrr(k), "}" & Sign)                              ' 查找运算符位置
            Str = Mid(Ifrr(k), i + Len("}" & Sign), 100)                ' 提取比较值Y
            
            ' 解析比较值Y（可能是数值或表达式）
            If Val(Str) > 0 Or Str = "0" Then
                CompareY = Val(Str)                                     ' 直接数值
                j = CompareY
            Else
                CompareY = "{" & GetX(Mid(Str, 2, Len(Str) - 2), ThisCard, InputEffect) & "}"   ' 表达式求值
                j = Val(Mid(CompareY, 2, 10))
            End If
            
            ' 解析比较值X（表达式求值）
            Str = Left(Ifrr(k), i)
            CompareX = "{" & GetX(Mid(Str, 2, Len(Str) - 2), ThisCard, InputEffect) & "}"
            
            ' 进行数值比较
            i = Val(Mid(CompareX, 2, 10))
            If Sign = "<>" And i = j Then result = False                ' 不等于
            If Sign = "<" And i >= j Then result = False                ' 小于
            If Sign = "=" And i <> j Then result = False                ' 等于
            If Sign = ">" And i <= j Then result = False                ' 大于
            
            ' 更新条件字符串（替换为实际比较表达式）
            Ifrr(k) = CompareX & Sign & CompareY
        End If
        
        ' 如果任一条件失败，提前退出循环
        If Not result Then Exit For
    Next k
    
    ' ========== 结果构建 ==========
    Ifrr(0) = result                    ' 将结果放入数组第一个元素
    GetIf = Join(Ifrr, "|")             ' 合并所有条件部分
    GetIf = Replace(GetIf, "|\", "")    ' 清理已合并的标记
End Function


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·9·1·文件·SaveGame.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·9·1·文件·SaveGame"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：保存游戏存档和Deck工作表
' 参数：gameData - 要保存的游戏数据字符串
' 返回：Boolean - 保存是否成功
' 说明：将游戏数据保存到文档文件夹，同时备份Deck工作表
' ==================================================
Function SaveGame(gameData As String) As Boolean
    ' ========== 应用程序状态保存 ==========
    Dim screenUpdatingState As Boolean
    Dim eventsState As Boolean
    Dim calculationState As XlCalculation
    Dim displayAlertsState As Boolean
    
    screenUpdatingState = Application.ScreenUpdating
    eventsState = Application.EnableEvents
    calculationState = Application.Calculation
    displayAlertsState = Application.DisplayAlerts
    
    ' ========== 优化设置 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    
    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler
    
    ' ========== 多路径保存策略 ==========
    ' 定义多个备用保存路径
    Dim savePaths(1 To 3) As String
    Dim currentPath As String
    Dim i As Integer
    
    ' 主路径：用户文档文件夹
    savePaths(1) = Environ("USERPROFILE") & "\Documents\GameSaves\"
    
    ' 备用路径1：应用程序所在目录
    savePaths(2) = Environ("TEMP") & "\GameSaves\"
    
    ' 备用路径2：临时文件夹
    savePaths(3) = ThisWorkbook.Path & "\GameSaves\"
    
    Dim saveSuccess As Boolean
    saveSuccess = False
    
    ' 尝试在多个路径中保存
    For i = 1 To 3
        currentPath = savePaths(i)
        
        ' 跳过无效路径（如应用程序路径为空时）
        If currentPath = "\GameSaves\" Then GoTo NextPath
        
        On Error Resume Next
        ' 创建文件夹（如果不存在）
        If Dir(currentPath, vbDirectory) = "" Then
            MkDir currentPath
        End If
        
        ' 检查文件夹是否创建成功
        If Dir(currentPath, vbDirectory) <> "" Then
            ' 尝试保存游戏数据
            Dim filePath As String
            filePath = currentPath & "szb_cloud.txt"
            
            Dim fileNumber As Integer
            fileNumber = FreeFile
            
            Open filePath For Output As fileNumber
            Print #fileNumber, gameData
            Close fileNumber
            
            ' 如果文件保存成功，继续保存Deck工作表
            If Dir(filePath) <> "" Then
                Dim deckSaved As Boolean
                deckSaved = SaveDeckSheet(currentPath)
                
                If deckSaved Then
                    saveSuccess = True
                    Exit For  ' 保存成功，退出循环
                Else
                    ' Deck保存失败，删除已保存的数据文件
                    Kill filePath
                End If
            End If
        End If
        
NextPath:
        On Error GoTo ErrorHandler
    Next i
    
    If Not saveSuccess Then
        Err.Raise 513, "SaveGame", "所有保存路径都不可用"
    End If
    
    SaveGame = True

CleanUp:
    ' ========== 应用程序状态恢复 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = screenUpdatingState
    Application.EnableEvents = eventsState
    Application.Calculation = calculationState
    Application.DisplayAlerts = displayAlertsState
    
    Exit Function
    
ErrorHandler:
    ' ========== 错误处理 ==========
    SaveGame = False
    MsgBox "保存失败: " & Err.Description, vbExclamation
    GoTo CleanUp
End Function

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：保存Deck工作表到CSV文件
' 参数：savePath - 保存路径
' 返回：Boolean - 保存是否成功
' 说明：将Deck工作表数据导出为CSV格式备份
' ==================================================
Private Function SaveDeckSheet(savePath As String) As Boolean
    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler
    
    Dim ws As Worksheet
    Dim deckFile As String
    deckFile = savePath & "szb_deck.csv"
    
    ' ========== 工作表存在性检查 ==========
    ' 检查Deck工作表是否存在
    Set ws = Nothing
    On Error Resume Next
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.Sheets("Deck")
    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler
    
    If ws Is Nothing Then
        ' 工作表不存在，返回False但不显示错误
        SaveDeckSheet = False
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' ========== 工作表状态保存 ==========
    ' 保存工作表当前的可见状态
    Dim originalVisibility As XlSheetVisibility
    originalVisibility = ws.Visible
    
    ' 如果工作表是隐藏的，临时取消隐藏
    If originalVisibility <> xlSheetVisible Then
        ws.Visible = xlSheetVisible
    End If
    
    ' ========== 文件保存处理 ==========
    ' 禁用警告提示以避免用户确认对话框
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    
    ' 保存工作表数据为CSV文件
    ws.Copy ' 复制工作表到一个新工作簿
    With ActiveWorkbook
        .SaveAs fileName:=deckFile, FileFormat:=xlCSV
        .Close SaveChanges:=False
    End With
    
    ' ========== 状态恢复 ==========
    ' 恢复工作表原来的可见状态
    If originalVisibility <> xlSheetVisible Then
        ws.Visible = originalVisibility
    End If
    
    SaveDeckSheet = True
    Exit Function
    
ErrorHandler:
    ' ========== 错误处理 ==========
    SaveDeckSheet = False
    ' 错误处理中也要恢复可见状态
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not ws Is Nothing And originalVisibility <> xlSheetVisible Then
        ws.Visible = originalVisibility
    End If
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
End Function

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：读取游戏存档和Deck工作表
' 返回：String - 读取的游戏数据或错误信息
' 说明：从文档文件夹读取保存的游戏数据和Deck工作表
' ==================================================
Function LoadGame() As String
    ' ========== 应用程序状态保存 ==========
    Dim screenUpdatingState As Boolean
    Dim eventsState As Boolean
    Dim calculationState As XlCalculation
    Dim displayAlertsState As Boolean
    
    screenUpdatingState = Application.ScreenUpdating
    eventsState = Application.EnableEvents
    calculationState = Application.Calculation
    displayAlertsState = Application.DisplayAlerts
    
    ' ========== 优化设置 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    
    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler
    
    ' ========== 多路径查找策略 ==========
    ' 定义多个备用查找路径（与SaveGame保持一致）
    Dim savePaths(1 To 3) As String
    Dim currentPath As String
    Dim i As Integer
    
    ' 主路径：用户文档文件夹
    savePaths(1) = Environ("USERPROFILE") & "\Documents\GameSaves\"
    
    ' 备用路径1：应用程序所在目录
    savePaths(2) = Environ("TEMP") & "\GameSaves\"
    
    ' 备用路径2：临时文件夹
    savePaths(3) = ThisWorkbook.Path & "\GameSaves\"
    
    Dim filePath As String
    Dim fileFound As Boolean
    fileFound = False
    
    ' 在多个路径中查找存档文件
    For i = 1 To 3
        currentPath = savePaths(i)
        
        ' 跳过无效路径
        If currentPath = "\GameSaves\" Then GoTo NextPath
        
        filePath = currentPath & "szb_cloud.txt"
        
        ' 检查文件是否存在
        If Dir(filePath) <> "" Then
            fileFound = True
            Exit For  ' 找到文件，退出循环
        End If
        
NextPath:
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 文件存在性检查 ==========
    If Not fileFound Then
        LoadGame = "可以去群内下载卡包zip，解压后将路径输入到这里，之后就可以查询图片"
        GoTo CleanUp
    End If
    
    ' ========== 游戏数据读取 ==========
    Dim fileNumber As Integer
    Dim fileContent As String
    Dim lineData As String
    
    fileNumber = FreeFile
    fileContent = ""
    
    Open filePath For Input As fileNumber
    Do Until EOF(fileNumber)
        Line Input #fileNumber, lineData
        fileContent = fileContent & lineData
        If Not EOF(fileNumber) Then fileContent = fileContent & vbCrLf
    Loop
    Close fileNumber
    
    ' ========== Deck工作表读取 ==========
    ' 从找到文件的同一目录加载Deck数据
    Dim deckLoaded As Boolean
    deckLoaded = LoadDeckSheet(currentPath)
    
    LoadGame = fileContent

CleanUp:
    ' ========== 应用程序状态恢复 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = screenUpdatingState
    Application.EnableEvents = eventsState
    Application.Calculation = calculationState
    Application.DisplayAlerts = displayAlertsState
    
    Exit Function
    
ErrorHandler:
    ' ========== 错误处理 ==========
    LoadGame = "读取失败: " & Err.Description
    GoTo CleanUp
End Function

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：读取Deck工作表从CSV文件
' 参数：savePath - 保存路径
' 返回：Boolean - 读取是否成功
' 说明：从CSV文件导入数据到Deck工作表
' ==================================================
Private Function LoadDeckSheet(savePath As String) As Boolean
    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler
    
    Dim deckFile As String
    deckFile = savePath & "szb_deck.csv"
    
    ' ========== 文件存在性检查 ==========
    ' 检查CSV文件是否存在
    If Dir(deckFile) = "" Then
        ' CSV文件不存在，返回False但不显示错误
        LoadDeckSheet = False
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' ========== 工作表准备 ==========
    ' 禁用警告提示
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    
    ' 获取或创建Deck工作表
    Dim ws As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.Sheets("Deck")
    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler
    
    If ws Is Nothing Then
        ' 如果Deck工作表不存在，则创建
        Set ws = ThisWorkbook.Sheets.Add
        ws.Name = "Deck"
    Else
        ' 清空现有数据
        ws.Cells.Clear
    End If
    
    ' ========== 数据导入处理 ==========
    ' 打开CSV文件并读取数据
    Dim csvWorkbook As Workbook
    Set csvWorkbook = Workbooks.Open(deckFile, ReadOnly:=True)
    
    ' 将数据复制到Deck工作表
    csvWorkbook.Sheets(1).UsedRange.Copy ws.Range("A1")
    
    ' 关闭CSV工作簿而不保存
    csvWorkbook.Close SaveChanges:=False
    
    LoadDeckSheet = True
