    Exit Function
    
ErrorHandler:
    ' ========== 错误处理 ==========
    LoadDeckSheet = False
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not csvWorkbook Is Nothing Then csvWorkbook.Close SaveChanges:=False
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
End Function


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·3·3·牌组·SaveDeck.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·3·3·牌组·SaveDeck"
' ==================================================
' 过程功能：保存卡组到Deck工作表
' 说明：将当前原卡组数据保存到Deck工作表，支持多卡组管理
' ==================================================
Sub SaveDeck()
    ' ========== 优化设置 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim DeckName As String       ' 卡组名称
    Dim Decks As Variant         ' Deck工作表数据数组
    Dim i As Integer, j As Integer, k As Integer   ' 循环计数器
    Dim OrigDeck As Variant      ' 原卡组数据数组
    Dim Class As String          ' 职业类型
    
    ' ========== 卡组清理与准备 ==========
    DeckName = Down("DeckName")                   ' 获取当前卡组名称
    Call DeleteDeckFunction(DeckName)             ' 删除同名旧卡组（如果存在）
    Call OrigDeckSorting                          ' 对原卡组进行排序
    
    ' ========== Deck工作表数据初始化 ==========
    ' 读取Deck工作表全部数据（42行×100列）
    Decks = Range(Sheets("Deck").Cells(1, 1), Sheets("Deck").Cells(42, 100))
    
    ' ========== 当前卡组数据获取 ==========
    OrigDeck = Down("OrigDeck")   ' 获取排序后的原卡组数据
    Class = Down("Class")         ' 获取当前职业
    
    ' ========== 卡组数据保存 ==========
    ' 在Deck工作表中查找空位置（每3列为一组卡组数据）
    For k = 1 To UBound(Decks, 2)
        ' 检查当前列组是否为空（第1行第k列为空表示该位置可用）
        If Decks(1, k) = "" Then
            ' ========== 卡组头信息设置 ==========
            Decks(1, k) = DeckName                   ' 第1行：卡组名称
            Decks(1, k + 2) = Class                  ' 第1行：职业（跳过1列）
            
            ' ========== 列标题设置 ==========
            Decks(2, k) = Translate("Cost")          ' 第2行：费用列标题
            Decks(2, k + 1) = Translate("CardName")  ' 第2行：卡牌名称列标题
            Decks(2, k + 2) = Translate("Counts")    ' 第2行：数量列标题
            
            ' ========== 卡牌数据填充 ==========
            For i = 1 To UBound(OrigDeck, 1)
                ' 复制原卡组数据到Deck工作表对应位置
                Decks(i + 2, k) = OrigDeck(i, 1)     ' 费用数据
                Decks(i + 2, k + 1) = OrigDeck(i, 2) ' 卡牌名称数据
                Decks(i + 2, k + 2) = OrigDeck(i, 3) ' 数量数据
            Next i
            
            Exit For   ' 找到空位并填充后退出循环
        End If
        
        k = k + 3   ' 每组卡组占用3列，所以跳过2列（循环会自动+1，所以总共+3）
    Next k
    
    ' ========== 卡组数量限制检查 ==========
    If k > UBound(Decks, 2) Then
        ' 超出最大卡组数量限制（100列÷3列/组 ≈ 33组，但提示为30个？）
        MsgBox "最多保存30个卡组", vbExclamation
    Else
        ' ========== 数据回写与保存 ==========
        ' 将更新后的数据写回Deck工作表
        Range(Sheets("Deck").Cells(1, 1), Sheets("Deck").Cells(42, 100)) = Decks
        
        ' 保存游戏数据到指定路径
        Call SaveGame(Down("Path"))
    End If
    
CleanUp:
    ' ========== 恢复应用程序状态 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
End Sub


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·3·5·录卡·SelectDeck.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·3·5·录卡·SelectDeck"
' ==================================================
' 过程功能：选择并加载已保存的卡组
' 说明：从Deck工作表中选择一个卡组并加载到原卡组界面
' ==================================================
Sub SelectDeck()
    ' ========== 优化设置 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim Decks As Variant         ' Deck工作表数据数组
    Dim i As Integer, j As Integer, k As Integer   ' 循环计数器
    Dim options() As String      ' 选择对话框选项数组
    Dim Str As String            ' 选项字符串
    Dim DeckName As String       ' 选择的卡组名称
    Dim OrigDeck As Variant      ' 原卡组数据数组
    
    ' ========== Deck工作表数据初始化 ==========
    ' 读取Deck工作表全部数据（42行×100列）
    Decks = Range(Sheets("Deck").Cells(1, 1), Sheets("Deck").Cells(42, 100))
    
    ' ========== 原卡组数据初始化 ==========
    OrigDeck = Down("OrigDeck")   ' 获取当前原卡组结构
    ' 重新初始化原卡组数组（清空现有数据）
    ReDim OrigDeck(1 To UBound(OrigDeck, 1), 1 To UBound(OrigDeck, 2))
    
    ' ========== 卡组选择对话框 ==========
    ' 构建选择对话框的选项字符串
    Str = Translate("Select") & Translate("Deck")   ' 基础提示：选择卡组
    
    ' 遍历所有卡组，构建选项列表
    For k = 1 To UBound(Decks, 2)
        If Decks(1, k) <> "" Then
            Str = Str & "|" & Decks(1, k)   ' 添加卡组名称到选项
        End If
        k = k + 3   ' 每组卡组占用3列，跳过3列
    Next k
    
    ' 显示选择对话框并获取用户选择
    options = Split(Str, "|")               ' 按竖线分割选项字符串
    i = Val(ShowChoice(options, 100, 4))    ' 显示选择对话框，按钮宽度100，4列
    
    ' ========== 用户选择处理 ==========
    If i > 0 Then
        DeckName = options(i)   ' 获取用户选择的卡组名称
    Else
        Exit Sub   ' 用户取消选择，直接退出过程
    End If
    
    ' ========== 卡组数据加载 ==========
    ' 遍历Deck工作表查找目标卡组
    For k = 1 To UBound(Decks, 2)
        ' 检查当前列组是否匹配目标卡组名称
        If Decks(1, k) = DeckName Then
            ' ========== 卡牌数据复制 ==========
            ' 将Deck工作表中的卡组数据复制到原卡组数组
            For i = 1 To UBound(OrigDeck, 1)
                OrigDeck(i, 1) = Decks(i + 2, k)       ' 复制费用数据（第1列）
                OrigDeck(i, 2) = Decks(i + 2, k + 1)   ' 复制卡牌名称数据（第2列）
                OrigDeck(i, 3) = Decks(i + 2, k + 2)   ' 复制数量数据（第3列）
            Next i
            
            ' ========== 界面数据更新 ==========
            Call Up("OrigDeck", OrigDeck)           ' 更新原卡组界面数据
            Call Up("Class", Decks(1, k + 2))       ' 更新职业信息
            Call Up("DeckName", Decks(1, k))        ' 更新卡组名称
            
            Exit For   ' 找到并加载后退出循环
        End If
        
        k = k + 3   ' 每组卡组占用3列，跳过3列
    Next k
    
CleanUp:
    ' ========== 恢复应用程序状态 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
End Sub


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·6·5·结算·GetX.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·6·5·结算·GetX"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：计算卡牌属性值
' 参数：
'   InputX - 计算规则字符串
'   ThisCard - 当前卡牌ID
'   InputEffect - 效果字符串
' 返回：String - 计算结果和计算规则的组合字符串
' ==================================================
Function GetX(InputX As String, ThisCard As String, InputEffect As String) As String
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim result As Integer                    ' 最终计算结果
    Dim ResultX As Integer                   ' 临时计算结果
    Dim Calculate As String                  ' 计算方式标识
    Dim Xrr() As String                      ' 输入规则分割数组
    
    Dim i%, j%, k%                           ' 循环计数器
    Dim Str As String                        ' 临时字符串
    Dim Str2 As String                       ' 临时字符串2
    Dim Arr As Variant                       ' 临时数组
    Dim Brr As Variant                       ' 临时数组2
    
    Dim EffectSplit() As String              ' 效果分割数组
    Dim Enemy As String                      ' 敌方标识

    ' ========== 初始化设置 ==========
    ' 根据当前卡牌确定敌方标识
    If Left(ThisCard, 1) = "F" Then Enemy = "S" Else Enemy = "F"
    
    ' 分割输入规则
    Xrr = Split(InputX, "|")

    ' ========== 规则遍历处理 ==========
    ' 逐个解析计算规则
    For k = 0 To UBound(Xrr)
        ' 检查是否为计算方式标识
        If Xrr(k) = "HalveCount" Or Xrr(k) = "DoubleCount" Or Xrr(k) = "Difference" Or Xrr(k) = "Sum" Then
            Calculate = Xrr(k)
            k = k + 1
        End If
            
        ' ========== 基础属性计算 ==========
        ' PP相关属性
        If InStr(Xrr(k), "MaxPP") > 0 Then
            If InStr(Xrr(k), "Enemy-") > 0 Then ResultX = Down("MaxPP", Enemy & 1000) Else ResultX = Down("MaxPP", ThisCard)
        ElseIf InStr(Xrr(k), "PP") Then
            If InStr(Xrr(k), "Enemy-") > 0 Then ResultX = Down("PP", Enemy & 1000) Else ResultX = Down("PP", ThisCard)
        
        ' EP相关属性
        ElseIf InStr(Xrr(k), "SEP") > 0 Then
            If InStr(Xrr(k), "Enemy-") > 0 Then ResultX = Down("SEP", Enemy & 1000) Else ResultX = Down("SEP", ThisCard)
        ElseIf InStr(Xrr(k), "EP") Then
            If InStr(Xrr(k), "Enemy-") > 0 Then ResultX = Down("EP", Enemy & 1000) Else ResultX = Down("EP", ThisCard)
        
        ' Shadows属性
        ElseIf InStr(Xrr(k), "Shadows") > 0 Then
            If InStr(Xrr(k), "Enemy-") > 0 Then ResultX = Down("Shadows", Enemy & 1000) Else ResultX = Down("Shadows", ThisCard)
        
        ' 防御相关属性
        ElseIf InStr(Xrr(k), "LeaderDefense") > 0 Then
            If InStr(Xrr(k), "Enemy-") > 0 Then ResultX = Down("Defense", Enemy & 1000) Else ResultX = Down("Defense", ThisCard)
        ElseIf InStr(Xrr(k), "MaxDefense") > 0 Then
            If InStr(Xrr(k), "Enemy-") > 0 Then ResultX = Down("MaxDefense", Enemy & 1000) Else ResultX = Down("MaxDefense", ThisCard)
        
        ' ========== 游戏状态计算 ==========
        ' 回合数
        ElseIf Xrr(k) = "Turns" Then
            ResultX = Val(Mid(Down("Turn"), 2, 10))
        
        ' 冠名计数
        ElseIf Left(Xrr(k), Len("NamingCount")) = "NamingCount" Then
            Str = Mid(Xrr(k), Len("NamingCount") + 2, 100)
            ResultX = Down(Str, ThisCard)
        
        ' ========== 特殊效果计算 ==========
        ' 魔力增幅数
        ElseIf Xrr(k) = "Spellboost" Then
            ' 从效果字符串中提取Spellboost值
            EffectSplit = SplitEffect(DownByMatchID(ThisCard, "Effect"))
            For i = 0 To UBound(EffectSplit)
                If Left(EffectSplit(i), Len("Spellboost&")) = "Spellboost&" Then
                    ResultX = Val(Mid(EffectSplit(i), 1 + Len("Spellboost&"), 10))
                End If
            Next i
            
            ' 从卡牌效果中提取额外的Spellboost值
            EffectSplit = GetEffect(ThisCard)
            For i = 1 To UBound(EffectSplit)
                If EffectSplit(i) = "" Then Exit For
                If Left(EffectSplit(i), Len("Spellboost|")) = "Spellboost|" Then
                    Str = Mid(EffectSplit(i), 1 + Len("Spellboost|"), 100)
                    If Left(Str, 2) = "X=" Then ResultX = ResultX + Val(Mid(Str, 1 + Len("X="), 10))
                    Exit For
                End If
            Next i
        
        ' 土之印计数
        ElseIf Xrr(k) = "EarthSigil" Then
            Arr = Down("Field", ThisCard)
            For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
                ' 查找EarthSigil效果
                j = InStr(Arr(i, 5), "EarthSigil&")
                If j > 0 Then
                    ResultX = Val(Mid(Arr(i, 5), j + Len("EarthSigil&"), 10))
                    Exit For
                End If
            Next i
        
        ' ========== 融合相关计算 ==========
        ElseIf Left(Xrr(k), 5) = "Fuse&" Then
            Brr = SplitEffect(DownByMatchID(ThisCard, "Effect"))
            Str = Empty
            ' 提取所有融合效果
            For i = 0 To UBound(Brr)
                If Left(Brr(i), Len("Fuse|")) = "Fuse|" Then Str = Str & "&" & Mid(Brr(i), 1 + Len("Fuse|"), 100)
            Next i
            
            If Str <> "" Then
                Arr = Split(Str, "&")
                ' 根据融合类型计算
                For i = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                    If Xrr(k) = "Fuse&Cards" Then
                        ResultX = ResultX + 1
                    ElseIf Xrr(k) = "Fuse&Types" Then
                        ' 检查类型是否重复
                        For j = 0 To i
                            If Arr(i) = Arr(j) Then Exit For
                        Next j
                        If i = j Then ResultX = ResultX + 1
                    ElseIf Xrr(k) = "Fuse&Costs" Then
                        ResultX = ResultX + DownByID(CStr(Arr(i)), "Cost")
                    End If
                Next i
            End If
            
        ' ========== 卡牌属性计算 ==========
        ElseIf Xrr(k) = "Cost" Or _
                Xrr(k) = "Attack" Or _
                Xrr(k) = "Defense" Or _
                Xrr(k) = "Countdown" Or _
                Xrr(k) = "OrigCost" Or _
                Xrr(k) = "OrigAttack" Or _
                Xrr(k) = "OrigDefense" Or _
                Xrr(k) = "OrigCountdown" Then
            k = k + 1
            Arr = GetGoal(Xrr(k), ThisCard, InputEffect)
            Xrr(k) = "Goal&" & PreviousGoal
            ' 累加所有目标的属性值
            For i = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                ResultX = ResultX + GetModel(Xrr(k - 1), DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(i)), "ID"), DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(i)), "Effect"))
            Next i
        
        ' ========== 伤害计算 ==========
        ElseIf Xrr(k) = "Damaged" Then
            k = k + 1
            Arr = GetGoal(Xrr(k), ThisCard, InputEffect)
            Xrr(k) = "Goal&" & PreviousGoal
            ' 累加所有目标的伤害值
            For i = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                Str = DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(i)), "Effect")
                j = InStr(Str, "Damaged&")
                If j > 0 Then ResultX = ResultX + Val(Mid(Str, j + Len("Damaged&"), 10))
            Next i
                
        ' ========== 计数计算 ==========
        ' 张数/种类数
        ElseIf Xrr(k) = "Cards" Or Xrr(k) = "Types" Then
            k = k + 1
            Arr = GetGoal(Xrr(k), ThisCard, InputEffect)
            Xrr(k) = "Goal&" & PreviousGoal
            For i = 1 To UBound(Arr)
                If Xrr(k - 1) = "Cards" Then
                    ResultX = ResultX + 1
                ElseIf Xrr(k - 1) = "Types" Then
                    ' 检查类型是否重复
                    For j = 0 To i
                        If DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(i)), "ID") = DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "ID") Then Exit For
                    Next j
                    If i = j Then ResultX = ResultX + 1
                End If
            Next i
        
        ' ========== 回合统计计算 ==========
        ElseIf InStr(Xrr(k), "ThisTurn") > 0 Or InStr(Xrr(k), "ThisMatch") > 0 Then
            Brr = Split(Xrr(k), "-")
            Arr = Down("Steps")
            Str = Down("Turn")
            
            ' 筛选符合条件的步骤记录
            For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
                If Arr(i, 2) <> Brr(1) Then Arr(i, 1) = Empty
                If Arr(i, 1) <> Str And Brr(0) = "ThisTurn" Then Arr(i, 1) = Empty
            Next i
            Arr = NonEmptyArray(Arr)
            
            ' 筛选目标匹配的记录
            k = k + 1
            Call GetGoal("AnyArea-" & Xrr(k), ThisCard, InputEffect, Xrr(k))
            Xrr(k) = "Goal"
            For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
                If InStr(PreviousGoal, Arr(i, 3)) = 0 Then
                    Arr(i, 1) = Empty
                Else
                    Xrr(k) = Xrr(k) & "&" & Arr(i, 3)
                End If
            Next i
            Arr = NonEmptyArray(Arr)
            
            ' 计算次数/种类数
            If Brr(2) = Types Then
                For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
                    For j = 1 To i
                        If DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(i)), "ID") = DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j)), "ID") Then Exit For
                    Next j
                    If i < j Then Arr(i, 1) = Empty
                Next i
                Arr = NonEmptyArray(Arr)
            End If
            If Arr(1, 1) <> "" Then result = UBound(Arr, 1) Else result = 0
            
        ' ========== 直接数值 ==========
        ElseIf Val(Xrr(k)) > 0 Then
            result = Val(Xrr(k))
        End If
        
        ' ========== 最终计算处理 ==========
        If ResultX > 0 Then
            If Calculate = "HalveCount" Then
                result = -Int(-(ResultX / 2))
            ElseIf Calculate = "DoubleCount" Then
                result = ResultX * 2
            ElseIf Calculate = "Difference" Then
                If result = 0 Then result = ResultX Else result = Abs(result - ResultX)
            ElseIf Calculate = "Sum" Then
                If result = 0 Then result = ResultX Else result = result + ResultX
            Else
                result = ResultX
            End If
            ResultX = 0
        End If
    Next k
    
    ' ========== 返回结果 ==========
    GetX = result & "=" & Join(Xrr, "|")
End Function



--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·6·6·结算·GetAction.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·6·6·结算·GetAction"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：获取符合条件的动作效果
' 参数：
'   InputAction - 输入的动作类型
'   ThisCard - 当前卡牌ID
'   InputValue - 是否只处理当前卡牌（可选，默认False）
' ==================================================
Function GetAction(InputAction As String, ThisCard As String, Optional InputValue As Variant = "")
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i%, j%, k%                          ' 循环计数器
    Dim Arr As Variant                      ' 临时数组
    Dim Flag As Boolean                     ' 标志位
    
    Dim Effectrr() As String                ' 效果数组
    Dim EffectSplit() As String             ' 分割后的效果数组
    Dim TimePointSplit() As String          ' 时间点分割数组
    
    Dim MatchID As String                   ' 匹配的卡牌ID
    Dim AllGoal As Variant                  ' 所有目标数组
    Dim Area As String                      ' 区域信息

    ' ========== 目标筛选处理 ==========
    ' 获取所有数据作为初始目标
    AllGoal = Down("Data")
    
    ' 根据InputValue参数决定处理范围
    If InputValue = True Then
        ' 只处理当前卡牌
        ReDim AllGoal(1 To 1, 1 To UBound(AllGoal))
        AllGoal(1, 1) = ThisCard
    Else
        ' 处理符合条件的其他卡牌
        For i = 1 To UBound(AllGoal, 1)
            ' 排除当前卡牌
            If AllGoal(i, 1) = ThisCard Then
                AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
            ' 检查场地区域的条件效果
            ElseIf (InStr(AllGoal(i, 5), "When||") > 0 Or InStr(AllGoal(i, 5), "{When|") > 0) And _
                (AllGoal(i, 4) = "Field" Or AllGoal(i, 4) = "Crest" Or AllGoal(i, 4) = "Faith" Or AllGoal(i, 4) = "Leader") Then
                ' 保留符合条件的场地区域卡牌
            ' 检查手牌区域的条件效果
            ElseIf (InStr(AllGoal(i, 5), "ActivateInHand-When||") > 0 Or InStr(AllGoal(i, 5), "{ActivateInHand-When|") > 0) And _
                AllGoal(i, 4) = "Hand" Then
                ' 保留符合条件的手牌区域卡牌
            ' 检查牌组区域的条件效果
            ElseIf (InStr(AllGoal(i, 5), "ActivateInDeck-When||") > 0 Or InStr(AllGoal(i, 5), "{ActivateInDeck-When|") > 0) And _
                AllGoal(i, 4) = "Deck" Then
                ' 保留符合条件的牌组区域卡牌
            Else
                ' 其他情况排除该卡牌
                AllGoal(i, 1) = Empty
            End If
        Next i
        
        ' 过滤掉空元素，只保留有效目标
        AllGoal = NonEmptyArray(AllGoal)
        
        ' 更新步骤信息
        Flag = True
        If InputAction = "Play" Then Flag = False
        If InputAction = "Evolve" And InputValue <> "" Then Flag = False
        If InputAction = "SuperEvolve" And InputValue <> "" Then Flag = False
        If InputAction = "Engage" Then Flag = False
        
        If Flag Then Call UpSteps(InputAction, ThisCard, CStr(InputValue))
    End If

    ' ========== 效果遍历处理 ==========
    ' 遍历所有目标卡牌
    For k = 1 To UBound(AllGoal, 1)
        MatchID = AllGoal(k, 1)
        Effectrr = GetEffect(MatchID)
        
        ' 遍历当前卡牌的所有效果
        For i = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
            If Effectrr(i) <> "" Then
                EffectSplit = Split(Effectrr(i), "||")
                
                ' 检查是否为When类型效果（排除Turn相关）
                If InStr(EffectSplit(0), "When") > 0 And InStr(EffectSplit(0), "Turn-") = 0 Then
                    TimePointSplit = Split(EffectSplit(0), "|")
                    
                    ' ========== 动作匹配检查 ==========
                    Flag = False
                    Area = DownByMatchID(MatchID, "Area")
                    
                    ' 检查动作类型是否匹配
                    If InputAction = TimePointSplit(2) Then
                        ' ========== 目标匹配检查 ==========
                        ' 获取目标并检查是否包含当前卡牌
                        If InputAction = "EnterField" Then
                            Call GetGoal("Field-" & TimePointSplit(1), MatchID)
                        Else
                            Call GetGoal("AnyArea-" & TimePointSplit(1), MatchID)
                        End If
                        
                        If InStr(PreviousGoal, ThisCard) > 0 Then
                            ' 更新时间点信息为目标卡牌
                            TimePointSplit(1) = "Goal&" & ThisCard
                            EffectSplit(0) = Join(TimePointSplit, "|")
                            Effectrr(i) = Join(EffectSplit, "||")
                            
                            ' 处理时间点效果
                            Call GetTimePoint(Effectrr(i), MatchID)
                        End If
                        
                        Arr = PreviousGoal
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        Next i
    Next k
End Function



--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·4·6·辅助·GetEffect.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·4·6·辅助·GetEffect"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：获取卡牌效果数组
' 参数：MatchID - 匹配ID值
' 返回：String() - 效果字符串数组
' 说明：从Data区域和卡牌数据中提取所有效果，包括局内效果和局外效果
' ==================================================
Function GetEffect(MatchID As String) As String()
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim Effectrr() As String    ' 效果临时数组
    Dim Resultrr() As String    ' 结果数组
    Dim i As Integer, j As Integer, k As Integer, m As Integer   ' 循环计数器和位置索引
    Dim ID As String            ' 卡牌ID
    Dim Effect As String        ' 效果字符串
    Dim Str As String        ' 效果字符串
    Dim Flag As Boolean         ' 标记是否在效果块内
    
    ' ========== 基础数据获取 ==========
    ' 根据MatchID获取卡牌ID和效果字符串
    ID = DownByMatchID(MatchID, "ID")
    Str = DownByMatchID(MatchID, "Effect")
    Effect = Str
    If InStr(Effect, "Remove|All") > 0 Then
        ReDim Resultrr(1 To 1)
        GetEffect = Resultrr
        Exit Function
    End If

    ' 初始化效果数组（最多20个效果）
    ReDim Effectrr(1 To 20)
    Flag = False
    k = 0   ' 效果计数器
    j = 0   ' 嵌套层数计数器
    
    
    ' ========== 局外效果提取 ==========
    ' 从卡牌数据的Effect1到Effect9字段获取额外效果
    For i = 1 To 9
        Effect = DownByID(ID, "Effect" & i)   ' 获取Effect1到Effect9
        If Effect = "" Then
            'Exit For
        Else
            k = k + 1
            Effectrr(k) = Effect   ' 添加到效果数组
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 局内效果提取 ==========
    ' 遍历效果字符串，解析嵌套的效果结构
    Effect = Str
    For i = 1 To Len(Effect)
        Select Case Mid(Effect, i, 1)
            Case "{"
                j = j + 1   ' 进入嵌套层
                If j = 1 Then
                    m = i   ' 记录最外层{的起始位置
                    Flag = True   ' 标记进入效果块
                End If
                
            Case "}"
                If j > 0 Then
                    j = j - 1   ' 退出嵌套层
                    If j = 0 And Flag Then
                        ' 提取最外层{}内的内容（不包含{}本身）
                        k = k + 1
                        Effectrr(k) = Mid(Effect, m + 1, i - m - 1)
                        Flag = False   ' 标记退出效果块
                    End If
                End If
        End Select
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 结果数组构建 ==========
    ' 确保至少有一个元素
    If k = 0 Then k = 1
    
    ' 创建最终结果数组
    ReDim Resultrr(1 To k)
    
    ' 复制有效效果到结果数组
    For i = 1 To UBound(Resultrr)
        Resultrr(i) = Effectrr(i)
    Next i
    
    GetEffect = Resultrr   ' 返回效果数组
End Function


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·7·4·对局·MatchStrike.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·7·4·对局·MatchStrike"
' ==================================================
' 过程功能：处理从者攻击/护符交战的入口函数
' 说明：根据用户选择的目标执行攻击或交战操作
' 包含回合检查、攻击权限判断、目标选择等逻辑
' ==================================================
Function MatchStrike()
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i%, j%, k%
    Dim Str As String
    Dim Arr As Variant
    Dim MatchID As String, ID As String, CardType As String, Turn As String, Effect As String
    Dim IsStrike As Integer
    Dim Effectrr() As String
    Dim EffectSplit() As String
    Dim options() As String
    
    Dim CantStrike As String
    
    ' ========== 战场信息获取 ==========
    ' 下载战场信息
    Arr = Down("Field", Left(Down("FirstMove"), 1) & 1000)
    ' 获取MatchID，如果为空则刷新并退出
    If Selection.Column - 2 > UBound(Arr, 1) Or Arr(1, 1) = "" Then
        Call UIRefresh
        GoTo CleanUp
    End If
    MatchID = Arr(Selection.Column - 2, 1)
    
    ' ========== 卡牌信息获取 ==========
    ' 获取ID和卡牌类型
    ID = DownByMatchID(MatchID, "ID")
    CardType = DownByID(ID, "Type")
    
    ' ========== 从者攻击处理 ==========
    If CardType = "Follower" Then
        ' 获取回合和效果信息
        Turn = DownByMatchID(MatchID, "Turn")
        Effect = DownByMatchID(MatchID, "Effect")
        Effectrr = GetEffect(MatchID)
        
        ' ========== 攻击权限判断 ==========
        ' 判断自己是否可攻击
        IsStrike = 0
        ' 不是登场回合则可以攻击主战者
        If Turn <> Down("Turn") Then IsStrike = 2
        
        ' 遍历效果找突进、疾驰、无法攻击
        For i = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
            If Effectrr(i) = "Rush" Then
                If IsStrike = 0 Then IsStrike = 1
            ElseIf Effectrr(i) = "Storm" Then
                IsStrike = 2
            ElseIf Left(Effectrr(i), Len("CantStrike|")) = "CantStrike|" Then
                CantStrike = Mid(Effectrr(i), 1 + Len("CantStrike|"), 100)
                Call GetGoal("Enemy-Field-" & CantStrike, MatchID)
                CantStrike = PreviousGoal
            End If
        Next i
        
        ' ========== 攻击次数检查 ==========
        If IsStrike > 0 Then
            ' 遍历效果找风怒（可攻击次数）
            k = 1
            For i = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
                If Left(Effectrr(i), Len("CanAttackTimes&")) = "CanAttackTimes&" Then
                    j = Val(Mid(Effectrr(i), 1 + Len("CanAttackTimes&"), 10))
                    If j > k Then k = j
                End If
            Next i
            j = k
            If j = 0 Then j = 1
            
            ' 遍历状态找已攻击次数
            i = InStr(Effect, "Striked&")
            If i > 0 Then i = Val(Mid(Effect, i + Len("Striked&"), 10))
            If j <= i Then IsStrike = 0
        End If
        
        ' 不能攻击则直接退出
        If IsStrike = 0 Then GoTo CleanUp
        
        ' ========== 敌方战场分析 ==========
        ' 下载敌方战场信息
        If Left(MatchID, 1) = "F" Then Str = "S" Else Str = "F"
        Arr = Down("Field", Str & 1000)
        
        ' ========== 敌方状态分析 ==========
        ' 遍历查找护符、守护、潜行、威慑等状态
        Flag = False
        For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
            If Arr(i, 1) = 0 Then
                Arr(i, 5) = 0
            ElseIf DownByID(CStr(Arr(i, 2)), "Type") = "Amulet" Then
                Arr(i, 5) = 0
            Else
                EffectSplit = SplitEffect(Arr(i, 5))
                Arr(i, 5) = 2
                For j = 0 To UBound(EffectSplit)
                    If EffectSplit(j) = "Ward" Or InStr(EffectSplit(j), "{Ward}") Then
                        If Arr(i, 5) > 1 Then Arr(i, 5) = 3
                    ElseIf EffectSplit(j) = "Remove|Ward" Then
                        If Arr(i, 5) = 3 Then Arr(i, 5) = 2
                    ElseIf EffectSplit(j) = "Ambush" Or InStr(EffectSplit(j), "{Ambush}") Or _
                            EffectSplit(j) = "Intimidate" Or InStr(EffectSplit(j), "{Intimidate}") Then
                        Arr(i, 5) = 1
                    ElseIf EffectSplit(j) = "Remove|Ambush" Or _
                            EffectSplit(j) = "Remove|Intimidate" Then
                        If Arr(i, 5) = 1 Then Arr(i, 5) = 2
                    End If
                Next j
                If Arr(i, 5) = 3 Then Flag = True
            End If
        Next i
        
        ' ========== 目标选择界面构建 ==========
        Str = Translate("Strike")
        For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
            If Flag And Arr(i, 5) = 3 And InStr(CantStrike, Arr(i, 1)) = 0 Then
                Str = Str & "|" & DownByID(CStr(Arr(i, 2)), "Name")
            ElseIf Not Flag And Arr(i, 5) = 2 And InStr(CantStrike, Arr(i, 1)) = 0 Then
                Str = Str & "|" & DownByID(CStr(Arr(i, 2)), "Name")
            Else
                Arr(i, 1) = Empty
            End If
        Next i
        Arr = NonEmptyArray(Arr)
        
        ' 加入主战者选项
        If IsStrike = 2 And Not Flag And InStr(CantStrike, "1000") = 0 Then Str = Str & "|" & Translate("Leader")
        
        ' ========== 用户选择处理 ==========
        ' 弹出选项供用户选择
        i = 0
        options = Split(Str, "|")
        If UBound(options) > 0 Then i = Val(ShowChoice(options, 150, 6))
        If i = 0 Then GoTo CleanUp
        
        ' ========== 攻击执行 ==========
        StrikeMatchID = MatchID
        ' 如果攻击了主战者
        If options(i) = Translate("Leader") Then
            If Left(MatchID, 1) = "F" Then Str = "S" & 1000 Else Str = "F" & 1000
            'ClashMatchID = Empty
        Else
            Str = Arr(i, 1)
            'ClashMatchID = Str
        End If
        Call StrikeFunction(MatchID, Str)
    Else
        ' ========== 护符交战处理 ==========
        Arr = EngageFunction(MatchID)
        If Not Arr Then GoTo CleanUp
    End If

    ' ========== 界面刷新 ==========
    Call UIRefresh
    Call UpStepsArea
    
CleanUp:
End Function

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：执行具体的攻击交战逻辑
' 参数：MatchID - 攻击方MatchID, InputClash - 被攻击方MatchID
' 说明：处理攻击时的各种效果触发、伤害计算、状态更新等
' 包括屏障、毁灭、虹吸等特殊效果的实现
' ==================================================
Function StrikeFunction(MatchID As String, InputClash As String)
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i%, j%, k%
    Dim Damage%
    Dim StrikeAttack%
    Dim StrikeDefense%
    Dim StrikeID As String
    Dim StrikeEffect As String
    Dim StrikeEffectrr() As String
    Dim StrikeEffectSplit() As String
    Dim StrikeArea As String
    Dim ClashAttack%
    Dim ClashDefense%
    Dim ClashID As String
    Dim ClashEffect As String
    Dim ClashEffectrr() As String
    Dim ClashEffectSplit() As String
    Dim ClashArea As String
    Dim KeywordStrikeFlag As Boolean
    Dim KeywordClashFlagA As Boolean
    Dim KeywordClashFlagB As Boolean
    
    ' ========== 初始化设置 ==========
    ' 清空能力队列
    QueueAbility = Empty
    QueueMatchID = Empty
    If Mid(InputClash, 2, 10) <> "1000" Then ClashMatchID = InputClash Else ClashMatchID = Empty
    
    ' ========== 数据下载 ==========
    ' 下载攻击方和被攻击方的效果信息
    StrikeEffect = DownByMatchID(MatchID, "Effect")
    StrikeEffectSplit = SplitEffect(StrikeEffect)
    StrikeEffectrr = GetEffect(MatchID)
    StrikeID = DownByMatchID(MatchID, "ID")
    
    ClashEffect = DownByMatchID(InputClash, "Effect")
    ClashEffectSplit = SplitEffect(ClashEffect)
    ClashEffectrr = GetEffect(InputClash)
    ClashID = DownByMatchID(InputClash, "ID")
    
    ' ========== 攻击次数记录 ==========
    ' 更新已攻击次数
    For i = 0 To UBound(StrikeEffectSplit)
        If Left(StrikeEffectSplit(i), Len("Striked&")) = "Striked&" Then Exit For
    Next i
    If i <= UBound(StrikeEffectSplit) Then
        j = Val(Mid(StrikeEffectSplit(i), 1 + Len("Striked&"), 10))
        StrikeEffectSplit(i) = "Striked&" & j + 1
        StrikeEffect = Join(StrikeEffectSplit, "||") & "||"
    Else
        StrikeEffect = StrikeEffect & "Striked&" & 1 & "||"
        StrikeEffectSplit = SplitEffect(StrikeEffect)
    End If
    Call UpByMatchID(MatchID, "Effect", StrikeEffect)
    
    ' ========== 关键字效果触发 ==========
    ' 攻击时关键字效果处理
    For i = 1 To UBound(StrikeEffectrr)
        If Left(StrikeEffectrr(i), Len("Strike")) = "Strike" And InStr(StrikeEffect, "Remove|Strike") = 0 Then
            Call GetTimePoint(StrikeEffectrr(i), MatchID)
            KeywordStrikeFlag = True
        ElseIf Mid(InputClash, 2, 10) <> "1000" And Left(StrikeEffectrr(i), Len("Clash")) = "Clash" And InStr(StrikeEffect, "Remove|Clash") = 0 Then
            Call GetTimePoint(StrikeEffectrr(i), MatchID)
            KeywordClashFlagA = True
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' 交战时关键字效果处理
    If Mid(InputClash, 2, 10) <> "1000" Then
        For i = 1 To UBound(ClashEffectrr)
            If Left(ClashEffectrr(i), Len("Clash")) = "Clash" And InStr(ClashEffect, "Remove|Clash") = 0 Then
                Call GetTimePoint(ClashEffectrr(i), InputClash)
                KeywordClashFlagB = True
            End If
        Next i
    End If
    
    ' ========== 状态移除 ==========
    ' 攻击后失去潜行状态
    For i = 0 To UBound(StrikeEffectSplit)
        If StrikeEffectSplit(i) = "Ambush" Or InStr(StrikeEffectSplit(i), "{Ambush}") > 0 Then
            StrikeEffectSplit(i) = "\"
            StrikeEffect = Join(StrikeEffectSplit, "||") & "||"
            StrikeEffect = Replace(StrikeEffect, "\||", "")
            Call UpByMatchID(MatchID, "Effect", StrikeEffect)
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 能力触发处理 ==========
    ' 其他卡牌响应攻击和交战事件
    Call GetAction("Strike", MatchID, True)
    If Mid(InputClash, 2, 10) <> "1000" Then
        Call GetAction("Clash", MatchID, True)
        Call GetAction("Clash", InputClash, True)
    End If
    Call GetAction("Strike", MatchID, DownByID(StrikeID, "Name"))
    If Mid(InputClash, 2, 10) <> "1000" Then
        Call GetAction("Clash", MatchID, DownByID(StrikeID, "Name"))
        Call GetAction("Clash", InputClash, DownByID(ClashID, "Name"))
    End If
    If KeywordStrikeFlag Then Call GetAction("Keyword&Strike", MatchID)
    If KeywordClashFlagA Then Call GetAction("Keyword&Clash", MatchID)
    If KeywordClashFlagB Then Call GetAction("Keyword&Clash", InputClash)
    
    ' 清空能力队列
    Call EraseQueue
    
    ' ========== 数据重新下载 ==========
    ' 重新下载更新后的效果信息
    StrikeEffect = DownByMatchID(MatchID, "Effect")
    StrikeEffectSplit = SplitEffect(StrikeEffect)
    StrikeEffectrr = GetEffect(MatchID)
    
    ClashEffect = DownByMatchID(InputClash, "Effect")
    ClashEffectSplit = SplitEffect(ClashEffect)
    ClashEffectrr = GetEffect(InputClash)
    
    ' ========== 状态验证 ==========
    ' 如果攻击方或被攻击方不在场，则直接退出
    StrikeArea = DownByMatchID(MatchID, "Area")
    ClashArea = DownByMatchID(InputClash, "Area")
    If StrikeArea <> "Field" Or (ClashArea <> "Field" And ClashArea <> "Leader") Then
        Call UIRefresh
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' ========== 主战者攻击处理 ==========
    ' 如果对主战者进行攻击
    If Mid(InputClash, 2, 10) = "1000" Then
        Flag = False
        For i = 0 To UBound(ClashEffectSplit)
            If InStr(ClashEffectSplit(i), "{Barrier}") > 0 Then
                ' 屏障效果处理
                Flag = True
                ClashEffectSplit(i) = "\"
                ClashEffect = Join(ClashEffectSplit, "||") & "||"
                ClashEffect = Replace(ClashEffect, "\||", "")
                Call UpByMatchID(InputClash, "Effect", ClashEffect)
            ElseIf ClashEffectSplit(i) = "Remove|Barrier" Then
                Flag = False
                Exit For
            End If
        Next i
        If Flag Then
            'Call GetAction("LostBarrier", InputClash, 1)
        Else
            ' 计算伤害并更新主战者防御力
            StrikeAttack = GetModel("Attack", StrikeID, StrikeEffect)
            Damage = StrikeAttack
            For i = 1 To UBound(ClashEffectrr)
                If Left(ClashEffectrr(i), Len("CantTakeMoreDamage&")) = "CantTakeMoreDamage&" Then
                    j = Val(Mid(ClashEffectrr(i), 1 + Len("CantTakeMoreDamage&"), 10))
                    If Damage > j Then Damage = j
                ElseIf Left(ClashEffectrr(i), Len("TakeDamage&")) = "TakeDamage&" Then
                    j = Val(Mid(ClashEffectrr(i), 1 + Len("TakeDamage&"), 10))
                    Damage = Damage + j
                End If
            Next i
            If Damage < 0 Then Damage = 0
            
            Call Up("Defense", Down("Defense", InputClash) - Damage, InputClash)
            Call Up("BloodPool", Down("BloodPool", InputClash) + Damage, InputClash) '血池+
        End If
        
    ' ========== 从者攻击处理 ==========
    Else
        StrikeAttack = GetModel("Attack", StrikeID, StrikeEffect)
        ClashAttack = GetModel("Attack", ClashID, ClashEffect)
        
        ' ========== 攻击方伤害处理 ==========
        Flag = False
        For i = 0 To UBound(StrikeEffectSplit)
            If StrikeEffectSplit(i) = "Barrier" Or InStr(StrikeEffectSplit(i), "{Barrier}") > 0 Then
                ' 屏障效果处理
                Flag = True
                StrikeEffectSplit(i) = "\"
                StrikeEffect = Join(StrikeEffectSplit, "||") & "||"
                StrikeEffect = Replace(StrikeEffect, "\||", "")
                Call UpByMatchID(MatchID, "Effect", StrikeEffect)
            ElseIf StrikeEffectSplit(i) = "Remove|Barrier" Then
                Flag = False
                Exit For
            End If
        Next i
        
        If Flag Then
            ' 屏障被打破时的能力触发
            'Call GetAction("LostBarrier", MatchID, True)
            'Call GetAction("LostBarrier", MatchID, DownByID(ClashID, "Name"))
        ElseIf InStr(StrikeEffect, "SuperEvolved||") > 0 Then
            ' 超进化处理（注释标明不严谨实现）
        ElseIf ClashAttack > 0 Then
            '计算伤害
            Damage = ClashAttack
            For i = 1 To UBound(ClashEffectrr)
                If Left(ClashEffectrr(i), Len("DoDamage&")) = "DoDamage&" Then
                    j = Val(Mid(ClashEffectrr(i), 1 + Len("DoDamage&"), 10))
                    Damage = Damage + j
                End If
            Next i
            For i = 1 To UBound(StrikeEffectrr)
                If Left(StrikeEffectrr(i), Len("TakeDamage&")) = "TakeDamage&" Then
                    j = Val(Mid(StrikeEffectrr(i), 1 + Len("TakeDamage&"), 10))
                    Damage = Damage + j
                End If
            Next i
            For i = 1 To UBound(StrikeEffectrr)
                If Left(StrikeEffectrr(i), Len("CantTakeMoreDamage&")) = "CantTakeMoreDamage&" Then
                    j = Val(Mid(StrikeEffectrr(i), 1 + Len("CantTakeMoreDamage&"), 10))
                    If Damage > j Then Damage = j
                End If
            Next i
            If Damage < 0 Then Damage = 0
            
            ' 受伤记录处理
            For i = 0 To UBound(StrikeEffectSplit)
                If Left(StrikeEffectSplit(i), Len("Damaged&")) = "Damaged&" Then Exit For
            Next i
            If i <= UBound(StrikeEffectSplit) Then
                j = Val(Mid(StrikeEffectSplit(i), 1 + Len("Damaged&"), 10))
                StrikeEffectSplit(i) = "Damaged&" & j + Damage
                StrikeEffect = Join(StrikeEffectSplit, "||") & "||"
            Else
                StrikeEffect = StrikeEffect & "Damaged&" & Damage & "||"
                StrikeEffectSplit = SplitEffect(StrikeEffect)
            End If
            Call UpByMatchID(MatchID, "Effect", StrikeEffect)
            
            ' 检查是否被破坏
            StrikeDefense = GetModel("Defense", StrikeID, StrikeEffect)
            If StrikeDefense <= 0 Then Call DestroyFunction(MatchID)
        End If
        
        ' 毁灭效果检查
        Flag = False
        For i = 0 To UBound(ClashEffectSplit)
            If ClashEffectSplit(i) = "Bane" Or InStr(ClashEffectSplit(i), "{Bane}") > 0 Then Flag = True
            If ClashEffectSplit(i) = "Remove|Bane" Then
                Flag = False
                Exit For
            End If
        Next i
        If Flag Then Call GetAbility("||Destroy|ThisCard", MatchID)
        
        ' 受伤未破坏时的能力触发
        If DownByMatchID(MatchID, "Area") = "Field" Then
            Call GetAction("Damaged", MatchID, True)
            Call GetAction("Damaged", MatchID, ClashAttack)
        End If
        
        ' ========== 被攻击方伤害处理 ==========
        Flag = False
        For i = 0 To UBound(ClashEffectSplit)
            If ClashEffectSplit(i) = "Barrier" Or InStr(ClashEffectSplit(i), "{Barrier}") > 0 Then
                ' 屏障效果处理
                Flag = True
                ClashEffectSplit(i) = "\"
                ClashEffect = Join(ClashEffectSplit, "||") & "||"
                ClashEffect = Replace(ClashEffect, "\||", "")
                Call UpByMatchID(InputClash, "Effect", ClashEffect)
            ElseIf ClashEffectSplit(i) = "Remove|Barrier" Then
                Flag = False
                Exit For
            End If
        Next i
        
        If Flag Then
            ' 屏障被打破时的能力触发
            'Call GetAction("LostBarrier", InputClash, True)
            'Call GetAction("LostBarrier", InputClash, DownByID(StrikeID, "Name"))
        ElseIf StrikeAttack > 0 Then
            '计算伤害
            Damage = StrikeAttack
            For i = 1 To UBound(StrikeEffectrr)
                If Left(StrikeEffectrr(i), Len("DoDamage&")) = "DoDamage&" Then
                    j = Val(Mid(StrikeEffectrr(i), 1 + Len("DoDamage&"), 10))
                    Damage = Damage + j
                End If
            Next i
            For i = 1 To UBound(ClashEffectrr)
                If Left(ClashEffectrr(i), Len("TakeDamage&")) = "TakeDamage&" Then
                    j = Val(Mid(ClashEffectrr(i), 1 + Len("TakeDamage&"), 10))
                    Damage = Damage + j
                End If
            Next i
            For i = 1 To UBound(ClashEffectrr)
                If Left(ClashEffectrr(i), Len("CantTakeMoreDamage&")) = "CantTakeMoreDamage&" Then
                    j = Val(Mid(ClashEffectrr(i), 1 + Len("CantTakeMoreDamage&"), 10))
                    If Damage > j Then Damage = j
                End If
            Next i
            If Damage < 0 Then Damage = 0
            
            ' 受伤记录处理
            For i = 0 To UBound(ClashEffectSplit)
                If Left(ClashEffectSplit(i), Len("Damaged&")) = "Damaged&" Then Exit For
            Next i
            If i <= UBound(ClashEffectSplit) Then
                j = Val(Mid(ClashEffectSplit(i), 1 + Len("Damaged&"), 10))
                ClashEffectSplit(i) = "Damaged&" & j + Damage
                ClashEffect = Join(ClashEffectSplit, "||") & "||"
            Else
                ClashEffect = ClashEffect & "Damaged&" & Damage & "||"
                ClashEffectSplit = SplitEffect(ClashEffect)
            End If
            Call UpByMatchID(InputClash, "Effect", ClashEffect)
            
            ' 检查是否被破坏
            ClashDefense = GetModel("Defense", ClashID, ClashEffect)
            If ClashDefense <= 0 Then Call DestroyFunction(InputClash)
        End If
        
        ' 毁灭效果检查
        Flag = False
        For i = 0 To UBound(StrikeEffectSplit)
            If StrikeEffectSplit(i) = "Bane" Or InStr(StrikeEffectSplit(i), "{Bane}") > 0 Then Flag = True
            If StrikeEffectSplit(i) = "Remove|Bane" Then
                Flag = False
                Exit For
            End If
        Next i
        If Flag Then Call GetAbility("||Destroy|ThisCard", InputClash)
        
        ' 受伤未破坏时的能力触发
        If DownByMatchID(InputClash, "Area") = "Field" Then
            Call GetAction("Damaged", MatchID, True)
            Call GetAction("Damaged", MatchID, StrikeAttack)
        ElseIf InStr(StrikeEffect, "SuperEvolved||") > 0 Then
            Call GetAbility("||Damage|Enemy-Leader|1", MatchID)
        End If
    End If
    
    ' ========== 虹吸效果处理 ==========
    For i = 1 To UBound(StrikeEffectrr)
        If StrikeEffectrr(i) = "Drain" And InStr(StrikeEffect, "Remove|Strike") = 0 Then
            Call GetAbility("||Restore|Allied-Leader|" & StrikeAttack, MatchID)
            Exit For
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 清理和刷新 ==========
    ' 清空能力队列
    Call EraseQueue
End Function



--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·8·2·对局·EngageFunction.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·8·2·对局·EngageFunction"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：处理护符启动（Engage）效果
' 参数：MatchID - 要启动的护符MatchID
' 说明：检查启动条件、消耗能量点、触发启动效果
' 包含启动费用检查、效果触发和状态更新等逻辑
' ==================================================
Function EngageFunction(MatchID As String) As Boolean
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i%, j%, k%
    Dim Arr As Variant
    Dim Flag As Boolean
    Dim Cost As Integer, AlliedPP As Integer, Effect As String
    Dim Effectrr() As String
    Dim EffectSplit() As String
    EngageFunction = True
    
    ' ========== 初始化设置 ==========
    ' 清空能力队列
    QueueAbility = Empty
    QueueMatchID = Empty
    
    ' ========== 启动状态检查 ==========
    ' 检查护符是否已启动，已启动则直接退出
    Effect = DownByMatchID(MatchID, "Effect")
    EffectSplit = SplitEffect(Effect)
    For i = 0 To UBound(EffectSplit)
        If EffectSplit(i) = "Engaged" Then Exit For
    Next i
    If i <= UBound(EffectSplit) Then
        EngageFunction = False
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' ========== 启动费用查找 ==========
    ' 查找启动效果的费用要求
    Cost = -1
    Effectrr = GetEffect(MatchID)
    For i = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
        If Left(Effectrr(i), Len("Engage&")) = "Engage&" Then
            Cost = Val(Mid(Effectrr(i), 1 + Len("Engage&"), 10))
            Exit For
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 启动效果验证 ==========
    ' 没有启动效果直接退出
    If Cost = -1 Then
        EngageFunction = False
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' ========== 启动费用修正 ==========
    ' 检查场地上启动费用的效应
    Arr = Down("Field", MatchID)
    For j = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
        If InStr(Arr(j, 5), "EngageCost") > 0 Then
            Brr = GetEffect(CStr(Arr(j, 1)))
            For k = 1 To UBound(Brr)
                If Left(Brr(k), Len("EngageCost&")) = "EngageCost&" Then
                    Cost = Cost + Val(Mid(Brr(k), 1 + Len("EngageCost&"), 10))
                    Exit For
                End If
            Next k
        End If
    Next j
    ' 检查纹章区域启动费用的效应
    Arr = Down("Crest", MatchID)
    For j = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
        If InStr(Arr(j, 5), "EngageCost") > 0 Then
            Brr = GetEffect(CStr(Arr(j, 1)))
            For k = 1 To UBound(Brr)
                If Left(Brr(k), Len("EngageCost&")) = "EngageCost&" Then
                    Cost = Cost + Val(Mid(Brr(k), 1 + Len("EngageCost&"), 10))
                    Exit For
                End If
            Next k
        End If
    Next j
    If Cost < 0 Then Cost = 0
    
    ' ========== 能量点检查 ==========
    ' 获取当前能量点并检查是否足够支付启动费用
    AlliedPP = Down("PP", MatchID)
    
    ' 判断费用是否足够
    If AlliedPP < Cost Then
        MsgBox "能量点不足", vbExclamation
        EngageFunction = False
        Exit Function
    End If
    Call UpSteps("Engage", MatchID, DownByID(DownByMatchID(MatchID, "ID"), "Name"))
    
    ' ========== 启动效果处理 ==========
    ' 遍历并触发启动相关效果
    Flag = True
    For i = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
        ' 处理启动效果
        If Left(Effectrr(i), Len("Engage")) = "Engage" And InStr(Effect, "Remove|Engage") = 0 Then
            Flag = GetTimePoint(Effectrr(i), MatchID)
        End If
        
        ' 如果选择未成功，立刻退出并清空队列
        If Not Flag Then
            QueueAbility = Empty
            QueueMatchID = Empty
            Call EraseSteps("Engage")
            EngageFunction = False
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 费用消耗 ==========
    ' 消耗启动所需的能量点
    Call Up("PP", AlliedPP - Cost, MatchID)
    
    ' ========== 状态标记 ==========
    ' 标记护符为已启动状态
    Effect = DownByMatchID(MatchID, "Effect")
    Effect = Effect & "Engaged||"
    Call UpByMatchID(MatchID, "Effect", Effect)
    
    ' ========== 能力触发处理 ==========
    ' 其他卡牌响应启动事件
    Call GetAction("Engage", MatchID, "")
    
    ' ========== 清理和刷新 ==========
    ' 清空能力队列
    Call EraseQueue
End Function


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·4·7·辅助·SplitEffect.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·4·7·辅助·SplitEffect"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：效果字符串分割器
' 参数：InputString - 输入的效果字符串
' 返回：String() - 分割后的字符串数组
' 说明：使用"||"作为分隔符分割字符串，但将{}内的内容视为一个整体不分割
' 支持嵌套花括号处理，保持复杂效果结构的完整性
' ==================================================
Function SplitEffect(ByVal InputString As String) As String()
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim Results() As String     ' 结果数组
    Dim i As Long, j As Long    ' 循环计数器和数组索引
    Dim InBrackets As Boolean   ' 标记是否在花括号内
    Dim CurrentPart As String   ' 当前正在构建的部分字符串
    Dim BraceLevel As Integer   ' 嵌套花括号层级计数器
    Dim CurrentChar As String   ' 当前处理的字符
    
    ' ========== 变量初始化 ==========
    ReDim Results(0)            ' 初始化为一个空元素
    InBrackets = False          ' 初始不在花括号内
    BraceLevel = 0              ' 初始嵌套层级为0
    CurrentPart = ""            ' 初始当前部分为空
    j = 0                       ' 结果数组索引初始化为0
    
    ' ========== 字符遍历处理 ==========
    ' 逐个字符遍历输入字符串
    For i = 1 To Len(InputString)
        CurrentChar = Mid(InputString, i, 1)   ' 获取当前字符
        
        ' ========== 花括号处理 ==========
        ' 处理左花括号 {
        If CurrentChar = "{" Then
            BraceLevel = BraceLevel + 1        ' 增加嵌套层级
            InBrackets = (BraceLevel > 0)      ' 更新花括号内状态
            CurrentPart = CurrentPart & CurrentChar   ' 将{添加到当前部分
            
        ' 处理右花括号 }
        ElseIf CurrentChar = "}" Then
            If BraceLevel > 0 Then
                BraceLevel = BraceLevel - 1    ' 减少嵌套层级
            End If
            InBrackets = (BraceLevel > 0)      ' 更新花括号内状态
            CurrentPart = CurrentPart & CurrentChar   ' 将}添加到当前部分
            
        ' ========== 分隔符处理 ==========
        ' 处理分隔符"||"（仅在花括号外处理）
        ElseIf Not InBrackets And CurrentChar = "|" And i < Len(InputString) Then
            ' 检查是否是双竖线分隔符"||"
            If Mid(InputString, i, 2) = "||" Then
                ' 将当前部分添加到结果数组
                If CurrentPart <> "" Then
                    ReDim Preserve Results(j)   ' 扩展数组大小
                    Results(j) = CurrentPart    ' 存储当前部分
                    j = j + 1                   ' 移动到下一个数组位置
                    CurrentPart = ""            ' 重置当前部分
                End If
                i = i + 1   ' 跳过下一个'|'字符
            Else
                ' 单个|字符，作为普通字符处理
                CurrentPart = CurrentPart & CurrentChar
            End If
            
        ' ========== 普通字符处理 ==========
        Else
            ' 将普通字符添加到当前部分
            CurrentPart = CurrentPart & CurrentChar
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 最后部分处理 ==========
    ' 处理字符串末尾的剩余内容
    If CurrentPart <> "" Then
        ReDim Preserve Results(j)   ' 扩展数组大小
        Results(j) = CurrentPart    ' 存储最后的部分
    End If
    
    ' ========== 返回结果 ==========
    SplitEffect = Results   ' 返回分割后的数组
End Function


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·8·3·对局·DestroyFunction.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·8·3·对局·DestroyFunction"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：处理卡牌破坏逻辑
' 参数：MatchID - 要被破坏的卡牌MatchID
' 说明：处理卡牌被破坏时的各种效果和状态变更
' 包括不可破坏效果检查、区域变更、谢幕曲触发等
' ==================================================
Function DestroyFunction(MatchID As String)
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i%, j%
    Dim Str As String
    Dim Area As String
    Dim Effect As String
    Dim Effectrr() As String
    Dim EffectSplit() As String
    Dim LastWordsFlag As Boolean
    
    ' ========== 效果信息获取 ==========
    ' 下载卡牌的当前效果信息
    Effect = DownByMatchID(MatchID, "Effect")
    Effectrr = GetEffect(MatchID)
    EffectSplit = SplitEffect(Effect)
    
    ' ========== 区域状态检查 ==========
    ' 如果本来就不在战场或纹章区，则空发（不执行破坏）
    Area = DownByMatchID(MatchID, "Area")
    If Area <> "Field" And Area <> "Crest" Then Exit Function
    
    ' ========== 不可破坏效果检查 ==========
    ' 检查不会被破坏的效果和特殊处理
    For i = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
        If Effectrr(i) = "CantBeDestroyed" Then
            ' 检查防御力或倒计时，如果大于0则不能被破坏
            j = GetModel("Defense", DownByMatchID(MatchID, "ID"), Effect)
            If j > 0 Then Exit Function
            j = GetModel("Countdown", DownByMatchID(MatchID, "ID"), Effect)
            If j > 0 Then Exit Function
        ElseIf Effectrr(i) = "LeaveBanish" Then
            ' 放逐效果处理：将卡牌区域改为放逐并触发相关能力
            Call UpByMatchID(MatchID, "Area", "Banish")
            Call GetAction("Banish", MatchID, True)
            Call GetAction("Banish", MatchID, DownByID(DownByMatchID(MatchID, "ID"), "Name"))
            Call GetAction("LeaveField", MatchID, True)
            Call GetAction("LeaveField", MatchID)
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 特殊状态检查 ==========
    ' 检查土之印和超进化状态
    Str = Down("Turn")
    For i = 0 To UBound(EffectSplit)
        If Left(EffectSplit(i), Len("EarthSigil&")) = "EarthSigil&" Then
            ' 土之印状态：不能被破坏
            Exit Function
        ElseIf EffectSplit(i) = "SuperEvolved" And Left(Str, 1) = Left(MatchID, 1) Then
            ' 超进化状态：检查防御力，如果大于0则不能被破坏
            j = GetModel("Defense", DownByMatchID(MatchID, "ID"), Effect)
            If j > 0 Then Exit Function
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 区域变更处理 ==========
    ' 将卡牌区域更改为破坏区
    Call UpByMatchID(MatchID, "Area", "Destroy")
    'Call UpByMatchID(MatchID, "Effect", "")
    
    ' ========== 墓场计数更新 ==========
    ' 如果是从战场破坏，则墓场计数+1
    If Area = "Field" Then Call Up("Shadows", Down("Shadows", MatchID) + 1, MatchID)
    
    ' ========== 谢幕曲效果处理 ==========
    ' 触发卡牌的谢幕曲效果
    LastWordsFlag = False
    For i = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
        If Left(Effectrr(i), Len("LastWords")) = "LastWords" And InStr(Effect, "Remove|LastWords") = 0 Then
            Call GetTimePoint(Effectrr(i), MatchID)
            LastWordsFlag = True
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 离开战场能力触发 ==========
    ' 触发离开战场时的能力
    Call GetAction("LeaveField", MatchID, True)
    
    ' ========== 其他能力触发处理 ==========
    ' 其他卡牌响应破坏事件和离开战场事件
    Call GetAction("Destroy", MatchID, DownByID(DownByMatchID(MatchID, "ID"), "Name"))
    Call GetAction("LeaveField", MatchID)
    If LastWordsFlag Then Call GetAction("Keyword&LastWords", MatchID)
End Function


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·7·5·对局·MatchEvolve.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·7·5·对局·MatchEvolve"
' ==================================================
' 过程功能：处理从者进化操作的入口函数
' 说明：检查进化条件并执行进化操作
' 包含回合检查、进化点检查、进化状态验证等逻辑
' ==================================================
Sub MatchEvolve()
    ' ========== 优化设置 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i%
    Dim Arr As Variant
    Dim Turn As String
    Dim MatchID As String
    
    ' ========== 回合权限检查 ==========
    ' 不是自己的回合直接退出
    Turn = Down("Turn")
    If Left(Turn, 1) <> Left(Down("FirstMove"), 1) Then GoTo CleanUp
    If Left(Turn, 1) = "F" Then
        If Val(Mid(Turn, 2, 10)) < 5 Then GoTo CleanUp
    Else
        If Val(Mid(Turn, 2, 10)) < 4 Then GoTo CleanUp
    End If
    
    ' ========== 战场选择检查 ==========
    ' 是否选择了战场
    Arr = IsSelected("AlliedField")
    If IsEmpty(Arr) Then GoTo CleanUp
    ' ========== 进化状态检查 ==========
    ' 是否已进化（检查按钮文本状态）
    If Sheets("Main").Shapes("EvolveBtn").TextFrame.Characters.Text = Translate("Evolved") Then GoTo CleanUp
    If Sheets("Main").Shapes("SuperEvolveBtn").TextFrame.Characters.Text = Translate("SuperEvolved") Then GoTo CleanUp
    
    ' ========== 战场信息获取 ==========
    ' 下载战场信息
    Arr = Down("Field", Left(Down("FirstMove"), 1) & 1000)
    ' 获取MatchID，如果为空则刷新并退出
    If Selection.Column - 2 > UBound(Arr, 1) Or Arr(1, 1) = "" Then
        Call UIRefresh
        GoTo CleanUp
    End If
    MatchID = Arr(Selection.Column - 2, 1)
    
    ' ========== 随从检查 ==========
    If DownByID(DownByMatchID(MatchID, "ID"), "Type") <> "Follower" Then GoTo CleanUp
    
    ' ========== 已进化状态检查 ==========
    ' 检查是否已经进化（注释标明不严谨实现）
    If InStr(Arr(Selection.Column - 2, 5), "Evolved||") > 0 Then GoTo CleanUp
    
    ' ========== 进化点检查 ==========
    ' 是否有EP（进化点）
    Arr = GetEffect(MatchID)
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr)
        If Arr(i) = "NoEP" Then Exit For
    Next i
    If i > UBound(Arr) Then
        If Val(Down("AlliedEP")) <= 0 Then GoTo CleanUp
        Arr = EvolveFunction(MatchID)
    Else
        Arr = EvolveFunction(MatchID, True)
    End If
    
    ' ========== 执行进化 ==========
    'Arr = EvolveFunction(MatchID)
    If Not Arr Then GoTo CleanUp

    ' ========== 界面刷新 ==========
    Call UIRefresh
    Call UpStepsArea
    
CleanUp:
    ' ========== 恢复应用程序状态 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
End Sub

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：执行具体的进化逻辑
' 参数：MatchID - 要进化的从者MatchID
' 说明：处理进化效果、消耗进化点、触发进化时能力等
' 包括进化失败的回滚处理
' ==================================================
Function EvolveFunction(MatchID As String, Optional NoEP As Boolean = False) As Boolean
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i%, j%, k%
    Dim Arr As Variant
    Dim Flag As Boolean
    Dim Effect As String
    Dim EffectSaved As String
    Dim Effectrr() As String
    Dim EvolveFlag As Boolean
    EvolveFunction = True
    
    ' ========== 初始化设置 ==========
    ' 清空能力队列
    QueueAbility = Empty
    QueueMatchID = Empty
    
    ' ========== 基础进化效果应用 ==========
    ' 应用+2/+2与突进效果，标记为已进化
    EffectSaved = DownByMatchID(MatchID, "Effect")
    Effect = EffectSaved & "{Rush}||Evolved||"
    Call UpByMatchID(MatchID, "Effect", Effect)
    Call GetAbility("||+2/+2|ThisCard", MatchID)
    
    ' ========== 进化点消耗 ==========
    ' 消耗1点EP（进化点）
    If Not NoEP Then Call Up("EP", Down("EP", MatchID) - 1, MatchID)
    Call UpSteps("Evolve", MatchID, DownByID(DownByMatchID(MatchID, "ID"), "Name"))
    
    ' ========== 进化效果处理 ==========
    ' 遍历并触发进化相关效果
    Flag = True
    Effectrr = GetEffect(MatchID)
    For i = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
        If Left(Effectrr(i), Len("Evolve")) = "Evolve" And InStr(Effect, "Remove|Evolve") = 0 Then
            Flag = GetTimePoint(Effectrr(i), MatchID)
            EvolveFlag = True
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 进化失败处理 ==========
    ' 如果选择失败，回滚进化操作
    If Not Flag Then
        If Not NoEP Then Call Up("EP", Down("EP", MatchID) + 1, MatchID)
        Call EraseSteps("Evolve")
        Call UpByMatchID(MatchID, "Effect", EffectSaved)
        QueueAbility = Empty
        QueueMatchID = Empty
        EvolveFunction = False
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' ========== 界面更新 ==========
    ' 更改进化按钮文本状态
    Sheets("Main").Shapes("EvolveBtn").TextFrame.Characters.Text = Translate("Evolved")
    
    ' ========== 能力触发处理 ==========
    ' 触发进场时进化效果
    Call GetAction("Evolve", MatchID, True)
    
    '奥义
    Arr = Down("Hand", MatchID)
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr)
        Effectrr = SplitEffect(CStr(Arr(i, 5)))
        For j = 0 To UBound(Effectrr)
            If Left(Effectrr(j), Len("Skybound&")) = "Skybound&" Then
                k = Val(Mid(Effectrr(j), 1 + Len("Skybound&"), 10))
                k = k + 1
                Effectrr(j) = "Skybound&" & k
                Exit For
            End If
        Next j
        Arr(i, 5) = Join(Effectrr, "||")
        If Arr(i, 5) <> "" Then Arr(i, 5) = Arr(i, 5) & "||"
        Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(i, 1)), "Effect", CStr(Arr(i, 5)))
    Next i
    
    ' 其他卡牌响应进化事件
    Call GetAction("Evolve", MatchID, "0")
    If EvolveFlag Then Call GetAction("Keyword&Evolve", MatchID)
    
    ' ========== 清理和刷新 ==========
    ' 清空能力队列
    Call EraseQueue
End Function

文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·7·6·对局·MatchSuperEvolve.bas

Attribute VB_Name = "·7·6·对局·MatchSuperEvolve"
' ==================================================
' 过程功能：处理从者超进化操作的入口函数
' 说明：检查超进化条件并执行超进化操作
' 包含回合检查、超进化点检查、进化状态验证等逻辑
' ==================================================
Sub MatchSuperEvolve()
    ' ========== 优化设置 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim Arr As Variant
    Dim Turn As String
    Dim MatchID As String
    
    ' ========== 回合权限检查 ==========
    ' 不是自己的回合直接退出
    Turn = Down("Turn")
    If Left(Turn, 1) <> Left(Down("FirstMove"), 1) Then GoTo CleanUp
    If Left(Turn, 1) = "F" Then
        If Val(Mid(Turn, 2, 10)) < 7 Then GoTo CleanUp
    Else
        If Val(Mid(Turn, 2, 10)) < 6 Then GoTo CleanUp
    End If
    
    ' ========== 战场选择检查 ==========
    ' 是否选择了战场
    Arr = IsSelected("AlliedField")
    If IsEmpty(Arr) Then GoTo CleanUp
    
    ' ========== 进化状态检查 ==========
    ' 是否已进化或超进化（检查按钮文本状态）
    If Sheets("Main").Shapes("EvolveBtn").TextFrame.Characters.Text = Translate("Evolved") Then GoTo CleanUp
    If Sheets("Main").Shapes("SuperEvolveBtn").TextFrame.Characters.Text = Translate("SuperEvolved") Then GoTo CleanUp
    
    ' ========== 战场信息获取 ==========
    ' 下载战场信息
    Arr = Down("Field", Left(Down("FirstMove"), 1) & 1000)
    ' 获取MatchID，如果为空则刷新并退出
    If Selection.Column - 2 > UBound(Arr, 1) Or Arr(1, 1) = "" Then
        Call UIRefresh
        GoTo CleanUp
    End If
    MatchID = Arr(Selection.Column - 2, 1)
    
    ' ========== 随从检查 ==========
    If DownByID(DownByMatchID(MatchID, "ID"), "Type") <> "Follower" Then GoTo CleanUp
    
    ' ========== 已进化状态检查 ==========
    ' 检查是否已经进化（注释标明不严谨实现）
    If InStr(Arr(Selection.Column - 2, 5), "Evolved||") > 0 Then GoTo CleanUp
    
    ' ========== 超进化点检查 ==========
    ' 是否有EP（进化点）
    Arr = GetEffect(MatchID)
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr)
        If Arr(i) = "NoSEP" Then Exit For
    Next i
    If i > UBound(Arr) Then
        If Val(Down("AlliedSEP")) <= 0 Then GoTo CleanUp
        Arr = SuperEvolveFunction(MatchID)
    Else
        Arr = SuperEvolveFunction(MatchID, True)
    End If
    
    ' ========== 执行超进化 ==========
    'Arr = SuperEvolveFunction(MatchID)
    If Not Arr Then GoTo CleanUp

    ' ========== 界面刷新 ==========
    Call UIRefresh
    Call UpStepsArea
    
CleanUp:
    ' ========== 恢复应用程序状态 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
End Sub

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：执行具体的超进化逻辑
' 参数：MatchID - 要超进化的从者MatchID
' 说明：处理超进化效果、消耗超进化点、触发超进化时能力等
' 包括超进化失败的回滚处理
' ==================================================
Function SuperEvolveFunction(MatchID As String, Optional NoSEP As Boolean = False) As Boolean
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i%, j%
    Dim Arr As Variant
    Dim Flag As Boolean
    Dim Effect As String
    Dim EffectSaved As String
    Dim Effectrr() As String
    Dim EvolveFlag As Boolean
    Dim SuperEvolveFlag As Boolean
    SuperEvolveFunction = True
    
    ' ========== 初始化设置 ==========
    ' 清空能力队列
    QueueAbility = Empty
    QueueMatchID = Empty
    
    ' ========== 基础超进化效果应用 ==========
    ' 应用+3/+3与突进效果，标记为已超进化
    EffectSaved = DownByMatchID(MatchID, "Effect")
    Effect = EffectSaved & "{Rush}||SuperEvolved||"
    Call UpByMatchID(MatchID, "Effect", Effect)
    Call GetAbility("||+3/+3|ThisCard", MatchID)
    
    ' ========== 超进化点消耗 ==========
    ' 消耗1点SEP（超进化点）
    If Not NoSEP Then Call Up("SEP", Down("SEP", MatchID) - 1, MatchID)
    Call UpSteps("Evolve", MatchID, DownByID(DownByMatchID(MatchID, "ID"), "Name"))
    Call UpSteps("SuperEvolve", MatchID, DownByID(DownByMatchID(MatchID, "ID"), "Name"))
    
    ' ========== 超进化效果处理 ==========
    ' 遍历并触发超进化和进化相关效果
    Flag = True
    Effectrr = GetEffect(MatchID)
    For i = 1 To UBound(Effectrr)
        ' 处理超进化效果（注释标明不严谨实现）
        If (Left(Effectrr(i), Len("SuperEvolve")) = "SuperEvolve" Or InStr(Effectrr(i), "Change|SuperEvolve") > 0) And InStr(Effect, "Remove|SuperEvolve") = 0 Then
            Flag = GetTimePoint(Effectrr(i), MatchID, "SuperEvolve")
            SuperEvolveFlag = True
        ' 处理进化效果（超进化时也会触发进化效果）
        ElseIf Left(Effectrr(i), Len("Evolve")) = "Evolve" And InStr(Effect, "Remove|Evolve") = 0 Then
            Flag = GetTimePoint(Effectrr(i), MatchID, "SuperEvolve")
            EvolveFlag = True
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 超进化失败处理 ==========
    ' 如果选择失败，回滚超进化操作
    If Not Flag Then
        If Not NoSEP Then Call Up("SEP", Down("SEP", MatchID) + 1, MatchID)
        Call EraseSteps("Evolve")
        Call UpByMatchID(MatchID, "Effect", EffectSaved)
        QueueAbility = Empty
        QueueMatchID = Empty
        SuperEvolveFunction = False
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' ========== 界面更新 ==========
    ' 更改超进化按钮文本状态
    Sheets("Main").Shapes("SuperEvolveBtn").TextFrame.Characters.Text = Translate("SuperEvolved")
    
    ' ========== 能力触发处理 ==========
    ' 触发进场时超进化效果
    Call GetAction("SuperEvolve", MatchID, True)
    
    '奥义
    Arr = Down("Hand", MatchID)
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr)
        Effectrr = SplitEffect(CStr(Arr(i, 5)))
        For j = 0 To UBound(Effectrr)
            If Left(Effectrr(j), Len("Skybound&")) = "Skybound&" Then
                k = Val(Mid(Effectrr(j), 1 + Len("Skybound&"), 10))
                k = k + 1
                Effectrr(j) = "Skybound&" & k
                Exit For
            End If
        Next j
        Arr(i, 5) = Join(Effectrr, "||")
        If Arr(i, 5) <> "" Then Arr(i, 5) = Arr(i, 5) & "||"
        Call UpByMatchID(CStr(Arr(i, 1)), "Effect", CStr(Arr(i, 5)))
    Next i
    
    ' 其他卡牌响应超进化事件
    Call GetAction("Evolve", MatchID, "0")
    Call GetAction("SuperEvolve", MatchID, "0")
    If EvolveFlag Then Call GetAction("Keyword&Evolve", MatchID)
    If SuperEvolveFlag Then Call GetAction("Keyword&SuperEvolve", MatchID)
    
    ' ========== 清理和刷新 ==========
    ' 清空能力队列
    Call EraseQueue
End Function

文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·7·7·对局·MatchGetPP.bas
