Attribute VB_Name = "·7·7·对局·MatchGetPP"
' ==================================================
' 过程功能：处理获取PP（能量点）操作
' 说明：后手玩家在特定回合获取额外能量点
' 包含使用条件检查、状态更新和界面刷新
' ==================================================
Sub MatchGetPP()
    Dim Str As String

    ' ========== 优化设置 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    
    ' ========== 使用权限检查 ==========
    ' 检查按钮状态，无法使用直接退出
    Str = Down("GetPP")
    If Str <> "能量值+1" Then GoTo CleanUp
    
    ' ========== 回合条件检查 ==========
    ' 不是后手回合直接退出
    If Left(Down("Turn"), 1) <> "S" Then GoTo CleanUp
    
    ' ========== 玩家身份检查 ==========
    ' 不是后手方直接退出
    If Left(Down("FirstMove"), 1) <> "S" Then GoTo CleanUp
    
    ' ========== PP获取处理 ==========
    ' 增加1点己方PP
    Call Up("AlliedPP", Val(Down("AlliedPP")) + 1)
    
    ' ========== 界面状态更新 ==========
    ' 更新按钮文本为已使用状态
    Call Up("GetPP", "已使用")
    Sheets("Main").Shapes("PPBtn").TextFrame.Characters.Text = Down("GetPP")
    
    ' ========== 步骤记录 ==========
    ' 记录获取PP的操作步骤
    Call UpSteps("GetPP", Left(Down("Turn"), 1) & "1000")
    
    ' ========== 界面刷新 ==========
    ' 刷新步骤显示区域
    Call UpStepsArea
    
CleanUp:
    ' ========== 恢复应用程序状态 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
End Sub


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·4·8·辅助·JoinEffect.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·4·8·辅助·JoinEffect"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：效果字符串合并器
' 参数：InputEffect - 输入的效果字符串
' 返回：String - 合并后的效果字符串
' 说明：合并效果字符串中的攻击力、生命值和费用修正，将分散的修正值汇总到字符串末尾
' ==================================================
Function JoinEffect(InputEffect As String)
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i As Integer, j As Integer, k As Integer   ' 循环计数器和位置索引
    Dim Str As String           ' 临时字符串
    Dim Str2 As String          ' 临时字符串
    Dim EffectSplit() As String ' 效果分割数组
    Dim Attack As Integer       ' 攻击力修正值
    Dim Defense As Integer      ' 生命值修正值
    Dim Cost As Integer         ' 费用修正值
    Dim AttackFlag As Boolean   ' 攻击力是否被设为固定值
    Dim DefenseFlag As Boolean  ' 生命值是否被设为固定值
    Dim CostFlag As Boolean     ' 费用是否被设为固定值
    
    ' ========== 输入验证 ==========
    If InputEffect = "" Then Exit Function
    
    ' ========== 效果分割 ==========
    EffectSplit = SplitEffect(InputEffect)
    
    ' ========== 倒序遍历效果 ==========
    ' 从后向前遍历效果数组，处理修正值
    For k = 0 To UBound(EffectSplit)
        i = UBound(EffectSplit) - k   ' 倒序索引
        
        ' 跳过Until效果和包含{}的复杂效果
        If InStr(EffectSplit(i), "Until") = 0 And InStr(EffectSplit(i), "{") = 0 Then
            ' ========== 身材修正处理 ==========
            ' 处理攻击力/生命值修正（格式：+1/+1）
            If InStr(EffectSplit(i), "/") > 0 Then
                j = InStr(EffectSplit(i), "/")   ' 找到分隔符位置
                Str = Left(EffectSplit(i), j - 1)   ' 提取攻击力部分
                
                ' ========== 攻击力修正计算 ==========
                If Left(Str, 1) = "$" Then
                    ' 设为固定值操作符
                    Attack = Attack + Val(Mid(Str, 2, 10))
                    AttackFlag = True   ' 标记攻击力已被设为固定值
                ElseIf Left(Str, 1) = "*" Or Left(Str, 1) = "%" Then
                    ' 遇到乘除操作符，停止向前合并
                    Exit For
                ElseIf Not AttackFlag Then
                    ' 加减操作符，且攻击力未被设为固定值
                    Attack = Attack + Val(Str)
                End If
                
                ' ========== 生命值修正计算 ==========
                Str = Mid(EffectSplit(i), j + 1, 10)   ' 提取生命值部分
                If Left(Str, 1) = "$" Then
                    ' 设为固定值操作符
                    Defense = Defense + Val(Mid(Str, 2, 10))
                    DefenseFlag = True   ' 标记生命值已被设为固定值
                ElseIf Left(Str, 1) = "*" Or Left(Str, 1) = "%" Then
                    ' 遇到乘除操作符，停止向前合并
                    Exit For
                ElseIf Not DefenseFlag Then
                    ' 加减操作符，且生命值未被设为固定值
                    Defense = Defense + Val(Str)
                End If
                
                ' 标记该效果已被处理
                EffectSplit(i) = "\"
                
            ' ========== 费用修正处理 ==========
            ' 处理费用修正（格式：Cost+1）
            ElseIf InStr(EffectSplit(i), "Cost") > 0 Then
                Str = Mid(EffectSplit(i), 1 + Len("Cost"), 10)   ' 提取费用修正部分
                If Left(Str, 1) = "$" Then
                    ' 设为固定值操作符
                    Cost = Cost + Val(Mid(Str, 2, 10))
                    CostFlag = True   ' 标记费用已被设为固定值
                ElseIf Left(Str, 1) = "*" Or Left(Str, 1) = "%" Then
                    ' 遇到乘除操作符，停止向前合并
                    Exit For
                ElseIf Not CostFlag Then
                    ' 加减操作符，且费用未被设为固定值
                    Cost = Cost + Val(Str)
                End If
                
                ' 标记该效果已被处理
                EffectSplit(i) = "\"
            End If
            
        ' ========== Until效果处理 ==========
        ' 遇到Until效果，停止向前合并
        ElseIf InStr(EffectSplit(i), "Until") > 0 And InStr(EffectSplit(i), "{") = 0 Then
            Exit For
        End If
    Next k
    
    ' ========== 清理已处理的效果 ==========
    Str = Join(EffectSplit, "||") & "||"   ' 重新连接效果字符串
    Str = Replace(Str, "\||", "")          ' 移除被标记的效果
    
    ' ========== 整合添加汇总效果 ==========
    ' 添加攻击力/生命值汇总效果
    If Attack <> 0 Or Defense <> 0 Or AttackFlag Or DefenseFlag Then
        If AttackFlag Then
            Str2 = "$" & Attack   ' 固定值格式
        Else
            ' 加减值格式，正数添加+号
            If Attack >= 0 Then Str2 = "+" & Attack Else Str2 = Attack
        End If
        Str2 = Str2 & "/"   ' 添加分隔符
        If DefenseFlag Then
            Str2 = Str2 & "$" & Defense   ' 固定值格式
        Else
            ' 加减值格式，正数添加+号
            If Defense >= 0 Then Str2 = Str2 & "+" & Defense Else Str2 = Str2 & Defense
        End If
        Str = Str & Str2 & "||"   ' 添加到效果字符串
    End If
    
    ' 添加费用汇总效果
    If Cost <> 0 Or CostFlag Then
        If CostFlag Then
            Str2 = "Cost$" & Cost   ' 固定值格式
        Else
            ' 加减值格式，正数添加+号
            If Cost >= 0 Then Str2 = "Cost+" & Cost Else Str2 = "Cost" & Cost
        End If
        Str = Str & Str2 & "||"   ' 添加到效果字符串
    End If
    
    JoinEffect = Str   ' 返回合并后的效果字符串
End Function


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·7·8·对局·MatchFuse.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·7·8·对局·MatchFuse"
' ==================================================
' 过程功能：处理卡牌融合操作的入口函数
' 说明：检查融合条件并执行融合操作
' 包含回合检查、手牌选择检查等逻辑
' ==================================================
Sub MatchFuse()
    ' ========== 优化设置 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim Arr As Variant
    Dim MatchID As String
    
    ' ========== 回合权限检查 ==========
    ' 不是自己的回合直接退出
    If Left(Down("Turn"), 1) <> Left(Down("FirstMove"), 1) Then GoTo CleanUp
    
    ' ========== 手牌选择检查 ==========
    ' 是否选择了手牌
    Arr = IsSelected("Hand")
    If IsEmpty(Arr) Then GoTo CleanUp
    
    ' ========== 手牌信息获取 ==========
    ' 下载手牌信息
    Arr = Down("Hand", Left(Down("FirstMove"), 1) & 1000)
    ' 获取MatchID，如果为空则刷新并退出
    If Selection.Column > UBound(Arr, 1) Or Arr(1, 1) = Empty Then
        Call UIRefresh
        GoTo CleanUp
    End If
    MatchID = Arr(Selection.Column, 1)
    
    ' ========== 融合状态检查 ==========
    ' 查询是否已融合，已融合则直接退出
    Arr = SplitEffect(DownByMatchID(MatchID, "Effect"))
    For i = 0 To UBound(Arr)
        If Arr(i) = "HaveFused" Then GoTo CleanUp
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 融合目标获取 ==========
    ' 获取所有可融合的目标选项
    Arr = GetEffect(MatchID)
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr)
        If Left(Arr(i), Len("Fuse|")) = "Fuse|" Then
            Call GetGoal("Select&0-Allied-Hand-Other-" & Mid(Arr(i), 1 + Len("Fuse|"), 100), MatchID)
            If PreviousGoal = "" Then GoTo CleanUp
            Exit For
        End If
    Next i
    If i > UBound(Arr) Then GoTo CleanUp
    
    ' ========== 执行融合 ==========
    Call FuseFunction(MatchID, PreviousGoal)
    
CleanUp:
    ' ========== 恢复应用程序状态 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
End Sub

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：执行具体的融合逻辑
' 参数：MatchID - 要进行融合的卡牌MatchID
' 说明：处理融合目标选择、融合记录生成、融合效果触发等
' 包括重复选项过滤和融合状态标记
' ==================================================
Function FuseFunction(MatchID As String, FuseCards As String) As Boolean
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i%, j%
    Dim Str As String
    Dim MatchIDrr() As String
    
    ' ========== 融合记录生成 ==========
    ' 生成融合记录并更新卡牌效果
    MatchIDrr = Split(FuseCards, "&")
    Str = "Fuse|"
    For i = 0 To UBound(MatchIDrr)
        Str = Str & DownByMatchID(MatchIDrr(i), "ID") & "&"
    Next i
    Str = DownByMatchID(MatchID, "Effect") & Left(Str, Len(Str) - 1) & "||HaveFused||"
    Call UpByMatchID(MatchID, "Effect", Str)
    
    ' ========== 能力触发处理 ==========
    ' 处理被融合卡牌的能力触发
    For i = 0 To UBound(MatchIDrr)
        Call UpByMatchID(MatchIDrr(i), "Area", "Fused")
    Next i
    
    ' 处理融合关键字能力触发
    Call GetAction("Keyword&Fuse", MatchID, True)
    Call GetAction("Keyword&Fuse", MatchID, UBound(MatchIDrr) + 1)
    
    ' 处理被融合卡牌的能力触发
    For i = 0 To UBound(MatchIDrr)
        Call GetAction("Fused", MatchIDrr(i), True)
        Call GetAction("Fused", MatchIDrr(i))
    Next i
    
    ' ========== 清理和刷新 ==========
    ' 清空能力队列
    Call EraseQueue
    
    ' 刷新界面和步骤区域
    Call UIRefresh
    Call UpStepsArea
End Function


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·5·5·步骤·UpStepsArea.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·5·5·步骤·UpStepsArea"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：更新步骤区域显示
' 说明：从Steps记录中提取重要动作并格式化显示在StepsArea区域
' 将游戏中的重要动作（如出牌、攻击、进化等）及其相关动作整理成可读格式
' ==================================================
Function UpStepsArea()
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i%, j%, k%, m%, n%  ' 循环计数器
    Dim Arr As Variant          ' Steps数据数组
    Dim StepsArea As Variant    ' StepsArea区域数据数组
    
    ' ========== 查找最近的重要动作 ==========
    ' 获取Steps区域的所有步骤记录
    Arr = Down("Steps")
    
    ' 从后向前遍历Steps数组，查找最近的重要动作
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr)
        j = UBound(Arr) - i + 1   ' 倒序索引
        
        ' 检查是否为重要动作类型
        If Arr(j, 2) = "Play" Or Arr(j, 2) = "Strike" Or _
            Arr(j, 2) = "Engage" Or Arr(j, 2) = "Keyword&Fuse" Or Arr(j, 2) = "GetPP" Or _
            Arr(j, 2) = "TurnStart" Or Arr(j, 2) = "TurnEnd" Then
            Exit For   ' 找到重要动作后退出循环
        ElseIf (Arr(j, 2) = "Evolve" Or Arr(j, 2) = "SuperEvolve") And Arr(j, 4) <> "" Then
            Exit For
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' 如果没有找到重要动作，直接退出函数
    If i > UBound(Arr) Then Exit Function
    
    ' ========== StepsArea区域空位查找 ==========
    ' 获取StepsArea区域数据
    StepsArea = Down("StepsArea")
    
    ' 查找StepsArea中的第一个空行
    For i = 1 To UBound(StepsArea, 1)
        If StepsArea(i, 1) = "" Then Exit For
    Next i
    
    ' 如果StepsArea已满，直接退出函数
    If i > UBound(StepsArea) Then Exit Function
    
    ' ========== 步骤记录格式化 ==========
    ' 构建主要动作描述：序号、【卡牌名称|动作描述】
    If Arr(j, 2) = "TurnStart" Then
        StepsArea(i, 1) = i & "、回合开始"
    ElseIf Arr(j, 2) = "TurnEnd" Then
        StepsArea(i, 1) = i & "、回合结束"
    ElseIf Arr(j, 2) = "Play" Then
        StepsArea(i, 1) = i & "、使用【" & Arr(j, 4) & "】"
    ElseIf Arr(j, 2) = "Strike" Then
        StepsArea(i, 1) = i & "、" & Arr(j, 4) & "|攻击|"
        If Arr(j + 1, 2) = "Clash" And Arr(j + 2, 2) = "Clash" Then StepsArea(i, 1) = StepsArea(i, 1) & Arr(j + 2, 4) Else StepsArea(i, 1) = StepsArea(i, 1) & "主战者"
    ElseIf Arr(j, 2) = "Engage" Then
        StepsArea(i, 1) = i & "、启动【" & Arr(j, 4) & "】"
    ElseIf Arr(j, 2) = "Keyword&Fuse" Then
        StepsArea(i, 1) = i & "、" & DownByID(DownByMatchID(CStr(Arr(j, 3)), "ID"), "Name") & "|融合" & Arr(j, 4) & "张手牌"
    ElseIf Arr(j, 2) = "Evolve" And Arr(j, 4) <> "" And Arr(j, 4) <> "0" Then
        StepsArea(i, 1) = i & "、进化|" & Arr(j, 4)
    ElseIf Arr(j, 2) = "SuperEvolve" And Arr(j, 4) <> "" And Arr(j, 4) <> "0" Then
        StepsArea(i, 1) = i & "、超进化|" & Arr(j, 4)
    ElseIf Arr(j, 2) = "GetPP" Then
        StepsArea(i, 1) = i & "、【能量点+1】"
    End If
    
    ' ========== 相关动作附加 ==========
    ' 从重要动作位置开始，向后查找相关的后续动作
    For k = j + 1 To UBound(Arr)
        ' 检查是否为相关的后续动作类型
        If Arr(k, 2) = "Discard" Then
            For m = 1 To 100
                If Arr(k + m, 2) <> "Discard" Then Exit For
            Next m
            k = k + m - 1
            StepsArea(i, 1) = StepsArea(i, 1) & "，舍弃" & m & "张手牌"
        ElseIf Arr(k, 2) = "DrawToHand" Then
            n = 1
            For m = 1 To 100
                If Arr(k + m, 2) = "DrawToHand" Then
                    n = n + 1
                ElseIf Arr(k + m, 2) = "AddToHand" Then
                
                Else
                    Exit For
                End If
            Next m
            k = k + m - 1
            StepsArea(i, 1) = StepsArea(i, 1) & "，抽取" & n & "张手牌"
        ElseIf Arr(k, 2) = "HandDestroy" Then
            StepsArea(i, 1) = StepsArea(i, 1) & "，" & Arr(k, 4) & "|爆牌"
            
        ElseIf Arr(k, 2) = "EnterField" Then
            For m = 1 To k - 1
                n = k - m
                If Arr(n, 2) = "Play" Then Exit For
            Next m
            
            If Arr(n, 3) <> Arr(k, 3) Then
                StepsArea(i, 1) = StepsArea(i, 1) & "，" & Arr(k, 4)
                For m = 1 To 100
                    If Arr(k + m, 2) = "EnterField" Then
                        StepsArea(i, 1) = StepsArea(i, 1) & "、" & Arr(k + m, 4)
                    ElseIf Arr(k + m, 2) = "EffectEnter" Then
                    
                    Else
                        Exit For
                    End If
                Next m
                k = k + m - 1
                StepsArea(i, 1) = StepsArea(i, 1) & "|进入战场"
            End If
        ElseIf Arr(k, 2) = "Destroy" Then
            StepsArea(i, 1) = StepsArea(i, 1) & "，" & Arr(k, 4)
            For m = 1 To 100
                If Arr(k + m, 2) = "Destroy" Then
                    StepsArea(i, 1) = StepsArea(i, 1) & "、" & Arr(k + m, 4)
                ElseIf Arr(k + m, 2) = "LeaveField" Then
                
                Else
                    Exit For
                End If
            Next m
            k = k + m - 1
            StepsArea(i, 1) = StepsArea(i, 1) & "|破坏"
        ElseIf Arr(k, 2) = "Banish" Then
            StepsArea(i, 1) = StepsArea(i, 1) & "，" & Arr(k, 4)
            For m = 1 To 100
                If Arr(k + m, 2) = "Banish" Then
                    StepsArea(i, 1) = StepsArea(i, 1) & "、" & Arr(k + m, 4)
                ElseIf Arr(k + m, 2) = "LeaveField" Then
                
                Else
                    Exit For
                End If
            Next m
            k = k + m - 1
            StepsArea(i, 1) = StepsArea(i, 1) & "|消失"
        ElseIf Arr(k, 2) = "ReturnToHand" Then
            StepsArea(i, 1) = StepsArea(i, 1) & "，" & Arr(k, 4)
            For m = 1 To 100
                If Arr(k + m, 2) = "ReturnToHand" Then
                    StepsArea(i, 1) = StepsArea(i, 1) & "、" & Arr(k + m, 4)
                ElseIf Arr(k + m, 2) = "LeaveField" Then
                
                Else
                    Exit For
                End If
            Next m
            k = k + m - 1
            StepsArea(i, 1) = StepsArea(i, 1) & "|返回手牌"
            
        'ElseIf Arr(k, 2) = "LostBarrier" Then
            'StepsArea(i, 1) = StepsArea(i, 1) & "，" & Arr(k, 4) & "|失去屏障"
            
            
        End If
    Next k
    
    ' ========== 数据更新 ==========
    ' 将格式化后的步骤记录写回StepsArea区域
    Call Up("StepsArea", StepsArea)
End Function

Attribute VB_Name = "EffectForm"
Attribute VB_Base = "0{E29AAC31-9755-43E9-8BBB-DAA4437F6CCD}{44392CC1-14F1-4381-B440-C7671F0C69E9}"
Attribute VB_GlobalNameSpace = False
Attribute VB_Creatable = False
Attribute VB_PredeclaredId = True
Attribute VB_Exposed = False
Attribute VB_TemplateDerived = False
Attribute VB_Customizable = False
' ==================================================
' 窗体名称: EffectForm
' 功能：卡牌效果查看和编辑窗体
' 说明：显示卡牌详细信息，支持动态高度调整
' ==================================================

' ========== 窗体激活事件 ==========
' 说明：窗体激活时自动设置卡牌名称
Private Sub UserForm_Activate()
    ' ========== 卡牌名称设置 ==========
    ' 使用窗体标题作为卡牌名称初始值
    CardName.Value = EffectForm.Caption
End Sub

' ==================================================
' 事件功能：卡牌名称变更检测
' 说明：当卡牌名称改变时，自动查询并显示对应卡牌的详细信息
' ==================================================
Private Sub CardName_Change()
    ' ========== 卡牌ID查询 ==========
    ' 根据卡牌名称查询对应的卡牌ID
    Dim StrID As String
    StrID = IDByName(CardName.Value)
    
    ' ========== 卡牌信息显示 ==========
    ' 如果查询到已有此卡牌，则显示详细信息
    If Val(StrID) > 0 Then
        ' ========== 效果文本更新 ==========
        ' 遍历所有效果控件并更新显示内容
        For Each ctrl In Me.Controls
            If ctrl.Tag = "Effect" Then
                If DownByID(StrID, ctrl.Name) <> "" Then ctrl.Caption = SentenceTranslate(DownByID(StrID, ctrl.Name))
            End If
        Next ctrl
        
        ' ========== 基础属性显示 ==========
        ' 继承职业、种类、稀有度信息
        ClassBtn.Caption = Translate(DownByID(StrID, "Class"))
        TypeBtn.Caption = Translate(DownByID(StrID, "Type"))
        RarityBtn.Caption = Translate(DownByID(StrID, "Rarity"))
        
        ' ========== 数值属性显示 ==========
        ' 显示费用/攻击力/生命值/吟唱等数值
        Cost.Value = DownByID(StrID, "Cost")
        Attack.Value = DownByID(StrID, "Attack")
        Defense.Value = DownByID(StrID, "Defense")
        Countdown.Value = DownByID(StrID, "Countdown")
        
        ' ========== 扩展属性显示 ==========
        ' 显示卡包/类型/附属卡牌信息
        CardSet.Value = DownByID(StrID, "CardSet")
        Trait.Value = DownByID(StrID, "Trait")
        
        ' ========== 令牌标识设置 ==========
        ' 根据ID格式判断是否为令牌卡牌
        If Mid(StrID, 4, 1) = "1" Then IsToken = False Else IsToken = True
        
        ' ========== 效果区域高度调整 ==========
        ' 根据效果文本长度调整控件高度
        Call EffectHeight
    End If
End Sub

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：效果区域高度调整
' 说明：根据效果文本的长度动态调整效果控件的高度
' 返回：无
' ==================================================
Function EffectHeight()
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i%, j%
    Dim Height%
    
    ' ========== 效果控件遍历 ==========
    ' 遍历9个效果控件，逐个调整高度
    For i = 1 To 9
        ' ========== 控件查找 ==========
        ' 查找对应的效果控件
        For Each ctrl In Me.Controls
            If ctrl.Name = "Effect" & i Then Exit For
        Next ctrl
        
        ' ========== 高度计算 ==========
        ' 根据文本长度计算需要调整的高度
        Height = Int(Len(ctrl.Caption) / 50) * 10 + 10 - ctrl.Height
        
        ' ========== 高度调整 ==========
        ' 如果计算出的高度变化不为零，则进行调整
        If Height <> 0 Then
            ' 调整当前效果控件的高度
            ctrl.Height = ctrl.Height + Height
            
            ' ========== 相关控件位置调整 ==========
            ' 调整效果框架控件的位置和高度
            For Each ctrl In Me.Controls
                If ctrl.Tag = "EffectFrame" Then
                    ' 调整后续效果框架的位置
                    If Val(Mid(ctrl.Name, Len("EffectFrame") + 1, 10)) > i Then ctrl.Top = ctrl.Top + Height
                    ' 调整当前效果框架的高度
                    If Val(Mid(ctrl.Name, Len("EffectFrame") + 1, 10)) = i Then ctrl.Height = ctrl.Height + Height
                End If
            Next ctrl
        End If
    Next i
End Function

文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·9·2·文件·ShowImageFromPath.bas

Attribute VB_Name = "·9·2·文件·ShowImageFromPath"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：从指定路径显示图片
' 参数：searchPath - 搜索路径, imageName - 图片文件名
' 返回：Boolean - 显示是否成功
' 说明：在指定路径中递归查找图片文件并使用窗体显示
' ==================================================
Function ShowImageFromPath(searchPath As String, imageName As String) As Boolean
    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler
    
    ' ========== 路径存在性检查 ==========
    ' 检查指定路径是否存在
    If Dir(searchPath, vbDirectory) = "" Then
        ShowImageFromPath = False
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' ========== 图片文件查找 ==========
    ' 递归查找图片文件
    Dim foundImagePath As String
    foundImagePath = FindImageRecursive(searchPath, imageName)
    
    ' ========== 查找结果验证 ==========
    ' 如果没找到图片，返回False
    If foundImagePath = "" Then
        ShowImageFromPath = False
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' ========== 图片显示处理 ==========
    ' 使用预创建窗体显示图片
    Call DisplayImageForm(foundImagePath, imageName)
    
    ShowImageFromPath = True
    Exit Function
    
ErrorHandler:
    ShowImageFromPath = False
End Function

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：递归查找图片文件
' 参数：folderPath - 文件夹路径, imageName - 图片文件名
' 返回：String - 找到的图片完整路径
' 说明：在文件夹及其子文件夹中递归搜索指定名称的图片文件
' 支持多种图片格式：jpg, jpeg, png, bmp, gif, tiff
' ==================================================
Private Function FindImageRecursive(folderPath As String, imageName As String) As String
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim fso As Object
    Dim folder As Object
    Dim subFolder As Object
    Dim file As Object
    
    ' ========== 创建文件系统对象 ==========
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    
    ' 检查文件夹是否存在
    If Not fso.FolderExists(folderPath) Then
        FindImageRecursive = ""
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    Set folder = fso.GetFolder(folderPath)
    
    ' ========== 当前文件夹搜索 ==========
    For Each file In folder.Files
        ' 检查文件名是否匹配（不区分大小写）
        If LCase(file.Name) = LCase(imageName) Or _
           LCase(file.Name) = LCase(imageName & ".jpg") Or _
           LCase(file.Name) = LCase(imageName & ".jpeg") Or _
           LCase(file.Name) = LCase(imageName & ".png") Or _
           LCase(file.Name) = LCase(imageName & ".bmp") Or _
           LCase(file.Name) = LCase(imageName & ".gif") Or _
           LCase(file.Name) = LCase(imageName & ".tiff") Then
            
            FindImageRecursive = file.Path
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next file
    
    ' ========== 子文件夹递归搜索 ==========
    For Each subFolder In folder.SubFolders
        Dim result As String
        result = FindImageRecursive(subFolder.Path, imageName)
        If result <> "" Then
            FindImageRecursive = result
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next subFolder
    
    ' ========== 未找到处理 ==========
    FindImageRecursive = ""
End Function

' ==================================================
' 过程功能：在窗体中显示图片
' 参数：imagePath - 图片完整路径, CardName - 卡片名称
' 说明：使用预创建的窗体加载并显示图片，保持原始比例
' ==================================================
Private Sub DisplayImageForm(imagePath As String, CardName As String)
    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler
    
    Dim finalImagePath As String
    finalImagePath = GetCompatibleImagePath(imagePath)
    
    If finalImagePath = "" Then
        MsgBox "无法加载或转换图片: " & imagePath, vbExclamation
        Exit Sub
    End If
    
    With ImageDisplayForm
        .Caption = CardName
        .imgPreview.PictureSizeMode = fmPictureSizeModeZoom
        .imgPreview.Picture = LoadPicture(finalImagePath)
        .Show vbModeless
    End With
    
    ' 清理临时文件（如果是转换生成的）
    If finalImagePath <> imagePath Then
        ' 可以在这里添加延迟删除逻辑，或者不删除缓存以提高性能
    End If
    
    Exit Sub
    
ErrorHandler:
    MsgBox "无法加载图片: " & imagePath & vbCrLf & "错误: " & Err.Description, vbExclamation
    ImageDisplayForm.Hide
End Sub

' 获取兼容的图片路径，自动转换不兼容的格式
Private Function GetCompatibleImagePath(originalPath As String) As String
    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler
    
    ' 检查文件是否存在
    If Dir(originalPath) = "" Then
        GetCompatibleImagePath = ""
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' 获取文件扩展名
    Dim fileExt As String
    fileExt = LCase(Right(originalPath, 4))
    
    ' 对于非PNG文件，直接尝试加载
    If fileExt <> ".png" Then
        ' 测试是否能直接加载
        TestLoadPicture originalPath
        GetCompatibleImagePath = originalPath
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' 对于PNG文件，尝试直接加载
    If CanLoadPicture(originalPath) Then
        GetCompatibleImagePath = originalPath
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    ' 如果直接加载失败，转换为BMP
    GetCompatibleImagePath = ConvertPngToBmp(originalPath)
    
    Exit Function
    
ErrorHandler:
    GetCompatibleImagePath = ""
End Function

' 测试图片是否能加载
Private Function CanLoadPicture(imagePath As String) As Boolean
    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler
    
    ' 创建临时图片对象测试加载
    Dim testControl As Object
    Set testControl = CreateObject("Forms.Image.1")
    testControl.Picture = LoadPicture(imagePath)
    
    CanLoadPicture = True
    Exit Function
    
ErrorHandler:
    CanLoadPicture = False
End Function

' 测试加载图片（不处理错误）
Private Sub TestLoadPicture(imagePath As String)
    Dim testPic As Object
    Set testPic = LoadPicture(imagePath)
End Sub

' 使用WIA转换PNG到BMP
Private Function ConvertPngToBmp(pngPath As String) As String
    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler
    
    ' 需要引用 "Windows Image Acquisition Library v2.0"
    Dim wia As Object
    Dim img As Object
    Dim bmpPath As String
    
    ' 生成BMP文件路径（临时文件）
    bmpPath = Left(pngPath, Len(pngPath) - 4) & "_temp.bmp"
    
    ' 如果临时文件已存在，先删除
    If Dir(bmpPath) <> "" Then Kill bmpPath
    
    ' 使用WIA转换
    Set wia = CreateObject("WIA.ImageFile")
    wia.LoadFile pngPath
    
    Set img = CreateObject("WIA.ImageProcess")
    
    Dim filter As Object
    Set filter = img.FilterInfos("Convert").FilterObject
    filter.FormatID = "{B96B3CAB-0728-11D3-9D7B-0000F81EF32E}" ' BMP格式GUID
    
    img.Filters.Add filter
    Set wia = img.Apply(wia)
    wia.SaveFile bmpPath
    
    ConvertPngToBmp = bmpPath
    Exit Function
    
ErrorHandler:
    ' WIA不可用，尝试备用方法
    ConvertPngToBmp = ConvertPngToBmp_Alternative(pngPath)
End Function

' 备用转换方法（如果WIA不可用）
Private Function ConvertPngToBmp_Alternative(pngPath As String) As String
    On Error GoTo ErrorHandler
    
    ' 使用简单的文件复制和重命名（这实际上不会转换格式，但可以测试）
    Dim bmpPath As String
    bmpPath = Left(pngPath, Len(pngPath) - 4) & "_copy.bmp"
    
    ' 复制文件并更改扩展名
    FileCopy pngPath, bmpPath
    
    ' 测试是否能加载这个"BMP"文件
    If CanLoadPicture(bmpPath) Then
        ConvertPngToBmp_Alternative = bmpPath
    Else
        Kill bmpPath ' 删除无效文件
        ConvertPngToBmp_Alternative = ""
    End If
    
    Exit Function
    
ErrorHandler:
    ConvertPngToBmp_Alternative = ""
End Function

' ==================================================
' 函数功能：从完整路径中提取文件名
' 参数：fullPath - 完整文件路径
' 返回：String - 文件名
' 说明：从完整路径字符串中提取文件名部分
' ==================================================
Private Function GetFileName(fullPath As String) As String
    GetFileName = Mid(fullPath, InStrRev(fullPath, "\") + 1)
End Function



--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·9·3·文件·ShowEffect.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·9·3·文件·ShowEffect"
' ==================================================
' 过程功能：打开查卡窗口
' 说明：根据当前选中的卡牌位置自动填入卡牌名称
' 优先尝试显示图片，失败则显示效果文本窗体
' ==================================================
Sub ShowEffect()
    ' ========== 优化设置 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim Flag As Boolean
    Dim Arr As Variant
    Dim CardName As String
    
    ' ========== 卡牌位置检测 ==========
    ' 按优先级顺序检测选中的卡牌位置
    ' 己方战场
    Arr = IsSelected("AlliedField")
    ' 敌方战场
    If IsEmpty(Arr) Then Arr = IsSelected("EnemyField")
    ' 手牌
    If IsEmpty(Arr) Then Arr = IsSelected("Hand")
    ' 纹章区
    If IsEmpty(Arr) Then Arr = IsSelected("Crest")
    ' 已使用区域
    If IsEmpty(Arr) Then Arr = IsSelected("PlayArea")
    ' 已破坏区域
    If IsEmpty(Arr) Then Arr = IsSelected("DestroyArea")
    ' 卡牌集合
    If IsEmpty(Arr) Then Arr = IsSelected("CardSet·Cards")
    ' 原始牌组
    If IsEmpty(Arr) Then Arr = IsSelected("OrigDeck")
    
    ' ========== 卡牌名称获取 ==========
    ' 如果检测到选中的卡牌，获取卡牌名称；否则直接退出
    If Not IsEmpty(Arr) Then
        CardName = CStr(Arr)
    Else
        GoTo CleanUp
    End If
    
    ' ========== 图片显示尝试 ==========
    ' 优先尝试从路径显示卡牌图片
    Flag = ShowImageFromPath(Down("Path"), CardName)
    
    ' ========== 效果文本显示 ==========
    ' 如果图片显示失败，则显示效果文本窗体
    If Not Flag Then
        EffectForm.Caption = CardName
        EffectForm.Show
    End If
    
CleanUp:
    ' ========== 恢复应用程序状态 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
End Sub

文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ImageDisplayForm.frm

Attribute VB_Name = "ImageDisplayForm"
Attribute VB_Base = "0{DD392B5C-3260-4301-9BE1-8DEDB888FEE8}{60E09BF6-888E-4B9A-AC57-B62C60F6980B}"

Attribute VB_Customizable = False
' 窗体初始化事件
Private Sub UserForm_Initialize()
    ' 设置默认大小
    Me.Width = 600
    Me.Height = 500
    ' 设置图片控件为自动调整大小
    Me.imgPreview.PictureSizeMode = fmPictureSizeModeAutoSize
End Sub

' 窗体激活时调整大小以适应图片
Private Sub UserForm_Activate()
    AdjustFormToImage
End Sub

' 调整窗体大小以适应图片
Private Sub AdjustFormToImage()
    On Error Resume Next
    
    Dim imgWidth As Double
    Dim imgHeight As Double
    Dim maxWidth As Double
    Dim maxHeight As Double
    Dim scaleFactor As Double
    
    ' 获取图片原始尺寸
    imgWidth = Me.imgPreview.Width
    imgHeight = Me.imgPreview.Height
    
    ' 如果图片尺寸为0（可能未加载），则退出
    If imgWidth = 0 Or imgHeight = 0 Then Exit Sub
    
    ' 设置最大显示尺寸（可根据需要调整）
    maxWidth = 1800
    maxHeight = 900

    ' 如果图片尺寸超过最大值，则按比例缩放
    If imgWidth > maxWidth Or imgHeight > maxHeight Then
        scaleFactor = Application.WorksheetFunction.Min(maxWidth / imgWidth, maxHeight / imgHeight)
        imgWidth = imgWidth * scaleFactor
        imgHeight = imgHeight * scaleFactor
    End If
    
    ' 设置窗体大小（加上边框和标题栏的空间）
    Me.Width = imgWidth + 30
    Me.Height = imgHeight + 50
    
    ' 设置图片控件大小
    Me.imgPreview.Width = imgWidth
    Me.imgPreview.Height = imgHeight
    
    ' 居中显示
    Me.imgPreview.Left = (Me.InsideWidth - imgWidth) / 2
    Me.imgPreview.Top = (Me.InsideHeight - imgHeight) / 2
    
    ' 确保窗体不会超出屏幕
    If Me.Width > Application.Width Then
        Me.Width = Application.Width * 0.9
    End If
    If Me.Height > Application.Height Then
        Me.Height = Application.Height * 0.9
    End If
    
    ' 重新居中窗体
    Me.StartUpPosition = 0
    Me.Left = (Application.Left + Application.Width - Me.Width) / 2
    Me.Top = (Application.Top + Application.Height - Me.Height) / 2
End Sub

' 双击图片打开文本窗体
Private Sub imgPreview_DblClick(ByVal Cancel As MSForms.ReturnBoolean)

    '打开文本窗体
    EffectForm.Caption = ImageDisplayForm.Caption
    Unload ImageDisplayForm
    EffectForm.Show
End Sub


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·8·4·对局·CoverHands.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·8·4·对局·CoverHands"
Sub CoverHands()
    Dim i As Integer
    Dim Arr As Variant
    Dim ws As Worksheet
    Dim copyRange As Range
    
    ' 设置工作表对象，避免重复引用
    Set ws = Sheets("Main")
    
    Arr = Down("FirstMove")
    Arr = Down("Hand", CStr(Arr))
    i = UBound(Arr, 1)
    
    If Arr(1, 1) <> "" Then
        If ws.Range("A14").Interior.ThemeColor = xlThemeColorDark1 Then
            ' 显示手牌分支
            ws.Range(ws.Cells(14, 1), ws.Cells(16, i)).Interior.ThemeColor = xlThemeColorLight1
            ws.Shapes("CoverBtn").TextFrame.Characters.Text = "显示手牌"
            
            Arr = Down("StepsArea")
            For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
                If Arr(i, 1) = "" Then Exit For
            Next i
            
            ' 直接复制，不通过选择
            Set copyRange = ws.Range(ws.Cells(1, 1), ws.Cells(i + 19, 9))
            copyRange.CopyPicture Appearance:=xlScreen, Format:=xlBitmap
            
        Else
            ' 遮住手牌分支
            ws.Range(ws.Cells(14, 1), ws.Cells(16, i)).Interior.ThemeColor = xlThemeColorDark1
            ws.Shapes("CoverBtn").TextFrame.Characters.Text = "遮住手牌&截图"
            
            ' 取消复制状态
            Application.CutCopyMode = False
            
            ' 直接激活单元格，不通过选择
            ws.Cells(16, i).Activate
        End If
    End If
    
    ' 清理对象
    Set ws = Nothing
    Set copyRange = Nothing
End Sub


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·5·3·步骤·OutputSteps.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·5·3·步骤·OutputSteps"
Function OutputSteps()
    Dim i%, j%
    Dim Str As String
    Dim Turn As String
    Dim Arr As Variant
    Dim OutputStr As String
    
    Dim objData As Object
    Set objData = CreateObject("New:{1C3B4210-F441-11CE-B9EA-00AA006B1A69}")
    
    ' ========== 查找最近的重要动作 ==========
    ' 获取Steps区域的所有步骤记录
    Turn = Down("Turn")
    
    '记录动作
    Arr = Down("Steps")
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr)
        If Arr(i, 1) = Turn Then
            If Arr(i, 2) = "Play" Or Arr(i, 2) = "Engage" Or _
                Arr(i, 2) = "Select" Or Arr(i, 2) = "Random" Then
                
                OutputStr = OutputStr & Arr(i, 2) & "|" & Arr(i, 3) & "||"
                
            ElseIf Arr(i, 2) = "Evolve" And Arr(i, 4) <> "" And Arr(i, 4) <> "0" Then
                If i = UBound(Arr) Then
                    OutputStr = OutputStr & Arr(i, 2) & "|" & Arr(i, 3) & "||"
                ElseIf Arr(i + 1, 2) <> "SuperEvolve" Or Arr(i, 3) <> Arr(i + 1, 3) Then
                    OutputStr = OutputStr & Arr(i, 2) & "|" & Arr(i, 3) & "||"
                Else
                    OutputStr = OutputStr & "SuperEvolve" & "|" & Arr(i, 3) & "||"
                End If
                
            ElseIf Arr(i, 2) = "Strike" Then
                OutputStr = OutputStr & Arr(i, 2) & "|" & Arr(i, 3) & "|"
                If Arr(i + 1, 2) = "Clash" And Arr(i + 2, 2) = "Clash" Then
                    OutputStr = OutputStr & Arr(i + 2, 3) & "||"
                ElseIf Left(Turn, 1) = "F" Then
                    OutputStr = OutputStr & "S1000||"
                ElseIf Left(Turn, 1) = "S" Then
                    OutputStr = OutputStr & "F1000||"
                End If
                
            ElseIf Arr(i, 2) = "Keyword&Fuse" Then
                OutputStr = OutputStr & Arr(i, 2) & "|" & Arr(i, 3) & "|"
                For j = i + 1 To i + 100
                    If Arr(j, 2) = "Fused" Then OutputStr = OutputStr & Arr(j, 3) & "&" Else Exit For
                Next j
                If Right(OutputStr, 1) = "&" Then OutputStr = Left(OutputStr, Len(OutputStr) - 1) & "||"
                
            ElseIf Arr(i, 2) = "GetPP" Then
                OutputStr = OutputStr & Arr(i, 2) & "||"
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    OutputStr = OutputStr & "TurnEnd"
    
    objData.SetText OutputStr
    objData.PutInClipboard
    Set objData = Nothing
    
    InputBox "「复制」发送给对手，然后任意按钮关闭", "回合结束", OutputStr
End Function


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·5·4·步骤·InputSteps.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·5·4·步骤·InputSteps"
Function InputSteps()
    Dim i%, j%
    Dim Str As String
    Dim Arr As Variant
    Dim Steps() As String
    Dim Action() As String
    
    Str = QueueAction
    
    'Call OutputLog(Str, "-")
    
    Steps = Split(Str, "||")
    For i = 0 To UBound(Steps, 1)
        Action = Split(Steps(i), "|")
        
        If Action(0) = "Play" Then
            Call PlayFunction(Action(1))
        ElseIf Action(0) = "Strike" Then
            Call StrikeFunction(Action(1), Action(2))
        ElseIf Action(0) = "Engage" Then
            Call EngageFunction(Action(1))
        ElseIf Action(0) = "Keyword&Fuse" Then
            Call FuseFunction(Action(1), Action(2))
        ElseIf Action(0) = "Evolve" Then
            Call EvolveFunction(Action(1))
        ElseIf Action(0) = "SuperEvolve" Then
            Call SuperEvolveFunction(Action(1))
        ElseIf Action(0) = "GetPP" Then
            Call Up("EnemyPP", Val(Down("EnemyPP")) + 1)
        End If
    Next i
    
    QueueAction = Empty
End Function


Function EraseQueueAction(InputAction As String) As String
    Dim i%
    Dim Steps() As String
    Dim Action() As String
    
    Steps = Split(QueueAction, "||")
    For i = 0 To UBound(Steps)
        Action = Split(Steps(i), "|")
        If Action(0) = InputAction Then
            Steps(i) = "\"
            EraseQueueAction = Action(1)
            Exit For
        Else
            Steps(i) = "\"
        End If
    Next i
    QueueAction = Replace(Join(Steps, "||"), "\||", "")
End Function


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·5·2·步骤·EraseSteps.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·5·2·步骤·EraseSteps"
' ==================================================
' 函数功能：更新步骤记录
' 参数：InputAction - 输入的动作描述
'        MatchID - 匹配ID值
' 说明：将当前回合的动作记录添加到Steps区域，用于游戏步骤追踪
' ==================================================
Function EraseSteps(InputAction As String)
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i%, j%, k%
    Dim Arr As Variant      ' 步骤数据数组
    
    ' ========== 步骤数据获取 ==========
    ' 获取Steps区域的所有步骤记录
    Arr = Down("Steps")
    
    ' ========== 空位查找与记录添加 ==========
    ' 遍历步骤数组查找第一个空位置
    For k = 1 To UBound(Arr)
        If Arr(k, 1) = "" Then Exit For
    Next k
    For i = 1 To k
        j = k - i + 1
        Arr(j, 1) = Empty
        If Arr(j, 2) = InputAction Then Exit For
    Next i
    Arr = NonEmptyArray(Arr, True)
    
    ' ========== 数据更新 ==========
    ' 将更新后的步骤数据写回Steps区域
    Call Up("Steps", Arr)
End Function



--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·2·8·录卡·DeleteCard.bas

Attribute VB_Name = "·2·8·录卡·DeleteCard"
Sub DelectCard()
    ' ========== 优化设置 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim CardName As String       ' 选中的卡牌名称
    Dim OrigDeck As Variant      ' 原卡组数据数组
    Dim i As Integer             ' 排序位置索引
    Dim Str As String            ' 排序位置索引
    
    ' ========== 选中卡牌检查 ==========
    CardName = CStr(IsSelected("CardSet·Cards"))   ' 获取选中区域的卡牌名称
    
    ' ========== 确认 ==========
    Str = MsgBox("确认删除吗？", vbYesNo, "删除卡牌")
    If Str = vbYes Then Str = MsgBox("真的确认删除吗？", vbYesNo, "删除卡牌") Else GoTo CleanUp
    If Str <> vbYes Then GoTo CleanUp
    
    ' 如果选中了有效卡牌，则进行添加操作
    If CardName <> "" Then
        ' ========== 删除 ==========
        Call DeleteEffect(IDByName(CardName), "")
    End If
    
CleanUp:
    ' ========== 恢复应用程序状态 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
End Sub


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·5·6·步骤·SearchSteps.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·5·6·步骤·SearchSteps"
Function SearchSteps(MatchID As String, InputAction As String, Optional InputTurn As String = "") As Boolean
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i%, j%, k%, m%, n%  ' 循环计数器
    Dim Arr As Variant          ' Steps数据数组
    SearchSteps = False
    
    ' ========== 查找最近的重要动作 ==========
    ' 获取Steps区域的所有步骤记录
    Arr = Down("Steps")
    
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
        If InputTurn = "" Or InputTurn = Arr(i, 1) Then
            If InputAction = Arr(i, 2) Then
                If InStr(Arr(i, 3), MatchID) > 0 Or MatchID = "AnyCard" Then
                    SearchSteps = True
                    Exit Function
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next i
End Function


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·8·5·对局·SwapMove.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·8·5·对局·SwapMove"
Sub SwapMove()
    Dim i%, j%
    Dim Str As String
    Dim Arr As Variant
    Dim Turn As String
    
    ' ========== 优化设置 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    
    
    ' 切换回合标识
    Turn = Down("Turn")
    If Left(Turn, 1) = "F" Then
        Turn = "S" & Val(Mid(Turn, 2, 10))
    Else
        Turn = "F" & Val(Mid(Turn, 2, 10)) + 1
    End If
    Call Up("Turn", Turn)
    
    ' 交换先后手标识
    If Left(Turn, 1) = "F" Then
        Call Up("FirstMove", "FirstMove")
    Else
        Call Up("FirstMove", "SecondMove")
    End If
    
    ' 交换生命值状态
    i = Down("Defense", "F1000")
    j = Down("MaxDefense", "F1000")
    Call Up("Defense", Down("Defense", "S1000"), "F1000")
    Call Up("MaxDefense", Down("MaxDefense", "S1000"), "F1000")
    Call Up("Defense", i, "S1000")
    Call Up("MaxDefense", j, "S1000")
    
    ' 交换能量点状态
    i = Val(Down("PP", "F1000"))
    j = Val(Down("MaxPP", "F1000"))
    Call Up("PP", Down("PP", "S1000"), "F1000")
    Call Up("MaxPP", Down("MaxPP", "S1000"), "F1000")
    Call Up("PP", i, "S1000")
    Call Up("MaxPP", j, "S1000")
    
    ' 交换进化点状态
    i = Down("EP", "F1000")
    j = Down("SEP", "F1000")
    Call Up("EP", Down("EP", "S1000"), "F1000")
    Call Up("SEP", Down("SEP", "S1000"), "F1000")
    Call Up("EP", i, "S1000")
    Call Up("SEP", j, "S1000")
        
    ' 清空步骤记录区域
    Call Up("StepsArea", Empty)
    Call UpSteps("TurnStart", Left(Turn, 1) & 1000)
    
    Call TurnStartFunction
    
    ' ========== 界面刷新 ==========
    ' 更新回合标题和界面显示
    If Left(Turn, 1) = "F" Then
        Call Up("Head", "先手第" & Mid(Turn, 2, 10) & "回合")
    Else
        Call Up("Head", "后手第" & Mid(Turn, 2, 10) & "回合")
    End If
    Call UIRefresh
    Call UpStepsArea
    
    Call CoverHands
CleanUp:
    ' ========== 恢复应用程序状态 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
End Sub

Attribute VB_Name = "UserInput"
Attribute VB_Base = "0{2D06F59E-5930-4B5D-BB8C-0433D5728015}{796EEC16-70D7-4B71-B436-E95B9ACC9141}"
Attribute VB_GlobalNameSpace = False
Attribute VB_Creatable = False
Attribute VB_PredeclaredId = True
Attribute VB_Exposed = False
Attribute VB_TemplateDerived = False
Attribute VB_Customizable = False
' 在用户窗体代码模块中
Public UserInput As String
Public Cancelled As Boolean

Private Sub UserForm_Initialize()
    Cancelled = True ' 默认设为取消状态
End Sub

Private Sub CommandButton_OK_Click()
    UserInput = TextBox1.Text
    Cancelled = False
    Me.Hide
End Sub

Private Sub CommandButton_Cancel_Click()
    Cancelled = True
    Me.Hide
End Sub

' 允许按Enter提交，按ESC取消
Private Sub TextBox1_KeyDown(ByVal KeyCode As MSForms.ReturnInteger, ByVal Shift As Integer)
    If KeyCode = 13 Then ' Enter键
        CommandButton_OK_Click
    ElseIf KeyCode = 27 Then ' ESC键
        CommandButton_Cancel_Click
    End If
End Sub

Attribute VB_Name = "·8·6·对局·OutputLog"
Function OutputLog(InputEffect As String, ThisCard As String)
    Dim i%, j%
    Dim Str As String
    Dim Arr As Variant
    
    Arr = Down("Log")
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
        If Arr(i, 1) = "" Then
            Str = Down("Turn")
            Arr(i, 1) = Str
            Arr(i, 2) = ThisCard
            Arr(i, 3) = InputEffect
            Exit For
        End If
    Next i
    Call Up("Log", Arr)
End Function


--------------------------------------------------
文件名: xl/vbaProject.bin | 模块名: ·8·7·对局·SwapCrest.bas
--------------------------------------------------
Attribute VB_Name = "·8·7·对局·SwapCrest"
Sub SwapCrest()
    ' ========== 变量声明 ==========
    Dim i%, j%
    Dim Str As String
    Dim Arr As Variant
    Dim Brr As Variant
    Dim AlliedMove As String
    Dim EnemyMove As String
    
    ' ========== 先后手确定 ==========
    ' 查询先后手并设置玩家标识
    If Down("FirstMove") = "FirstMove" Then
        AlliedMove = "F"
        EnemyMove = "S"
    Else
        AlliedMove = "S"
        EnemyMove = "F"
    End If
    
    ' ========== 优化设置 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    
    
    ' ========== 纹章区域刷新 ==========
    ' 下载纹章信息并格式化显示
    If Sheets("Main").Shapes("CrestBtn").TextFrame.Characters.Text = "查看对手纹章" Then
        Arr = Down("Crest", EnemyMove & "1000")
        Sheets("Main").Shapes("CrestBtn").TextFrame.Characters.Text = "查看自己纹章"
    Else
        Arr = Down("Crest", AlliedMove & "1000")
        Sheets("Main").Shapes("CrestBtn").TextFrame.Characters.Text = "查看对手纹章"
    End If
    
    ReDim Brr(1 To 3, 1 To 5)
    For i = 1 To UBound(Arr, 1)
        Brr(1, i) = DownByID(CStr(Arr(i, 2)), "Name")
        j = GetModel("Countdown", CStr(Arr(i, 2)), CStr(Arr(i, 5)))
        If j > 0 Then
            Brr(2, i) = Translate("Countdown") & j
            Arr(i, 5) = Replace(Arr(i, 5), "Countdown&" & j & "||", "")
        End If
        Brr(3, i) = SentenceTranslate(CStr(Arr(i, 5)))
    Next i
    Call Up("Crest", Brr)
    
CleanUp:
    ' ========== 恢复应用程序状态 ==========
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
End Sub
